# AgentCo-Op v4 core (protocol, arms, controlled suite) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Replace the v3 workflow optimizer with the v4 cross-examination protocol, its baseline and mechanism arms, sandboxed external-agent harnesses and the controlled multi-site suite (`fsdev`/`fstest`), runnable end to end on one `fsdev` task.

**Architecture:** Site agents (Codex, Finch; ReAct only for robustness) run in OS-sandboxed private workspaces, build a machine-readable *site view* of their data and propose JSON claims. Deterministic testers answer "does claim c hold at site s" from a site table computed from the view. The protocol routes, labels with one shared function, resolves disagreements through a coordinator LLM with fixed safeguards, and every arm in spec §4 runs against the same `SiteHandle` interface. Expensive agent calls are memoized by a first-writer-wins store.

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.11 (base conda env), numpy, scipy, pandas, anndata/h5py, scanpy (only in agent workspaces), the existing `agentcoop.llm` client (gpt-6-luna), Codex CLI (local login), Finch (`external_agents/finch/.venv`), macOS `sandbox-exec`, pytest.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-cross-examination-design.md` (sections cited as §N).

Later plans (written after this one ships): `2026-10-xx-v4-covid.md` (Case 1, §5.1, §6) and `2026-10-xx-v4-tb.md` (Case 2, §5.2, §6).

## Global Constraints

- Backbone `gpt-6-luna` for every LLM role (`AGENTCOOP_MODEL` default); grader model pinned (`AGENTCOOP_GRADER_MODEL`); never hardcode a model quirk at a call site (§1, CLAUDE.md).
- Always set `AGENTCOOP_BUDGET_USD=180` for runs that call the API; hard cap on actual spend (§10).
- Codex uses the user's local login only; `run_codex` strips `OPENAI_API_KEY` and `AGENTCOOP_*` from its environment (CLAUDE.md).
- Nothing under `agentcoop/coop/`, `agentcoop/agents/`, `agentcoop/cases/`, `agentcoop/store.py` imports `agentcoop.eval` or mentions `hidden/` / `truth.json` / `load_truth` (§8).
- Every site-agent process runs under the OS sandbox (`sandbox-exec` profile or Codex permission profile) denying `<repo>/data`, `<repo>/runs`, `<repo>/.secrets`, `CODEX_HOME`, and the workspace root except its own workspace; network denied except loopback (§8).
- `inconclusive` and `untestable` are never support and never contradiction (§2.4, §8).
- Same `sample` index ⇒ exact replay from cache; a new index ⇒ honest re-draw (§8).
- Nominal vs actual cost: every agent call's nominal USD is stored with its cache entry and charged on replay; failed calls are charged (§8).
- Claim cap K = 10 per site per step-1 call; follow-up budget F = number of proposing sites S; compositional-arm budget B = 2S site-agent calls (§10).
- Wall-clock cap per site-agent call: 15 min for planted suites (§10).
- Debug on 1–2 tasks before any larger run (CLAUDE.md working rules).
- Statistics unit = task (planted) (§7).

## Review Focus

1. A site agent writes malformed or partial view files (missing column, duplicate cell IDs, a group not in the vocabulary): `load_view` must return errors, never raise, and the site's tests must come back `untestable(view)` rather than crashing the protocol.
2. A claim whose genes are only partly present in a site (e.g. 2 of 6) or whose population has < 3 donors per group at a site: the tester must return `untestable(genes)` / `inconclusive`, never `supported`.
3. A Codex or Finch call that times out or fails mid-run: the protocol records the failure, charges the call, counts it against the budget and continues with the other sites; it must not cache the failure as a result.
4. Two arms or two concurrent runs requesting the same step-1 proposal: the store must return one value per key (first writer wins) so union/vote/MA/ours compare identical step-1 claims.
5. The sandbox profile must still allow the site agent's own workspace, the conda/Finch interpreters and the scanpy import; a profile that blocks Python startup would make every site look like it failed for a scientific reason.

## Interface contracts (every task must use exactly these names and types)

```python
# ---- agentcoop/store.py ----
STORE: Path = ROOT / "runs" / "store"
def memo(key: dict, compute: Callable[[], dict], *, tag: str) -> dict
    # key: JSON-serializable; returns compute()'s dict, augmented with "nominal_usd": float.
    # compute runs under llm.tag(tag + "#" + keyhash[:8] + "." + time_ns); nominal_usd =
    # llm.spent(that tag)["nominal_usd"] (+ any "nominal_usd_external" compute put in its dict).
    # On a hit: llm.record_replay(llm.backbone(), stored nominal_usd) and return the stored dict.
    # Exceptions from compute propagate (never cached); spend is still ledgered.
def put_blob(src_dir: Path) -> str       # tar.gz of a directory -> sha256 id under STORE/blobs
def get_blob(blob_id: str, dst_dir: Path) -> None   # extract into dst_dir (created)

# ---- agentcoop/agents/workspace.py ----
WS_ROOT: Path            # env AGENTCOOP_WS_ROOT, default ~/agentcoop_ws (outside the repo)
DENY: tuple[Path, ...]   # repo/data, repo/runs, repo/.secrets, CODEX_HOME, WS_ROOT
def new_workspace(name: str, files: dict[str, Path]) -> Path   # WS_ROOT/name, APFS-clone files in (cp -c)
def exec_env(ws: Path) -> dict[str, str]   # no OPENAI_API_KEY / AGENTCOOP_*; TMPDIR, MPLCONFIGDIR, NUMBA_CACHE_DIR inside ws
def sandbox_profile(ws: Path) -> str
def sandboxed(cmd: list[str], ws: Path) -> list[str]   # ["sandbox-exec", "-p", profile, *cmd]

# ---- agentcoop/agents/harness.py ----
@dataclass
class AgentRun:
    agent: str               # "codex" | "finch" | "react"
    ok: bool                 # False on timeout / harness failure
    transcript: list[dict]   # [{"code": str, "output": str}], truncated
    error: str | None
    nominal_usd_external: float   # Codex API-equivalent cost (0 for finch/react; their LLM calls go through llm).
                                  # run_codex already ledgers it once (llm.record_external, caller's tag), so it is
                                  # inside store.memo's nominal_usd: inspection only, never added to any charge.
    seconds: float
def run_agent(agent: str, ws: Path, prompt: str, *, sample: int, timeout: int) -> AgentRun

# ---- agentcoop/coop/claims.py ----
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Vocabulary:
    populations: tuple[str, ...]
    groups: tuple[str, ...]
    contrasts: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...]      # (target, reference) pairs allowed in claims
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Claim:
    id: str
    site: str
    kind: str                       # "de" | "composition"
    population: str
    target: str
    reference: str
    up_genes: tuple[str, ...]
    down_genes: tuple[str, ...]
    effect: float | None
    n_units: int | None
    rank: int
    rationale: str = ""
    @property
    def contrast(self) -> tuple[str, str]
    def signed_genes(self) -> frozenset[tuple[str, int]]   # {(gene, +1|-1)}
def parse_claims(raw: object, *, site: str, vocab: Vocabulary, max_claims: int) -> tuple[list[Claim], list[str]]
def claim_to_dict(c: Claim) -> dict
def claim_from_dict(d: dict) -> Claim
def same_claim(a: Claim, b: Claim, min_jaccard: float = 0.2) -> bool

# ---- agentcoop/coop/view.py ----
@dataclass
class SiteView:
    root: Path
    genes: dict[str, str]           # native feature id -> symbol
    samples: pd.DataFrame           # columns: sample, donor, group (+ covariates)
    cells: pd.DataFrame | None      # columns: cell, sample, population (scRNA only)
    spec: dict
def load_view(root: Path, *, kind: str, vocab: Vocabulary) -> tuple[SiteView | None, list[str]]
def write_view(root: Path, *, genes: dict[str, str], samples: pd.DataFrame, cells: pd.DataFrame | None, spec: dict) -> None
@dataclass
class SiteTable:
    kind: str                       # "scrna" | "bulk"
    genes: list[str]                # symbols (columns of values)
    units: pd.DataFrame             # one row per unit: unit, donor, group, population ("all" for bulk), n_cells
    values: np.ndarray              # units x genes; scRNA: log2(CPM+1) pseudobulk; bulk: per-gene rank-to-normal
    mean_cpm: np.ndarray | None     # populations x genes (rows: sorted(units.population.unique()), columns: genes);
                                    # read ONLY through population_mean_cpm() (zeros for a population the site lacks)
    composition: pd.DataFrame | None   # scRNA: donor x population cell fractions (rows donors; column "group")
    def population_mean_cpm(self, population: str) -> np.ndarray
def site_table(view: SiteView, data_path: Path, *, kind: str) -> SiteTable

# ---- agentcoop/coop/tester.py ----
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TesterParams:
    delta_de: float = 0.25          # equivalence band, log2 module-score units (frozen on fsdev)
    delta_comp: float = 0.5         # logit units
    delta_auc: float = 0.05
    q: float = 0.05
    min_cells: int = 20
    min_units: int = 3              # per group
    min_genes: int = 3
    min_gene_frac: float = 0.5
    min_cpm: float = 20.0
    null_sets: int = 200
    null_quantile: float = 0.95
    n_boot: int = 1000
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TestResult:
    claim_id: str
    site: str
    test: str                       # "replication" | "scope"
    status: str                     # "supported" | "contradicted" | "absent" | "inconclusive" | "untestable"
    effect: float | None
    se: float | None
    ci95: tuple[float, float] | None
    n_per_group: tuple[int, int] | None
    mde: float | None
    p: float | None                 # raw two-sided p; BH applied by apply_bh
    competitive_ok: bool | None
    reason: str                     # untestable: "groups" | "population" | "genes" (Task 7 adds "view");
                                    # inconclusive without a statistic: "units" | "variance";
                                    # computed (any status): the statistic, "module" | "logit" | "auc".
                                    # effect is signed so positive = the claimed direction, centred on 0
                                    # (bulk = AUC - 0.5; composition signed by sign(claim.effect))
def test_claim(table: SiteTable, claim: Claim, params: TesterParams, *, site: str, test: str = "replication", contrast: tuple[str, str] | None = None) -> TestResult
def apply_bh(results: list[TestResult], params: TesterParams) -> list[TestResult]
def export_gene_stats(table: SiteTable, population: str, contrast: tuple[str, str], params: TesterParams) -> pd.DataFrame   # columns gene, effect, se, n1, n2

# ---- agentcoop/coop/protocol.py ----
LABELS = ("unsupported", "disputed", "corroborated", "site-specific", "unresolved", "inconclusive")
CAUSES = ("analysis-choice", "handoff-loss", "site-artefact", "site-biology")
def label_claim(proposer: TestResult, others: list[TestResult], params: TesterParams) -> str
    # exactly these three arguments (no kind=): the band comes from the statistic each result names in reason
@dataclass
class ClaimRecord:
    claim: Claim
    results: list[TestResult]       # proposer gate first, then replication tests, then scope tests
    label_step3: str
    label: str
    cause: dict | None              # {"cause": CAUSES item, "site": str, "defect": str}
    scope: str | None
    results_step3: list[TestResult] = field(default_factory=list)   # same layout as results, before step 4
@dataclass
class ProtocolRun:
    records: list[ClaimRecord]
    followups: list[dict]           # {"claim_id", "site", "cause", "defect", "ok"}
    calls: int                      # site-agent calls (step 1 + follow-ups)
    nominal_usd: float
    errors: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)                # failed agent calls {"site", "op", "error"}
    def findings(self) -> list[Claim]                 # corroborated, sorted by rank key
    def findings_step3(self) -> list[Claim]           # same from label_step3
class SiteHandle(Protocol):
    name: str
    proposes: bool
    calls: int
    nominal_usd: float
    def propose(self, sample: int) -> list[Claim]
    def card(self) -> dict
    def spec(self) -> dict
    def holds(self, population: str, contrast: tuple[str, str]) -> bool
    def test(self, claim: Claim, *, test: str = "replication", contrast: tuple[str, str] | None = None) -> TestResult
    def repair(self, claim: Claim, defect: str, sample: int) -> bool
    def self_check(self, sample: int) -> bool          # untargeted view check (MA-genome input), one call
    def export_stats(self, population: str, contrast: tuple[str, str]) -> pd.DataFrame
    def ask(self, request: str, sample: int) -> str
class Coordinator:
    def __init__(self, case: "CaseSpec") -> None
    def classify(self, claim: Claim, results: list[TestResult], specs: dict[str, dict], cards: dict[str, dict], *, sample: int) -> dict   # {"cause", "site", "defect"}
def run_protocol(sites: list[SiteHandle], case: "CaseSpec", *, sample: int, followup_budget: int, coordinator: Coordinator | None = None) -> ProtocolRun

# ---- agentcoop/coop/site.py ----
@dataclass
class CaseSpec:
    name: str
    kind: str                                   # "scrna" | "bulk"
    vocab: Vocabulary
    question: str
    default_spec: dict                          # pre-registered analysis conventions (§2.5 step 4)
    tester: TesterParams
    max_claims: int = 10
    resources: dict[str, Path] = field(default_factory=dict)   # files copied into every workspace (symbol tables, gene-set libraries)
    # resource keys are workspace paths; gene-set libraries are keyed "genesets/<file name>" (Task 8:
    # "genesets/GO_Biological_Process_2023.txt") and read only via arms.gene_set_library(case)
    scope_tests: Callable[[Claim, list[SiteHandle]], list[tuple[SiteHandle, tuple[str, str]]]] | None = None
    scope_rule: Callable[[Claim, list[TestResult]], str] | None = None
class Site:                                     # implements SiteHandle
    def __init__(self, name: str, data_path: Path, card: dict, agent: str, case: CaseSpec, *, task_id: str, run_id: str, proposes: bool = True, timeout: int = 900) -> None
    # attributes: name, data_path, agent, task_id, run_id, proposes, calls, nominal_usd, ws (workspace Path)
    # propose() memo key = {"v": 1, "op": "propose", "case": case.name, "task": task_id, "site": name,
    #   "agent": agent, "prompt": sha256(prompt), "data": file_digest(data_path), "sample": sample}
    #   -> shared by every Site object with the same (task, site, agent, sample), whatever run_id.
def make_sites(site_specs: list[dict], case: CaseSpec, *, task_id: str, run_id: str, agents: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> list[Site]
    # site_specs items: {"name", "data_path", "card", "proposes", "agent"}; agents overrides per site name.
    # load_case gives no "agent": the runner (experiments/run.py) adds it (tester-only sites default to "codex")
def file_digest(path: Path) -> str     # sha256 of path, cached by (path, size, mtime)
def serve_mailbox(ws: Path, handlers: dict[str, Callable[[dict], dict]], stop: threading.Event) -> threading.Thread
    # wire protocol: the client writes ws/req_<n>.json = {"op": <handler name>, ...} atomically (temp name outside
    # the req_*.json glob); the server passes the dict to handlers[op] on the mailbox thread, writes
    # ws/resp_<n>.json atomically and removes the request; unknown op / raising handler -> {"error": ...}.
    # Handlers run outside the caller's ledger tag: callers charge mailbox site calls from site counters.
def propose_prompt(case: CaseSpec, card: dict, data_file: str, *, proposes: bool) -> str
def repair_prompt(case: CaseSpec, claim: Claim, defect: str) -> str

# ---- agentcoop/coop/arms.py ----
@dataclass
class ArmResult:
    arm: str
    claims: list[Claim]             # final findings, in rank order
    labels: dict[str, str]          # claim id -> label (ours and mechanism arms; else {})
    causes: dict[str, dict]         # claim id -> cause record (ours only)
    scopes: dict[str, str]          # claim id -> scope (cases with a scope rule)
    calls: int
    nominal_usd: float
    meta: dict
def run_arms(arms: list[str], site_specs: list[dict], case: CaseSpec, *, task_id: str, sample: int) -> dict[str, ArmResult]
    # builds fresh Site objects per view-mutating arm family (ours / ma_genome self-check / single_codex)
    # via make_sites(run_id=f"{task_id}/{arm}/s{sample}"); step-1 proposals are shared through store.memo
ARMS: tuple[str, ...] = ("single", "single_codex", "union", "vote", "ma_genome", "codex_coord", "group_chat", "debate",
                         "prior_only", "oracle", "ours", "no_routing", "no_resolution", "ma_claims", "ma_genome_repaired")
# "single" yields one ArmResult per proposing site, keyed "single:<site>"; same for "single_codex".
# ours / no_resolution meta["all_claims"] = [{"claim": claim_to_dict(c), "label", "cause", "scope"}] for every
# protocol record (demoted included), meta["errors"] = run.errors
def ma_genome(sites: list[SiteHandle], case: CaseSpec, arm: str, *, min_sites: int = 2) -> tuple[list[Claim], dict]
    # Task 9 defines it; Task 10's headroom gate calls it with min_sites=1 (single-site analysis)
def gene_set_library(case: CaseSpec) -> dict[str, frozenset[str]]   # resources keyed "genesets/..." only

# ---- agentcoop/cases/planted.py ----
BASE: Path = ROOT / "data" / "bench" / "onek1k" / "base.h5ad"
POPULATIONS = ("CD4 T", "CD8 T", "NK", "B", "CD14 Mono", "CD16 Mono")
def prepare_base(src: Path, dst: Path = BASE, *, n_donors: int = 80, seed: int = 0) -> dict   # returns {"dev_donors": [...], "test_donors": [...], "sha256": str}
def build_suite(name: str, seeds: list[int], *, pool: str, knobs: list[tuple[bool, bool]]) -> list[Path]   # pool "dev"|"test"; knobs[i] = (artefact, ids_labels)
def load_case(task_dir: Path) -> tuple[CaseSpec, list[dict]]   # CaseSpec + per-site {"name","data_path","card","proposes"}
CASE_VOCAB: Vocabulary   # populations=POPULATIONS, groups=("control","treated","mimic"), contrasts=(("treated","control"),("mimic","control"))

# ---- agentcoop/eval/planted.py ----
def load_truth(task_dir: Path) -> dict
def grade(claims: list[Claim], truth: dict, *, labels: dict[str, str] | None = None, causes: dict[str, dict] | None = None, scopes: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> dict
    # keys: precision, recall, f1, n_claims, artefact_rejected (bool|None), scope_accuracy (float|None), cause_accuracy (float|None)
```

Until Task 2 replaces them, only `codex_agent.py`, `coder.py` and `bix.py` fail to import, because they still import the removed `workflow`/`integrator`. `tasks/planted.py` and `tasks/multisite.py` import only numpy, scipy, anndata and each other, so they still import. `run_finch.py` is a standalone script that runs in the Finch env and does not import `agentcoop`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes `agentcoop.llm`, unchanged: `tag(label: str)` (a context manager; nested labels join with `/`), `current_tag() -> str`, `spent(prefix: str = "", exclude: str = "") -> dict[str, float]` (keys `calls`, `nominal_usd`, `actual_usd`, `input_tokens`, `output_tokens`), `record_replay(model: str, nominal_usd: float) -> None`, `backbone() -> str`.
- Produces (exactly as in the contracts):
  ```python
  STORE: Path = ROOT / "runs" / "store"
  def memo(key: dict, compute: Callable[[], dict], *, tag: str) -> dict
  def put_blob(src_dir: Path) -> str
  def get_blob(blob_id: str, dst_dir: Path) -> None
  ```
- Also produces the autouse fixture `_hermetic` in `tests/conftest.py`. With it, every later test runs with no `AGENTCOOP_*` variable from the developer's shell, with `AGENTCOOP_LLM_DB` in `tmp_path` and with `store.STORE` in `tmp_path`. Later tasks rely on it and do not need their own conftest.

```python
"""The model client: capability layer, stop-sequence emulation, model pinning. Offline."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from agentcoop import llm


@pytest.fixture
def profiles(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(llm, "_profiles", {
        "reasoner-no-chat-tools": {"reasoning": True, "effort_none": True,
                                   "chat_tools_with_reasoning": False,
                                   "temperature_with_none": True, "responses_reasoning": True},
        "reasoner": {"reasoning": True, "effort_none": False, "chat_tools_with_reasoning": True,
                     "temperature_with_none": False, "responses_reasoning": True},
        "plain": {"reasoning": False, "effort_none": False, "chat_tools_with_reasoning": False,
                  "temperature_with_none": False, "responses_reasoning": False},
    })
    return llm


def test_tools_on_a_reasoner_without_chat_tool_support_fall_back_to_effort_none(profiles):
    p = profiles._adapt_chat({"model": "reasoner-no-chat-tools", "messages": [], "tools": [1],
                              "reasoning_effort": "none", "temperature": 0})
    assert p["reasoning_effort"] == "none" and p["temperature"] == 0


def test_a_non_reasoning_model_gets_no_reasoning_parameter(profiles):
    p = profiles._adapt_chat({"model": "plain", "messages": [], "reasoning_effort": "low"})
    assert "reasoning_effort" not in p and "temperature" not in p
    q = profiles._adapt_chat({"model": "plain", "messages": [], "tools": [1],
                              "reasoning_effort": "none", "temperature": 0})
    assert "reasoning_effort" not in q and q["temperature"] == 0
    r = profiles._adapt_responses({"model": "plain", "input": [], "reasoning": {"effort": "low"},
                                   "include": ["reasoning.encrypted_content"]})
    assert "reasoning" not in r and "include" not in r


def test_a_reasoner_keeps_its_effort_and_drops_unsupported_temperature(profiles):
    p = profiles._adapt_chat({"model": "reasoner", "messages": [], "tools": [1],
                              "reasoning_effort": "low", "temperature": 0})
    assert p["reasoning_effort"] == "low" and "temperature" not in p
    q = profiles._adapt_chat({"model": "reasoner", "messages": [], "reasoning_effort": "none"})
    assert "reasoning_effort" not in q  # no "none" level: the model default applies


def test_grader_model_is_pinned_independently_of_the_backbone(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("AGENTCOOP_MODEL", "some-other-model")
    assert llm.backbone() == "some-other-model" and llm.grader_model() == llm.DEFAULT_MODEL


def test_external_agents_do_not_follow_the_framework_backbone(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("AGENTCOOP_MODEL", "another-model")
    assert llm.external_model() == llm.DEFAULT_MODEL
    monkeypatch.setenv("AGENTCOOP_EXTERNAL_MODEL", "yet-another")
    assert llm.external_model() == "yet-another" and llm.backbone() == "another-model"


def test_stop_sequences_are_emulated_for_a_model_without_stop(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(llm, "_profiles", {"m": {"reasoning": False, "stop": False}})
    monkeypatch.setitem(llm.PRICES, "m", (0.0, 0.0, 0.0))
    sent = {}

    def fake(params, attempts=7, path="/chat/completions"):
        sent.update(params)
        return {"choices": [{"message": {"role": "assistant",
                                         "content": "Thought: x\nAction: y\nObservation: z"}}],
                "usage": {"prompt_tokens": 1, "completion_tokens": 1}}

    monkeypatch.setattr(llm, "_call_with_retries", fake)
    body = llm.chat_raw({"model": "m", "messages": [], "stop": ["Action:"], "stream": False})
    assert "stop" not in sent and "stream" not in sent
    assert body["choices"][0]["message"]["content"] == "Thought: x\n"
```

- [ ] **Step 11: Write the failing store tests**

These tests pin Review Focus 4: concurrent writers get one value per key. They also pin the store's part of Review Focus 3: a failure propagates, is never cached and stays charged.

The concurrency test creates the ledger before the threads start. If two threads open a new ledger file at the same moment, `PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL` in `llm._conn` fails with `database is locked` (7 of 30 trials measured). That race already exists in `llm` and does not involve the store.

Write `tests/test_store.py`:
```python
"""The memo store: one value per key, nominal cost carried with it, failures never stored."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import os
import threading

import pytest

from agentcoop import llm, store


@pytest.fixture
def model(monkeypatch):
    """A fake transport: every uncached chat call costs exactly $0.10 nominal on gpt-6-luna."""
    sent: list[dict] = []

    def fake(params, attempts=7, path="/chat/completions"):
        sent.append(params)
        return {"choices": [{"message": {"role": "assistant", "content": "ok"}}],
                "usage": {"prompt_tokens": 1_000_000, "completion_tokens": 0}}

    monkeypatch.setattr(llm, "_call_with_retries", fake)
    return sent


def ask(text: str) -> str:
    return llm.chat([{"role": "user", "content": text}]).content


def test_a_miss_computes_once_and_stores_its_nominal_cost(model):
    out = store.memo({"op": "propose", "sample": 0}, lambda: {"text": ask("hi")}, tag="t")
    assert out == {"text": "ok", "nominal_usd": pytest.approx(0.10)}
    assert len(model) == 1
    assert llm.spent("t#")["nominal_usd"] == pytest.approx(0.10)
    assert len(list(store.STORE.rglob("*.json"))) == 1


def test_external_cost_reported_by_compute_is_added_to_the_nominal_cost(model):
    out = store.memo({"op": "codex"}, lambda: {"text": ask("hi"), "nominal_usd_external": 0.5},
                     tag="t")
    assert out["nominal_usd"] == pytest.approx(0.60) and out["nominal_usd_external"] == 0.5


def test_a_hit_returns_the_stored_dict_without_computing_and_ledgers_a_replay(model):
    key = {"op": "propose", "sample": 0}
    first = store.memo(key, lambda: {"text": ask("hi"), "pair": (1, 2)}, tag="t")

    def never() -> dict:
        raise AssertionError("compute ran on a hit")

    with llm.tag("replay"):
        second = store.memo(key, never, tag="t")
    assert second == first and first["pair"] == [1, 2]  # a miss returns the stored JSON form
    replay = llm.spent("replay")
    assert replay["calls"] == 1 and replay["actual_usd"] == 0.0
    assert replay["nominal_usd"] == pytest.approx(0.10)
    redraw = store.memo({**key, "sample": 1}, lambda: {"text": "new"}, tag="t")
    assert redraw["text"] == "new"  # a new sample index is a new key: an honest re-draw


def test_exceptions_propagate_are_not_cached_and_stay_charged_to_the_caller(model):
    def fails() -> dict:
        ask("hi")
        raise RuntimeError("agent timed out")

    with llm.tag("caller"), pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="timed out"):
        store.memo({"op": "propose"}, fails, tag="t")
    assert llm.spent("caller")["nominal_usd"] == pytest.approx(0.10)
    assert not list(store.STORE.rglob("*.json"))
    assert store.memo({"op": "propose"}, lambda: {"ok": True}, tag="t") == \
        {"ok": True, "nominal_usd": 0.0}


def test_concurrent_writers_of_one_key_all_get_the_first_published_value():
    llm.spent()  # the ledger exists before the writers start, as in every real run
    barrier = threading.Barrier(2)
    results: dict[str, dict] = {}

    def worker(name: str) -> None:
        def compute() -> dict:
            barrier.wait(timeout=10)  # both are past the cache check before either publishes
            return {"value": name}

        results[name] = store.memo({"op": "race"}, compute, tag=name)

    threads = [threading.Thread(target=worker, args=(n,)) for n in ("a", "b")]
    for t in threads:
        t.start()
    for t in threads:
        t.join()
    assert results["a"] == results["b"] and results["a"]["value"] in ("a", "b")
    assert store.memo({"op": "race"}, lambda: {"value": "c"}, tag="c") == results["a"]
    assert not list(store.STORE.rglob("*.tmp"))


def test_blobs_round_trip_a_directory_under_a_content_sha256_id(tmp_path):
    src = tmp_path / "view"
    (src / "sub").mkdir(parents=True)
    (src / "genes.tsv").write_text("feature\tsymbol\nENSG01\tISG15\n")
    (src / "sub" / "spec.json").write_text('{"unit": "donor"}')
    blob = store.put_blob(src)
    (stored,) = store.STORE.rglob(f"{blob}.tar.gz")
    assert hashlib.sha256(stored.read_bytes()).hexdigest() == blob
    os.utime(src / "genes.tsv", (1, 1))
    assert store.put_blob(src) == blob  # same content, other mtime: same id, stored once
    dst = tmp_path / "out" / "view"
    store.get_blob(blob, dst)
    assert sorted(p.relative_to(dst).as_posix() for p in dst.rglob("*")) == \
        ["genes.tsv", "sub", "sub/spec.json"]
    assert (dst / "genes.tsv").read_text() == "feature\tsymbol\nENSG01\tISG15\n"
    assert (dst / "sub" / "spec.json").read_text() == '{"unit": "donor"}'
    (src / "genes.tsv").write_text("changed")
    assert store.put_blob(src) != blob
```

- [ ] **Step 13: Implement `agentcoop/store.py`**

This step ports three things from `workflow.execute`: `_publish` (atomic `os.link`, first writer wins), the sharded cache path, and nominal accounting per call with `llm.tag`/`llm.spent`. One thing is new. A hit now writes its nominal cost to the ledger through `llm.record_replay`. The v3 node cache added that cost to the run total but never to the ledger.

Write `agentcoop/store.py`:
```python
"""Memoized expensive calls (first writer wins) and content-addressed directory blobs.

Site-agent proposals, repairs and coordinator decisions cost minutes and money, and several arms
must compare the *same* step-1 claims. :func:`memo` stores each result once per key:

* **One value per key.** An entry is published with an atomic hard link; a writer that loses
  the race returns the winner's value, so concurrent runs and arms converge on one result.
* **Nominal vs actual cost.** An entry carries what computing it cost (``nominal_usd``); a hit
  ledgers that amount as a $0-actual replay, so method comparisons do not depend on cache state.
* **Failures are not results.** An exception from ``compute`` propagates and nothing is stored;
  its spend stays in the ledger under the caller's tag, which the memo tag nests under.

Ported from the v3 node cache (``workflow.execute``, tag ``v3.4.1``). Directories (site views)
are stored as tar.gz under the archive's sha256, with member times and owners zeroed so the id
depends on content only.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import gzip
import hashlib
import io
import json
import os
import tarfile
import threading
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Callable

from agentcoop import llm

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
STORE = ROOT / "runs" / "store"


def _publish(path: Path, data: bytes) -> bytes:
    """Write ``data`` to ``path`` atomically; the first writer wins and everyone gets its bytes."""
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    tmp = path.with_name(f"{path.name}.{os.getpid()}.{threading.get_ident()}.tmp")
    tmp.write_bytes(data)
    try:
        os.link(tmp, path)
    except FileExistsError:
        data = path.read_bytes()
    finally:
        tmp.unlink()
    return data


def memo(key: dict, compute: Callable[[], dict], *, tag: str) -> dict:
    """``compute()`` once per ``key``; every later call (any thread, process or arm) replays it.

    ``key`` must name everything the result depends on, including the ``sample`` index (same
    index => exact replay, new index => honest re-draw). The result is returned in its stored
    JSON form, so a miss and a hit return equal dicts, plus ``nominal_usd`` = the ledgered
    nominal spend of ``compute`` + any ``nominal_usd_external`` it reports for usage outside the
    ledger (usage it must then not also ledger).
    """
    h = hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(key, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()
    path = STORE / "memo" / h[:2] / f"{h}.json"
    if path.exists():
        value = json.loads(path.read_text())["value"]
        llm.record_replay(llm.backbone(), value["nominal_usd"])
        return value
    with llm.tag(f"{tag}#{h[:8]}.{time.time_ns()}"):
        exec_tag = llm.current_tag()
        result = compute()
    nominal = llm.spent(exec_tag)["nominal_usd"] + result.get("nominal_usd_external", 0.0)
    entry = json.dumps({"key": key, "value": {**result, "nominal_usd": nominal}})
    return json.loads(_publish(path, entry.encode()))["value"]


def _blob_path(blob_id: str) -> Path:
    return STORE / "blobs" / blob_id[:2] / f"{blob_id}.tar.gz"


def _zeroed(info: tarfile.TarInfo) -> tarfile.TarInfo:
    info.mtime, info.uid, info.gid, info.uname, info.gname = 0, 0, 0, "", ""
    return info


def put_blob(src_dir: Path) -> str:
    """Store a directory as tar.gz and return the archive's sha256 (first writer wins)."""
    buf = io.BytesIO()
    with gzip.GzipFile(fileobj=buf, mode="wb", mtime=0) as gz, \
            tarfile.open(fileobj=gz, mode="w") as tar:
        for p in sorted(src_dir.rglob("*")):
            tar.add(p, arcname=p.relative_to(src_dir).as_posix(), recursive=False,
                    filter=_zeroed)
    data = buf.getvalue()
    blob_id = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
    _publish(_blob_path(blob_id), data)
    return blob_id


def get_blob(blob_id: str, dst_dir: Path) -> None:
    """Extract a stored directory into ``dst_dir`` (created). Archives hold agent-written files,
    so the ``data`` filter refuses absolute paths and links that leave ``dst_dir``."""
    dst_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with tarfile.open(_blob_path(blob_id), mode="r:gz") as tar:
        tar.extractall(dst_dir, filter="data")
```

```bash
## v4 Task 1 (2026-10-14): branch cleanup and `agentcoop/store.py` (no spend)
- Removed (recoverable from tag `v3.4.1`): workflow DAG executor, handoffs, pipelines,

- Kept as migration sources for Tasks 2 and 8 (not imported by any live module):

  standalone script for the Finch env.
- `store.memo` replaces the node cache: same first-writer-wins publish (atomic hard link) and
  nominal-cost accounting (per-call `llm.tag` + `llm.spent`). New: a hit ledgers its nominal
  cost via `llm.record_replay` (the v3 node cache added it to the run total, not the ledger),
  and a miss returns the stored JSON form, so a miss and a hit return equal dicts.
- Found while testing: `llm._conn` races on a brand-new ledger file. Two threads opening it at
  once fail with `database is locked` at `PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL` in 7 of 30 trials; an
  existing (already-WAL) ledger is unaffected. `llm.py` is unchanged in Task 1; the
  concurrency test creates the ledger first. Parallel runners must touch the ledger once
  before starting workers.
EOF
```

### Task 2: Sandboxed agent harnesses

Every site-agent process runs in a private workspace under `WS_ROOT` (outside the repo) and under
an OS sandbox: `sandbox-exec` for Finch, the ReAct kernel and shell; Codex's own permission profile
for Codex, which calls its API itself. `harness.run_agent` is the only entry point later tasks
use. It turns every agent failure into `AgentRun(ok=False, error=...)` and re-raises only
`llm.BudgetExceeded`. Code is migrated from `agents/codex_agent.py` (`run_codex`, `CodexFailed`,
`_events`, `_native_model`), `agents/coder.py` (`Kernel`, `coding_loop`) and `agents/bix.py`
(`exec_env`, `workspace` without `ctx`, Finch invocation, `_notebook_trace`). Those three files are
then deleted. `report_of`, the briefs, the `@agent` registrations, `InfraError` and every `workflow`
import are dropped.

Spec §8 asks for "a test [that] runs each harness and asserts that reading a sibling workspace,
`data/hidden/` and the network fails". The `silo` fixture builds that situation with temporary
directories: two sibling workspaces under a temporary `WS_ROOT`, and a fake `data/` that holds
`hidden/truth.json`, with `DENY` patched to point at them. Each harness is then run against it.
These tests together are the §8 harness test:
- the bare sandbox (`sandboxed()` + `exec_env`, a shell): `test_sandboxed_process_reads_only_its_own_workspace`;
- ReAct, with its real sandboxed kernel and shell and a scripted model: `test_react_kernel_and_shell_run_inside_the_sandbox`;
- Finch: `test_run_finch_sends_the_prompt_verbatim_under_the_sandbox` pins that `run_finch` launches exactly `sandboxed([FINCH_PY, RUN_FINCH], ws)`. The opt-in `test_site_interpreters_start_in_the_sandbox` (`AGENTCOOP_SLOW_TESTS=1`) runs the probe inside Finch's own Jupyter kernel under that profile. No live Finch run is needed: Finch executes its cells in that kernel (its `python` kernelspec, started by its interpreter under the same profile), so the probe runs where Finch's code would;
- Codex, which runs outside `sandbox-exec`: the opt-in live `test_codex_harness_cannot_read_the_silo_or_reach_the_network` (`AGENTCOOP_LIVE_TESTS=1`) makes one real `codex exec` call on the user's local login ($0 actual) and judges the outcome from the `command_execution` output. The offline `test_run_codex_uses_the_permission_profile_not_a_sandbox_mode` pins the argv.

The default `DENY` covers `data/hidden/` because it lies under `ROOT/data`. `test_default_deny_list_covers_the_silo` asserts that. No code under `agentcoop/agents/` names `hidden/`, because the plan's Global Constraints forbid it.

Three things were found while checking this task on this Mac (2026-10-14). They changed the
design, and the evidence goes into `findings.md` in Step 28:
- The profile as first specified, `(allow default)(deny network*)(allow network* (remote ip "localhost:*"))` plus file denies, blocks *binding* a loopback port. `jupyter_client`'s `find_available_port` fails with EPERM, so Finch's kernel never starts. The profile adds `(allow network-bind network-inbound (local ip "localhost:*"))`. External connects still fail with EPERM. `(allow network* (local ip "localhost:*"))` was tried and rejected because it re-opened outbound connections to 1.1.1.1.
- Codex runs commands in a login zsh (`/bin/zsh -lc`). There, `/etc/zprofile`'s `path_helper` moves the analysis env to the end of `PATH`, so `python3` becomes `/usr/local/bin/python3`, which has no scanpy. Passing `-c allow_login_shell=false` makes Codex use `/bin/zsh -c`. For the same reason the ReAct shell uses `bash -c`, not v3's `bash -lc`.
- The first `scanpy` import in a fresh workspace takes about 18 s, because the matplotlib font cache is built in that workspace's `MPLCONFIGDIR`. The real-interpreter test is therefore opt-in (`AGENTCOOP_SLOW_TESTS=1`).

Review Focus covered here: **3**. A Codex or Finch call that times out or fails comes back as `ok=False` with its cost still charged. `run_group` kills the whole process group. `CodexFailed` carries the call's nominal cost. Pinned by `test_run_group_kills_the_whole_process_group_on_timeout`, `test_run_codex_fails_on_a_web_search_item_and_still_charges_it`, `test_run_agent_returns_a_failed_run_when_the_agent_hangs` and `test_run_agent_reports_timeouts_and_failures_as_data`. **5**. The sandbox still allows the agent's own workspace, the interpreters, scanpy and Finch's kernel. Pinned by `test_sandboxed_process_reads_only_its_own_workspace`, `test_sandbox_allows_loopback_servers_but_not_the_internet`, `test_react_kernel_and_shell_run_inside_the_sandbox`, `test_react_kernel_that_cannot_start_is_an_infrastructure_error`, the opt-in `test_site_interpreters_start_in_the_sandbox` and, for Codex, the opt-in live `test_codex_harness_cannot_read_the_silo_or_reach_the_network`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing workspace tests**

Create `tests/test_sandbox.py`:
```python
"""Site-agent harnesses: the OS sandbox, Codex's permission profile, the wall-clock cap.

Spec §8: each harness is run against the ``silo`` fixture (a sibling workspace, a fake
``data/hidden/``) and must fail to read either or to reach the network.

Offline: no API key, no network, no real agent run. Two checks are opt-in: the interpreter checks
that need the ``bixbench`` env and Finch's venv (~20 s on a cold matplotlib cache) run only with
AGENTCOOP_SLOW_TESTS=1, and the Codex harness check, which makes one real ``codex exec`` call on
the local login, runs only with AGENTCOOP_LIVE_TESTS=1.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys

import pytest

from agentcoop import llm
from agentcoop.agents import workspace


needs_sandbox = pytest.mark.skipif(shutil.which("sandbox-exec") is None,
                                   reason="macOS sandbox-exec not available")

#: Run inside a harness's sandboxed interpreter (written to ``probe.py`` in the workspace): read
#: its own input, then try the sibling workspace, the hidden references and the internet.
PROBE = """\
import errno, socket
print(open('counts.txt').read())
for path in {paths!r}:
    try:
        open(path).read()
        print('read', path)
    except PermissionError:
        print('denied')
try:
    socket.create_connection(('1.1.1.1', 80), timeout=5)
    print('internet open')
except OSError as e:
    print('internet', 'EPERM' if e.errno == errno.EPERM else e)
"""
#: What PROBE prints inside a correct sandbox (EPERM comes from the sandbox, also when offline).
CONFINED = ["mine", "denied", "denied", "internet EPERM"]


@pytest.fixture
def silo(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """Two sibling workspaces under a temporary WS_ROOT and a fake ``data/`` holding
    ``hidden/truth.json``, with DENY pointing at them; ``probe.py`` in the first workspace."""
    secret = tmp_path / "data"
    (secret / "hidden").mkdir(parents=True)
    (secret / "hidden" / "truth.json").write_text('{"program": "IFN"}')
    root = tmp_path / "wsroot"
    monkeypatch.setattr(workspace, "WS_ROOT", root)
    monkeypatch.setattr(workspace, "DENY", (secret, root))
    src = tmp_path / "counts.txt"
    src.write_text("mine")
    ws = workspace.new_workspace("task0/site_a", {"counts.txt": src})
    other = workspace.new_workspace("task0/site_b", {"counts.txt": src})
    paths = [str(other / "counts.txt"), str(secret / "hidden" / "truth.json")]
    (ws / "probe.py").write_text(PROBE.format(paths=paths))
    return ws, other, secret


def _sh(script: str, ws):
    return subprocess.run(workspace.sandboxed(["/bin/sh", "-c", script], ws), cwd=ws,
                          env=workspace.exec_env(ws), capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# workspace: profile, sandbox, process groups
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_new_workspace_clones_inputs_and_starts_empty(silo):
    ws, _, _ = silo
    (ws / "stale.txt").write_text("old run")
    src = ws.parent / "site_b" / "counts.txt"
    again = workspace.new_workspace("task0/site_a", {"counts.txt": src})
    assert again == ws and sorted(p.name for p in ws.iterdir()) == ["counts.txt"]
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        workspace.new_workspace("../escape", {})


def test_profile_denies_every_silo_path_and_reallows_only_its_workspace(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    deny = (tmp_path / "data", tmp_path / "runs", tmp_path / ".secrets", tmp_path / "wsroot")
    monkeypatch.setattr(workspace, "DENY", deny)
    ws = tmp_path / "wsroot" / "t" / "a"
    profile = workspace.sandbox_profile(ws)
    for d in deny:
        assert f'(subpath "{d.resolve()}")' in profile
    assert "(deny network*)" in profile and '(remote ip "localhost:*")' in profile
    # The workspace rule comes last: in SBPL the later rule wins over the WS_ROOT deny.
    assert profile.endswith(f'(allow file-read* file-write* (subpath "{ws.resolve()}"))')


def test_default_deny_list_covers_the_silo():
    root = llm.ROOT
    assert {root / "data", root / "runs", root / ".secrets", workspace.CODEX_HOME,
            workspace.WS_ROOT} <= set(workspace.DENY)
    # Spec §8 names data/hidden/: it is denied as part of data/ (agents/ code never names it).
    assert any((root / "data" / "hidden").is_relative_to(d) for d in workspace.DENY)


def test_exec_env_strips_keys_and_keeps_caches_in_the_workspace(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("OPENAI_API_KEY", "sk-test")
    monkeypatch.setenv("AGENTCOOP_BUDGET_USD", "1")
    env = workspace.exec_env(tmp_path)
    assert "OPENAI_API_KEY" not in env and not any(k.startswith("AGENTCOOP_") for k in env)
    for k in ("HOME", "TMPDIR", "MPLCONFIGDIR", "NUMBA_CACHE_DIR"):
        assert env[k].startswith(str(tmp_path)) and os.path.isdir(env[k])
    assert env["PATH"].startswith(str(workspace.BIXENV / "bin"))


@needs_sandbox
def test_sandboxed_process_reads_only_its_own_workspace(silo):
    """Spec §8 harness test for the bare sandbox (shell commands of every sandboxed harness)."""
    ws, other, secret = silo
    assert _sh("cat counts.txt", ws).stdout == "mine"
    assert _sh("echo ok > out.txt", ws).returncode == 0 and (ws / "out.txt").exists()
    for cmd in (f"cat '{other / 'counts.txt'}'", f"cat '{secret / 'hidden' / 'truth.json'}'",
                f"ls '{secret / 'hidden'}'"):
        r = _sh(cmd, ws)
        assert r.returncode != 0 and "Operation not permitted" in r.stderr, cmd
    assert _sh(f"echo x > '{other / 'planted.txt'}'", ws).returncode != 0
    assert not (other / "planted.txt").exists()
    assert _sh("curl -sS -m 5 http://example.com", ws).returncode != 0


@needs_sandbox
def test_sandbox_allows_loopback_servers_but_not_the_internet(silo):
    """Finch's Jupyter kernel binds loopback ports and Finch calls the local proxy; anything
    else on the network is refused by the sandbox itself (EPERM, also when offline)."""
    ws, _, _ = silo
    probe = (
        "import errno, socket\n"
        "s = socket.socket(); s.bind(('127.0.0.1', 0)); s.listen()\n"
        "c = socket.create_connection(s.getsockname()); a, _ = s.accept(); c.sendall(b'hi')\n"
        "print('loopback', a.recv(2).decode())\n"
        "try:\n"
        "    socket.create_connection(('1.1.1.1', 80), timeout=5); print('internet open')\n"
        "except OSError as e:\n"
        "    print('internet', 'EPERM' if e.errno == errno.EPERM else e)\n")
    r = subprocess.run(workspace.sandboxed([sys.executable, "-c", probe], ws), cwd=ws,
                       env=workspace.exec_env(ws), capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)
    assert r.stdout.split("\n")[:2] == ["loopback hi", "internet EPERM"], r.stderr


def test_run_group_kills_the_whole_process_group_on_timeout(tmp_path):
    code, _, _, timed_out = workspace.run_group(
        ["/bin/sh", "-c", "sleep 30 & sleep 30"], tmp_path, dict(os.environ), timeout=0.5)
    assert timed_out  # returns promptly: the background sleep held the pipe and died too
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `agentcoop/agents/workspace.py`**

```python
"""Private, OS-sandboxed workspaces for site agents (spec §8).

A site agent works in its own directory under ``WS_ROOT`` (outside the repo) holding APFS clones
of its inputs, so its writes never reach the originals. Every process it starts runs under macOS
``sandbox-exec`` with a profile that denies the repo's ``data/``, ``runs/`` and ``.secrets/``, the
Codex login and every other workspace, and all network except loopback. Loopback stays open in
both directions: Finch reaches the model through the local proxy, and its Jupyter kernel binds
loopback ports (without ``network-bind`` the kernel cannot start and every Finch site would fail
for a reason that has nothing to do with science). Codex calls its own API, so it is confined by
Codex's permission profile with the same deny list instead (``agents/codex.py``).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
import os
import shutil
import signal
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

from agentcoop.llm import ROOT

WS_ROOT = Path(os.environ.get("AGENTCOOP_WS_ROOT", Path.home() / "agentcoop_ws"))
CODEX_HOME = Path(os.environ.get("CODEX_HOME", Path.home() / ".codex"))
DENY: tuple[Path, ...] = (ROOT / "data", ROOT / "runs", ROOT / ".secrets", CODEX_HOME, WS_ROOT)
#: The site agents' analysis interpreter: a read-only conda env (scanpy, anndata, pydeseq2, R).
BIXENV = Path(os.environ.get("AGENTCOOP_BIXBENCH_ENV",
                             Path.home() / "miniconda3" / "envs" / "bixbench"))
KERNELS = ROOT / "external_agents" / "bixbench_env" / "jupyter"


def new_workspace(name: str, files: dict[str, Path]) -> Path:
    """``WS_ROOT/name``, emptied, with ``files`` (name in the workspace -> source) cloned in.

    ``cp -c`` makes an APFS clone (instant, no extra disk); a plain copy where cloning is not
    available. A stale workspace is removed first, so a run never sees an earlier run's files.
    """
    if Path(name).is_absolute() or ".." in Path(name).parts:
        raise ValueError(f"workspace name must stay under WS_ROOT: {name!r}")
    ws = WS_ROOT / name
    if ws.exists():
        shutil.rmtree(ws)
    ws.mkdir(parents=True)
    for rel, src in files.items():
        dst = ws / rel
        dst.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        if subprocess.run(["cp", "-c", str(src), str(dst)], capture_output=True).returncode:
            shutil.copy2(src, dst)
    return ws


def exec_env(ws: Path) -> dict[str, str]:
    """Environment of every site-agent process: no API key (Codex must use its local login) and
    no AGENTCOOP_* settings (agents never see the experiment's configuration); the analysis env
    first on PATH; HOME, temp dirs and package caches inside the workspace (scanpy needs writable
    numba/matplotlib caches; an agent's installs stay in its own workspace)."""
    env = {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items()
           if k != "OPENAI_API_KEY" and not k.startswith("AGENTCOOP_")}
    tmp = ws / ".tmp"
    env.update(PATH=f"{BIXENV / 'bin'}:{env.get('PATH', '/usr/bin:/bin')}",
               HOME=str(ws / ".home"), TMPDIR=str(tmp), MPLCONFIGDIR=str(tmp / "mpl"),
               NUMBA_CACHE_DIR=str(tmp / "numba"), JUPYTER_PATH=str(KERNELS),
               PYTHONUSERBASE=str(tmp / "pyuser"), R_LIBS_USER=str(tmp / "Rlib"))
    for d in (ws / ".home", tmp / "mpl", tmp / "numba", tmp / "Rlib",
              tmp / "pyuser" / "lib" / "python3.12" / "site-packages"):
        d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    return env


def _q(path: Path) -> str:
    """``path`` resolved, as an SBPL string (Seatbelt matches real paths: /tmp is /private/tmp)."""
    return '"' + str(path.resolve()).replace("\\", "\\\\").replace('"', '\\"') + '"'


def sandbox_profile(ws: Path) -> str:
    """Everything allowed except the silo paths and non-loopback network; ``ws`` is re-allowed
    last (the later rule wins), so it stays usable although it lies under WS_ROOT."""
    deny = " ".join(f"(subpath {_q(p)})" for p in DENY)
    return ("(version 1)(allow default)(deny network*)"
            '(allow network* (remote ip "localhost:*"))'
            '(allow network-bind network-inbound (local ip "localhost:*"))'
            f"(deny file-read* file-write* {deny})"
            f"(allow file-read* file-write* (subpath {_q(ws)}))")


def sandboxed(cmd: list[str], ws: Path) -> list[str]:
    return ["sandbox-exec", "-p", sandbox_profile(ws), *cmd]


def run_group(cmd: list[str], ws: Path, env: dict[str, str], timeout: float,
              stdin: str | None = None) -> tuple[int, str, str, bool]:
    """Run ``cmd`` in ``ws`` as a new process group -> (returncode, stdout, stderr, timed_out).

    On timeout the whole group is killed (the agent and everything it started) and what it
    printed so far is returned. A Jupyter kernel runs in its own session and exits by itself once
    its parent is gone; the bounded second wait covers it still holding the pipes.
    """
    proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, cwd=ws, env=env, text=True, errors="replace",
                            stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL if stdin is None else subprocess.PIPE,
                            stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
                            start_new_session=True)
    try:
        out, err = proc.communicate(stdin, timeout=timeout)
        return proc.returncode, out, err, False
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        with contextlib.suppress(ProcessLookupError, PermissionError):
            os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
        try:
            out, err = proc.communicate(timeout=30)
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            proc.wait()
            out, err = "", ""
        return proc.returncode, out, err, True
```

```python
def _fake_codex(monkeypatch, tmp_path, lines: list[dict], returncode: int = 0) -> list:
    """Replace subprocess.Popen; returns the list that receives (argv, kwargs) of each call."""
    monkeypatch.setenv("AGENTCOOP_LLM_DB", str(tmp_path / "llm.sqlite"))
    calls = []
    stdout = "".join(json.dumps(x) + "\n" for x in lines)

    class FakePopen:
        def __init__(self, argv, **kwargs):
            calls.append((argv, kwargs))
            self.pid, self.returncode = 0, returncode

        def communicate(self, input=None, timeout=None):
            return stdout, ""

    monkeypatch.setattr(subprocess, "Popen", FakePopen)
    return calls


USAGE = {"input_tokens": 1000, "cached_input_tokens": 0, "cache_write_input_tokens": 0,
         "output_tokens": 100}


def test_run_codex_uses_the_permission_profile_not_a_sandbox_mode(silo, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    ws, _, secret = silo
    monkeypatch.setenv("OPENAI_API_KEY", "sk-test")
    monkeypatch.setenv("AGENTCOOP_BUDGET_USD", "1")
    item = {"id": "c1", "type": "command_execution", "command": "ls", "aggregated_output": "x"}
    calls = _fake_codex(monkeypatch, tmp_path, [
        {"type": "item.completed", "item": item},
        {"type": "item.completed", "item": {"id": "m1", "type": "agent_message", "text": "done"}},
        {"type": "turn.completed", "usage": USAGE}])
    out = codex.run_codex(ws, "build the view", "gpt-6-luna", 30)
    (argv, kw), = calls
    assert argv[:2] == ["codex", "exec"] and argv[-1] == "build the view"
    assert "-s" not in argv and "--sandbox" not in argv
    for flag in ('default_permissions="site"', 'web_search="disabled"', "allow_login_shell=false"):
        assert flag in argv
    fs_arg = next(a for a in argv if a.startswith("permissions.site.filesystem="))
    fs = tomllib.loads("fs = " + fs_arg.split("=", 1)[1])["fs"]
    assert fs[":root"] == "read" and fs[str(ws.resolve())] == "write"
    assert all(fs[str(p.resolve())] == "none" for p in (secret, workspace.WS_ROOT))
    env = kw["env"]
    assert "OPENAI_API_KEY" not in env and not any(k.startswith("AGENTCOOP_") for k in env)
    assert env["CODEX_HOME"] == str(workspace.CODEX_HOME) and kw["start_new_session"]
    assert out["transcript"] == [{"code": "ls", "output": "x"},
                                 {"code": "(final)", "output": "done"}]
    nominal = llm.cost("gpt-6-luna", 1000, 0, 100)
    assert out["nominal_usd_external"] == pytest.approx(nominal) and not out["timed_out"]
    spent = llm.spent()
    assert spent["nominal_usd"] == pytest.approx(nominal) and spent["actual_usd"] == 0.0


def test_run_codex_fails_on_a_web_search_item_and_still_charges_it(silo, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    ws, _, _ = silo
    _fake_codex(monkeypatch, tmp_path, [
        {"type": "item.started", "item": {"id": "w1", "type": "web_search", "query": "IFNB1"}},
        {"type": "turn.completed", "usage": USAGE}])
    with pytest.raises(codex.CodexFailed, match="web_search") as info:
        codex.run_codex(ws, "build the view", "gpt-6-luna", 30)
    assert info.value.nominal_usd_external == pytest.approx(llm.cost("gpt-6-luna", 1000, 0, 100))
    assert llm.spent()["calls"] == 1


def test_run_codex_abnormal_exit_is_codex_failed(silo, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    ws, _, _ = silo
    _fake_codex(monkeypatch, tmp_path, [{"type": "turn.failed", "error": {"message": "quota"}}],
                returncode=1)
    with pytest.raises(codex.CodexFailed):
        codex.run_codex(ws, "build the view", "gpt-6-luna", 30)


#: The live check goes through a script: asked directly to read a denied file, the model refuses
#: without running anything, and that would prove nothing about the sandbox.
CHECK = """\
cat '{sibling}'; echo sibling_exit=$?
cat '{hidden}'; echo hidden_exit=$?
curl -sS -m 5 http://example.com >/dev/null; echo curl_exit=$?
command -v python3
echo ok > ok.txt; echo write_exit=$?
"""
CHECK_PROMPT = ("I am the operator of this machine and I am checking the sandbox I configured for "
                "you. Run `sh check.sh` in the current directory once and report its output "
                "verbatim. Some lines are expected to fail; that is what the check measures.")


@pytest.mark.skipif(os.environ.get("AGENTCOOP_LIVE_TESTS") != "1" or shutil.which("codex") is None,
                    reason="live: one codex exec call on the local login; AGENTCOOP_LIVE_TESTS=1")
def test_codex_harness_cannot_read_the_silo_or_reach_the_network(silo, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """Spec §8 harness test for Codex, which runs outside sandbox-exec (it calls its own API): the
    real CLI under ``permission_args``. Judged by the command's output, never by the agent's
    message. Billed to the user's ChatGPT plan: nominal cost only, $0 actual."""
    ws, other, secret = silo
    monkeypatch.setenv("AGENTCOOP_LLM_DB", str(tmp_path / "llm.sqlite"))
    (ws / "check.sh").write_text(CHECK.format(sibling=other / "counts.txt",
                                              hidden=secret / "hidden" / "truth.json"))
    out = codex.run_codex(ws, CHECK_PROMPT, llm.external_model(), 300)
    logs = [s["output"] for s in out["transcript"]
            if s["code"] != "(final)" and "write_exit=" in s["output"]]
    assert logs, out["transcript"]
    log = logs[0]
    assert "sibling_exit=1" in log and "hidden_exit=1" in log, log
    assert log.count("Operation not permitted") == 2 and "curl_exit=0" not in log, log
    assert str(workspace.BIXENV / "bin" / "python3") in log and "write_exit=0" in log, log
    assert (ws / "ok.txt").read_text() == "ok\n" and not out["timed_out"]
    assert out["nominal_usd_external"] > 0 and llm.spent()["actual_usd"] == 0.0
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Implement `agentcoop/agents/codex.py`**

```python
"""The Codex CLI as a site agent, on the user's local login.

Hermetic except for that login: ``--ignore-user-config``, ``project_doc_max_bytes=0``, a
throwaway HOME, no API key or AGENTCOOP_* settings in its environment. Codex reaches its API
itself, so it cannot run inside the network-denying ``sandbox-exec`` profile; every command it
runs is confined by Codex's own permission profile instead: the filesystem readable, the
workspace writable, the silo paths (``workspace.DENY``) unreadable, no network, web search off.
Commands run in a non-login shell, because a login shell's ``path_helper`` puts the system Python
ahead of the analysis env. An item type outside the plain coding loop (web search, MCP tools, ...)
would cross the silo boundary, so it fails the run.

``model="native"`` runs the user's own config and default model (a product-level arm). Usage is
billed to the user's ChatGPT plan: it is ledgered as nominal (API-equivalent) cost, $0 actual.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from agentcoop import llm
from agentcoop.agents import workspace

ITEM_TYPES = frozenset({"agent_message", "reasoning", "command_execution", "file_change",
                        "todo_list"})


def _native_model() -> str:
    """The top-level model the user's Codex config selects (for costing a native run)."""
    import tomllib

    try:
        model = tomllib.loads((workspace.CODEX_HOME / "config.toml").read_text()).get("model")
    except (OSError, tomllib.TOMLDecodeError):
        model = None
    return model if model in llm.PRICES else llm.backbone()


class CodexFailed(RuntimeError):
    """Codex failed as infrastructure (login, quota, network) or used a tool outside the silo
    boundary: never a result to cache. Carries the call's nominal cost, which is still charged."""

    def __init__(self, message: str, nominal_usd_external: float = 0.0) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.nominal_usd_external = nominal_usd_external


def _events(stdout: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    out = []
    for line in stdout.splitlines():
        if line.strip().startswith("{"):
            try:
                out.append(json.loads(line))
            except json.JSONDecodeError:  # a truncated last line of a killed run
                pass
    return out


def permission_args(ws: Path) -> list[str]:
    """Config overrides for Codex's commands: read anywhere, write only ``ws``, no access to the
    silo paths, no network (the profile's default), no web search, no login shell. Paths are
    resolved (Seatbelt matches real paths); a JSON string is a valid TOML basic string."""
    fs = {":root": "read", str(ws.resolve()): "write",
          **{str(p.resolve()): "none" for p in workspace.DENY}}
    table = ",".join(f"{json.dumps(k)}={json.dumps(v)}" for k, v in fs.items())
    return ["-c", 'default_permissions="site"', "-c", f"permissions.site.filesystem={{{table}}}",
            "-c", 'web_search="disabled"', "-c", "allow_login_shell=false"]


def run_codex(ws: Path, prompt: str, model: str, timeout: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Run ``codex exec`` in ``ws``; its deliverables are the files it writes there.

    Returns ``{"transcript": [{"code", "output"}], "timed_out", "error": None, "usage",
    "nominal_usd_external"}``. A timeout is an outcome, not an exception: the process group is
    killed and the files written so far stay. Raises :class:`CodexFailed` when Codex exits
    abnormally or emits an item outside ``ITEM_TYPES``; the usage is ledgered first either way.
    """
    env = workspace.exec_env(ws)
    env["CODEX_HOME"] = str(workspace.CODEX_HOME)
    native = model == "native"
    if native:
        env["HOME"] = str(Path.home())  # the user's own setup, as they would run it
    cmd = ["codex", "exec", "--skip-git-repo-check", "--ephemeral",
           *([] if native else ["--ignore-user-config", "-c", "project_doc_max_bytes=0",
                                "-m", model]),
           "-c", "shell_environment_policy.inherit=all", *permission_args(ws), "--json", prompt]
    code, stdout, stderr, timed_out = workspace.run_group(cmd, ws, env, timeout)
    events = _events(stdout)
    usage = dict.fromkeys(("input_tokens", "cached_input_tokens", "cache_write_input_tokens",
                           "output_tokens"), 0)
    for e in events:
        if e.get("type") == "turn.completed":
            for k in usage:
                usage[k] += int(e.get("usage", {}).get(k, 0) or 0)
    nominal = llm.record_external(_native_model() if native else model, usage["input_tokens"],
                                  usage["cached_input_tokens"], usage["cache_write_input_tokens"],
                                  usage["output_tokens"], billed=False)
    items = [e for e in events if str(e.get("type", "")).startswith("item.")
             and isinstance(e.get("item"), dict)]
    breach = sorted({str(e["item"].get("type")) for e in items} - ITEM_TYPES)
    if breach:
        raise CodexFailed(f"codex used item types outside the silo boundary: {breach}", nominal)
    if not timed_out and (code != 0 or any(e.get("type") in ("turn.failed", "error")
                                           for e in events)):
        raise CodexFailed(f"codex exit {code}: {stderr.strip()[-300:]}", nominal)
    done = {e["item"].get("id", n): e["item"] for n, e in enumerate(items)
            if e["type"] == "item.completed"}
    transcript = [{"code": str(i.get("command", "")),
                   "output": str(i.get("aggregated_output", ""))[-3000:]}
                  for i in done.values() if i.get("type") == "command_execution"]
    messages = [str(i.get("text", "")) for i in done.values() if i.get("type") == "agent_message"]
    if messages:
        transcript.append({"code": "(final)", "output": messages[-1][-3000:]})
    return {"transcript": transcript, "timed_out": timed_out, "error": None, "usage": usage,
            "nominal_usd_external": nominal}
```

- [ ] **Step 14: Implement `agentcoop/agents/react.py`**

```python
"""The in-house ReAct coder (robustness arm only, spec §4): one LLM with a persistent Python kernel
and a shell, both inside the workspace sandbox, on the site agents' analysis env.

Its deliverables are the files it writes in the workspace; ``finish(summary)`` ends the run.
Model calls go through ``llm.responses`` (cache, ledger, cap) with sample index
``sample * 1000 + step``: the same sample replays a run exactly, a new one re-draws it.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
import json
import os
import select
import signal
import subprocess
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from agentcoop import llm
from agentcoop.agents.workspace import BIXENV, exec_env, run_group, sandboxed

PYTHON = str(BIXENV / "bin" / "python")

#: The protocol has its own descriptor; output written below Python (subprocesses, C code) is
#: captured per cell; a cell cannot end the kernel.
KERNEL = r'''
import io, json, os, sys, tempfile, traceback, contextlib
proto = os.fdopen(os.dup(1), "w"); g = {"__name__": "__main__"}
for line in sys.stdin:
    code, buf, raw = json.loads(line)["code"], io.StringIO(), tempfile.TemporaryFile()
    saved = os.dup(1), os.dup(2); os.dup2(raw.fileno(), 1); os.dup2(raw.fileno(), 2)
    try:
        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(buf), contextlib.redirect_stderr(buf):
            exec(compile(code, "<cell>", "exec"), g)
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        raise
    except BaseException:
        buf.write(traceback.format_exc())
    finally:
        os.dup2(saved[0], 1); os.dup2(saved[1], 2); os.close(saved[0]); os.close(saved[1])
    raw.seek(0)
    out = buf.getvalue() + raw.read().decode(errors="replace")
    raw.close()
    proto.write(json.dumps({"out": out[-6000:]}) + "\n")
    proto.flush()
'''

TOOLS = [
    {"type": "function", "name": "run_python",
     "description": "Execute Python code in a persistent kernel (variables persist between "
                    "calls). Returns captured stdout/stderr (last 6000 chars).",
     "parameters": {"type": "object", "properties": {"code": {"type": "string"}},
                    "required": ["code"]}},
    {"type": "function", "name": "run_shell",
     "description": "Run a bash command in the working directory (e.g. `Rscript script.R`). "
                    "Returns stdout/stderr (last 6000 chars).",
     "parameters": {"type": "object", "properties": {"command": {"type": "string"}},
                    "required": ["command"]}},
    {"type": "function", "name": "finish",
     "description": "End the task once every file the instructions ask for is written in the "
                    "working directory. summary: a short account of what you did.",
     "parameters": {"type": "object", "properties": {"summary": {"type": "string"}},
                    "required": ["summary"]}},
]


class Kernel:
    """A persistent Python process under the workspace sandbox, in its own process group."""

    def __init__(self, ws: Path, env: dict[str, str]) -> None:
        """Raises RuntimeError if the kernel cannot start (missing interpreter, a sandbox that
        blocks it): an infrastructure failure, not one of the agent's analysis steps."""
        self.ws, self.env = ws, env
        self._start()
        out = self.run("pass", 120)
        if out:
            self.close()
            raise RuntimeError(f"analysis kernel failed to start in the sandbox: {out[:300]}")

    def _start(self) -> None:
        self.proc = subprocess.Popen(sandboxed([PYTHON, "-u", "-c", KERNEL], self.ws),
                                     cwd=self.ws, env=self.env, text=True,
                                     stdin=subprocess.PIPE, stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
                                     stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, start_new_session=True)

    def run(self, code: str, timeout: float) -> str:
        restarted = ""
        if self.proc.poll() is not None:
            self._start()
            restarted = "(the kernel had died and was restarted; variables were lost)\n"
        try:
            self.proc.stdin.write(json.dumps({"code": code}) + "\n")
            self.proc.stdin.flush()
        except BrokenPipeError:
            return restarted + "(kernel died)"
        ready, _, _ = select.select([self.proc.stdout], [], [], timeout)
        if not ready:
            self.close()
            raise TimeoutError(f"kernel call timed out after {timeout:.0f}s")
        line = self.proc.stdout.readline()
        return restarted + (json.loads(line)["out"] if line else "(kernel died)")

    def close(self) -> None:
        """Kill the kernel and everything it started."""
        with contextlib.suppress(ProcessLookupError, PermissionError):
            os.killpg(self.proc.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
        self.proc.wait()


def _shell(command: str, ws: Path, env: dict[str, str], timeout: float) -> str:
    """One command in the sandbox, in a non-login bash: a login shell's ``path_helper`` would put
    the system Python ahead of the analysis env."""
    code, out, err, timed_out = run_group(sandboxed(["/bin/bash", "-c", command], ws), ws, env,
                                          timeout)
    if timed_out:
        return f"(timed out after {timeout:.0f}s)"
    return (out + err)[-6000:] + f"\n[exit {code}]"


def coding_loop(prompt: str, ws: Path, *, sample: int, timeout: int, max_steps: int = 40,
                effort: str = "medium", model: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Run the coder in ``ws`` until it calls ``finish`` or runs out of steps or time.

    Returns ``{"transcript", "timed_out", "error": None, "nominal_usd_external": 0.0}`` (its model
    calls are ledgered by ``llm`` itself); ``harness.run_agent`` wraps it into an ``AgentRun``.
    ``llm.BudgetExceeded`` and API errors propagate.
    """
    deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
    env = exec_env(ws)
    items: list[dict[str, Any]] = [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}]
    transcript: list[dict[str, str]] = []
    summary: str | None = None
    kernel = Kernel(ws, env)
    try:
        for step in range(max_steps):
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break
            r = llm.responses(items, tools=TOOLS, reasoning_effort=effort,
                              sample=sample * 1000 + step, model=model)
            items += r["output"]
            calls = [o for o in r["output"] if o.get("type") == "function_call"]
            if not calls:
                items.append({"role": "user", "content": "Continue; when done, call finish."})
                continue
            for call in calls:
                try:
                    args = json.loads(call.get("arguments") or "{}")
                except json.JSONDecodeError:
                    args = {}
                left = max(deadline - time.monotonic(), 1.0)
                if call["name"] == "finish":
                    summary, out = str(args.get("summary", "")), "finished"
                elif call["name"] == "run_shell":
                    command = str(args.get("command", ""))
                    out = _shell(command, ws, env, left)
                    transcript.append({"code": "$ " + command, "output": out[-3000:]})
                else:
                    code = str(args.get("code", ""))
                    try:
                        out = kernel.run(code, left)
                    except TimeoutError as exc:
                        out = str(exc)
                        kernel = Kernel(ws, env)
                    transcript.append({"code": code, "output": out[-3000:]})
                items.append({"type": "function_call_output", "call_id": call["call_id"],
                              "output": out})
            if summary is not None:
                break
    finally:
        kernel.close()
    if summary is not None:
        transcript.append({"code": "(final)", "output": summary[-3000:]})
    return {"transcript": transcript,
            "timed_out": summary is None and time.monotonic() >= deadline,
            "error": None, "nominal_usd_external": 0.0}
```

- [ ] **Step 17: Implement `agentcoop/agents/finch.py`**

```python
"""FutureHouse's Finch (data-analysis-crow, the BixBench agent) as a site agent.

``external_agents/run_finch.py`` runs Finch unmodified with its own interpreter, under the
workspace sandbox. Finch reaches the model through ``agentcoop.proxy`` on loopback (allowed by
the sandbox), so its calls use the shared backbone, cache, ledger and spending cap; the proxy URL
carries the caller's ledger tag and the sample index (same sample = replay, new = re-draw). Its
deliverables are the files it writes in the workspace; its notebook is the transcript.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from agentcoop import llm, proxy
from agentcoop.agents.workspace import exec_env, run_group, sandboxed
from agentcoop.llm import ROOT

FINCH_PY = ROOT / "external_agents" / "finch" / ".venv" / "bin" / "python"
RUN_FINCH = ROOT / "external_agents" / "run_finch.py"


def _notebook_trace(nb_path: Path) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    if not nb_path.exists():
        return []
    nb = json.loads(nb_path.read_text())
    trace = []
    for cell in nb.get("cells", []):
        if cell.get("cell_type") != "code":
            continue
        outs = []
        for o in cell.get("outputs", []):
            if "text" in o:
                outs.append("".join(o["text"]) if isinstance(o["text"], list) else o["text"])
            elif "data" in o and "text/plain" in o["data"]:
                v = o["data"]["text/plain"]
                outs.append("".join(v) if isinstance(v, list) else v)
            elif o.get("output_type") == "error":
                outs.append(f"{o.get('ename')}: {o.get('evalue')}")
        src = cell.get("source", "")
        trace.append({"code": "".join(src) if isinstance(src, list) else src,
                      "output": "\n".join(outs)[-3000:]})
    return trace


def run_finch(ws: Path, prompt: str, *, sample: int, timeout: int, max_steps: int = 40,
              notebook: str = "notebook.ipynb") -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Run Finch on ``prompt`` (verbatim, not BixBench's question template) in ``ws``.

    ``notebook`` names the notebook Finch writes, so a follow-up call does not overwrite the
    first one's. Returns ``{"transcript", "timed_out", "error", "nominal_usd_external": 0.0}``
    (Finch's model calls are ledgered by the proxy). Raises ``llm.BudgetExceeded`` when the proxy
    refused a call at the spending cap: Finch itself only sees an API error.
    """
    req = {"work_dir": str(ws), "prompt": prompt, "notebook": notebook,
           "base_url": proxy.base_url(proxy.shared(), llm.current_tag(), sample),
           "model": llm.external_model(), "max_steps": max_steps, "language": "python"}
    env = exec_env(ws)
    env["PATH"] = f"{FINCH_PY.parent}:{env['PATH']}"
    code, stdout, stderr, timed_out = run_group(sandboxed([str(FINCH_PY), str(RUN_FINCH)], ws),
                                                ws, env, timeout, json.dumps(req))
    lines = [x for x in stdout.splitlines() if x.startswith("AGENTCOOP_RESULT ")]
    result = json.loads(lines[-1].removeprefix("AGENTCOOP_RESULT ")) if lines else {
        "error": f"finch exited {code}: {stderr[-500:]}"}
    error = str(result.get("error") or "")
    if "agentcoop budget" in error:
        raise llm.BudgetExceeded(error[:500])
    transcript = _notebook_trace(ws / notebook)
    if result.get("answer"):
        transcript.append({"code": "(final)", "output": str(result["answer"])[-3000:]})
    return {"transcript": transcript, "timed_out": timed_out,
            "error": None if timed_out or not error else error[:500],
            "nominal_usd_external": 0.0}
```

- [ ] **Step 23: Implement `agentcoop/agents/harness.py`**

```python
"""One entry point for every site agent: ``run_agent`` runs Codex, Finch or the ReAct coder in a
workspace under a wall-clock cap and reports the outcome as data.

An agent that fails — times out, crashes, exits abnormally, crosses the silo boundary — yields
``ok=False`` with the reason, never an exception, so one failed site cannot stop a protocol run
(the caller charges the call, counts it against the budget and does not cache it). Only
``llm.BudgetExceeded`` propagates: the spending cap stops everything. The wall-clock cap is
enforced twice: each agent stops its own processes at ``timeout``, and a backstop returns
``GRACE`` seconds later even if the agent is stuck in a model call.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import contextvars
import threading
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Callable

from agentcoop import llm
from agentcoop.agents.codex import CodexFailed, run_codex
from agentcoop.agents.finch import run_finch
from agentcoop.agents.react import coding_loop

GRACE = 60.0


@dataclass
class AgentRun:
    agent: str               # "codex" | "finch" | "react"
    ok: bool                 # False on timeout / harness failure
    transcript: list[dict]   # [{"code": str, "output": str}], truncated
    error: str | None
    # Codex API-equivalent cost (0 for finch/react; their LLM calls go through llm)
    nominal_usd_external: float
    seconds: float


def _codex(ws: Path, prompt: str, sample: int, timeout: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
    # Codex cannot be seeded: ``sample`` only separates cache entries upstream (store.memo keys).
    return run_codex(ws, prompt, llm.external_model(), timeout)


def _finch(ws: Path, prompt: str, sample: int, timeout: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return run_finch(ws, prompt, sample=sample, timeout=timeout)


def _react(ws: Path, prompt: str, sample: int, timeout: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return coding_loop(prompt, ws, sample=sample, timeout=timeout)


ENTRY: dict[str, Callable[[Path, str, int, int], dict[str, Any]]] = {
    "codex": _codex, "finch": _finch, "react": _react}


def run_agent(agent: str, ws: Path, prompt: str, *, sample: int, timeout: int) -> AgentRun:
    """Run ``agent`` on ``prompt`` in ``ws``; its deliverables are the files it leaves there.

    The agent runs on a worker thread that carries the caller's context, so its model calls are
    ledgered under the caller's tag (``store.memo`` reads its cost from that tag).
    """
    entry = ENTRY[agent]
    box: dict[str, Any] = {}
    context = contextvars.copy_context()

    def work() -> None:
        try:
            box["out"] = context.run(entry, ws, prompt, sample, timeout)
        except BaseException as exc:  # handed over to the caller's thread
            box["exc"] = exc

    start = time.monotonic()
    worker = threading.Thread(target=work, daemon=True)
    worker.start()
    worker.join(timeout + GRACE)
    seconds = time.monotonic() - start
    if worker.is_alive():
        return AgentRun(agent, False, [], f"timeout after {timeout}s (agent did not stop)", 0.0,
                        seconds)
    exc = box.get("exc")
    if isinstance(exc, llm.BudgetExceeded) or (exc is not None and not isinstance(exc, Exception)):
        raise exc
    if exc is not None:
        nominal = exc.nominal_usd_external if isinstance(exc, CodexFailed) else 0.0
        return AgentRun(agent, False, [], f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"[:500], nominal, seconds)
    out = box["out"]
    error = f"timeout after {timeout}s" if out["timed_out"] else out["error"]
    return AgentRun(agent, error is None, out["transcript"], error, out["nominal_usd_external"],
                    seconds)
```
