```bash
## v4 Task 2: sandboxed site-agent harnesses (2026-10-14/15, $0 actual)
- sandbox-exec profile: the first form ((allow default)(deny network*)(allow network* (remote ip
  "localhost:*")) + silo denies) blocks *binding* loopback ports: jupyter_client's

  web_search="disabled"). Smoke (codex-cli 0.159.0): sibling workspace and repo data unreadable

  to read a denied file was refused by the model without executing anything: judge sandbox checks
  by command_execution items, not by the agent's message.
- Codex runs commands in a login zsh; /etc/zprofile's path_helper moved the analysis env to the end
  of PATH (python3 -> /usr/local/bin/python3, no scanpy). `-c allow_login_shell=false` -> /bin/zsh -c,
  python3 = bixbench env. Same reason for `bash -c` (not v3's `bash -lc`) in the ReAct shell.
- Codex items outside {agent_message, reasoning, command_execution, file_change, todo_list} (web
  search, MCP, ...) fail the call as CodexFailed (charged, never cached).
- Spec §8 harness test (each harness, sibling workspace + data/hidden/ + network): bare sandbox and
  ReAct kernel/shell offline; Finch's interpreter + Jupyter kernel under the same profile
  (AGENTCOOP_SLOW_TESTS=1); Codex live through run_codex (AGENTCOOP_LIVE_TESTS=1, 2026-10-15:
  sibling and hidden reads EPERM, curl cannot resolve, ok.txt written, $0.0018 nominal / $0 actual).
  data/hidden/ is denied as part of data/; agents/ code never names it.
- Cold scanpy import in a fresh workspace ≈ 18 s (matplotlib font cache built in the workspace's
  MPLCONFIGDIR); accepted per site call, so the real-interpreter test is opt-in
  (AGENTCOOP_SLOW_TESTS=1, ≈ 25 s).
EOF
```

### Task 3: Claims

Claims come from external agents as JSON, so this module is where bad output gets caught. A bad item is skipped and a readable reason is recorded; the parser never raises. The parser assigns the ids, gene symbols are normalized once here, and every arm uses the same matching rule (`same_claim`). The module uses only the standard library.

**Files:**
- Create: `agentcoop/coop/__init__.py`
- Create: `agentcoop/coop/claims.py`
- Create: `tests/test_claims.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing from other tasks (standard library only).
- Produces (exactly as in the contracts):
  ```python
  @dataclass(frozen=True)
  class Vocabulary:
      populations: tuple[str, ...]
      groups: tuple[str, ...]
      contrasts: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...]
  @dataclass(frozen=True)
  class Claim:
      id: str; site: str; kind: str; population: str; target: str; reference: str
      up_genes: tuple[str, ...]; down_genes: tuple[str, ...]
      effect: float | None; n_units: int | None; rank: int; rationale: str = ""
      @property
      def contrast(self) -> tuple[str, str]
      def signed_genes(self) -> frozenset[tuple[str, int]]
  def parse_claims(raw: object, *, site: str, vocab: Vocabulary, max_claims: int) -> tuple[list[Claim], list[str]]
  def claim_to_dict(c: Claim) -> dict
  def claim_from_dict(d: dict) -> Claim
  def same_claim(a: Claim, b: Claim, min_jaccard: float = 0.2) -> bool
  ```
  Also exported: the constants `KINDS = ("de", "composition")` and `MIN_DE_GENES = 3`.
- Behaviour other tasks rely on:
  - Gene symbols in a `Claim` are upper-case, stripped and de-duplicated.
  - Lower `rank` means stronger, and `parse_claims` returns claims in rank order.
  - Ids are `"<site>:<k>"`, where k is the 1-based position in the returned list.
  - `claim_to_dict` writes `target`/`reference`, not `contrast`. `parse_claims` accepts either form.
  - `Vocabulary` turns JSON lists into tuples. It raises `ValueError` if a contrast uses a group that is not in `groups`.
- Review Focus: no item is owned here. Item 4 needs every arm to read back identical step-1 claims from the store. On the claims side, two tests pin this:
  - `test_claim_dict_round_trip_is_exact_through_json`
  - `test_parse_accepts_a_claims_object_and_is_deterministic`

```python
"""Claim schema, validation at submission and the shared matching rule (spec §2.3)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json

import pytest

from agentcoop.coop.claims import Claim, Vocabulary, claim_from_dict, claim_to_dict, same_claim

VOCAB = Vocabulary(populations=("CD4 T", "CD14 Mono", "B"), groups=("control", "treated", "mimic"),
                   contrasts=(("treated", "control"), ("mimic", "control")))


def make(**kw) -> Claim:
    base = dict(id="S1:1", site="S1", kind="de", population="CD14 Mono", target="treated",
                reference="control", up_genes=("IFIT1", "MX1", "ISG15"), down_genes=(),
                effect=1.25, n_units=8, rank=1, rationale="interferon response")
    return Claim(**{**base, **kw})


def test_vocabulary_normalizes_json_lists_to_tuples():
    v = Vocabulary(populations=["B"], groups=["control", "treated"],
                   contrasts=[["treated", "control"]])
    assert v.populations == ("B",) and v.groups == ("control", "treated")
    assert v.contrasts == (("treated", "control"),) and ("treated", "control") in v.contrasts
    hash(v)


def test_vocabulary_rejects_contrast_groups_outside_its_groups():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="mimic"):
        Vocabulary(populations=("B",), groups=("control", "treated"),
                   contrasts=(("mimic", "control"),))


def test_contrast_and_signed_genes():
    c = make(up_genes=("IFIT1", "MX1"), down_genes=("CD52",))
    assert c.contrast == ("treated", "control")
    assert c.signed_genes() == frozenset({("IFIT1", 1), ("MX1", 1), ("CD52", -1)})
    assert make(kind="composition", up_genes=(), effect=-0.4).signed_genes() == frozenset()


def test_claim_dict_round_trip_is_exact_through_json():
    """Step-1 claims are memoized as JSON; every arm must read back identical claims (RF4)."""
    c = make(down_genes=("CD52",), effect=0.1 + 0.2, rationale="ISGs ↑")
    d = claim_to_dict(c)
    assert d["up_genes"] == ["IFIT1", "MX1", "ISG15"] and d["down_genes"] == ["CD52"]
    assert (d["target"], d["reference"]) == ("treated", "control")
    back = claim_from_dict(json.loads(json.dumps(d)))
    assert back == c and hash(back) == hash(c)
    assert isinstance(back.up_genes, tuple) and isinstance(back.down_genes, tuple)
    comp = make(kind="composition", up_genes=(), effect=-0.4, n_units=None)
    assert claim_from_dict(json.loads(json.dumps(claim_to_dict(comp)))) == comp


def test_claim_from_dict_ignores_report_extras_and_defaults_rationale():
    d = {**claim_to_dict(make()), "label": "corroborated", "scope": None, "cause": None}
    assert claim_from_dict(d) == make()
    del d["rationale"]
    assert claim_from_dict(d).rationale == ""


def test_claim_from_dict_requires_the_schema_fields():
    d = claim_to_dict(make())
    del d["population"]
    with pytest.raises(TypeError):
        claim_from_dict(d)


def test_same_claim_de_uses_signed_gene_jaccard_with_an_inclusive_threshold():
    a = make(up_genes=("A", "B", "C"))
    assert same_claim(a, make(up_genes=("A", "D", "E")))                       # 1/5 = 0.2
    assert not same_claim(a, make(up_genes=("A", "D", "E", "F")))              # 1/6
    assert not same_claim(a, make(up_genes=(), down_genes=("A", "B", "C")))    # opposite signs
    assert same_claim(a, make(up_genes=("A", "B", "X")), min_jaccard=0.5)      # 2/4
    assert not same_claim(a, make(up_genes=("A", "D", "E")), min_jaccard=0.5)


def test_same_claim_needs_the_same_kind_population_and_contrast():
    a = make()
    assert same_claim(a, make(id="S2:4", site="S2", rank=7, effect=None, n_units=None))
    assert not same_claim(a, make(population="B"))
    assert not same_claim(a, make(target="mimic"))
    assert not same_claim(a, make(kind="composition", up_genes=(), effect=1.0))


def test_same_claim_composition_matches_by_direction():
    up = make(kind="composition", up_genes=(), effect=0.3)
    assert same_claim(up, make(kind="composition", up_genes=(), effect=2.0, site="S2"))
    assert not same_claim(up, make(kind="composition", up_genes=(), effect=-0.3))
    assert not same_claim(up, make(kind="composition", up_genes=(), effect=None))


def test_same_claim_is_symmetric_and_reflexive():
    pool = [make(), make(up_genes=("IFIT1", "MX1", "OAS1", "IFI6")),
            make(up_genes=("IFIT1",), down_genes=("MX1", "ISG15")),
            make(up_genes=("A", "B", "C")), make(population="B"), make(target="mimic"),
            make(kind="composition", up_genes=(), effect=0.5),
            make(kind="composition", up_genes=(), effect=-0.5),
            make(kind="composition", up_genes=(), effect=0.1, site="S3")]
    for x in pool:
        assert same_claim(x, x)
        for y in pool:
            for t in (0.0, 0.2, 0.5, 1.0):
                assert same_claim(x, y, t) == same_claim(y, x, t)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Create the package and the schema / matching half of `claims.py`**

Create `agentcoop/coop/__init__.py`:

```python
"""Cross-examination between external agents across resource silos (v4, spec §2).

claims → view → tester → protocol → site → arms. Nothing in this package may import the graders
or read held-out files (spec §8, tested).
"""
```

Create `agentcoop/coop/claims.py`:

```python
"""Claims: the one schema every arm emits (spec §2.3), its validation and the matching rule.

Claims come from external agents as JSON, so `parse_claims` is the boundary where malformed
output is caught: a bad item is skipped with a readable reason instead of raising (one sloppy
claim must not void a site's whole step-1 call), ids are assigned here (an agent cannot collide
with another site's ids), and gene symbols are upper-cased once so every later comparison is
plain string equality. `same_claim` is the single matching rule shared by every arm that pairs
claims (majority vote, debate, the mechanism arms).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, fields


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Vocabulary:
    """A case's controlled names; claims and site views may use only these."""

    populations: tuple[str, ...]
    groups: tuple[str, ...]
    contrasts: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...]      # (target, reference) pairs allowed in claims

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        # Case specs are read back from JSON (lists); claim validation tests tuple membership.
        object.__setattr__(self, "populations", tuple(self.populations))
        object.__setattr__(self, "groups", tuple(self.groups))
        object.__setattr__(self, "contrasts", tuple((t, r) for t, r in self.contrasts))
        unknown = sorted({g for pair in self.contrasts for g in pair} - set(self.groups))
        if unknown:
            raise ValueError(f"contrasts use groups outside the vocabulary: {unknown}")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Claim:
    """One validated claim. `rank` is the emitting arm's confidence order (lower = stronger)."""

    id: str
    site: str
    kind: str                       # "de" | "composition"
    population: str
    target: str
    reference: str
    up_genes: tuple[str, ...]
    down_genes: tuple[str, ...]
    effect: float | None            # composition: its sign is the claimed direction
    n_units: int | None
    rank: int
    rationale: str = ""

    @property
    def contrast(self) -> tuple[str, str]:
        return (self.target, self.reference)

    def signed_genes(self) -> frozenset[tuple[str, int]]:
        """{(gene, +1|-1)}: a gene's direction is part of what the claim asserts."""
        return frozenset([(g, 1) for g in self.up_genes] + [(g, -1) for g in self.down_genes])


def claim_to_dict(c: Claim) -> dict:
    """JSON-ready form (tuples → lists) for the store and reports; inverse of claim_from_dict."""
    return {**asdict(c), "up_genes": list(c.up_genes), "down_genes": list(c.down_genes)}


def claim_from_dict(d: dict) -> Claim:
    """Inverse of claim_to_dict; extra keys (label, scope, cause in final reports) are ignored."""
    kw = {f.name: d[f.name] for f in fields(Claim) if f.name in d}
    return Claim(**{**kw, "up_genes": tuple(d["up_genes"]), "down_genes": tuple(d["down_genes"])})


def _sign(x: float | None) -> int:
    return 0 if x is None or x == 0 else (1 if x > 0 else -1)


def same_claim(a: Claim, b: Claim, min_jaccard: float = 0.2) -> bool:
    """The shared matching rule: same kind, population and contrast, and the same content.

    de: Jaccard of signed genes ≥ min_jaccard (a gene up in one claim and down in the other is
    no overlap); composition: the same direction. Symmetric by construction.
    """
    if (a.kind, a.population, a.contrast) != (b.kind, b.population, b.contrast):
        return False
    if a.kind == "composition":
        return _sign(a.effect) != 0 and _sign(a.effect) == _sign(b.effect)
    sa, sb = a.signed_genes(), b.signed_genes()
    union = sa | sb
    return bool(union) and len(sa & sb) / len(union) >= min_jaccard
```

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# parse_claims: validation at submission
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def de(**kw) -> dict:
    item = {"kind": "de", "population": "CD14 Mono", "contrast": ["treated", "control"],
            "up_genes": ["IFIT1", "MX1", "ISG15"], "down_genes": [], "effect": 1.25,
            "n_units": 8, "rationale": "interferon response"}
    return {**item, **kw}


def comp(**kw) -> dict:
    item = {"kind": "composition", "population": "B", "contrast": ["treated", "control"],
            "effect": -0.7, "n_units": 8}
    return {**item, **kw}


def without(item: dict, *keys: str) -> dict:
    return {k: v for k, v in item.items() if k not in keys}


def parse(raw: object, max_claims: int = 10) -> tuple[list[Claim], list[str]]:
    return parse_claims(raw, site="S1", vocab=VOCAB, max_claims=max_claims)


def test_parse_valid_claims_assigns_site_ids_and_default_ranks():
    claims, errors = parse([de(), comp()])
    assert errors == []
    assert claims == [make(), make(id="S1:2", kind="composition", population="B", up_genes=(),
                                   effect=-0.7, rank=2, rationale="")]


def test_parse_accepts_a_claims_object_and_is_deterministic():
    raw = [de(), comp()]
    assert parse({"claims": raw}) == parse(raw) == parse(json.loads(json.dumps(raw)))


@pytest.mark.parametrize("raw", [None, '[{"kind": "de"}]', 42, {"claims": None},
                                 {"findings": [de()]}, {"claims": "IFIT1"}])
def test_parse_malformed_container_returns_an_error_not_an_exception(raw):
    claims, errors = parse(raw)
    assert claims == [] and len(errors) == 1 and isinstance(errors[0], str)


def test_parse_skips_non_object_items_and_keeps_the_rest():
    claims, errors = parse([None, "IFIT1 up", ["IFIT1"], de()])
    assert [c.id for c in claims] == ["S1:1"] and claims[0].rank == 4    # default rank = position
    assert [e.split(":")[0] for e in errors] == ["item[0]", "item[1]", "item[2]"]
    assert all("not an object" in e for e in errors)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("item, word", [
    (without(de(), "kind"), "kind"),
    (de(kind="gsea"), "kind"),
    (de(kind=1), "kind"),
    (without(de(), "population"), "population"),
    (de(population="Monocytes"), "population"),
    (de(population=["CD14 Mono"]), "population"),
    (without(de(), "contrast"), "contrast"),
    (de(contrast=["control", "treated"]), "contrast"),       # reversed: not a vocabulary contrast
    (de(contrast=["treated", "mimic"]), "contrast"),
    (de(contrast=["treated"]), "contrast"),
    (de(contrast="treated vs control"), "contrast"),
    (without(de(), "up_genes", "down_genes"), "genes"),
    (de(up_genes="IFIT1, MX1, ISG15"), "up_genes"),
    (de(up_genes=["IFIT1", 7, "ISG15"]), "up_genes"),
    (de(up_genes=["IFIT1", "  ", "MX1", "ISG15"]), "up_genes"),
    (de(down_genes={"CD52": -1}), "down_genes"),
    (de(effect="large"), "effect"),
    (de(effect=float("nan")), "effect"),
    (de(n_units=-1), "n_units"),
    (de(n_units="8"), "n_units"),
    (de(rank=1.5), "rank"),
    (de(rank=True), "rank"),
])
def test_parse_skips_an_invalid_item_with_a_readable_reason(item, word):
    claims, errors = parse([item, comp()])
    assert [c.population for c in claims] == ["B"]
    assert len(errors) == 1 and errors[0].startswith("item[0]: ") and word in errors[0]


def test_parse_normalizes_case_and_whitespace():
    claims, errors = parse([de(kind=" DE ", population=" CD14 Mono ",
                               contrast=[" treated", "control "],
                               up_genes=[" ifit1 ", "Mx1", "IFIT1", "isg15"], down_genes=["cd52"],
                               rationale="  ISGs  ")])
    assert errors == []
    c = claims[0]
    assert (c.kind, c.population, c.contrast) == ("de", "CD14 Mono", ("treated", "control"))
    assert c.up_genes == ("IFIT1", "MX1", "ISG15") and c.down_genes == ("CD52",)
    assert c.rationale == "ISGs"


def test_parse_de_gene_rules():
    _, errors = parse([de(up_genes=["IFIT1", "ifit1", "MX1"])])          # 2 distinct genes
    assert "3 genes" in errors[0]
    _, errors = parse([de(down_genes=["mx1"])])
    assert "both" in errors[0] and "MX1" in errors[0]
    claims, errors = parse([de(up_genes=["IFIT1", "MX1"], down_genes=["CD52"], effect=None),
                            without(de(), "down_genes", "effect", "n_units", "rationale")])
    assert errors == [] and [c.effect for c in claims] == [None, None]
    assert claims[1].down_genes == () and claims[1].n_units is None and claims[1].rationale == ""


@pytest.mark.parametrize("item", [
    comp(up_genes=["CD79A"]), comp(down_genes=["MS4A1"]), without(comp(), "effect"),
    comp(effect=None), comp(effect=0), comp(effect=0.0), comp(effect="-0.7"),
    comp(effect=float("inf")), comp(effect=True),
])
def test_parse_composition_needs_no_genes_and_a_nonzero_effect(item):
    claims, errors = parse([item])
    assert claims == [] and len(errors) == 1


def test_parse_composition_accepts_empty_gene_lists_and_integer_effects():
    claims, errors = parse([comp(up_genes=[], down_genes=None, effect=-2)])
    assert errors == [] and claims[0].effect == -2.0 and isinstance(claims[0].effect, float)
    assert claims[0].up_genes == claims[0].down_genes == ()


def test_parse_accepts_integral_floats_and_numpy_numbers():
    claims, errors = parse([de(n_units=8.0, rank=2.0),
                            comp(effect=np.float64(-0.7), n_units=np.int64(8), rank=np.int64(1))])
    assert errors == []
    assert [(c.kind, c.rank, c.n_units) for c in claims] == [("composition", 1, 8), ("de", 2, 8)]
    assert all(type(c.rank) is int and type(c.n_units) is int for c in claims)
    assert type(claims[0].effect) is float


def test_parse_orders_by_rank_then_position_and_ids_follow_that_order():
    raw = [de(up_genes=["A1", "A2", "A3"], rank=3),
           de(up_genes=["B1", "B2", "B3"]),                  # default rank = position 2
           de(up_genes=["C1", "C2", "C3"], rank=1),
           de(up_genes=["D1", "D2", "D3"], rank=2)]          # ties with B; B came first
    claims, errors = parse(raw)
    assert errors == []
    assert [x.up_genes[0] for x in claims] == ["C1", "B1", "D1", "A1"]
    assert [x.id for x in claims] == ["S1:1", "S1:2", "S1:3", "S1:4"]
    assert [x.rank for x in claims] == [1, 2, 2, 3]


def test_parse_ignores_agent_supplied_id_and_site():
    claims, _ = parse([de(id="S2:1", site="S2")])
    assert (claims[0].id, claims[0].site) == ("S1:1", "S1")


def test_parse_drops_duplicates_keeping_the_better_ranked():
    first = de(up_genes=["IFIT1", "MX1", "ISG15"], rank=2, rationale="first")
    again = de(up_genes=["isg15", "IFIT1", "mx1"], rank=1, rationale="again")   # same signed set
    claims, errors = parse([first, again])
    assert [c.rationale for c in claims] == ["again"]
    assert errors == ["item[0]: duplicate of item[1]"]


def test_parse_duplicates_need_identical_kind_population_contrast_and_signed_content():
    raw = [de(), de(up_genes=["IFIT1", "MX1"], down_genes=["ISG15"]), de(population="CD4 T"),
           de(contrast=["mimic", "control"]), de(up_genes=["IFIT1", "MX1", "ISG15", "OAS1"]),
           comp(), comp(effect=0.7), comp(effect=-0.1)]
    claims, errors = parse(raw)
    assert len(claims) == 7 and errors == ["item[7]: duplicate of item[5]"]


def test_parse_caps_claims_in_rank_order():
    raw = [de(up_genes=[f"G{i}A", f"G{i}B", f"G{i}C"], rank=12 - i) for i in range(12)]
    claims, errors = parse(raw)
    assert [c.rank for c in claims] == list(range(1, 11))
    assert errors == ["item[1]: beyond the cap of 10 claims",
                      "item[0]: beyond the cap of 10 claims"]


def test_parse_duplicates_do_not_use_up_the_cap():
    claims, errors = parse([de(), de(), comp()], max_claims=2)
    assert [c.kind for c in claims] == ["de", "composition"]
    assert errors == ["item[1]: duplicate of item[0]"]


def test_parse_reads_back_claim_to_dict_output():
    """claim_to_dict writes target/reference instead of contrast; the parser takes either form."""
    claims, _ = parse([de(down_genes=["CD52"]), comp()])
    again, errors = parse([claim_to_dict(c) for c in claims])
    assert errors == [] and again == claims
```

In `agentcoop/coop/claims.py`, replace the line

```python


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Validation at submission
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

KINDS = ("de", "composition")
MIN_DE_GENES = 3


def parse_claims(raw: object, *, site: str, vocab: Vocabulary,
                 max_claims: int) -> tuple[list[Claim], list[str]]:
    """An agent's decoded JSON → (valid claims in rank order, one reason per dropped item).

    `raw` is a list of claim objects or {"claims": [...]}; a contrast is `contrast: [target,
    reference]` (spec schema) or `target`/`reference` (claim_to_dict's form). Never raises:
    malformed items, duplicates of a better-ranked claim (same kind, population, contrast and
    signed content) and items beyond `max_claims` are skipped and reported. Order is by `rank`
    (default: 1-based input position), ties by input position; ids are `<site>:<k>` with k the
    1-based position in the result, so any id or site the agent wrote is ignored.
    """
    if isinstance(raw, dict):
        items = raw.get("claims")
        if not isinstance(items, list):
            return [], ["expected {'claims': [...]}: 'claims' is missing or not a list"]
    elif isinstance(raw, list):
        items = raw
    else:
        return [], [f"expected a list of claims or {{'claims': [...]}}, got {type(raw).__name__}"]
    errors: list[str] = []
    valid: list[tuple[int, Claim]] = []
    for i, item in enumerate(items):
        try:
            valid.append((i, _parse_item(item, site=site, vocab=vocab, default_rank=i + 1)))
        except ValueError as e:
            errors.append(f"item[{i}]: {e}")
    valid.sort(key=lambda ic: (ic[1].rank, ic[0]))
    kept: list[Claim] = []
    first: dict[tuple, int] = {}
    for i, c in valid:
        key = _signature(c)
        if key in first:
            errors.append(f"item[{i}]: duplicate of item[{first[key]}]")
        elif len(kept) >= max_claims:
            errors.append(f"item[{i}]: beyond the cap of {max_claims} claims")
        else:
            first[key] = i
            kept.append(c)
    return [replace(c, id=f"{site}:{k}") for k, c in enumerate(kept, 1)], errors


def _parse_item(item: object, *, site: str, vocab: Vocabulary, default_rank: int) -> Claim:
    """One raw claim → Claim with an empty id; ValueError names the first problem found."""
    if not isinstance(item, dict):
        raise ValueError(f"not an object ({type(item).__name__})")
    kind = _text(item.get("kind"))
    kind = kind.lower() if isinstance(kind, str) else kind
    if kind not in KINDS:
        raise ValueError(f"kind must be one of {list(KINDS)}, got {item.get('kind')!r}")
    population = _text(item.get("population"))
    if population not in vocab.populations:
        raise ValueError(f"population {population!r} not in {list(vocab.populations)}")
    pair = item["contrast"] if "contrast" in item else [item.get("target"), item.get("reference")]
    if not isinstance(pair, (list, tuple)) or len(pair) != 2:
        raise ValueError(f"contrast must be [target, reference], got {pair!r}")
    target, reference = (_text(g) for g in pair)
    if (target, reference) not in vocab.contrasts:
        raise ValueError(f"contrast {[target, reference]!r} not in "
                         f"{[list(c) for c in vocab.contrasts]}")
    up, down = _genes(item, "up_genes"), _genes(item, "down_genes")
    effect = _number(item.get("effect"), "effect")
    n_units = None if item.get("n_units") is None else _integer(item["n_units"], "n_units")
    if n_units is not None and n_units < 0:
        raise ValueError(f"n_units must be >= 0, got {n_units}")
    rank = default_rank if item.get("rank") is None else _integer(item["rank"], "rank")
    if kind == "de":
        if len(up) + len(down) < MIN_DE_GENES:
            raise ValueError(f"a de claim needs >= {MIN_DE_GENES} genes, got {len(up) + len(down)}")
        both = sorted(set(up) & set(down))
        if both:
            raise ValueError(f"genes in both up_genes and down_genes: {both}")
    elif up or down:
        raise ValueError("a composition claim takes no genes")
    elif not effect:
        raise ValueError("a composition claim needs a nonzero effect (its sign is the direction)")
    rationale = item.get("rationale")
    return Claim(id="", site=site, kind=kind, population=population, target=target,
                 reference=reference, up_genes=up, down_genes=down, effect=effect,
                 n_units=n_units, rank=rank,
                 rationale="" if rationale is None else str(rationale).strip())


def _text(value: object) -> object:
    return value.strip() if isinstance(value, str) else value


def _genes(item: dict, key: str) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    """Stripped, upper-cased, de-duplicated (first occurrence kept) symbols; missing = none."""
    value = item.get(key)
    if value is None:
        return ()
    if not isinstance(value, (list, tuple)) or not all(isinstance(g, str) and g.strip()
                                                       for g in value):
        raise ValueError(f"{key} must be a list of non-empty strings, got {value!r}")
    return tuple(dict.fromkeys(g.strip().upper() for g in value))


def _number(value: object, key: str) -> float | None:
    if value is None:
        return None
    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, numbers.Real) or not math.isfinite(value):
        raise ValueError(f"{key} must be a finite number, got {value!r}")
    return float(value)


def _integer(value: object, key: str) -> int:
    if isinstance(value, numbers.Integral) and not isinstance(value, bool):
        return int(value)
    if isinstance(value, float) and value.is_integer():
        return int(value)
    raise ValueError(f"{key} must be an integer, got {value!r}")


def _signature(c: Claim) -> tuple:
    """Duplicate key within one site: kind, population, contrast and signed content (signed
    genes for de, direction for composition)."""
    direction = _sign(c.effect) if c.kind == "composition" else 0
    return (c.kind, c.population, c.contrast, c.signed_genes(), direction)
```

### Task 4: Site views and site tables

**Files:**
- Create: `agentcoop/coop/view.py`
- Create: `tests/test_view.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `agentcoop.coop.claims.Vocabulary` (Task 3; only `.populations` and `.groups` are read). `agentcoop/coop/__init__.py` exists from Task 3.
- Produces (contract names and signatures; the field comments state the layouts this task fixes):

```python
@dataclass
class SiteView:
    root: Path
    genes: dict[str, str]           # native feature id -> symbol (uppercase; rows without a symbol dropped)
    samples: pd.DataFrame           # columns: sample, donor, group (+ covariates), all strings
    cells: pd.DataFrame | None      # columns: cell, sample, population (scRNA only)
    spec: dict
def load_view(root: Path, *, kind: str, vocab: Vocabulary) -> tuple[SiteView | None, list[str]]
def write_view(root: Path, *, genes: dict[str, str], samples: pd.DataFrame, cells: pd.DataFrame | None, spec: dict) -> None
@dataclass
class SiteTable:
    kind: str                       # "scrna" | "bulk"
    genes: list[str]                # symbols, sorted (columns of values)
    units: pd.DataFrame             # unit, donor, group, population ("all" for bulk), n_cells (0 for bulk)
    values: np.ndarray              # units x genes; scRNA log2(CPM+1) pseudobulk; bulk per-gene rank-to-normal
    mean_cpm: np.ndarray | None     # scRNA: populations (sorted) x genes; read it via population_mean_cpm()
    composition: pd.DataFrame | None   # scRNA: one row per sample (index "sample"); population fraction columns + "group" + "donor"
    def population_mean_cpm(self, population: str) -> np.ndarray   # zeros for a population the site lacks; ValueError for bulk
def site_table(view: SiteView, data_path: Path, *, kind: str) -> SiteTable
```

  Also module constants used by later tasks: `EXCLUDE = "exclude"` (sample group outside every contrast), `OTHER = "other"` (cell population outside the vocabulary), `KINDS = ("scrna", "bulk")`.

  Behaviour later tasks rely on:
  - `load_view` never raises on site files (only on an invalid `kind`, a caller bug). It returns `(None, errors)` listing *every* defect at once (missing/unreadable/empty/ragged file, duplicate or missing column, empty or duplicate ids, group not in `vocab.groups` + `"exclude"`, population not in `vocab.populations` + `"other"`, cell referencing an unknown sample, no mapped gene, `spec.json` missing/malformed/not an object), or `(view, warnings)` when usable. Site (Task 7) turns `None` into `untestable(view)` (Review Focus 1).
  - `site_table` never raises on a view/data mismatch: native ids, cells or samples the data lacks are dropped, so the table has few genes or units and the tester answers `untestable`/`inconclusive` (the step-4 trigger), never `supported`. Only a broken `data_path` (case-script fault) raises.
  - scRNA units are all (sample, population) pairs with at least one kept cell, unit id `"<sample>|<population>"`; the `min_cells` filter is the tester's (Task 5), via `units.n_cells`. Library size = a unit's total count over *all* native features, so the number of features the agent mapped does not change the CPM of the genes it did map.
  - Bulk: per symbol the probe with the largest IQR across the kept (non-excluded) samples, on the data's native scale (ties: smallest native id); probes with a missing value in any kept sample are dropped; then per-gene van der Waerden scores `norm.ppf(rank / (n + 1))`, ties averaged.

```python
"""Site views and site tables: validation never raises; pseudobulk and bulk transforms are exact.

Offline and fast: a hand-computable toy h5ad and a toy bulk matrix written to tmp_path.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import anndata as ad
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import pytest
import scipy.sparse as sp

from agentcoop.coop.claims import Vocabulary
from agentcoop.coop.view import load_view, write_view

VOCAB = Vocabulary(populations=("CD4 T", "B", "NK"), groups=("control", "treated", "mimic"),
                   contrasts=(("treated", "control"), ("mimic", "control")))

FEATURES = ["ENSG1", "ENSG2", "ENSG3", "ENSG4"]
#: ENSG2 and ENSG3 share a symbol (summed); ENSG4 is unmapped (dropped from the genes but still
#: in the library size); ENSG9 is mapped but absent from the data (ignored).
GENES = {"ENSG1": "gzmb", "ENSG2": "CD3E", "ENSG3": " cd3e ", "ENSG4": "", "ENSG9": "MS4A1"}
#: cell -> (sample, population, counts over FEATURES)
CELLS = {
    "c1": ("s1", "CD4 T", [1, 2, 3, 4]),
    "c2": ("s1", "CD4 T", [0, 1, 1, 2]),
    "c3": ("s1", "B", [5, 0, 0, 5]),
    "c4": ("s1", "other", [9, 9, 9, 9]),      # outside the vocabulary: never in a unit
    "c5": ("s2", "CD4 T", [2, 2, 2, 2]),
    "c6": ("s2", "B", [1, 0, 3, 0]),
    "c7": ("s2", "B", [0, 4, 0, 4]),
    "c8": ("s3", "CD4 T", [7, 7, 7, 7]),      # sample s3 is excluded
}
UNLISTED = ("c9", [3, 3, 3, 3])               # in the data, not in cells.tsv
SAMPLES = pd.DataFrame({"sample": ["s1", "s2", "s3"], "donor": ["d1", "d2", "d3"],
                        "group": ["control", "treated", "exclude"],
                        "assay": ["10x v3", "10x v3", "10x v2"]})


def _cells_frame() -> pd.DataFrame:
    return pd.DataFrame([(c, s, p) for c, (s, p, _) in CELLS.items()],
                        columns=["cell", "sample", "population"])


def _scrna(tmp_path: Path, **view) -> tuple[Path, Path]:
    """Toy h5ad (sparse raw counts) plus a valid view; keyword arguments replace view parts."""
    x = np.array([v[2] for v in CELLS.values()] + [UNLISTED[1]], dtype=np.float32)
    tmp_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    data = tmp_path / "site.h5ad"
    ad.AnnData(X=sp.csr_matrix(x), obs=pd.DataFrame(index=[*CELLS, UNLISTED[0]]),
               var=pd.DataFrame(index=FEATURES)).write_h5ad(data)
    parts = {"genes": GENES, "samples": SAMPLES.copy(), "cells": _cells_frame(),
             "spec": {"normalization": "cpm", "unit": "donor"}}
    parts.update(view)
    root = tmp_path / "view"
    write_view(root, **parts)
    return root, data


def test_valid_view_normalizes_symbols_and_reports_dropped_rows(tmp_path):
    root, _ = _scrna(tmp_path)
    view, warnings = load_view(root, kind="scrna", vocab=VOCAB)
    assert view is not None
    assert view.genes == {"ENSG1": "GZMB", "ENSG2": "CD3E", "ENSG3": "CD3E", "ENSG9": "MS4A1"}
    assert warnings == ["genes.tsv: 1 row(s) without a symbol dropped"]
    assert list(view.samples.columns) == ["sample", "donor", "group", "assay"]
    assert view.cells["cell"].tolist() == list(CELLS)
    assert view.spec == {"normalization": "cpm", "unit": "donor"}


def _edit(path: Path, change) -> None:
    df = pd.read_csv(path, sep="\t", dtype=str, keep_default_na=False)
    change(df).to_csv(path, sep="\t", index=False)


MALFORMED = {
    "missing column": (lambda r: _edit(r / "samples.tsv", lambda d: d.drop(columns="group")),
                       "samples.tsv: missing column(s) group"),
    "unknown group": (lambda r: _edit(r / "samples.tsv",
                                      lambda d: d.replace({"group": {"control": "ctrl"}})),
                      "group(s) 'ctrl' not in the vocabulary"),
    "no vocabulary group": (lambda r: _edit(r / "samples.tsv", lambda d: d.assign(group="exclude")),
                            "no sample in a vocabulary group"),
    "duplicate sample": (lambda r: _edit(r / "samples.tsv",
                                         lambda d: d.replace({"sample": {"s2": "s1"}})),
                         "duplicate sample 's1'"),
    "empty donor": (lambda r: _edit(r / "samples.tsv", lambda d: d.assign(donor="")),
                    "3 row(s) with an empty donor"),
    "duplicate cell id": (lambda r: _edit(r / "cells.tsv",
                                          lambda d: d.replace({"cell": {"c2": "c1"}})),
                          "duplicate cell 'c1'"),
    "unknown population": (lambda r: _edit(r / "cells.tsv",
                                           lambda d: d.replace({"population": {"B": "B cells"}})),
                           "population(s) 'B cells' not in the vocabulary"),
    "unknown sample": (lambda r: _edit(r / "cells.tsv",
                                       lambda d: d.replace({"sample": {"s2": "s7"}})),
                       "3 cell(s) reference unknown sample(s) 's7'"),
    "duplicate gene id": (lambda r: _edit(r / "genes.tsv",
                                          lambda d: d.replace({"native_id": {"ENSG2": "ENSG1"}})),
                          "duplicate native_id 'ENSG1'"),
    "no mapped gene": (lambda r: _edit(r / "genes.tsv", lambda d: d.assign(symbol="")),
                       "no feature mapped to a symbol"),
    "missing cells.tsv": (lambda r: (r / "cells.tsv").unlink(), "cells.tsv: missing"),
    "empty samples.tsv": (lambda r: (r / "samples.tsv").write_text(""), "samples.tsv: empty"),
    "duplicate column": (lambda r: (r / "samples.tsv").write_text(
                             "sample\tdonor\tgroup\tgroup\ns1\td1\tcontrol\ttreated\n"),
                         "samples.tsv: duplicate column(s) 'group'"),
    "binary genes.tsv": (lambda r: (r / "genes.tsv").write_bytes(bytes(range(256)) * 4),
                         "genes.tsv: unreadable"),
    "ragged cells.tsv": (lambda r: (r / "cells.tsv").write_text(
                             "cell\tsample\tpopulation\nc1\ts1\tB\textra\tmore\n"),
                         "cells.tsv: 1 row(s) whose field count differs from the header"),
    "spec not an object": (lambda r: (r / "spec.json").write_text("[1, 2]"),
                           "spec.json: must be a JSON object"),
    "spec malformed": (lambda r: (r / "spec.json").write_text("{"), "spec.json: unreadable"),
    "spec missing": (lambda r: (r / "spec.json").unlink(), "spec.json: missing"),
}


@pytest.mark.parametrize("case", sorted(MALFORMED))
def test_malformed_view_returns_errors_never_raises(tmp_path, case):
    mutate, expected = MALFORMED[case]
    root, _ = _scrna(tmp_path)
    mutate(root)
    view, errors = load_view(root, kind="scrna", vocab=VOCAB)
    assert view is None
    assert any(expected in e for e in errors), errors


def test_every_defect_is_reported_at_once(tmp_path):
    root, _ = _scrna(tmp_path)
    MALFORMED["unknown group"][0](root)
    MALFORMED["duplicate cell id"][0](root)
    MALFORMED["spec malformed"][0](root)
    view, errors = load_view(root, kind="scrna", vocab=VOCAB)
    assert view is None and len(errors) == 3


def test_missing_view_directory_is_an_error(tmp_path):
    assert load_view(tmp_path / "nope", kind="scrna", vocab=VOCAB) == (
        None, [f"view directory {tmp_path / 'nope'} missing"])
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the view files and their validation**

Create `agentcoop/coop/view.py`:

```python
"""Site views: the machine-readable handoff a site agent writes, and the table testers read.

A site agent describes its private data in fixed files (spec §2.2): ``genes.tsv`` (native
feature id -> symbol), ``samples.tsv`` (sample -> donor, case group, covariates), ``cells.tsv``
(scRNA only: cell -> sample, population) and ``spec.json`` (its analysis spec). ``load_view``
checks them against the case vocabulary and returns every defect as text instead of raising: a
malformed view is a finding about the site (a handoff loss step 4 can repair), not a crash.

``site_table`` turns a valid view plus the site's raw data into the only object testers read,
one row per unit: (sample, population) pseudobulk for scRNA, sample for bulk. Ids in the view
that the data lacks are dropped, so a mismatched view yields few genes or units and the testers
answer ``untestable`` — the signal that triggers resolution — rather than an exception.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import io
import json
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

import pandas as pd

from agentcoop.coop.claims import Vocabulary

KINDS = ("scrna", "bulk")
#: Sample group for samples in the data but outside every contrast.
EXCLUDE = "exclude"
#: Cell population outside the case vocabulary (kept in the view, never in a unit).
OTHER = "other"
REQUIRED = {"genes.tsv": ("native_id", "symbol"),
            "samples.tsv": ("sample", "donor", "group"),
            "cells.tsv": ("cell", "sample", "population")}
#: Offending values quoted per message; messages go into repair prompts, so they stay short.
SHOW = 5


@dataclass
class SiteView:
    root: Path
    genes: dict[str, str]           # native feature id -> symbol (uppercase, unmapped rows dropped)
    samples: pd.DataFrame           # columns: sample, donor, group (+ covariates), all strings
    cells: pd.DataFrame | None      # columns: cell, sample, population (scRNA only)
    spec: dict


def _few(values) -> str:
    vals = sorted({str(v) for v in values})
    more = f" (+{len(vals) - SHOW} more)" if len(vals) > SHOW else ""
    return ", ".join(repr(v) for v in vals[:SHOW]) + more


def _read_tsv(path: Path, errors: list[str]) -> pd.DataFrame | None:
    """Strict TSV: every row has the header's field count, every cell a stripped string.

    Parsed with ``csv`` rather than pandas, which silently truncates ragged rows or turns the
    first column into an index; an absent, unreadable or ragged file becomes an error message.
    """
    if not path.is_file():
        errors.append(f"{path.name}: missing")
        return None
    try:
        rows = [r for r in csv.reader(io.StringIO(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")),
                                         delimiter="\t") if r]
    except (OSError, ValueError, csv.Error) as e:      # ValueError covers UnicodeDecodeError
        errors.append(f"{path.name}: unreadable ({type(e).__name__}: {e})")
        return None
    if not rows:
        errors.append(f"{path.name}: empty")
        return None
    header = [c.strip() for c in rows[0]]
    problems = []
    dup = {c for c in header if header.count(c) > 1}
    if dup:
        problems.append(f"duplicate column(s) {_few(dup)}")
    ragged = [i for i, r in enumerate(rows[1:], start=2) if len(r) != len(header)]
    if ragged:
        problems.append(f"{len(ragged)} row(s) whose field count differs from the header "
                        f"(first: line {ragged[0]})")
    missing = [c for c in REQUIRED[path.name] if c not in header]
    if missing:
        problems.append(f"missing column(s) {', '.join(missing)} (has: {', '.join(header)})")
    if problems:
        errors.extend(f"{path.name}: {p}" for p in problems)
        return None
    return pd.DataFrame(rows[1:], columns=header).apply(lambda s: s.str.strip())


def _check_ids(df: pd.DataFrame, col: str, name: str, errors: list[str], *, unique: bool) -> None:
    """Ids must be non-empty and, where they key a table, unique."""
    empty = int((df[col] == "").sum())
    if empty:
        errors.append(f"{name}: {empty} row(s) with an empty {col}")
    if unique:
        dup = df.loc[df[col].duplicated() & (df[col] != ""), col]
        if len(dup):
            errors.append(f"{name}: duplicate {col} {_few(dup)}")


def _read_spec(path: Path, errors: list[str]) -> dict | None:
    if not path.is_file():
        errors.append("spec.json: missing")
        return None
    try:
        spec = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except (OSError, ValueError) as e:      # JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError
        errors.append(f"spec.json: unreadable ({type(e).__name__}: {e})")
        return None
    if not isinstance(spec, dict):
        errors.append(f"spec.json: must be a JSON object, got {type(spec).__name__}")
        return None
    return spec


def load_view(root: Path, *, kind: str, vocab: Vocabulary) -> tuple[SiteView | None, list[str]]:
    """``(view, warnings)`` when the view is usable, else ``(None, errors)``.

    Never raises on the site's files; every defect is collected (not just the first) so a single
    repair call can fix them all. Only an invalid ``kind`` (a caller bug) raises.
    """
    if kind not in KINDS:
        raise ValueError(f"unknown data kind {kind!r}")
    root = Path(root)
    if not root.is_dir():
        return None, [f"view directory {root} missing"]
    errors: list[str] = []
    warnings: list[str] = []

    genes = _read_tsv(root / "genes.tsv", errors)
    mapping: dict[str, str] = {}
    if genes is not None:
        _check_ids(genes, "native_id", "genes.tsv", errors, unique=True)
        symbols = genes["symbol"].str.upper()
        mapped = symbols != ""
        if (~mapped).any():
            warnings.append(f"genes.tsv: {int((~mapped).sum())} row(s) without a symbol dropped")
        mapping = dict(zip(genes.loc[mapped, "native_id"], symbols[mapped]))
        if not mapping:
            errors.append("genes.tsv: no feature mapped to a symbol")

    samples = _read_tsv(root / "samples.tsv", errors)
    if samples is not None:
        _check_ids(samples, "sample", "samples.tsv", errors, unique=True)
        _check_ids(samples, "donor", "samples.tsv", errors, unique=False)
        allowed = (*vocab.groups, EXCLUDE)
        bad = samples.loc[~samples["group"].isin(allowed), "group"]
        if len(bad):
            errors.append(f"samples.tsv: group(s) {_few(bad)} not in the vocabulary "
                          f"(allowed: {', '.join(allowed)})")
        elif not (samples["group"] != EXCLUDE).any():
            errors.append("samples.tsv: no sample in a vocabulary group")

    cells = None
    if kind == "scrna":
        cells = _read_tsv(root / "cells.tsv", errors)
        if cells is not None:
            _check_ids(cells, "cell", "cells.tsv", errors, unique=True)
            allowed = (*vocab.populations, OTHER)
            bad = cells.loc[~cells["population"].isin(allowed), "population"]
            if len(bad):
                errors.append(f"cells.tsv: population(s) {_few(bad)} not in the vocabulary "
                              f"(allowed: {', '.join(allowed)})")
            elif not (cells["population"] != OTHER).any():
                errors.append("cells.tsv: no cell in a vocabulary population")
            if samples is not None:
                unknown = cells.loc[~cells["sample"].isin(samples["sample"]), "sample"]
                if len(unknown):
                    errors.append(f"cells.tsv: {len(unknown)} cell(s) reference unknown "
                                  f"sample(s) {_few(unknown)}")

    spec = _read_spec(root / "spec.json", errors)
    if errors:
        return None, errors
    return SiteView(root=root, genes=mapping, samples=samples.reset_index(drop=True),
                    cells=None if cells is None else cells.reset_index(drop=True),
                    spec=spec), warnings


def write_view(root: Path, *, genes: dict[str, str], samples: pd.DataFrame,
               cells: pd.DataFrame | None, spec: dict) -> None:
    """Write the view files in the format site agents are asked for (case scripts, tests)."""
    root = Path(root)
    root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    pd.DataFrame({"native_id": list(genes), "symbol": list(genes.values())}).to_csv(
        root / "genes.tsv", sep="\t", index=False)
    samples.to_csv(root / "samples.tsv", sep="\t", index=False)
    if cells is not None:
        cells.to_csv(root / "cells.tsv", sep="\t", index=False)
    (root / "spec.json").write_text(json.dumps(spec, indent=1, sort_keys=True), encoding="utf-8")
```


and append to the end of `tests/test_view.py`:

```python
def _scrna_table(tmp_path: Path, **view):
    root, data = _scrna(tmp_path, **view)
    loaded, problems = load_view(root, kind="scrna", vocab=VOCAB)
    assert loaded is not None, problems
    return site_table(loaded, data, kind="scrna")


def test_pseudobulk_sums_symbols_and_cpm(tmp_path):
    table = _scrna_table(tmp_path)
    assert table.kind == "scrna"
    assert table.genes == ["CD3E", "GZMB"]
    assert table.units.to_dict("list") == {
        "unit": ["s1|B", "s1|CD4 T", "s2|B", "s2|CD4 T"], "donor": ["d1", "d1", "d2", "d2"],
        "group": ["control", "control", "treated", "treated"],
        "population": ["B", "CD4 T", "B", "CD4 T"], "n_cells": [1, 2, 2, 1]}
    # per unit: CD3E = ENSG2 + ENSG3, GZMB = ENSG1; library = all features, unmapped ENSG4 included
    sums = np.array([[0, 5], [7, 1], [7, 1], [4, 2]], dtype=float)
    library = np.array([10, 14, 12, 8], dtype=float)
    cpm = sums / library[:, None] * 1e6
    np.testing.assert_allclose(table.values, np.log2(cpm + 1))
    np.testing.assert_allclose(table.population_mean_cpm("B"), cpm[[0, 2]].mean(axis=0))
    np.testing.assert_allclose(table.population_mean_cpm("CD4 T"), cpm[[1, 3]].mean(axis=0))


def test_other_excluded_and_unlisted_cells_never_enter_a_unit(tmp_path):
    table = _scrna_table(tmp_path)
    assert "other" not in set(table.units["population"])
    assert "exclude" not in set(table.units["group"])
    assert table.units["n_cells"].sum() == 6            # c1-c3, c5-c7; not c4, c8, c9
    # a population the site lacks has no measurable gene (tester -> untestable, never supported)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(table.population_mean_cpm("NK"), [0.0, 0.0])


def test_composition_fractions_of_kept_cells(tmp_path):
    comp = _scrna_table(tmp_path).composition
    assert list(comp.index) == ["s1", "s2"]
    assert list(comp.columns) == ["B", "CD4 T", "group", "donor"]
    np.testing.assert_allclose(comp[["B", "CD4 T"]].to_numpy(dtype=float),
                               [[1 / 3, 2 / 3], [2 / 3, 1 / 3]])  # c4 ("other") not counted
    assert comp["group"].tolist() == ["control", "treated"]
    assert comp["donor"].tolist() == ["d1", "d2"]


def test_pseudobulk_matches_a_naive_loop_on_dense_counts(tmp_path):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    n_cells, n_feat = 400, 30
    x = rng.poisson(2.0, size=(n_cells, n_feat)).astype(np.int64)
    feats = [f"F{j}" for j in range(n_feat)]
    genes = {f: f"G{j % 20}" for j, f in enumerate(feats)}          # F20..F29 duplicate G0..G9
    samples = pd.DataFrame({"sample": [f"s{i}" for i in range(6)],
                            "donor": [f"d{i}" for i in range(6)],
                            "group": ["control", "control", "treated", "treated", "mimic",
                                      "exclude"]})
    cells = pd.DataFrame({"cell": [f"c{i}" for i in range(n_cells)],
                          "sample": rng.choice(samples["sample"], n_cells),
                          "population": rng.choice(["CD4 T", "B", "NK", "other"], n_cells)})
    data = tmp_path / "dense.h5ad"
    ad.AnnData(X=x, obs=pd.DataFrame(index=cells["cell"].tolist()),
               var=pd.DataFrame(index=feats)).write_h5ad(data)
    write_view(tmp_path / "v", genes=genes, samples=samples, cells=cells, spec={})
    view, problems = load_view(tmp_path / "v", kind="scrna", vocab=VOCAB)
    assert view is not None, problems
    table = site_table(view, data, kind="scrna")

    assert table.genes == sorted({f"G{j}" for j in range(20)})
    kept = cells[(cells["population"] != "other") & (cells["sample"] != "s5")]
    for row, unit in zip(table.values, table.units.itertuples()):
        sample, pop = unit.unit.split("|")
        mask = cells["cell"].isin(kept.loc[(kept["sample"] == sample)
                                           & (kept["population"] == pop), "cell"]).to_numpy()
        raw = x[mask].sum(axis=0)
        by_symbol = pd.Series(raw, index=[genes[f] for f in feats]).groupby(level=0).sum()
        expected = np.log2(by_symbol[table.genes].to_numpy() / raw.sum() * 1e6 + 1)
        np.testing.assert_allclose(row, expected)
        assert unit.n_cells == mask.sum()


def test_view_ids_missing_from_the_data_give_an_empty_table_not_an_error(tmp_path):
    no_genes = _scrna_table(tmp_path / "a", genes={"GZMB": "GZMB", "CD3E": "CD3E"})
    assert no_genes.genes == [] and no_genes.values.shape == (4, 0)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(no_genes.population_mean_cpm("B"), np.zeros(0))
    renamed = _cells_frame().assign(cell=lambda d: "x" + d["cell"])
    no_cells = _scrna_table(tmp_path / "b", cells=renamed)
    assert len(no_cells.units) == 0 and no_cells.values.shape == (0, 2)
    assert len(no_cells.composition) == 0


BULK = pd.DataFrame({
    "native_id": ["P1", "P2", "P3", "P4", "P5", "0006"],
    "b1": [1, 0, 5, 7, 1, 3], "b2": [2, 10, 5, 7, np.nan, 3],
    "b3": [3, 20, 1, 7, 1, 3], "b4": [4, 30, 9, 7, 1, 3],
    "b5": [100, 15, 0, 0, 0, 0], "b6": [100, 15, 0, 0, 0, 0],   # excluded samples
    "b7": [0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]})                                  # not in samples.tsv
#: P1/P2 share a symbol: over the kept samples b1-b4 P2 has the larger IQR (15 vs 1.5); with the
#: excluded b5/b6 included P1 would win (73.75 vs 7.5). P4 is unmapped, P5 has a missing value,
#: "0006" is a numeric-looking id that must stay a string.
BULK_GENES = {"P1": "IFI44L", "P2": "ifi44l", "P3": "GBP5", "P4": "", "P5": "STAT1",
              "0006": "CONST"}
BULK_SAMPLES = pd.DataFrame({"sample": [f"b{i}" for i in range(1, 7)],
                             "donor": [f"p{i}" for i in range(1, 7)],
                             "group": ["control", "control", "treated", "treated", "exclude",
                                       "exclude"]})


def _bulk_table(tmp_path: Path, matrix: pd.DataFrame, genes: dict[str, str],
                samples: pd.DataFrame):
    tmp_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    data = tmp_path / "expr.tsv"
    matrix.to_csv(data, sep="\t", index=False)
    write_view(tmp_path / "view", genes=genes, samples=samples, cells=None,
               spec={"platform": "HT-12"})
    view, problems = load_view(tmp_path / "view", kind="bulk", vocab=VOCAB)
    assert view is not None and view.cells is None, problems
    return site_table(view, data, kind="bulk")


def test_bulk_keeps_the_largest_iqr_probe_and_rank_normalizes(tmp_path):
    table = _bulk_table(tmp_path, BULK, BULK_GENES, BULK_SAMPLES)
    assert table.kind == "bulk" and table.mean_cpm is None and table.composition is None
    assert table.genes == ["CONST", "GBP5", "IFI44L"]
    assert table.units.to_dict("list") == {
        "unit": ["b1", "b2", "b3", "b4"], "donor": ["p1", "p2", "p3", "p4"],
        "group": ["control", "control", "treated", "treated"], "population": ["all"] * 4,
        "n_cells": [0] * 4}
    expected = np.column_stack([
        np.zeros(4),                                            # constant: all ties -> 0
        norm.ppf(np.array([2.5, 2.5, 1, 4]) / 5),               # P3, ties averaged
        norm.ppf(np.array([1, 2, 3, 4]) / 5)])                  # P2, not P1
    np.testing.assert_allclose(table.values, expected)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        table.population_mean_cpm("all")


def test_rank_normal_is_scale_free_monotone_and_centred(tmp_path):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(1)
    raw = rng.lognormal(5, 1, size=(8, 10))                     # probes x samples, no ties
    probes = [f"P{i}" for i in range(8)]
    samples = pd.DataFrame({"sample": [f"b{j}" for j in range(10)],
                            "donor": [f"p{j}" for j in range(10)],
                            "group": ["control"] * 5 + ["treated"] * 5})
    genes = {p: f"G{i}" for i, p in enumerate(probes)}
    linear = pd.DataFrame(raw, columns=samples["sample"]).assign(native_id=probes)
    logged = pd.DataFrame(np.log2(raw), columns=samples["sample"]).assign(native_id=probes)
    a = _bulk_table(tmp_path / "lin", linear[["native_id", *samples["sample"]]], genes, samples)
    b = _bulk_table(tmp_path / "log", logged[["native_id", *samples["sample"]]], genes, samples)
    np.testing.assert_allclose(a.values, b.values)              # platform scale does not matter
    np.testing.assert_allclose(a.values.sum(axis=0), 0.0, atol=1e-12)
    for j in range(8):
        assert (np.argsort(a.values[:, j]) == np.argsort(raw[j])).all()


def test_bulk_samples_missing_from_the_data_give_an_empty_table(tmp_path):
    renamed = BULK_SAMPLES.assign(sample=lambda d: "x" + d["sample"])
    table = _bulk_table(tmp_path, BULK, BULK_GENES, renamed)
    assert len(table.units) == 0 and table.genes == [] and table.values.shape == (0, 0)
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Implement site tables**

In `agentcoop/coop/view.py` replace the import line

```python
import pandas as pd
```

with

```python
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import scipy.sparse as sp
from scipy.stats import norm, rankdata
```

and append to the end of `agentcoop/coop/view.py`:

```python
@dataclass
class SiteTable:
    """What testers read. Rows of ``values`` follow ``units``; columns follow ``genes`` (sorted).

    units: unit, donor, group, population ("all" for bulk), n_cells (0 for bulk).
    values: scRNA log2(CPM+1) pseudobulk per (sample, population); bulk per-gene rank-to-normal.
    mean_cpm: scRNA only, populations (sorted) x genes, mean CPM over each population's units.
    composition: scRNA only, one row per sample: fraction of its kept cells in each population,
    plus "group" and "donor".
    """
    kind: str
    genes: list[str]
    units: pd.DataFrame
    values: np.ndarray
    mean_cpm: np.ndarray | None
    composition: pd.DataFrame | None

    def population_mean_cpm(self, population: str) -> np.ndarray:
        """Mean CPM per gene over the population's units.

        A population the site lacks gets zeros: no gene is measurable there, so a test of it is
        untestable, never supported.
        """
        if self.mean_cpm is None:
            raise ValueError("bulk site tables carry no CPM")
        pops = sorted(self.units["population"].unique())
        if population not in pops:
            return np.zeros(len(self.genes))
        return self.mean_cpm[pops.index(population)]


def site_table(view: SiteView, data_path: Path, *, kind: str) -> SiteTable:
    """Compute the site table of a valid view (once per view; testers read nothing else)."""
    if kind == "scrna":
        return _scrna_table(view, Path(data_path))
    if kind == "bulk":
        return _bulk_table(view, Path(data_path))
    raise ValueError(f"unknown data kind {kind!r}")


def _kept_samples(view: SiteView) -> pd.DataFrame:
    return view.samples[view.samples["group"] != EXCLUDE].set_index("sample")


def _scrna_table(view: SiteView, data_path: Path) -> SiteTable:
    """Pseudobulk by sparse indicator products: cells -> units, native features -> symbols.

    The library size of a unit is its total count over *all* native features, so how many
    features the agent mapped does not change the CPM of the genes it did map.
    """
    import anndata as ad    # heavy; only scRNA sites need it

    adata = ad.read_h5ad(data_path)
    samples = _kept_samples(view)
    cells = view.cells[(view.cells["population"] != OTHER)
                       & view.cells["sample"].isin(samples.index)]
    rows = pd.Index(adata.obs_names).get_indexer(cells["cell"])
    cells, rows = cells[rows >= 0], rows[rows >= 0]

    grouped = cells.groupby(["sample", "population"], sort=True)
    codes = grouped.ngroup().to_numpy()
    units = grouped.size().rename("n_cells").reset_index()
    to_unit = sp.csr_matrix((np.ones(len(rows)), (codes, rows)), shape=(len(units), adata.n_obs))
    counts = (to_unit @ sp.csr_matrix(adata.X)).tocsr()          # units x native features
    library = np.asarray(counts.sum(axis=1), dtype=float).ravel()

    cols = pd.Index(adata.var_names).get_indexer(list(view.genes))
    found = cols >= 0
    symbols, sym_codes = np.unique(np.array(list(view.genes.values()), dtype=str)[found],
                                   return_inverse=True)
    to_symbol = sp.csr_matrix((np.ones(int(found.sum())), (cols[found], sym_codes)),
                              shape=(adata.n_vars, len(symbols)))
    cpm = (counts @ to_symbol).toarray() / np.maximum(library, 1.0)[:, None] * 1e6

    pops = sorted(units["population"].unique())
    mean_cpm = np.array([cpm[(units["population"] == p).to_numpy()].mean(axis=0) for p in pops])
    tab = pd.crosstab(cells["sample"], cells["population"])
    composition = tab.div(tab.sum(axis=1), axis=0)
    composition.columns.name = None
    composition["group"] = composition.index.map(samples["group"])
    composition["donor"] = composition.index.map(samples["donor"])

    units.insert(0, "unit", units["sample"] + "|" + units["population"])
    units["donor"] = units["sample"].map(samples["donor"])
    units["group"] = units["sample"].map(samples["group"])
    return SiteTable(kind="scrna", genes=symbols.tolist(),
                     units=units[["unit", "donor", "group", "population", "n_cells"]],
                     values=np.log2(cpm + 1.0),
                     mean_cpm=mean_cpm.reshape(len(pops), len(symbols)),
                     composition=composition)


def _bulk_table(view: SiteView, data_path: Path) -> SiteTable:
    """One probe per symbol (largest IQR across the kept samples — frozen rule), then a per-gene
    van der Waerden rank-to-normal transform, so sites on different platforms and scales
    (raw linear, log2, RNA-seq) give comparable scores."""
    header = pd.read_csv(data_path, sep="\t", nrows=0).columns
    samples = _kept_samples(view)
    kept = [s for s in samples.index if s in header[1:]]
    data = pd.read_csv(data_path, sep="\t", usecols=[header[0], *kept], index_col=header[0],
                       dtype={header[0]: str})          # ids stay strings ("0012" is not 12)

    x = data.loc[data.index.isin(list(view.genes)), kept].apply(pd.to_numeric, errors="coerce")
    arr = x.to_numpy(dtype=float)
    complete = np.isfinite(arr).all(axis=1) & bool(kept)       # probes with a value everywhere
    x, arr = x[complete], arr[complete]
    iqr = np.subtract(*np.percentile(arr, [75, 25], axis=1)) if arr.size else np.zeros(0)
    probes = pd.DataFrame({"native_id": x.index, "symbol": x.index.map(view.genes), "iqr": iqr})
    probes = (probes.sort_values(["symbol", "iqr", "native_id"], ascending=[True, False, True])
              .drop_duplicates("symbol"))
    expr = x.loc[probes["native_id"]].to_numpy(dtype=float).T     # samples x symbols
    values = norm.ppf(rankdata(expr, axis=0) / (len(kept) + 1))

    units = pd.DataFrame({"unit": kept, "donor": samples.loc[kept, "donor"].tolist(),
                          "group": samples.loc[kept, "group"].tolist(),
                          "population": ["all"] * len(kept), "n_cells": [0] * len(kept)})
    return SiteTable(kind="bulk", genes=probes["symbol"].tolist(), units=units,
                     values=values.reshape(len(kept), len(probes)), mean_cpm=None,
                     composition=None)
```

---

### Task 5: Testers

Deterministic site testers (spec §2.4): given one site's `SiteTable` and a claim, answer "does the claim hold here?" with summary statistics only. scRNA `de` = Welch t on donor module scores plus the v3 competitive null (ported from `evidence/checks.py::_competitive_ok`: 200 random measurable gene sets, 95th percentile, sha256-seeded), scRNA `composition` = Welch t on donor logit fractions, bulk `de` = AUC of sample scores with a bootstrap CI. Statuses come from the 95% CI, the equivalence band and the raw p; `apply_bh` then demotes within the run's BH family.

**Files:**
- Create: `agentcoop/coop/tester.py`
- Create: `tests/test_tester.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Task 3, `agentcoop/coop/claims.py`): `Claim` (fields `id`, `kind`, `population`, `up_genes`, `down_genes`, `effect`; property `contrast`).
- Consumes (Task 4, `agentcoop/coop/view.py`): `SiteTable` (`kind`, `genes`, `units` with columns `unit, donor, group, population, n_cells` and rows aligned with `values`, `values`, `composition` with one row per donor, one column per population plus `group`) and `SiteTable.population_mean_cpm(population) -> np.ndarray` aligned with `genes`. The tester reads CPM only through that method, never `mean_cpm` directly.
- Produces (contract, exact):
```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TesterParams: ...          # fields and defaults exactly as in the contract
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TestResult: ...            # fields exactly as in the contract
def test_claim(table: SiteTable, claim: Claim, params: TesterParams, *, site: str, test: str = "replication", contrast: tuple[str, str] | None = None) -> TestResult
def apply_bh(results: list[TestResult], params: TesterParams) -> list[TestResult]
def export_gene_stats(table: SiteTable, population: str, contrast: tuple[str, str], params: TesterParams) -> pd.DataFrame   # columns gene, effect, se, n1, n2
```
- Produces (conventions Tasks 6, 7 and 9 rely on):
  - `TestResult.reason` vocabulary: `untestable` -> `"groups" | "population" | "genes"`; `inconclusive` without a statistic -> `"units" | "variance"`; computed results -> `"module"` (scRNA de), `"logit"` (composition), `"auc"` (bulk de). Step 4 (Task 6) triggers on `status == "untestable" and reason in ("genes", "groups")`. Task 7 builds `untestable(view)` itself: `TestResult(claim.id, site, test, "untestable", None, None, None, None, None, None, None, "view")`.
  - `effect` is signed so that positive = the claimed direction (de: module score up minus down; composition: sign of `claim.effect`; bulk: AUC - 0.5).
  - `test_claim` statuses are provisional on the raw p. The caller passes one BH family per run to `apply_bh`, which only demotes (supported/contradicted -> absent/inconclusive) and keeps `p` raw.
  - Random draws (null sets, bootstrap) are seeded by sha256(claim.id, site), so a re-test is an exact replay.
  - `TesterParams`, `TestResult` and `test_claim` set `__test__ = False`, so test modules can import them by name without pytest collecting them as tests.
- Review Focus 2 is pinned by `test_too_few_units_is_inconclusive_even_with_a_planted_effect` and `test_partial_gene_coverage_is_untestable`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests for `test_claim`**

Create `tests/test_tester.py`. Site tables are built in memory. `_Table` pins `population_mean_cpm` so the tests do not depend on how Task 4 stores `mean_cpm` across populations. The bulk null is exact by construction: on the program genes, TB samples copy the HC samples.

```python
"""Site testers on synthetic site tables built in memory (no files, no agents, no network)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import replace

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import pytest
from scipy import stats

from agentcoop.coop.claims import Claim
from agentcoop.coop.tester import TesterParams, test_claim
from agentcoop.coop.view import SiteTable

P = TesterParams()
POP = "CD14 Mono"
GENES = [f"G{i}" for i in range(60)]
PROGRAM = GENES[:6]


class _Table(SiteTable):
    """The tester reads CPM only through population_mean_cpm(); pinning that accessor keeps
    these tests independent of how view.py lays out ``mean_cpm`` across populations."""

    def population_mean_cpm(self, population: str) -> np.ndarray:
        return self.mean_cpm


def scrna_table(shift: dict[str, dict[str, float]] | None = None, *,
                groups: tuple[str, ...] = ("control", "treated"), donors: int = 4,
                n_cells: dict[str, int] | None = None, genes: list[str] = GENES,
                low_cpm: tuple[str, ...] = (), seed: int = 0) -> _Table:
    """Donor pseudobulk of one population: log2 CPM = gene baseline + donor offset + noise,
    plus ``shift[group][gene]`` in that group's donors."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    base = rng.uniform(5, 8, len(genes))
    rows, values = [], []
    for g in groups:
        for k in range(donors):
            unit = f"{g}{k}"
            rows.append({"unit": unit, "donor": unit, "group": g, "population": POP,
                         "n_cells": (n_cells or {}).get(unit, 100)})
            v = base + rng.normal(0, 0.05) + rng.normal(0, 0.05, len(genes))
            for gene, s in (shift or {}).get(g, {}).items():
                v[genes.index(gene)] += s
            values.append(v)
    cpm = np.where(np.isin(genes, low_cpm), 5.0, 100.0)
    return _Table(kind="scrna", genes=list(genes), units=pd.DataFrame(rows),
                  values=np.array(values), mean_cpm=cpm, composition=None)


def bulk_table(shift: float, *, n: int = 12, seed: int = 0) -> _Table:
    """``n`` TB and ``n`` HC samples, per-gene rank-to-normal within the site. On PROGRAM the TB
    samples copy the HC samples' raw values plus ``shift``, so shift 0 is an exact null."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    raw = rng.normal(0, 1, (2 * n, len(GENES)))
    raw[:n, :len(PROGRAM)] = raw[n:, :len(PROGRAM)] + shift
    values = stats.norm.ppf((stats.rankdata(raw, axis=0) - 0.5) / (2 * n))
    units = pd.DataFrame({"unit": [f"s{i}" for i in range(2 * n)],
                          "donor": [f"s{i}" for i in range(2 * n)],
                          "group": ["TB"] * n + ["HC"] * n, "population": "all", "n_cells": 0})
    return _Table(kind="bulk", genes=list(GENES), units=units, values=values, mean_cpm=None,
                  composition=None)


def claim(up=PROGRAM, down=(), *, kind="de", population=POP, contrast=("treated", "control"),
          effect=None, cid="c1") -> Claim:
    return Claim(id=cid, site="A", kind=kind, population=population, target=contrast[0],
                 reference=contrast[1], up_genes=tuple(up), down_genes=tuple(down),
                 effect=effect, n_units=None, rank=1)


def planted(s: float, groups=("treated",), genes=PROGRAM) -> dict[str, dict[str, float]]:
    return {g: {gene: s for gene in genes} for g in groups}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# scRNA de
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_planted_program_is_supported():
    r = test_claim(scrna_table(planted(1.0)), claim(), P, site="B")
    assert (r.status, r.reason, r.test, r.site, r.claim_id) == (
        "supported", "module", "replication", "B", "c1")
    assert r.competitive_ok is True and r.ci95[0] > 0 and r.p <= P.q
    assert abs(r.effect - 1.0) < 0.15 and r.n_per_group == (4, 4) and 0 < r.mde < 1.0


def test_up_and_down_genes_form_one_module():
    shift = {"treated": {**{g: 1.0 for g in PROGRAM[:3]}, **{g: -1.0 for g in PROGRAM[3:]}}}
    r = test_claim(scrna_table(shift), claim(up=PROGRAM[:3], down=PROGRAM[3:]), P, site="B")
    assert r.status == "supported" and abs(r.effect - 2.0) < 0.2


def test_reversed_program_is_contradicted():
    r = test_claim(scrna_table(planted(-1.0)), claim(), P, site="B")
    assert r.status == "contradicted" and r.ci95[1] < 0 and r.competitive_ok is False


def test_null_program_is_not_called():
    r = test_claim(scrna_table(), claim(), P, site="B")
    assert r.status in ("absent", "inconclusive")


def test_global_shift_is_not_supported_thanks_to_competitive_null():
    """A compositional artefact moves every gene (here by 0.5..1.5); the claim's genes move by
    1.0, clearly non-zero, but no more than random gene sets do."""
    shift = dict(zip(GENES, np.linspace(0.5, 1.5, len(GENES))))
    shift.update({g: 1.0 for g in PROGRAM})
    r = test_claim(scrna_table({"treated": shift}), claim(), P, site="B")
    assert r.ci95[0] > 0 and r.p <= P.q          # a self-contained test would call it
    assert r.competitive_ok is False and r.status == "inconclusive"


def test_too_few_units_is_inconclusive_even_with_a_planted_effect():
    """Review focus 2: donors below min_cells are dropped; < min_units per group -> inconclusive."""
    small = {"treated0": 10, "treated1": 19}
    r = test_claim(scrna_table(planted(1.0), n_cells=small), claim(), P, site="B")
    assert (r.status, r.reason, r.n_per_group, r.effect, r.p) == (
        "inconclusive", "units", (2, 4), None, None)
    r = test_claim(scrna_table(planted(1.0), donors=2), claim(), P, site="B")
    assert (r.status, r.reason, r.n_per_group) == ("inconclusive", "units", (2, 2))


def test_partial_gene_coverage_is_untestable():
    """Review focus 2: 2 of 6 claim genes measurable (2 low-CPM, 2 absent) -> untestable(genes);
    3 of 6 meets max(min_genes, 50%) and is tested."""
    table = scrna_table(planted(1.0), genes=GENES + ["LOWA", "LOWB"], low_cpm=("LOWA", "LOWB"))
    r = test_claim(table, claim(up=PROGRAM[:2] + ["LOWA", "LOWB", "MISS1", "MISS2"]), P,
                   site="B")
    assert (r.status, r.reason, r.effect, r.p) == ("untestable", "genes", None, None)
    r = test_claim(table, claim(up=PROGRAM[:3] + ["LOWA", "LOWB", "MISS1"]), P, site="B")
    assert r.status == "supported"


def test_missing_groups_or_population_is_untestable():
    table = scrna_table(planted(1.0))
    r = test_claim(table, claim(contrast=("mimic", "control")), P, site="B")
    assert (r.status, r.reason) == ("untestable", "groups")
    r = test_claim(table, claim(population="NK"), P, site="B")
    assert (r.status, r.reason) == ("untestable", "population")


def test_scope_contrast_overrides_the_claims_contrast():
    groups = ("control", "treated", "mimic")
    specific = scrna_table(planted(1.0), groups=groups)
    r = test_claim(specific, claim(), P, site="M", test="scope", contrast=("mimic", "control"))
    assert (r.test, r.status) == ("scope", "absent")
    shared = scrna_table(planted(1.0, groups=("treated", "mimic")), groups=groups)
    r = test_claim(shared, claim(), P, site="M", test="scope", contrast=("mimic", "control"))
    assert (r.test, r.status) == ("scope", "supported")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# scRNA composition
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def with_composition(fractions: dict[str, float], *, seed: int = 0) -> _Table:
    """Four donors per group; ``POP`` fraction per group plus small noise, NK is the rest."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    table = scrna_table(groups=tuple(fractions))
    rows = []
    for g, f in fractions.items():
        for k in range(4):
            x = f + rng.normal(0, 0.01)
            rows.append({"donor": f"{g}{k}", POP: x, "NK": 1 - x, "pDC": 0.0, "group": g})
    return replace(table, composition=pd.DataFrame(rows).set_index("donor"))


def test_composition_shift_is_signed_by_the_claimed_direction():
    table = with_composition({"control": 0.15, "treated": 0.30})
    up = test_claim(table, claim((), kind="composition", effect=1.0), P, site="B")
    assert (up.status, up.reason, up.competitive_ok) == ("supported", "logit", None)
    assert up.ci95[0] > 0 and up.n_per_group == (4, 4)
    down = test_claim(table, claim((), kind="composition", effect=-0.5), P, site="B")
    assert down.status == "contradicted" and down.effect == pytest.approx(-up.effect)


def test_composition_without_change_is_absent():
    table = with_composition({"control": 0.2, "treated": 0.2})
    r = test_claim(table, claim((), kind="composition", effect=1.0), P, site="B")
    assert r.status == "absent"


def test_composition_untestable_or_degenerate():
    table = with_composition({"control": 0.15, "treated": 0.30})
    r = test_claim(table, claim((), kind="composition", population="B", effect=1.0), P, site="B")
    assert (r.status, r.reason) == ("untestable", "population")
    r = test_claim(table, claim((), kind="composition", population="pDC", effect=1.0), P,
                   site="B")
    assert (r.status, r.reason, r.p) == ("inconclusive", "variance", None)
    r = test_claim(bulk_table(0.0), claim((), kind="composition", contrast=("TB", "HC"),
                                          effect=1.0), P, site="B")
    assert (r.status, r.reason) == ("untestable", "population")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# bulk de
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def bulk_claim(up=PROGRAM) -> Claim:
    return claim(up, population="whole blood", contrast=("TB", "HC"))


def test_bulk_auc_supported_contradicted_and_null():
    r = test_claim(bulk_table(0.8), bulk_claim(), P, site="S2")
    assert (r.status, r.reason, r.competitive_ok) == ("supported", "auc", None)
    assert r.effect > 0.3 and r.ci95[0] > 0 and r.p <= P.q and r.n_per_group == (12, 12)
    assert test_claim(bulk_table(-0.8), bulk_claim(), P, site="S2").status == "contradicted"
    null = test_claim(bulk_table(0.0), bulk_claim(), P, site="S2")
    assert (null.status, null.effect) == ("inconclusive", 0.0)   # 12 v 12 cannot show absence


def test_bulk_partial_gene_coverage_is_untestable():
    r = test_claim(bulk_table(0.8), bulk_claim(PROGRAM[:2] + ["X1", "X2", "X3", "X4"]), P,
                   site="S2")
    assert (r.status, r.reason) == ("untestable", "genes")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Determinism
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_same_inputs_give_identical_results():
    for make, c in ((lambda: scrna_table({"treated": dict.fromkeys(GENES, 0.5)}), claim()),
                    (lambda: bulk_table(0.3), bulk_claim())):
        first, second = test_claim(make(), c, P, site="B"), test_claim(make(), c, P, site="B")
        assert first == second
    # the bootstrap is seeded per (claim, site): another site draws other resamples
    other = test_claim(bulk_table(0.3), bulk_claim(), P, site="C")
    assert other.ci95 != test_claim(bulk_table(0.3), bulk_claim(), P, site="B").ci95
```
