- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the testers**

Create `agentcoop/coop/tester.py`:

```python
"""Deterministic site testers: does claim c hold at site s? (spec §2.4)

Testers run inside the site boundary on the site table and return summary statistics only. A
status is read off a 95% confidence interval and an equivalence band, so a site too small to
measure an effect says ``inconclusive``, not ``absent``. ``inconclusive`` and ``untestable``
are never support and never contradiction.

``TestResult.reason`` names the statistic of a computed result (``module``, ``logit``, ``auc``)
and the obstacle otherwise (``groups``, ``population``, ``genes``, ``units``, ``variance``);
``apply_bh`` reads it to pick the equivalence band. Random draws (competitive null, bootstrap)
are seeded by (claim id, site), so testing the same claim at the same site again is an exact
replay.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import math
from dataclasses import dataclass

import numpy as np
from scipy import stats

from agentcoop.coop.claims import Claim
from agentcoop.coop.view import SiteTable


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TesterParams:
    __test__ = False  # not a pytest class (name starts with "Test")
    delta_de: float = 0.25          # equivalence band, log2 module-score units (frozen on fsdev)
    delta_comp: float = 0.5         # logit units
    delta_auc: float = 0.05
    q: float = 0.05
    min_cells: int = 20
    min_units: int = 3              # per group
    min_genes: int = 3
    min_gene_frac: float = 0.5
    min_cpm: float = 20.0
    null_sets: int = 200
    null_quantile: float = 0.95
    n_boot: int = 1000


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TestResult:
    __test__ = False  # not a pytest class (name starts with "Test")
    claim_id: str
    site: str
    test: str                       # "replication" | "scope"
    status: str                     # "supported" | "contradicted" | "absent" | "inconclusive" | "untestable"
    effect: float | None
    se: float | None
    ci95: tuple[float, float] | None
    n_per_group: tuple[int, int] | None
    mde: float | None
    p: float | None                 # raw two-sided p; BH applied by apply_bh
    competitive_ok: bool | None
    reason: str


def _seed(claim_id: str, site: str) -> int:
    return int.from_bytes(hashlib.sha256(f"{claim_id}\x00{site}".encode()).digest()[:8], "big")


def _blocked(claim: Claim, site: str, test: str, status: str, reason: str,
             n: tuple[int, int] | None = None) -> TestResult:
    """A result without a statistic: the site could not test the claim, or not reliably."""
    return TestResult(claim.id, site, test, status, None, None, None, n, None, None, None, reason)


def _within(ci: tuple[float, float], band: float) -> bool:
    return -band < ci[0] and ci[1] < band


def _status(ci: tuple[float, float], p: float, q: float, band: float, *,
            up_ok: bool = True, down_ok: bool = True) -> str:
    """First match: supported, contradicted, absent (CI inside the band), inconclusive."""
    if ci[0] > 0 and p <= q and up_ok:
        return "supported"
    if ci[1] < 0 and p <= q and down_ok:
        return "contradicted"
    if _within(ci, band):
        return "absent"
    return "inconclusive"


def _welch(a: np.ndarray, b: np.ndarray) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
    """Difference of means, its standard error and the Welch–Satterthwaite df."""
    va, vb = a.var(ddof=1) / len(a), b.var(ddof=1) / len(b)
    diff = float(a.mean() - b.mean())
    if va + vb == 0:
        return diff, 0.0, math.nan
    df = (va + vb) ** 2 / (va ** 2 / (len(a) - 1) + vb ** 2 / (len(b) - 1))
    return diff, float(math.sqrt(va + vb)), float(df)


def _t_inference(diff: float, se: float, df: float) -> tuple[tuple[float, float], float, float]:
    """95% CI, two-sided p, and the effect detectable with 80% power at alpha 0.05."""
    t975 = float(stats.t.ppf(0.975, df))
    p = float(2 * stats.t.sf(abs(diff) / se, df))
    mde = (t975 + float(stats.t.ppf(0.8, df))) * se
    return (diff - t975 * se, diff + t975 * se), p, mde


def _unit_rows(table: SiteTable, population: str, target: str, reference: str,
               params: TesterParams) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """Rows of the target and reference units: in scRNA the population's donors with at least
    ``min_cells`` cells (smaller pseudobulks are noise); in bulk every sample."""
    u = table.units
    keep = np.ones(len(u), bool) if table.kind == "bulk" else (
        u["population"].eq(population) & u["n_cells"].ge(params.min_cells)).to_numpy()
    group = u["group"].to_numpy()
    return np.flatnonzero(keep & (group == target)), np.flatnonzero(keep & (group == reference))


def _measurable(table: SiteTable, population: str, params: TesterParams) -> np.ndarray:
    """Genes the site can measure: in scRNA mean CPM >= ``min_cpm`` in the population (sparse
    genes make noisy module scores); in bulk every gene (values are within-site ranks)."""
    if table.kind == "bulk":
        return np.ones(len(table.genes), bool)
    return table.population_mean_cpm(population) >= params.min_cpm


def _claim_genes(table: SiteTable, claim: Claim, measurable: np.ndarray,
                 params: TesterParams) -> tuple[list[int], list[int]] | None:
    """Columns of the claim's measurable up and down genes; None when fewer than
    max(min_genes, min_gene_frac of the claim's genes) are measurable — a site that sees a
    fragment of a program cannot say whether the program holds there."""
    col = {g: i for i, g in enumerate(table.genes)}
    up = [col[g] for g in claim.up_genes if g in col and measurable[col[g]]]
    down = [col[g] for g in claim.down_genes if g in col and measurable[col[g]]]
    n_claim = len(claim.up_genes) + len(claim.down_genes)
    if len(up) + len(down) < max(params.min_genes, math.ceil(params.min_gene_frac * n_claim)):
        return None
    return up, down


def _score(values: np.ndarray, up: list[int] | np.ndarray,
           down: list[int] | np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """Per-row mean of the up genes minus mean of the down genes; an empty side contributes 0."""
    s = np.zeros(values.shape[0])
    if len(up):
        s += values[:, up].mean(axis=1)
    if len(down):
        s -= values[:, down].mean(axis=1)
    return s


def _null_effects(gene_diff: np.ndarray, pool: np.ndarray, n_up: int, n_down: int,
                  params: TesterParams, seed: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """Module effects of random measurable gene sets with the claim's up/down sizes. The module
    effect is linear in the per-gene mean differences, so a set's effect is computed from them."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    null = np.empty(params.null_sets)
    for j in range(params.null_sets):
        pick = rng.choice(pool, size=n_up + n_down, replace=False)
        null[j] = _score(gene_diff[None, :], pick[:n_up], pick[n_up:])[0]
    return null


def _module_result(claim: Claim, site: str, test: str, table: SiteTable, up: list[int],
                   down: list[int], measurable: np.ndarray, t: np.ndarray, r: np.ndarray,
                   params: TesterParams) -> TestResult:
    """scRNA de: Welch test on donor module scores plus the competitive null — a global or
    compositional shift moves every gene, so the claim's genes must move more than random sets."""
    n = (len(t), len(r))
    score = _score(table.values, up, down)
    diff, se, df = _welch(score[t], score[r])
    if not se > 0:
        return _blocked(claim, site, test, "inconclusive", "variance", n)
    ci, p, mde = _t_inference(diff, se, df)
    gene_diff = table.values[t].mean(axis=0) - table.values[r].mean(axis=0)
    null = _null_effects(gene_diff, np.flatnonzero(measurable), len(up), len(down), params,
                         _seed(claim.id, site))
    hi, lo = np.quantile(null, [params.null_quantile, 1 - params.null_quantile])
    status = _status(ci, p, params.q, params.delta_de, up_ok=diff > hi, down_ok=diff < lo)
    return TestResult(claim.id, site, test, status, diff, se, ci, n, mde, p, bool(diff > hi),
                      "module")


def _boot_auc(a: np.ndarray, b: np.ndarray, n_boot: int, seed: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """AUCs of resamples drawn with replacement within each group; computed from ranks so memory
    stays O(n_boot * n) for cohorts of hundreds of samples."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    xa = a[rng.integers(0, len(a), (n_boot, len(a)))]
    xb = b[rng.integers(0, len(b), (n_boot, len(b)))]
    ranks = stats.rankdata(np.hstack([xa, xb]), axis=1)
    u = ranks[:, :len(a)].sum(axis=1) - len(a) * (len(a) + 1) / 2
    return u / (len(a) * len(b))


def _auc_result(claim: Claim, site: str, test: str, a: np.ndarray, b: np.ndarray,
                params: TesterParams) -> TestResult:
    """bulk de: effect = AUC − 0.5 of sample scores (Mann–Whitney), bootstrap 95% CI."""
    n = (len(a), len(b))
    mw = stats.mannwhitneyu(a, b, alternative="two-sided")
    effect = float(mw.statistic / (n[0] * n[1]) - 0.5)
    boot = _boot_auc(a, b, params.n_boot, _seed(claim.id, site)) - 0.5
    lo, hi = (float(x) for x in np.quantile(boot, [0.025, 0.975]))
    se = float(boot.std(ddof=1))
    mde = float((stats.norm.ppf(0.975) + stats.norm.ppf(0.8)) * se)
    p = float(mw.pvalue)
    status = _status((lo, hi), p, params.q, params.delta_auc)
    return TestResult(claim.id, site, test, status, effect, se, (lo, hi), n, mde, p, None, "auc")


def _test_de(table: SiteTable, claim: Claim, params: TesterParams, site: str, test: str,
             target: str, reference: str) -> TestResult:
    """Bulk sites hold one population (the whole sample), so the claim's population is matched
    only in scRNA."""
    if table.kind != "bulk" and claim.population not in set(table.units["population"]):
        return _blocked(claim, site, test, "untestable", "population")
    measurable = _measurable(table, claim.population, params)
    genes = _claim_genes(table, claim, measurable, params)
    if genes is None:
        return _blocked(claim, site, test, "untestable", "genes")
    up, down = genes
    t, r = _unit_rows(table, claim.population, target, reference, params)
    if min(len(t), len(r)) < params.min_units:
        return _blocked(claim, site, test, "inconclusive", "units", (len(t), len(r)))
    if table.kind == "bulk":
        score = _score(table.values, up, down)
        return _auc_result(claim, site, test, score[t], score[r], params)
    return _module_result(claim, site, test, table, up, down, measurable, t, r, params)


def _test_composition(table: SiteTable, claim: Claim, params: TesterParams, site: str,
                      test: str, target: str, reference: str) -> TestResult:
    """Donor-level logit cell fraction, Welch t, signed so that a positive effect is the claimed
    direction (sign of ``claim.effect``; a missing effect reads as an increase)."""
    comp = table.composition
    if comp is None or claim.population not in comp.columns:
        return _blocked(claim, site, test, "untestable", "population")
    group = comp["group"].to_numpy()
    frac = np.clip(comp[claim.population].to_numpy(dtype=float), 1e-3, 1 - 1e-3)
    logit = np.log(frac / (1 - frac)) * (-1.0 if (claim.effect or 0.0) < 0 else 1.0)
    a, b = logit[group == target], logit[group == reference]
    n = (len(a), len(b))
    if min(n) < params.min_units:
        return _blocked(claim, site, test, "inconclusive", "units", n)
    diff, se, df = _welch(a, b)
    if not se > 0:
        return _blocked(claim, site, test, "inconclusive", "variance", n)
    ci, p, mde = _t_inference(diff, se, df)
    return TestResult(claim.id, site, test, _status(ci, p, params.q, params.delta_comp), diff,
                      se, ci, n, mde, p, None, "logit")


def test_claim(table: SiteTable, claim: Claim, params: TesterParams, *, site: str,
               test: str = "replication", contrast: tuple[str, str] | None = None) -> TestResult:
    """Test ``claim`` on one site's table. ``contrast`` replaces the claim's own (scope tests,
    e.g. mimic vs control) and keeps the claimed direction. Status is provisional on the raw p;
    ``apply_bh`` demotes it within the run's BH family."""
    target, reference = contrast or claim.contrast
    present = set(table.units["group"])
    if target not in present or reference not in present:
        return _blocked(claim, site, test, "untestable", "groups")
    if claim.kind == "composition":
        return _test_composition(table, claim, params, site, test, target, reference)
    return _test_de(table, claim, params, site, test, target, reference)


test_claim.__test__ = False  # imported by test modules; not a pytest test
```

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# BH family and per-gene export (MA-genome input)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def result(status: str, p: float | None, ci: tuple[float, float] | None,
           reason: str = "module", cid: str = "c") -> TestResult:
    return TestResult(cid, "B", "replication", status, None if ci is None else sum(ci) / 2,
                      0.1, ci, (4, 4), 0.3, p, None, reason)


def test_apply_bh_demotes_marginal_results_and_keeps_raw_p():
    results = [result("supported", 0.001, (0.5, 1.5), cid="strong"),
               result("supported", 0.03, (0.05, 0.95), cid="wide"),
               result("supported", 0.04, (0.02, 0.20), cid="small"),
               result("contradicted", 0.02, (-0.90, -0.05), cid="against"),
               result("inconclusive", 0.5, (-0.6, 0.9)),
               result("inconclusive", 0.8, (-0.7, 0.8)),
               result("untestable", None, None, reason="genes", cid="none"),
               result("supported", 0.03, (0.01, 0.10), reason="", cid="no-band")]
    out = apply_bh(results, P)          # adjusted p: 0.007, then 0.0525..0.056 > q, 0.58, 0.8
    assert [r.status for r in out] == ["supported", "inconclusive", "absent", "inconclusive",
                                       "inconclusive", "inconclusive", "untestable",
                                       "inconclusive"]
    assert [r.p for r in out] == [r.p for r in results]
    assert apply_bh(results[:1], P) == results[:1]


def test_export_gene_stats_uses_the_testers_filters():
    table = scrna_table(planted(1.0), genes=GENES + ["LOWA"], low_cpm=("LOWA",),
                        n_cells={"treated0": 5})
    df = export_gene_stats(table, POP, ("treated", "control"), P)
    assert list(df.columns) == ["gene", "effect", "se", "n1", "n2"]
    assert "LOWA" not in set(df["gene"]) and len(df) == len(GENES)
    g0 = df.set_index("gene").loc["G0"]
    assert abs(g0["effect"] - 1.0) < 0.2 and g0["se"] > 0 and (g0["n1"], g0["n2"]) == (3, 4)
    assert export_gene_stats(table, POP, ("mimic", "control"), P).empty
    assert export_gene_stats(table, "NK", ("treated", "control"), P).empty
    bulk = export_gene_stats(bulk_table(0.8), "anything", ("TB", "HC"), P)
    assert len(bulk) == len(GENES) and set(bulk["n1"]) == {12}
```

```python
import hashlib
import math
from dataclasses import dataclass, replace

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from scipy import stats
```

Append to the end of `agentcoop/coop/tester.py`, after two blank lines following `test_claim.__test__ = False`:

```python
#: Equivalence band per statistic (the ``reason`` of a computed result).
_BANDS = {"module": "delta_de", "logit": "delta_comp", "auc": "delta_auc"}
_GENE_STATS = ["gene", "effect", "se", "n1", "n2"]


def apply_bh(results: list[TestResult], params: TesterParams) -> list[TestResult]:
    """Benjamini–Hochberg over the results' raw p (the caller passes one family: every
    non-proposer test of a run). Adjustment only demotes: a supported or contradicted result
    whose adjusted p exceeds q becomes absent (CI inside its band) or inconclusive. ``p`` stays
    raw; results without p are unchanged. A result whose ``reason`` names no statistic (built
    outside this module) has no known band and is demoted to inconclusive."""
    idx = [i for i, r in enumerate(results) if r.p is not None]
    adjusted = dict(zip(idx, stats.false_discovery_control([results[i].p for i in idx])))
    out = []
    for i, r in enumerate(results):
        if r.status in ("supported", "contradicted") and adjusted.get(i, 0.0) > params.q:
            band = getattr(params, _BANDS[r.reason]) if r.reason in _BANDS else None
            r = replace(r, status="absent" if band is not None and _within(r.ci95, band)
                        else "inconclusive")
        out.append(r)
    return out


def export_gene_stats(table: SiteTable, population: str, contrast: tuple[str, str],
                      params: TesterParams) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Per-gene effect (target − reference mean of the table's values) and Welch SE, for
    MA-genome. Same unit and gene filters as ``test_claim``; empty when the site cannot test the
    contrast; genes with zero variance are dropped (they would get infinite weight)."""
    target, reference = contrast
    if table.kind != "bulk" and population not in set(table.units["population"]):
        return pd.DataFrame(columns=_GENE_STATS)
    t, r = _unit_rows(table, population, target, reference, params)
    if min(len(t), len(r)) < params.min_units:
        return pd.DataFrame(columns=_GENE_STATS)
    keep = _measurable(table, population, params)
    a, b = table.values[t][:, keep], table.values[r][:, keep]
    se = np.sqrt(a.var(axis=0, ddof=1) / len(t) + b.var(axis=0, ddof=1) / len(r))
    out = pd.DataFrame({"gene": np.asarray(table.genes)[keep],
                        "effect": a.mean(axis=0) - b.mean(axis=0), "se": se,
                        "n1": len(t), "n2": len(r)})
    return out[out["se"] > 0].reset_index(drop=True)
```

### Task 6: Protocol (`coop/protocol.py`)

Spec §2.5 steps 1–5 against the `SiteHandle` interface: one shared label function, test routing, a single resolution pass driven by a coordinator LLM with fixed safeguards, and final re-labelling. No real site, agent or API call is involved: the tests use fake sites, a fake coordinator, an identity BH and a faked `llm.chat_json`.

**Files:**
- Create: `agentcoop/coop/protocol.py`
- Create: `tests/test_protocol.py`

**Interfaces:**

Consumes:
- `agentcoop.coop.claims` (Task 3): `Claim` (fields + `.contrast`), `claim_to_dict(c: Claim) -> dict`.
- `agentcoop.coop.tester` (Task 5): `TesterParams`, `TestResult`, `apply_bh(results: list[TestResult], params: TesterParams) -> list[TestResult]` (assumed to return one result per input, in input order). Also Task 5's published conventions:
  - `reason` of a computed result names its statistic (`"module"`, `"logit"`, `"auc"`). A blocked result names the obstacle (`"genes"`, `"groups"`, `"population"`, `"units"`, `"variance"`; Task 7 adds `"view"`).
  - `effect` is signed so that positive = the claimed direction; bulk = AUC − 0.5.
  - The module-level table `_BANDS` (statistic -> `TesterParams` field) is the one `apply_bh` uses. It is imported here so the pooled check and `apply_bh` read one table.
- `agentcoop.coop.site` (Task 7, built after this task): `CaseSpec` is duck-typed. The protocol reads only `question`, `default_spec`, `tester`, `scope_tests` and `scope_rule`, and imports it under `TYPE_CHECKING` only, because `site.py` builds on this module. Task 7's `Site` never raises on an agent failure: it charges the call, appends `{"op", "error"}` to `site.failures` and returns `[]` / `False`. The protocol reads that list with `getattr(site, "failures", [])`, so other `SiteHandle`s need not have it.
- `agentcoop.llm` (existing): `chat_json(messages, *, sample) -> tuple[Any, ChatResult]` (raises `ValueError` after its re-asks on non-JSON replies), `tag`, `current_tag`, `spent`, `BudgetExceeded`. Tests also use `record_replay`, `backbone` and `ChatResult`.
- `tests/conftest.py` (Task 1): the autouse `_hermetic` fixture puts `AGENTCOOP_LLM_DB` in `tmp_path`, so the coordinator tests ledger their fake calls offline.

Produces (contract names and signatures exactly; additions are marked and backward-compatible: Task 9 builds `ClaimRecord` / `ProtocolRun` positionally from the contract fields, and Task 10 calls `label_claim(proposer, others, case.tester)`):
```python
LABELS = ("unsupported", "disputed", "corroborated", "site-specific", "unresolved", "inconclusive")
CAUSES = ("analysis-choice", "handoff-loss", "site-artefact", "site-biology")
VIEW_CAUSES = ("genes", "groups")                          # added: untestable reasons that trigger step 4
ARTEFACT_REASON = "explained: site artefact"               # added
COORDINATOR_PROMPT: str                                    # added: the fixed coordinator prompt
def label_claim(proposer: TestResult, others: list[TestResult], params: TesterParams) -> str
@dataclass
class ClaimRecord:                                         # contract fields, plus:
    results_step3: list[TestResult] = []                   # added: same layout as `results`, before step 4
@dataclass
class ProtocolRun:                                         # contract fields and methods, plus:
    errors: list[dict] = []                                # added: failed agent calls {"site", "op", "error"}
class SiteHandle(Protocol): ...                            # exactly as in the contract
class Coordinator:                                         # contract __init__ / classify, plus:
    nominal_usd: float                                     # added: accumulated LLM cost (from the ledger)
def run_protocol(sites: list[SiteHandle], case: "CaseSpec", *, sample: int, followup_budget: int,
                 coordinator: Coordinator | None = None) -> ProtocolRun
```

Design decisions:
1. **Band of the pooled "absent" check.** `label_claim` keeps the contract signature. It reads the band from the statistic each result names in `reason`, through Task 5's `_BANDS`: `delta_de` for `module`, `delta_comp` for `logit`, `delta_auc` for `auc`. That is the table `apply_bh` uses, so a pooled absence and a per-site absence cannot use different bands. Every tester effect is centred on 0 (bulk = AUC − 0.5), so the band is (−δ, δ) on every scale and strict, as in the testers. Only results that name a statistic and carry a finite effect with SE > 0 are pooled. A result explained as a site artefact keeps its numbers in the record but is not pooled. Effects on different scales are never pooled. Both exclusions are conservative: absence is never asserted from such results.
2. **Scope never moves a label.** `label_claim` drops every result that is not a non-proposer replication test. Scope results never enter the BH family, the coordinator input or step 4. `case.scope_rule(claim, scope_results)` receives only the claim's scope results. A case that needs a proposer-side figure (TB: proposer TB-vs-HC AUC) routes it as a scope test at the proposer through `scope_tests`.
3. **One BH family per run** = every non-proposer replication test performed, re-tests included. Superseded originals stay in the family (§2.4); proposer gates and scope tests are not in it. Artefact explanations are applied *after* BH, so an adjustment can never revive an explained result.
4. **Step 4 is one pass over the step-3 triggers** (spec §2.5 step 4). The triggers are `contradicted` results and `untestable` results with reason `genes` or `groups`. `population` and `view` are not triggers: a missing population is a routing matter (`holds`), and `untestable(view)` marks a site whose own step-1 call failed, which is already in `errors`. Only claims whose proposer gate is still `supported` are resolved. The coordinator names the faulty site among {proposer, triggering site}, and the protocol acts on its verdict:
   - *handoff loss*: one repair call to the named site with the coordinator's defect, then a re-test.
   - *analysis choice*: both involved sites get one follow-up to re-run under `case.default_spec`; each successful one is re-tested and that result is final. The named site goes first, so a budget that runs out mid-pair still serves the blamed site. Only the named site also receives the coordinator's diagnosis, so no site reads about another site's analysis. The re-run is a site-agent call because the analysis spec belongs to the agent-built view (`spec.json`), while the testers are fixed.
   - *site artefact*: no call. That site's result becomes `inconclusive` with reason `ARTEFACT_REASON`. At the proposer this sets the gate to `inconclusive`, so the claim becomes `unsupported`.
   - *site biology*: recorded only.

   Every follow-up counts against F and against the limit of one per (claim, site). The proposer's re-test replaces its gate. If that re-test fails, the claim's resolution stops.
5. **Step 1 calls `propose(sample)` on every site.** A tester-only site's call builds its view (§2.5 step 1). Claims returned by non-proposing sites are dropped.
6. **Failures (Review Focus 3).** A failed `propose` or `repair` lands in `errors` as `{"site", "op", "error"}`, whether the site recorded it in `failures` (Task 7) or raised. Only entries added during this run are copied, and the run continues; the site has already charged the call. A failed repair is listed in `followups` with `ok: False`, counts against F and is not re-tested. A site whose propose failed has no view; Task 7's `holds()` is then True and its tests answer `untestable(view)`, which shows in the record. `llm.BudgetExceeded` propagates, because a run cut short by the cap is not a scientific result (CLAUDE.md: never record an infrastructure failure as a score). Coordinator `LLMError`s propagate too.
7. **Cost.** `calls` and `nominal_usd` are what the run added to the sites' counters, so `Site` objects may be reused. `nominal_usd` also includes the coordinator's LLM cost. The coordinator reads its cost from the ledger under its own tag (`llm.tag` + `llm.spent`, as `store.memo` does), so `chat_json`'s re-asks after non-JSON replies are charged too.
8. **Findings order:** more supporting non-proposer sites, then |Stouffer z| with z = effect/se over those results, then `claim.rank`. `findings_step3()` ranks with `results_step3`, so the no-resolution arm is not ranked with step-4 evidence.

Review Focus pinned here: **1** (a broken view is recorded as `untestable(view)`, is not resolved and does not crash) by `test_view_and_population_untestables_are_not_resolved`. **3** by `test_failures_recorded_by_the_site_reach_the_run` (Task 7's non-raising failures), `test_failed_repair_is_charged_and_not_retested`, `test_failed_propose_does_not_stop_the_run` and `test_spending_cap_is_not_swallowed`.

```python
"""Protocol tests with fake sites and a fake coordinator: offline, no agents, no API."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from agentcoop import llm
from agentcoop.coop import protocol, tester
from agentcoop.coop.claims import Claim
from agentcoop.coop.protocol import label_claim

PARAMS = tester.TesterParams()

# (status, effect, se, reason) of a fake site's answer. Reasons follow Task 5: a computed result
# names its statistic ("module" | "logit" | "auc"), a blocked one names the obstacle.
SUP = ("supported", 0.8, 0.1, "module")
CON = ("contradicted", -0.8, 0.1, "module")
ABS = ("absent", 0.02, 0.05, "module")
INC = ("inconclusive", 0.3, 0.3, "module")
GENES = ("untestable", None, None, "genes")
GROUPS = ("untestable", None, None, "groups")
POP = ("untestable", None, None, "population")
VIEW = ("untestable", None, None, "view")


def claim(cid: str, site: str = "P", *, rank: int = 1, kind: str = "de") -> Claim:
    genes = ("ISG15", "IFI6", "MX1") if kind == "de" else ()
    return Claim(id=cid, site=site, kind=kind, population="CD14 Mono", target="treated",
                 reference="control", up_genes=genes, down_genes=(), effect=1.0, n_units=8,
                 rank=rank)


def result(site: str, answer: tuple, *, cid: str = "c1",
           test: str = "replication") -> tester.TestResult:
    status, effect, se, reason = answer
    ci = None if effect is None else (effect - 1.96 * se, effect + 1.96 * se)
    return tester.TestResult(claim_id=cid, site=site, test=test, status=status, effect=effect,
                             se=se, ci95=ci, n_per_group=(4, 4), mde=None,
                             p=0.001 if status == "supported" else 0.5, competitive_ok=None,
                             reason=reason)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# label_claim: the one shared decision rule
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@pytest.mark.parametrize("proposer, others, expected", [
    (ABS, [SUP, SUP], "unsupported"),
    (GENES, [SUP], "unsupported"),
    (SUP, [SUP, CON], "disputed"),
    (SUP, [SUP, SUP, ABS], "corroborated"),
    (SUP, [ABS, ABS], "site-specific"),
    (SUP, [ABS, INC], "site-specific"),
    (SUP, [GENES, POP], "unresolved"),
    (SUP, [VIEW], "unresolved"),
    (SUP, [], "unresolved"),
    (SUP, [INC], "inconclusive"),
    (SUP, [INC, GENES], "inconclusive"),
    # the band follows the statistic each result names: 0.30 ± 0.10 is outside the module band
    # (±0.25) but inside the logit band (±0.5)
    (SUP, [("inconclusive", 0.3, 0.05, "module")], "inconclusive"),
    (SUP, [("inconclusive", 0.3, 0.05, "logit")], "site-specific"),
    # bulk effects are AUC − 0.5, centred on 0 like the others (band ±0.05)
    (SUP, [("inconclusive", 0.02, 0.01, "auc")], "site-specific"),
    (SUP, [("inconclusive", 0.04, 0.01, "auc")], "inconclusive"),
    # effects on different scales are never pooled into an absence
    (SUP, [("absent", 0.0, 0.01, "module"), ("absent", 0.0, 0.01, "auc")], "inconclusive"),
    # a result explained as a site artefact keeps its numbers but is not pooled
    (SUP, [ABS, ("inconclusive", -0.8, 0.1, protocol.ARTEFACT_REASON)], "site-specific"),
])
def test_label_table(proposer, others, expected):
    got = label_claim(result("P", proposer), [result(f"S{i}", a) for i, a in enumerate(others)],
                      PARAMS)
    assert got == expected


def test_label_ignores_scope_tests():
    scope_only = [result("M", SUP, test="scope"), result("M2", CON, test="scope")]
    assert label_claim(result("P", SUP), scope_only, PARAMS) == "unresolved"
    mixed = [result("B", SUP), result("M", CON, test="scope")]
    assert label_claim(result("P", SUP), mixed, PARAMS) == "corroborated"
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the shared label rule**

Create `agentcoop/coop/protocol.py`. The import block is already the module's final one; steps 1–5 are appended in Step 7.

```python
"""Cross-examination protocol (spec §2.5): propose, route tests, label, resolve, re-label.

Objective-free by construction: a claim is kept by agreement between independently held
resources, never by comparing alternatives on a score, and no reference answer is read. Step 4
resolves a disagreement at most once per (claim, site), in one pass, with a re-test spec fixed
in advance and a fixed follow-up budget.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
import json
import math
import time
from collections.abc import Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from functools import partial
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any, Protocol

from agentcoop import llm
from agentcoop.coop.claims import Claim, claim_to_dict
# _BANDS (statistic named in TestResult.reason -> TesterParams field) is the table apply_bh uses;
# reading the same one keeps a pooled "absent" and a per-site "absent" on one band.
from agentcoop.coop.tester import _BANDS, TesterParams, TestResult, apply_bh

if TYPE_CHECKING:  # site.py implements SiteHandle on top of this module
    import pandas as pd

    from agentcoop.coop.site import CaseSpec

LABELS = ("unsupported", "disputed", "corroborated", "site-specific", "unresolved", "inconclusive")
CAUSES = ("analysis-choice", "handoff-loss", "site-artefact", "site-biology")
#: Untestable reasons that step 4 resolves (spec §2.5 step 4; Task 5's reason tokens). A missing
#: population is a routing matter (``holds``), and ``view`` marks a site whose own step-1 call
#: failed, not a claim-level handoff loss.
VIEW_CAUSES = ("genes", "groups")
ARTEFACT_REASON = "explained: site artefact"
_Z95 = 1.959963984540054


def _has_estimate(r: TestResult) -> bool:
    return (r.effect is not None and r.se is not None and math.isfinite(r.effect)
            and math.isfinite(r.se) and r.se > 0)


def _pooled_absent(results: list[TestResult], params: TesterParams) -> bool:
    """Is the inverse-variance fixed-effect 95% CI of the computed results inside the
    equivalence band? Every tester effect is centred on 0 with positive = the claimed direction
    (Task 5; bulk = AUC − 0.5), and the band is the one of the statistic the results name in
    ``reason``. Results that name no statistic (an explained artefact) are left out, and effects
    on different scales are never pooled, so absence is never asserted from them."""
    usable = [r for r in results if r.reason in _BANDS and _has_estimate(r)]
    scales = {r.reason for r in usable}
    if len(scales) != 1:
        return False
    delta = getattr(params, _BANDS[scales.pop()])
    weights = [1.0 / r.se ** 2 for r in usable]
    mean = sum(w * r.effect for w, r in zip(weights, usable)) / sum(weights)
    half = _Z95 / math.sqrt(sum(weights))
    return -delta < mean - half and mean + half < delta     # strict, as the testers' band


def label_claim(proposer: TestResult, others: list[TestResult], params: TesterParams) -> str:
    """The one shared decision rule (spec §2.5 step 3; ordered, first match wins).

    ``others`` are the non-proposer results; anything that is not a non-proposer replication test
    is dropped here, so scope evidence can never move a label. ``inconclusive`` and ``untestable``
    are never support nor contradiction.
    """
    if proposer.status != "supported":
        return "unsupported"
    others = [r for r in others if r.test == "replication" and r.site != proposer.site]
    statuses = {r.status for r in others}
    if "contradicted" in statuses:
        return "disputed"
    if "supported" in statuses:
        return "corroborated"
    if _pooled_absent(others, params):
        return "site-specific"
    if all(r.status == "untestable" for r in others):
        return "unresolved"
    return "inconclusive"
```

```python
class FakeSite:
    """SiteHandle double: canned claims and answers; every agent call costs 1 call and $0.01."""

    def __init__(self, name: str, *, claims: list[Claim] | None = None, answers: dict | None = None,
                 repaired: dict | None = None, scope: dict | None = None, holds: bool = True,
                 proposes: bool = True, propose_error: Exception | None = None,
                 repair_ok: bool = True, repair_error: Exception | None = None) -> None:
        self.name, self.proposes, self.calls, self.nominal_usd = name, proposes, 0, 0.0
        self.claims, self.answers = list(claims or []), dict(answers or {})
        self.repaired_answers, self.scope_answers = dict(repaired or {}), dict(scope or {})
        self._holds, self.propose_error = holds, propose_error
        self.repair_ok, self.repair_error = repair_ok, repair_error
        self.repairs: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
        self.tests: list[tuple[str, str]] = []

    def _charge(self) -> None:
        self.calls += 1
        self.nominal_usd += 0.01

    def propose(self, sample: int) -> list[Claim]:
        self._charge()
        if self.propose_error:
            raise self.propose_error
        return list(self.claims)

    def card(self) -> dict:
        return {"assay": f"{self.name}-assay"}

    def spec(self) -> dict:
        return {"unit": "donor", "site": self.name}

    def holds(self, population: str, contrast: tuple[str, str]) -> bool:
        return self._holds

    def test(self, claim: Claim, *, test: str = "replication",
             contrast: tuple[str, str] | None = None) -> tester.TestResult:
        self.tests.append((claim.id, test))
        if test == "scope":
            answer = self.scope_answers[claim.id]
        elif any(cid == claim.id for cid, _ in self.repairs) and claim.id in self.repaired_answers:
            answer = self.repaired_answers[claim.id]
        else:
            answer = self.answers.get(claim.id, POP)
        return result(self.name, answer, cid=claim.id, test=test)

    def repair(self, claim: Claim, defect: str, sample: int) -> bool:
        self._charge()
        self.repairs.append((claim.id, defect))
        if self.repair_error:
            raise self.repair_error
        return self.repair_ok

    def self_check(self, sample: int) -> bool:
        raise AssertionError("the protocol never runs an untargeted self-check")

    def export_stats(self, population: str, contrast: tuple[str, str]):
        raise AssertionError("the protocol never exports per-gene statistics")

    def ask(self, request: str, sample: int) -> str:
        raise AssertionError("the protocol never asks free-form questions")


class QuietSite(FakeSite):
    """Fails like Task 7's Site: the call is charged, appended to ``failures`` and answered with
    [] / False instead of an exception."""

    def __init__(self, name: str, *, fail: tuple[str, ...], **kw) -> None:
        super().__init__(name, **kw)
        self.fail = fail
        self.failures: list[dict] = []

    def propose(self, sample: int) -> list[Claim]:
        if "propose" not in self.fail:
            return super().propose(sample)
        self._charge()
        self.failures.append({"op": "propose", "error": "codex: timed out after 900 s"})
        return []

    def repair(self, claim: Claim, defect: str, sample: int) -> bool:
        if "repair" not in self.fail:
            return super().repair(claim, defect, sample)
        self._charge()
        self.repairs.append((claim.id, defect))
        self.failures.append({"op": "repair", "error": "finch: harness crashed"})
        return False


class FakeCoordinator:
    """Coordinator double: a fixed verdict per (claim id, triggering site); records each call."""

    def __init__(self, verdicts: dict | None = None) -> None:
        self.verdicts = dict(verdicts or {})
        self.seen: list[tuple[str, str, list[str], list[str]]] = []
        self.nominal_usd = 0.0

    def classify(self, claim, results, specs, cards, *, sample):
        trigger = next(s for s in specs if s != claim.site)
        self.seen.append((claim.id, trigger, sorted(specs), [r.test for r in results]))
        self.nominal_usd += 0.001
        cause, site = self.verdicts.get((claim.id, trigger), ("site-biology", trigger))
        return {"cause": cause, "site": site, "defect": f"{cause} at {site}"}


def case(**kw) -> SimpleNamespace:
    base = dict(name="fake", kind="scrna", question="What changes in treated donors?",
                default_spec={"unit": "donor", "normalization": "log-CPM"}, tester=PARAMS,
                max_claims=10, scope_tests=None, scope_rule=None)
    return SimpleNamespace(**{**base, **kw})


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def bh_families(monkeypatch):
    """Identity BH that records every family it is given (BH itself is tested in test_tester)."""
    families: list[list[tester.TestResult]] = []

    def identity(results, params):
        families.append(list(results))
        return list(results)

    monkeypatch.setattr(protocol, "apply_bh", identity)
    return families


def mimic_scope(index: int):
    """A scope table that sends every claim to ``sites[index]`` for a mimic-vs-control test."""
    def scope_tests(cl: Claim, sites: list) -> list:
        return [(sites[index], ("mimic", "control"))]
    return scope_tests


def by_id(run) -> dict:
    return {r.claim.id: r for r in run.records}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# run_protocol
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_artefact_explained_contradiction_becomes_corroborated():
    c = claim("c1")
    sites = [FakeSite("P", claims=[c], answers={"c1": SUP}), FakeSite("B", answers={"c1": SUP}),
             FakeSite("C", answers={"c1": SUP}), FakeSite("D", answers={"c1": CON})]
    coord = FakeCoordinator({("c1", "D"): ("site-artefact", "D")})
    run = run_protocol(sites, case(), sample=0, followup_budget=4, coordinator=coord)
    rec = run.records[0]
    assert (rec.label_step3, rec.label) == ("disputed", "corroborated")
    assert rec.cause == {"cause": "site-artefact", "site": "D", "defect": "site-artefact at D"}
    final_d = next(r for r in rec.results if r.site == "D")
    assert (final_d.status, final_d.reason) == ("inconclusive", ARTEFACT_REASON)
    assert next(r for r in rec.results_step3 if r.site == "D").status == "contradicted"
    assert run.followups == [] and sites[3].repairs == []
    assert coord.seen == [("c1", "D", ["D", "P"], ["replication"] * 4)]
    assert run.findings() == [c] and run.findings_step3() == []

@pytest.mark.parametrize("budget, repaired", [(3, ["D", "P"]), (1, ["D"])])
def test_analysis_choice_retests_both_sites_under_the_default_spec(budget, repaired):
    c = claim("c1")
    sites = [FakeSite("P", claims=[c], answers={"c1": SUP}), FakeSite("B", answers={"c1": SUP}),
             FakeSite("D", answers={"c1": CON}, repaired={"c1": SUP})]
    coord = FakeCoordinator({("c1", "D"): ("analysis-choice", "D")})
    case_ = case()
    run = run_protocol(sites, case_, sample=0, followup_budget=budget, coordinator=coord)
    assert [(f["site"], f["cause"], f["ok"]) for f in run.followups] == \
        [(s, "analysis-choice", True) for s in repaired]       # the named site first
    defects = {f["site"]: f["defect"] for f in run.followups}
    spec = json.dumps(case_.default_spec, sort_keys=True)
    assert all(spec in d for d in defects.values())
    assert "analysis-choice at D" in defects["D"]            # the diagnosis goes to D only
    assert all("analysis-choice at D" not in d for s, d in defects.items() if s != "D")
    assert sites[0].tests == [("c1", "replication")] * (1 + ("P" in repaired))
    assert sites[2].tests == [("c1", "replication")] * 2
    assert run.records[0].label == "corroborated"
    assert next(r for r in run.records[0].results if r.site == "D").status == "supported"

def test_scope_results_never_change_labels():
    def federation():
        claims = [claim("c1", rank=1), claim("c2", rank=2), claim("c3", rank=3)]
        return [FakeSite("P", claims=claims, answers={"c1": SUP, "c2": SUP, "c3": SUP}),
                FakeSite("B", answers={"c1": SUP, "c2": CON, "c3": ABS}),
                FakeSite("M", proposes=False, holds=False,
                         scope={"c1": CON, "c2": SUP, "c3": SUP})]

    def shared_if_supported(cl: Claim, results: list[tester.TestResult]) -> str:
        return "shared" if results[0].status == "supported" else "specific"

    plain = run_protocol(federation(), case(), sample=0, followup_budget=0,
                         coordinator=FakeCoordinator())
    scoped_coord = FakeCoordinator()
    scoped = run_protocol(federation(),
                          case(scope_tests=mimic_scope(2), scope_rule=shared_if_supported),
                          sample=0, followup_budget=0, coordinator=scoped_coord)
    assert [(r.label_step3, r.label) for r in scoped.records] == \
        [(r.label_step3, r.label) for r in plain.records]
    assert [r.scope for r in scoped.records] == ["specific", "shared", "shared"]
    assert [r.scope for r in plain.records] == [None, None, None]
    assert all("scope" not in tests for *_, tests in scoped_coord.seen)

              claim("one_weak_late", rank=3), claim("disputed", rank=0)]

             FakeSite("B", answers=answers_b), FakeSite("C", answers=answers_c)]

                                              "one_weak_late"]

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Coordinator: fixed prompt, validated output, every model call charged
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _classify(monkeypatch, reply) -> tuple[dict, list, Coordinator]:
    """Classify one disagreement with llm.chat_json faked. Each model attempt is ledgered as a
    $0.002 call (chat_json re-asks twice before giving up on non-JSON output)."""
    calls = []

    def fake_chat_json(messages, **kw):
        calls.append((messages, kw))
        for _ in range(3 if isinstance(reply, Exception) else 1):
            llm.record_replay(llm.backbone(), 0.002)
        if isinstance(reply, Exception):
            raise reply
        return reply, llm.ChatResult(content=json.dumps(reply), nominal_usd=0.002)

    monkeypatch.setattr(llm, "chat_json", fake_chat_json)
    results = [result("P", SUP), result("D", CON), result("B", SUP)]
    specs = {"P": {"unit": "donor"}, "D": {"unit": "cell"}}
    cards = {"P": {"assay": "10x v3"}, "D": {"assay": "Seq-Well"}}
    coordinator = Coordinator(case())
    verdict = coordinator.classify(claim("c1"), results, specs, cards, sample=4)
    return verdict, calls, coordinator


def test_coordinator_accepts_valid_verdict(monkeypatch):
    reply = {"cause": "analysis-choice", "site": "D", "defect": "cells used as the sample unit"}
    verdict, calls, coordinator = _classify(monkeypatch, reply)
    assert verdict == reply
    (messages, kw), = calls
    assert kw["sample"] == 4 and messages[0]["content"] == protocol.COORDINATOR_PROMPT
    payload = json.loads(messages[-1]["content"])
    assert set(payload["sites"]) == {"P", "D"}
    assert payload["sites"]["D"] == {"spec": {"unit": "cell"}, "card": {"assay": "Seq-Well"}}
    assert [r["site"] for r in payload["results"]] == ["P", "D", "B"]
    assert coordinator.nominal_usd == pytest.approx(0.002)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("reply, usd", [
    ({"cause": "bad luck", "site": "D", "defect": "x"}, 0.002),
    ({"cause": "handoff-loss", "site": "B", "defect": "B is not involved"}, 0.002),
    ({"cause": "handoff-loss", "site": "D"}, 0.002),
    (["handoff-loss", "D"], 0.002),
    (ValueError("model did not return JSON"), 0.006),
])
def test_coordinator_invalid_output_falls_back_and_is_charged(monkeypatch, reply, usd):
    verdict, _, coordinator = _classify(monkeypatch, reply)
    assert verdict == {"cause": "site-biology", "site": "D", "defect": "unclassified"}
    assert coordinator.nominal_usd == pytest.approx(usd)
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement records, the coordinator and `run_protocol`**

Append to the end of `agentcoop/coop/protocol.py` (two blank lines after `label_claim`):

```python
@dataclass
class ClaimRecord:
    claim: Claim
    results: list[TestResult]       # final: proposer gate, then replication tests, then scope tests
    label_step3: str
    label: str
    cause: dict | None              # first resolution of this claim: {"cause", "site", "defect"}
    scope: str | None
    results_step3: list[TestResult] = field(default_factory=list)   # same layout, before step 4


def _ranked(items: list[tuple[Claim, list[TestResult]]]) -> list[Claim]:
    """More supporting non-proposer sites first, then larger |Stouffer z| (z = effect / se over
    the supporting results; effects are centred on 0), then the proposer's own rank."""
    def key(item: tuple[Claim, list[TestResult]]) -> tuple[int, float, int]:
        claim, results = item
        support = [r for r in results if r.test == "replication" and r.site != claim.site
                   and r.status == "supported"]
        z = [r.effect / r.se for r in support if _has_estimate(r)]
        stouffer = abs(sum(z)) / math.sqrt(len(z)) if z else 0.0
        return -len(support), -stouffer, claim.rank

    return [claim for claim, _ in sorted(items, key=key)]


@dataclass
class ProtocolRun:
    records: list[ClaimRecord]
    followups: list[dict]           # {"claim_id", "site", "cause", "defect", "ok"}
    calls: int                      # site-agent calls (step 1 + follow-ups)
    nominal_usd: float
    errors: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)   # failed agent calls: {"site", "op", "error"}

    def findings(self) -> list[Claim]:
        return _ranked([(r.claim, r.results) for r in self.records if r.label == "corroborated"])

    def findings_step3(self) -> list[Claim]:
        return _ranked([(r.claim, r.results_step3) for r in self.records
                        if r.label_step3 == "corroborated"])


class SiteHandle(Protocol):
    """Everything the protocol and the arms may do with a site: summaries, never cell-level data."""

    name: str
    proposes: bool
    calls: int
    nominal_usd: float

    def propose(self, sample: int) -> list[Claim]: ...
    def card(self) -> dict: ...
    def spec(self) -> dict: ...
    def holds(self, population: str, contrast: tuple[str, str]) -> bool: ...
    def test(self, claim: Claim, *, test: str = "replication",
             contrast: tuple[str, str] | None = None) -> TestResult: ...
    def repair(self, claim: Claim, defect: str, sample: int) -> bool: ...
    def self_check(self, sample: int) -> bool: ...
    def export_stats(self, population: str, contrast: tuple[str, str]) -> pd.DataFrame: ...
    def ask(self, request: str, sample: int) -> str: ...


COORDINATOR_PROMPT = """\
You coordinate a federation of independent analysis sites. Each site holds private data, built \
its own machine-readable view of it (gene mapping, sample groups, cell populations; described by \
its analysis spec) and ran a fixed statistical test of one claim on that view. The sites disagree \
about the claim, or a site could not test it. Using only the claim, the test results, the sites' \
analysis specs and their metadata cards, classify the most likely cause:

- "analysis-choice": the sites made different analysis choices (sample unit, normalization, \
exclusions, covariates, contrast definition) that can explain the disagreement.
- "handoff-loss": a site's view lost or garbled information the test needs (gene identifiers not \
mapped to symbols, cell-type or condition codes mapped to the wrong vocabulary label, groups \
swapped or merged).
- "site-artefact": the compared groups at one site differ in something technical recorded in its \
metadata card (assay, chemistry, source lab, batch), so that site's result cannot be trusted.
- "site-biology": none of the above is evident; the site's population may genuinely differ.

Name the one site whose view or analysis is at fault ("site", one of the sites given) and describe \
the defect in one or two sentences ("defect"). The defect is shown to that site's agent so it can \
fix its own view: describe only that site's own view or spec, never another site's results or \
statistics. Answer with a JSON object {"cause": ..., "site": ..., "defect": ...}."""


class Coordinator:
    """Classifies why sites disagree about a claim (spec §2.5 step 4).

    It sees claims, summary results, analysis specs and metadata cards — never sample- or
    cell-level data — and it does not choose the re-test spec: an analysis-choice verdict is acted
    on with the case's pre-registered default spec. Output that fails validation becomes
    "site-biology / unclassified", which triggers no follow-up. ``nominal_usd`` is read from the
    ledger, so chat_json's re-asks after non-JSON replies are charged too.
    """

    def __init__(self, case: "CaseSpec") -> None:
        self.case = case
        self.nominal_usd = 0.0

    def classify(self, claim: Claim, results: list[TestResult], specs: dict[str, dict],
                 cards: dict[str, dict], *, sample: int) -> dict:
        involved = list(specs)
        fallback = {"cause": "site-biology", "defect": "unclassified",
                    "site": next((s for s in involved if s != claim.site), claim.site)}
        payload = {
            "question": self.case.question,
            "claim": claim_to_dict(claim),
            "results": [{k: v for k, v in dataclasses.asdict(r).items() if k != "claim_id"}
                        for r in results],
            "sites": {s: {"spec": specs[s], "card": cards.get(s, {})} for s in involved},
        }
        messages = [{"role": "system", "content": COORDINATOR_PROMPT},
                    {"role": "user", "content": json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, default=str)}]
        with llm.tag(f"coordinator#{claim.id}.{time.time_ns()}"):
            tag = llm.current_tag()
            try:
                verdict, _ = llm.chat_json(messages, sample=sample)
            except ValueError:      # no JSON object after chat_json's re-asks
                verdict = None
        self.nominal_usd += llm.spent(tag)["nominal_usd"]
        if (not isinstance(verdict, dict) or verdict.get("cause") not in CAUSES
                or verdict.get("site") not in involved
                or not isinstance(verdict.get("defect"), str)):
            return fallback
        return {"cause": verdict["cause"], "site": verdict["site"], "defect": verdict["defect"]}


@dataclass
class _Track:
    """Mutable state of one claim while a run is in progress."""

    claim: Claim
    gate: TestResult
    reps: dict[str, int]            # site -> index of its current test in the run's BH family
    scope: list[TestResult]
    explained: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)   # sites whose result is a site artefact

    def replication(self, adjusted: list[TestResult]) -> list[TestResult]:
        """Current replication results. Explanations are applied after BH, so an adjustment can
        never revive a result that was explained away."""
        return [dataclasses.replace(adjusted[i], status="inconclusive", reason=ARTEFACT_REASON)
                if site in self.explained else adjusted[i] for site, i in self.reps.items()]

    def explain(self, site: str) -> None:
        if site == self.claim.site:
            self.gate = dataclasses.replace(self.gate, status="inconclusive",
                                            reason=ARTEFACT_REASON)
        else:
            self.explained.add(site)

    def retest(self, site: SiteHandle, family: list[TestResult]) -> None:
        if site.name == self.claim.site:
            self.gate = site.test(self.claim)
        else:
            self.reps[site.name] = len(family)
            family.append(site.test(self.claim))


def _bh(family: list[TestResult], params: TesterParams) -> list[TestResult]:
    return apply_bh(family, params) if family else []


def _needs_resolution(result: TestResult) -> bool:
    return result.status == "contradicted" or (
        result.status == "untestable" and result.reason in VIEW_CAUSES)


def _followup_sites(verdict: dict, involved: list[str]) -> list[str]:
    """Who gets a follow-up (spec §2.5 step 4). Handoff loss: the site whose view lost
    information. Analysis choice: both involved sites, the one the coordinator named first, so a
    budget that runs out mid-pair still serves the site it blamed. Artefact, biology: nobody."""
    if verdict["cause"] == "handoff-loss":
        return [verdict["site"]]
    if verdict["cause"] == "analysis-choice":
        return [verdict["site"], *(s for s in involved if s != verdict["site"])]
    return []


def _defect_text(verdict: dict, site: str, case: "CaseSpec") -> str:
    """What a site agent is told. Analysis choice: re-run under the pre-registered default spec
    (never one the coordinator chose); the coordinator's diagnosis is added only for the site it
    describes, so no site reads about another site's analysis."""
    if verdict["cause"] == "handoff-loss":
        return verdict["defect"]
    spec = json.dumps(case.default_spec, sort_keys=True)
    rerun = ("Re-run the analysis of this claim under the pre-registered default spec and update "
             f"your view and spec.json to follow it: {spec}")
    return f"{verdict['defect']}\n{rerun}" if site == verdict["site"] else rerun


def _attempt(site: SiteHandle, op: str, call: Callable[[], Any], errors: list[dict]) -> Any:
    """One site-agent call, which the site has charged whatever happens. A failure is recorded in
    ``errors`` and the run goes on: Task 7's Site appends it to ``site.failures`` and returns
    [] / False, another SiteHandle may raise. The spending cap propagates, because a run cut
    short by it is not a scientific result."""
    seen = len(getattr(site, "failures", []))
    try:
        return call()
    except llm.BudgetExceeded:
        raise
    except Exception as exc:  # timeout or harness crash: one site must not stop the federation
        errors.append({"site": site.name, "op": op, "error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"})
        return None
    finally:
        errors.extend({"site": site.name, **f} for f in getattr(site, "failures", [])[seen:])


def run_protocol(sites: list[SiteHandle], case: "CaseSpec", *, sample: int, followup_budget: int,
                 coordinator: Coordinator | None = None) -> ProtocolRun:
    """Steps 1–5 of spec §2.5 over one federation, for draw ``sample``.

    ``calls`` and ``nominal_usd`` count what this run added to the sites' counters (plus the
    coordinator's LLM cost), so Site objects may be reused across runs.
    """
    coordinator = coordinator or Coordinator(case)
    params = case.tester
    by_name = {site.name: site for site in sites}
    calls0 = sum(site.calls for site in sites)
    usd0 = sum(site.nominal_usd for site in sites) + coordinator.nominal_usd
    errors: list[dict] = []

    # Step 1: one budgeted call per site; a tester-only site's call builds its view only.
    claims: list[Claim] = []
    for site in sites:
        proposed = _attempt(site, "propose", partial(site.propose, sample), errors)
        if site.proposes and proposed:
            claims.extend(proposed)

    # Step 2: proposer gate; replication at every other site holding the population and both
    # groups; scope tests from the case's scope table (they feed only the scope rule).
    family: list[TestResult] = []   # every non-proposer replication test, re-tests included
    tracks: list[_Track] = []
    for claim in claims:
        gate = by_name[claim.site].test(claim)
        reps: dict[str, int] = {}
        for site in sites:
            if site.name != claim.site and site.holds(claim.population, claim.contrast):
                reps[site.name] = len(family)
                family.append(site.test(claim))
        routed = case.scope_tests(claim, sites) if case.scope_tests else []
        scope = [site.test(claim, test="scope", contrast=contrast) for site, contrast in routed]
        tracks.append(_Track(claim, gate, reps, scope))

    # Step 3: the shared label function over BH-adjusted results.
    adjusted = _bh(family, params)
    step3 = [(t.gate, t.replication(adjusted)) for t in tracks]
    labels3 = [label_claim(gate, reps3, params) for gate, reps3 in step3]

    # Step 4: one pass over contradictions and view-caused untestables of claims whose proposer
    # gate passed; at most one follow-up per (claim, site), none once the budget is spent. The
    # coordinator sees the step-3 record.
    followups: list[dict] = []
    causes: list[dict | None] = [None] * len(tracks)
    followed: set[tuple[int, str]] = set()
    for i, (track, (gate3, reps3)) in enumerate(zip(tracks, step3)):
        for trigger in reps3:
            if track.gate.status != "supported":
                break               # unsupported, or the proposer's evidence fell after a repair
            if not _needs_resolution(trigger):
                continue
            involved = [track.claim.site, trigger.site]
            verdict = coordinator.classify(
                track.claim, [gate3, *reps3], {s: by_name[s].spec() for s in involved},
                {s: by_name[s].card() for s in involved}, sample=sample)
            causes[i] = causes[i] or verdict
            if verdict["cause"] == "site-artefact":
                track.explain(verdict["site"])
            for name in _followup_sites(verdict, involved):
                if track.gate.status != "supported":
                    break           # the proposer's own re-test failed: nothing left to resolve
                if (i, name) in followed or len(followups) >= followup_budget:
                    continue
                followed.add((i, name))
                site, defect = by_name[name], _defect_text(verdict, name, case)
                repair = partial(site.repair, track.claim, defect, sample)
                ok = bool(_attempt(site, "repair", repair, errors))
                followups.append({"claim_id": track.claim.id, "site": name,
                                  "cause": verdict["cause"], "defect": defect, "ok": ok})
                if ok:
                    track.retest(site, family)

    # Step 5: BH over the final family, the same label function, the case's scope rule.
    adjusted = _bh(family, params)
    records = []
    for i, (track, (gate3, reps3)) in enumerate(zip(tracks, step3)):
        reps = track.replication(adjusted)
        records.append(ClaimRecord(
            claim=track.claim, results=[track.gate, *reps, *track.scope],
            label_step3=labels3[i], label=label_claim(track.gate, reps, params), cause=causes[i],
            scope=case.scope_rule(track.claim, track.scope) if case.scope_rule else None,
            results_step3=[gate3, *reps3, *track.scope]))
    return ProtocolRun(
        records=records, followups=followups,
        calls=sum(site.calls for site in sites) - calls0,
        nominal_usd=sum(site.nominal_usd for site in sites) + coordinator.nominal_usd - usd0,
        errors=errors)
```

---

### Task 7: Real sites (`coop/site.py`)

**Files:**
- Create: `agentcoop/coop/site.py`
- Create: `tests/test_site.py`

**Interfaces:**

Consumes:
- `agentcoop.store` (Task 1): `memo(key, compute, *, tag) -> dict`, `put_blob(src_dir) -> str`, `get_blob(blob_id, dst_dir) -> None`. Tests monkeypatch `store.STORE`, so `memo`/`put_blob`/`get_blob` must read the module global at call time. `put_blob` archives the directory's *contents*, so `get_blob(id, ws / "view")` recreates the view at `ws/view`. `memo` runs `compute` under a tag nested in the caller's tag and stores `llm.spent(<that tag>)["nominal_usd"]` (+ any `nominal_usd_external` in the dict) as `nominal_usd`.
- `agentcoop.agents.workspace` (Task 2): `new_workspace(name, files) -> Path`, called with the nested name `f"{run_id}/{name}"` (parents created, a stale directory replaced, returns `WS_ROOT / name`). Tests monkeypatch `workspace.WS_ROOT`.
- `agentcoop.agents.harness` (Task 2): `run_agent(agent, ws, prompt, *, sample, timeout) -> AgentRun` and `AgentRun`. It is always called as `harness.run_agent` so tests can monkeypatch it. A timeout or harness failure is returned as `ok=False`, never raised. `run_agent` runs the agent on a worker thread that carries the caller's context, so the agent's model usage is ledgered under the caller's tag: Codex usage by `run_codex` through `llm.record_external(..., billed=False)`, Finch and ReAct calls through `llm`. `AgentRun.nominal_usd_external` therefore repeats an amount that is already in the ledger.
- `agentcoop.coop.claims` (Task 3): `Vocabulary`, `Claim`, `parse_claims(raw, *, site, vocab, max_claims) -> tuple[list[Claim], list[str]]` (claim ids become `<site>:<k>`; ids an agent writes are ignored).
- `agentcoop.coop.view` (Task 4): `SiteView`, `SiteTable`, `load_view(root, *, kind, vocab)`, `site_table(view, data_path, *, kind)`, `EXCLUDE` (`"exclude"`), `OTHER` (`"other"`). Tests also use `write_view`. The view-file formats in the prompts are the ones `load_view` reads: `genes.tsv` columns `native_id, symbol`; `samples.tsv` columns `sample, donor, group` with group in the vocabulary or `exclude`; `cells.tsv` columns `cell, sample, population` with population in the vocabulary or `other`. An scRNA data file is an h5ad (cells = `obs_names`, features = `var_names`). A bulk data file is a TSV matrix (first column = feature id, one column per sample, named by its header).
- `agentcoop.coop.tester` (Task 5): `TesterParams`, `TestResult`, `test_claim(table, claim, params, *, site, test, contrast)`, `export_gene_stats(table, population, contrast, params)`.
- `agentcoop.coop.protocol.SiteHandle` (Task 6): imported only under `TYPE_CHECKING`, because `protocol.py` may import `CaseSpec` from here.
- `agentcoop.llm`: `tag`, `current_tag`, `spent`. Tests also use `cost` and `record_external`.

Produces (exactly as in the contract):
```python
@dataclass
class CaseSpec:
    name: str; kind: str; vocab: Vocabulary; question: str; default_spec: dict; tester: TesterParams
    max_claims: int = 10
    resources: dict[str, Path] = field(default_factory=dict)
    scope_tests: Callable[[Claim, list[SiteHandle]], list[tuple[SiteHandle, tuple[str, str]]]] | None = None
    scope_rule: Callable[[Claim, list[TestResult]], str] | None = None
class Site:   # implements SiteHandle
    def __init__(self, name: str, data_path: Path, card: dict, agent: str, case: CaseSpec, *, task_id: str, run_id: str, proposes: bool = True, timeout: int = 900) -> None
def make_sites(site_specs: list[dict], case: CaseSpec, *, task_id: str, run_id: str, agents: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> list[Site]
def file_digest(path: Path) -> str
def serve_mailbox(ws: Path, handlers: dict[str, Callable[[dict], dict]], stop: threading.Event) -> threading.Thread
def propose_prompt(case: CaseSpec, card: dict, data_file: str, *, proposes: bool) -> str
def repair_prompt(case: CaseSpec, claim: Claim, defect: str) -> str
```

Also produced (used by Tasks 6, 9 and 10):
- `SiteFailed(RuntimeError)`: raised inside a memo compute when `AgentRun.ok` is False, so the failure is never cached. `Site` catches it. It carries no cost: a failure is charged from the ledger.
- Constants `SPEC_KEYS` (the keys of `spec.json`) and `STATS_COLUMNS` (`["gene", "effect", "se", "n1", "n2"]`).
- `Site` attributes beyond the contract: `case`, `timeout`, `view` (`SiteView | None`), `table` (`SiteTable | None`), `view_blob` (the store id of the view now in `ws/view`), `errors` (`"<op>: ..."` validation messages) and `failures` (`[{"op", "error"}]`).
- **No `Site` method raises on an agent failure.** `propose` returns `[]`, `repair` and `self_check` return `False`, and `ask` returns `"(site X did not answer: its agent call failed)"`. Only `llm.BudgetExceeded`, which `run_agent` re-raises, propagates.
- **The ledger is the only source of cost.** Every call (store hit, run or failure) adds 1 to `calls` and its nominal USD to `nominal_usd`. A run or hit adds the memo's `nominal_usd`. A failed call adds `llm.spent(<its tag>/)["nominal_usd"]`, the same amount `memo` would have stored. A stored call keeps the run's `AgentRun.nominal_usd_external` only as `agent_nominal_usd`, for inspection. It is never returned as `nominal_usd_external`, which `memo` would add on top of the ledger, counting Codex twice. Task 9's own `run_agent` callers follow the same rule.
- **`holds()` returns True for a site without a usable view.** The test is then routed to that site and comes back `untestable(view)` (`status="untestable"`, `reason="view"`), so the broken view shows in the record. For bulk tables the population is ignored: their units are whole samples.
- **Mailbox handlers run on the mailbox thread, outside the caller's ledger tag.** A caller (Task 9's Codex coordinator) charges the site calls made through the mailbox from the sites' `calls` / `nominal_usd` counters. Wire protocol: request `ws/req_<n>.json` = `{"op": <handler name>, ...}`, response `ws/resp_<n>.json` written atomically, request file removed. An unknown op or a raising handler is answered `{"error": ...}`.
- Store keys:
  - `propose`: the contract key.
  - Follow-ups: the site identity `{"v", "case", "task", "site", "agent", "data"}` plus `{"op", "after": <propose key>, "view": view_blob, "prompt": sha256(prompt), "sample"}`. `repair` also adds `{"claim": claim.id, "defect": sha256(defect)}`. The prompt hash covers the claim's content, and the view id makes a follow-up relative to the view it started from.
- Prompt markers (the fake agent in the tests dispatches on them):
  - repair: `"Defect:"`
  - self-check: `"Checklist"`
  - ask: `"answer.md"`
  - claim schema, proposing sites only: `"up_genes"`

Review Focus items pinned here:
- **1** (malformed view gives `untestable(view)`, no crash): `test_malformed_view_makes_tests_untestable_instead_of_crashing`.
- **3** (a failed call is charged and counted but never cached): `test_failed_call_is_charged_and_recorded_but_never_cached`, plus the failed repair in `test_repair_changes_the_view_and_the_next_test_sees_it`. The fake agent ledgers its usage *and* reports it as `nominal_usd_external`, as `run_codex` does. So the exact-cost assertions in these tests and in `test_propose_builds_the_site_table_and_keeps_valid_claims` also pin that a call is charged once, not twice.
- **4** (one step-1 value per key, including the view): `test_same_request_from_another_run_replays_claims_and_view` and `test_concurrent_requests_for_one_proposal_converge_on_one_value`.

Two tests pin the prompt's file formats to Task 4: `test_prompt_view_format_is_what_load_view_reads` (scRNA) and `test_bulk_prompt_describes_the_matrix_that_site_table_reads` (bulk: hand-written files go through `load_view` and `site_table`). If either fails, change `_view_format` / `SPEC_KEYS` to what `view.py` reads, and keep the test's intent.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests for prompts, view formats, file digest and mailbox**

Create `tests/test_site.py`:

```python
"""Real sites (coop/site.py) with a fake agent: offline, no API key, no agent process."""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
import hashlib
import json
import os
import threading
import time
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from agentcoop.coop import site as site_mod
from agentcoop.coop import tester
from agentcoop.coop.claims import Claim, Vocabulary
from agentcoop.coop.site import CaseSpec, file_digest, propose_prompt, repair_prompt, serve_mailbox
from agentcoop.coop.view import load_view, site_table

VOCAB = Vocabulary(populations=("B", "NK"), groups=("control", "treated"),
                   contrasts=(("treated", "control"),))
CARD = {"codebook": {"cond": {"ctl": "control", "trt": "treated"}, "ct": {"b": "B", "nk": "NK"}},
        "assay": "10x 3' v3", "donors_per_group": {"control": 4, "treated": 4}}
PLANTED = ("SYM0", "SYM1", "SYM2", "SYM3", "SYM4", "SYM5")
CLAIM = Claim(id="Z:1", site="Z", kind="de", population="B", target="treated",
              reference="control", up_genes=PLANTED, down_genes=(), effect=1.6, n_units=8, rank=1)
DEFECT = "genes.tsv keeps Ensembl ids as symbols; map them with symbols.tsv"


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def _clean_agentcoop_env(monkeypatch):
    """Tests must not depend on AGENTCOOP_* settings exported in the developer's shell."""
    for k in [k for k in os.environ if k.startswith("AGENTCOOP_")]:
        monkeypatch.delenv(k)


def toy_case(**kw) -> CaseSpec:
    """A two-population case; ``kw`` sets further fields (e.g. resources)."""
    return CaseSpec(name="toy", kind="scrna", vocab=VOCAB,
                    question="Which programs change in treated donors?",
                    default_spec={"sample_unit": "donor"},
                    tester=tester.TesterParams(null_sets=20, n_boot=50), **kw)


def test_prompts_state_question_boundary_vocabulary_formats_and_cap():
    case = toy_case(resources={"symbols.tsv": Path("symbols.tsv")})
    p = propose_prompt(case, CARD, "site_A.h5ad", proposes=True)
    for s in (case.question, "site_A.h5ad", "codebook.json", "symbols.tsv", CARD["assay"],
              "Only the files in this directory", "cannot leave the site", "treated vs control",
              "view/genes.tsv", "view/samples.tsv", "view/cells.tsv", "view/spec.json",
              "claims.json", "at most 10 claims", '"up_genes"', '"rank"', "composition",
              *VOCAB.populations, *VOCAB.groups):
        assert s in p, s
    view_only = propose_prompt(case, CARD, "site_A.h5ad", proposes=False)
    assert "builds its view only" in view_only and "up_genes" not in view_only
    bulk = propose_prompt(dataclasses.replace(case, kind="bulk"), CARD, "x.tsv",
                          proposes=True)
    assert "cells.tsv" not in bulk and "composition" not in bulk
    r = repair_prompt(case, CLAIM, DEFECT)
    for s in (DEFECT, "SYM0", "in B, treated vs control", "Change only files under view/",
              "view/genes.tsv"):
        assert s in r, s
    assert "claims.json" not in r


def test_prompt_view_format_is_what_load_view_reads(tmp_path):
    """Files written by following the prompt's format must load: pins the prompt to view.py."""
    p = propose_prompt(toy_case(), CARD, "site_A.h5ad", proposes=True)
    for s in ("columns native_id, symbol", "columns sample, donor, group",
              "columns cell, sample, population", "or exclude for a sample",
              "or other for a cell", ", ".join(site_mod.SPEC_KEYS)):
        assert s in p, s
    root = tmp_path / "handmade"
    root.mkdir()
    (root / "genes.tsv").write_text("native_id\tsymbol\nENSG1\tSYM0\nENSG2\tSYM1\n")
    (root / "samples.tsv").write_text("sample\tdonor\tgroup\tassay\nS1\tD1\tcontrol\tv3\n"
                                      "S2\tD2\ttreated\tv3\nS3\tD3\texclude\tv2\n")
    (root / "cells.tsv").write_text("cell\tsample\tpopulation\nc1\tS1\tB\nc2\tS2\tNK\n"
                                    "c3\tS3\tother\n")
    (root / "spec.json").write_text(json.dumps({k: "x" for k in site_mod.SPEC_KEYS}))
    view, errors = load_view(root, kind="scrna", vocab=VOCAB)
    assert view is not None, errors
    assert view.genes == {"ENSG1": "SYM0", "ENSG2": "SYM1"}
    assert list(view.cells["population"]) == ["B", "NK", "other"]


def test_bulk_prompt_describes_the_matrix_that_site_table_reads(tmp_path):
    """A bulk site's data file is a TSV matrix (first column = feature id, one column per
    sample): a view written by the bulk prompt's rules must load and give a site table."""
    p = propose_prompt(dataclasses.replace(toy_case(), kind="bulk"), CARD, "expr.tsv",
                       proposes=True)
    for s in ("tab-separated expression matrix", "native_id = the matrix's first-column id",
              "sample = a column header of the matrix"):
        assert s in p, s
    assert "h5ad" not in p and "obs_names" not in p
    data = tmp_path / "expr.tsv"
    data.write_text("probe\tS1\tS2\tS3\tS4\tS5\tS6\n"
                    "P1\t1\t2\t3\t7\t8\t9\nP2\t5\t4\t6\t5\t4\t6\nP3\t1\t1\t1\t1\t1\t1\n")
    root = tmp_path / "view"
    root.mkdir()
    (root / "genes.tsv").write_text("native_id\tsymbol\nP1\tSTAT1\nP2\tGBP5\n")
    (root / "samples.tsv").write_text("sample\tdonor\tgroup\n" + "".join(
        f"S{i}\tD{i}\t{'control' if i <= 3 else 'treated'}\n" for i in range(1, 7)))
    (root / "spec.json").write_text(json.dumps({k: "x" for k in site_mod.SPEC_KEYS}))
    view, errors = load_view(root, kind="bulk", vocab=VOCAB)
    assert view is not None, errors
    table = site_table(view, data, kind="bulk")
    assert table.genes == ["GBP5", "STAT1"]
    assert list(table.units["unit"]) == [f"S{i}" for i in range(1, 7)]
    assert list(table.units["group"]) == ["control"] * 3 + ["treated"] * 3


def test_file_digest_follows_the_content(tmp_path):
    f = tmp_path / "x.bin"
    f.write_bytes(b"abc")
    assert file_digest(f) == hashlib.sha256(b"abc").hexdigest()
    f.write_bytes(b"abcd")
    assert file_digest(f) == hashlib.sha256(b"abcd").hexdigest()


def wait_for(path: Path, seconds: float = 5.0) -> Path:
    deadline = time.monotonic() + seconds
    while not path.exists():
        assert time.monotonic() < deadline, f"no {path.name}"
        time.sleep(0.05)
    return path


def test_mailbox_answers_each_request_once_until_stopped(tmp_path):
    def boom(req: dict) -> dict:
        raise ValueError("bad request")

    stop = threading.Event()
    thread = serve_mailbox(tmp_path, {"echo": lambda req: {"echo": req["text"]}, "boom": boom},
                           stop)
    (tmp_path / "req_1.json.tmp").write_text(json.dumps({"op": "echo", "text": "hi"}))
    os.replace(tmp_path / "req_1.json.tmp", tmp_path / "req_1.json")
    (tmp_path / "req_2.json").write_text(json.dumps({"op": "nope"}))
    (tmp_path / "req_3.json").write_text(json.dumps({"op": "boom"}))
    assert json.loads(wait_for(tmp_path / "resp_1.json").read_text()) == {"echo": "hi"}
    assert "error" in json.loads(wait_for(tmp_path / "resp_2.json").read_text())
    assert "bad request" in json.loads(wait_for(tmp_path / "resp_3.json").read_text())["error"]
    assert not list(tmp_path.glob("req_*.json"))    # answered requests are removed
    stop.set()
    thread.join(timeout=2)
    assert thread.daemon and not thread.is_alive()
```
