- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the case spec, prompts, file digest and mailbox**

Create `agentcoop/coop/site.py`:

```python
"""Real sites: one external agent bound to one site's private data, behind ``SiteHandle``.

A ``Site`` owns a private workspace holding its data file, the case resources and its codebook.
Its agent builds the site view there and proposes claims; deterministic testers answer for the
site from the site table computed from that view, so only summary statistics leave the site.

Every agent call goes through ``store.memo``. A step-1 proposal is shared by every arm and run
that asks for the same (task, site, agent, sample), and its view is restored from the stored
blob, so arms compare identical claims on identical views. Every call is counted and charged at
nominal cost, cache hits included. The ledger is the only source of that cost: the harness
ledgers an agent's model usage (Codex through ``llm.record_external``) under the caller's tag,
so a call costs what its tag spent. A failed call is charged and recorded but never cached; the
site is then left without a view and its tests come back ``untestable(view)``.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import threading
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Callable

from agentcoop.coop.claims import Claim, Vocabulary
from agentcoop.coop.tester import TesterParams, TestResult
from agentcoop.coop.view import EXCLUDE, OTHER

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from agentcoop.coop.protocol import SiteHandle

SPEC_KEYS = ("contrasts", "sample_unit", "normalization", "gene_mapping", "covariates",
             "exclusions")
POLL_S = 0.2            # mailbox polling interval
BAD_POLLS = 10          # polls a request may stay unparsable (still being written) before an error


@dataclass
class CaseSpec:
    name: str
    kind: str                                   # "scrna" | "bulk"
    vocab: Vocabulary
    question: str
    default_spec: dict                          # pre-registered analysis conventions (§2.5 step 4)
    tester: TesterParams
    max_claims: int = 10
    resources: dict[str, Path] = field(default_factory=dict)   # copied into every workspace
    scope_tests: Callable[[Claim, list[SiteHandle]],
                          list[tuple[SiteHandle, tuple[str, str]]]] | None = None
    scope_rule: Callable[[Claim, list[TestResult]], str] | None = None


_DIGESTS: dict[tuple[str, int, int], str] = {}


def file_digest(path: Path) -> str:
    """sha256 of a file, cached by (path, size, mtime): site files are large and their digest
    enters every proposal key."""
    path = Path(path).resolve()
    st = path.stat()
    k = (str(path), st.st_size, st.st_mtime_ns)
    if k not in _DIGESTS:
        with open(path, "rb") as f:
            _DIGESTS[k] = hashlib.file_digest(f, "sha256").hexdigest()
    return _DIGESTS[k]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Prompts. Each agent call is a fresh run, so every prompt restates the setting.


def _setting(case: CaseSpec) -> str:
    """What every call of a site agent is told: the question, the boundary, the vocabulary."""
    resources = "".join(f", {n}" for n in sorted(case.resources))
    contrasts = "; ".join(f"{t} vs {r}" for t, r in case.vocab.contrasts)
    return (
        f"Research question: {case.question}\n\n"
        "You are the analysis agent of one site in a multi-site study. Only the files in this "
        "directory exist for you: your site's data file, codebook.json (your site's metadata "
        f"card: what its columns and codes mean){resources}. There is no network and no other "
        "site. Your site's data cannot leave the site: the study reads only the files described "
        "below, and only summary statistics computed from them leave the site. Python "
        "(`python`) with scanpy, anndata, numpy, pandas, scipy and statsmodels is available.\n\n"
        "Case vocabulary (use these exact strings):\n"
        f"- populations: {', '.join(case.vocab.populations)}\n"
        f"- groups: {', '.join(case.vocab.groups)}\n"
        f"- contrasts (target vs reference): {contrasts}\n"
    )


def _view_format(kind: str) -> str:
    """The site-view files exactly as ``agentcoop.coop.view.load_view`` reads them, with ids
    that refer to the data file the way ``site_table`` reads it (h5ad for scRNA, a TSV matrix
    for bulk)."""
    if kind == "bulk":
        data = ("Your data file is a tab-separated expression matrix: its first column holds the "
                "feature ids and every other column is one sample, named by its column header.")
        feature, sample = "the matrix's first-column id", "a column header of the matrix"
    else:
        data = ("Your data file is an AnnData .h5ad file: var_names are its feature ids, "
                "obs_names its cell ids and obs its cell metadata.")
        feature = "the data file's feature id (a var_names entry)"
        sample = "the sample id as the data file's obs records it"
    files = [
        f"view/genes.tsv: columns native_id, symbol. One row per feature you could map: "
        f"native_id = {feature}, symbol = its HGNC gene symbol. Leave out features you cannot "
        "map.",
        "view/samples.tsv: columns sample, donor, group, then optional covariate columns (e.g. "
        f"assay, sex, age). One row per sample: sample = {sample}, donor = its donor id, group = "
        "a case group (translate the site's own codes with codebook.json) or "
        f"{EXCLUDE} for a sample outside every case group. One sample per donor: contrasts are "
        "between donors.",
    ]
    if kind == "scrna":
        files.append(
            "view/cells.tsv: columns cell, sample, population. One row per cell you keep (leave "
            "out cells you remove, e.g. in quality control): cell = its obs_names entry, sample = "
            f"a sample of view/samples.tsv, population = a case population or {OTHER} for a cell "
            "of another type.")
    files.append(
        f"view/spec.json: a JSON object with keys {', '.join(SPEC_KEYS)}: how you defined the "
        "contrasts, the sample unit, the normalization, the source of the gene mapping, the "
        "covariates and every exclusion.")
    return (data + "\nWrite these tab-separated files with a header row into the directory "
            "view/:\n" + "\n".join(f"- {f}" for f in files))


def _claims_format(case: CaseSpec) -> str:
    target, reference = case.vocab.contrasts[0]
    example = [{"kind": "de", "population": case.vocab.populations[0],
                "contrast": [target, reference], "up_genes": ["GENE_A", "GENE_B", "GENE_C"],
                "down_genes": ["GENE_D"], "effect": 1.2, "n_units": 8, "rank": 1,
                "rationale": "one sentence on the evidence"}]
    kinds = '"de" (a gene program changes in the population)'
    if case.kind == "scrna":
        kinds += (' or "composition" (the population\'s share of cells changes; no genes; '
                  "effect = log odds ratio of its cell fraction, positive = higher in the "
                  "target group)")
    return (
        f"claims.json: a JSON list of at most {case.max_claims} claims (any beyond that are "
        "dropped), each an object with\n"
        f"- kind: {kinds}\n"
        "- population: a case population; contrast: [target group, reference group], a case "
        "contrast\n"
        "- up_genes, down_genes: HGNC symbols higher / lower in the target group; a \"de\" "
        "claim names at least 3 genes in total\n"
        '- effect: your effect estimate (for "de": log2 fold change of the program); n_units: '
        "the number of donors behind the claim\n"
        "- rank: 1 for your most confident claim, then 2, 3, ...; rationale: one sentence\n"
        f"Example (placeholder genes):\n{json.dumps(example, indent=1)}"
    )


def propose_prompt(case: CaseSpec, card: dict, data_file: str, *, proposes: bool) -> str:
    """Step 1 (§2.5): build the site view and, at a proposing site, propose up to K claims."""
    task = ("Then propose claims that answer the research question from your site's data:\n"
            + _claims_format(case)) if proposes else (
        "This site builds its view only: it proposes no claims (do not write claims.json). "
        "Claims from other sites are tested on your view.")
    return (
        _setting(case)
        + f"\nYour site's data file: {data_file}. Your site's metadata card (also in "
        f"codebook.json):\n{json.dumps(card, indent=1)}\n\n"
        "First build your site view. Deterministic code at your site reads it to test claims "
        "from every site, so it must describe your data exactly.\n"
        + _view_format(case.kind) + "\n\n" + task
        + "\n\nWrite every file into this directory; nothing you print is read."
    )


def repair_prompt(case: CaseSpec, claim: Claim, defect: str) -> str:
    """Step 4 handoff-loss follow-up: the claim and the defect, never other sites' results."""
    genes = (f"up {', '.join(claim.up_genes) or '-'}; "
             f"down {', '.join(claim.down_genes) or '-'}")
    return (
        _setting(case)
        + f"\nA claim could not be tested correctly on your site view.\nClaim: {claim.kind} in "
        f"{claim.population}, {claim.target} vs {claim.reference}; genes {genes}.\n"
        f"Defect: {defect}\n\n"
        "Fix your site view so that it describes your data correctly. Change only files under "
        "view/ and keep their formats.\n" + _view_format(case.kind)
    )


def _dispatch(msg: object, handlers: dict[str, Callable[[dict], dict]]) -> dict:
    if not isinstance(msg, dict) or msg.get("op") not in handlers:
        return {"error": f"expected a JSON object with 'op' in {sorted(handlers)}"}
    try:
        return handlers[msg["op"]](msg)
    except Exception as e:   # a bad request is answered with an error, not a dead mailbox
        return {"error": f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"}


def serve_mailbox(ws: Path, handlers: dict[str, Callable[[dict], dict]],
                  stop: threading.Event) -> threading.Thread:
    """Answer ``req_<n>.json`` files in ``ws`` with ``resp_<n>.json`` until ``stop`` is set.

    The Codex coordinator runs sandboxed in its own workspace and reaches the sites only by
    writing requests ``{"op": <handler name>, ...}`` there. A response is written atomically and
    its request removed, so each request is answered once. Handlers run on the mailbox thread,
    outside the caller's ledger tag: a caller charges the site calls made through the mailbox
    from the sites' ``calls`` / ``nominal_usd`` counters.
    """
    def loop() -> None:
        unparsable: dict[str, int] = {}
        while not stop.is_set():
            for req in sorted(ws.glob("req_*.json")):
                n = req.name[len("req_"):-len(".json")]
                try:
                    msg = json.loads(req.read_text())
                except json.JSONDecodeError:
                    unparsable[n] = unparsable.get(n, 0) + 1
                    if unparsable[n] < BAD_POLLS:
                        continue   # probably still being written
                    msg = None
                tmp = ws / f".resp_{n}.json.tmp"
                tmp.write_text(json.dumps(_dispatch(msg, handlers), default=str))
                os.replace(tmp, ws / f"resp_{n}.json")
                req.unlink()
            stop.wait(POLL_S)

    thread = threading.Thread(target=loop, name=f"mailbox:{ws}", daemon=True)
    thread.start()
    return thread
```

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
import hashlib
import json

import time
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace

import anndata as ad
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import pytest
import scipy.sparse as sp

from agentcoop import llm, store
from agentcoop.agents import harness, workspace
from agentcoop.agents.harness import AgentRun
from agentcoop.coop import site as site_mod
```

Then append to the end of the file:

```python
RUN_TOKENS, FAIL_TOKENS = 500_000, 1_000_000    # output tokens one fake run / failed run uses
RUN_USD = llm.cost("gpt-6-luna", 0, 0, RUN_TOKENS)
FAIL_USD = llm.cost("gpt-6-luna", 0, 0, FAIL_TOKENS)


def make_h5ad(path: Path) -> None:
    """8 donors (4 ctl, 4 trt) x 2 cell types x 30 cells x 60 genes under native ids and codes;
    the first 6 genes are 3x higher in treated B cells."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    base = rng.uniform(5, 15, size=60)
    blocks, rows = [], []
    for d in range(8):
        cond = "trt" if d >= 4 else "ctl"
        for ct in ("b", "nk"):
            mu = base.copy()
            if cond == "trt" and ct == "b":
                mu[:6] *= 3
            blocks.append(rng.poisson(mu, size=(30, 60)))
            rows += [{"donor": f"D{d}", "cond": cond, "ct": ct}] * 30
    obs = pd.DataFrame(rows, index=[f"cell{i}" for i in range(len(rows))])
    var = pd.DataFrame(index=[f"ENSG{i:011d}" for i in range(60)])
    ad.AnnData(X=sp.csr_matrix(np.vstack(blocks).astype(np.float32)), obs=obs,
               var=var).write_h5ad(path)


def write_fake_view(ws: Path, *, map_genes: bool, drop_group: bool, draw: int) -> None:
    """The view a site agent builds from its data file and codebook."""
    adata = ad.read_h5ad(ws / "site_A.h5ad")
    book = json.loads((ws / "codebook.json").read_text())["codebook"]
    obs = adata.obs.astype(str)
    donors = obs.drop_duplicates("donor")
    samples = pd.DataFrame({"sample": donors["donor"].to_numpy(),
                            "donor": donors["donor"].to_numpy(),
                            "group": donors["cond"].map(book["cond"]).to_numpy()})
    cells = pd.DataFrame({"cell": adata.obs_names.to_numpy(), "sample": obs["donor"].to_numpy(),
                          "population": obs["ct"].map(book["ct"]).to_numpy()})
    genes = {f: (f"SYM{i}" if map_genes else f) for i, f in enumerate(adata.var_names)}
    (ws / "view").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    write_view(ws / "view", genes=genes, samples=samples, cells=cells,
               spec={"contrasts": [["treated", "control"]], "sample_unit": "donor",
                     "normalization": "log2 CPM", "gene_mapping": "symbols.tsv" if map_genes
                     else "none", "covariates": [], "exclusions": [], "draw": draw})
    if drop_group:   # a malformed view: samples.tsv without its group column
        path = ws / "view" / "samples.tsv"
        pd.read_csv(path, sep="\t").drop(columns="group").to_csv(path, sep="\t", index=False)


class FakeAgent:
    """Stands in for ``harness.run_agent``: does what the prompt asks, in the workspace.

    Like ``run_codex`` it ledgers its usage under the caller's tag *and* reports the same amount
    as ``nominal_usd_external``, so a site that charged both would show twice the cost.
    ``fail``: the run times out after writing half a view. ``map_genes``: whether step 1 maps
    native ids to symbols (repair and self-check always do). ``barrier``: wait for a second
    concurrent call before writing, so two runs race on one store key.
    """

    def __init__(self, *, fail: bool = False, map_genes: bool = True, drop_group: bool = False,
                 barrier: threading.Barrier | None = None) -> None:
        self.fail, self.map_genes, self.drop_group = fail, map_genes, drop_group
        self.barrier = barrier
        self.prompts: list[str] = []
        self.lock = threading.Lock()

    def __call__(self, agent: str, ws: Path, prompt: str, *, sample: int, timeout: int) -> AgentRun:
        with self.lock:
            self.prompts.append(prompt)
            n = len(self.prompts)
        if self.barrier is not None:
            try:
                self.barrier.wait(timeout=1)
            except threading.BrokenBarrierError:   # the store serialized the calls
                pass
        if self.fail:
            (ws / "view").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
            (ws / "view" / "genes.tsv").write_text("native_id\tsym")
            usd = llm.record_external("gpt-6-luna", 0, 0, 0, FAIL_TOKENS, billed=False)
            return AgentRun(agent, False, [], "timeout", usd, float(timeout))
        if "answer.md" in prompt:
            (ws / "answer.md").write_text(f"site answer {n}")
            (ws / "view" / "spec.json").write_text("{}")   # a careless agent touches its view
        elif "Defect:" in prompt or "Checklist" in prompt:
            write_fake_view(ws, map_genes=True, drop_group=False, draw=n)
        else:
            write_fake_view(ws, map_genes=self.map_genes, drop_group=self.drop_group, draw=n)
            if "up_genes" in prompt:   # a proposing site's prompt carries the claim schema
                (ws / "claims.json").write_text(json.dumps([
                    {"kind": "de", "population": "B", "contrast": ["treated", "control"],
                     "up_genes": list(PLANTED), "down_genes": [], "effect": 1.6, "n_units": 8,
                     "rank": 1, "rationale": f"draw {n}"},
                    {"kind": "de", "population": "Platelet", "contrast": ["treated", "control"],
                     "up_genes": ["SYM6", "SYM7", "SYM8"], "down_genes": [], "effect": 1.0,
                     "n_units": 8, "rank": 2, "rationale": "not a case population"}]))
        usd = llm.record_external("gpt-6-luna", 0, 0, 0, RUN_TOKENS, billed=False)
        return AgentRun(agent, True, [{"code": "...", "output": "..."}], None, usd, 1.0)


def install(monkeypatch, **kw) -> FakeAgent:
    fake = FakeAgent(**kw)
    monkeypatch.setattr(harness, "run_agent", fake)
    return fake


@pytest.fixture
def env(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """Store, workspaces and ledger under tmp_path; one site file and a toy case."""
    monkeypatch.setattr(store, "STORE", tmp_path / "store")
    monkeypatch.setattr(workspace, "WS_ROOT", tmp_path / "ws")
    monkeypatch.setenv("AGENTCOOP_LLM_DB", str(tmp_path / "llm.sqlite"))
    (tmp_path / "data").mkdir()
    data = tmp_path / "data" / "site_A.h5ad"
    make_h5ad(data)
    symbols = tmp_path / "data" / "symbols.tsv"
    symbols.write_text("".join(f"ENSG{i:011d}\tSYM{i}\n" for i in range(60)))
    return SimpleNamespace(tmp=tmp_path, data=data,
                           case=toy_case(resources={"symbols.tsv": symbols}))


def new_site(env, run_id: str = "r1") -> Site:
    return Site("A", env.data, CARD, "codex", env.case, task_id="t1", run_id=run_id)


def view_files(site: Site) -> dict[str, str]:
    return {f: (site.ws / "view" / f).read_text()
            for f in ("genes.tsv", "samples.tsv", "cells.tsv", "spec.json")}


def test_propose_builds_the_site_table_and_keeps_valid_claims(env, monkeypatch):
    fake = install(monkeypatch)
    site = new_site(env)
    claims = site.propose(0)
    assert [c.population for c in claims] == ["B"]   # the off-vocabulary claim is dropped ...
    assert any(e.startswith("claims:") for e in site.errors)   # ... and its error kept
    assert claims[0].site == "A" and claims[0].contrast == ("treated", "control")
    assert claims[0].up_genes == PLANTED
    assert site.table is not None and "SYM0" in site.table.genes
    assert site.holds("B", ("treated", "control")) and not site.holds("B", ("treated", "mimic"))
    assert site.test(claims[0]).status != "untestable"
    assert site.spec()["sample_unit"] == "donor" and site.card() == CARD
    assert site.ws == env.tmp / "ws" / "r1" / "A"
    assert (site.ws / "site_A.h5ad").exists() and (site.ws / "symbols.tsv").exists()
    assert json.loads((site.ws / "codebook.json").read_text()) == CARD
    assert "site_A.h5ad" in fake.prompts[0]
    # charged once, from the ledger: the run's own nominal_usd_external is not added again
    assert site.calls == 1 and site.nominal_usd == pytest.approx(RUN_USD)


def test_same_request_from_another_run_replays_claims_and_view(env, monkeypatch):
    fake = install(monkeypatch)
    first = new_site(env, run_id="t1/union/s0")
    claims = first.propose(0)
    second = new_site(env, run_id="t1/ours/s0")
    assert second.propose(0) == claims and len(fake.prompts) == 1   # served from the store
    assert view_files(second) == view_files(first)                  # with the same view
    assert second.table.genes == first.table.genes
    assert second.calls == 1 and second.nominal_usd == pytest.approx(RUN_USD)   # nominal on hits
    second.propose(1)                       # a new sample index is an honest re-draw
    assert len(fake.prompts) == 2


def test_concurrent_requests_for_one_proposal_converge_on_one_value(env, monkeypatch):
    install(monkeypatch, barrier=threading.Barrier(2))
    sites = [new_site(env, run_id=f"r{i}") for i in range(2)]
    llm.spent()   # create the ledger first: two threads creating a fresh sqlite file can lock
    out: list[list[Claim] | None] = [None, None]
    threads = [threading.Thread(target=lambda i=i: out.__setitem__(i, sites[i].propose(0)))
               for i in range(2)]
    for t in threads:
        t.start()
    for t in threads:
        t.join()
    assert out[0] is not None and out[0] == out[1]
    assert view_files(sites[0]) == view_files(sites[1])
    assert sites[0].spec() == sites[1].spec()


def test_failed_call_is_charged_and_recorded_but_never_cached(env, monkeypatch):
    install(monkeypatch, fail=True)
    site = new_site(env)
    assert site.propose(0) == []
    assert site.calls == 1 and site.nominal_usd == pytest.approx(FAIL_USD)   # charged once
    assert [f["op"] for f in site.failures] == ["propose"]
    assert not (site.ws / "view").exists()          # the half-written view is not kept
    r = site.test(CLAIM)
    assert (r.status, r.reason) == ("untestable", "view")
    fake = install(monkeypatch)
    retry = new_site(env, run_id="r2")
    assert len(retry.propose(0)) == 1 and len(fake.prompts) == 1   # the failure was not cached


def test_malformed_view_makes_tests_untestable_instead_of_crashing(env, monkeypatch):
    install(monkeypatch, drop_group=True)
    site = new_site(env)
    claims = site.propose(0)
    assert site.table is None and any(e.startswith("propose:") for e in site.errors)
    r = site.test(claims[0])
    assert (r.status, r.reason, r.site) == ("untestable", "view", "A")
    assert site.holds("B", ("treated", "control"))   # routed, so the broken view is on record
    assert site.spec() == {}
    assert list(site.export_stats("B", ("treated", "control")).columns) == site_mod.STATS_COLUMNS


def test_make_sites_applies_agent_overrides(env, monkeypatch):
    specs = [{"name": "A", "data_path": env.data, "card": CARD, "proposes": True,
              "agent": "codex"},

              "agent": "finch"}]
    sites = make_sites(specs, env.case, task_id="t1", run_id="t1/ours/s0", agents={"M": "react"})
    assert [(s.name, s.agent, s.proposes, s.timeout) for s in sites] == [

    fake = install(monkeypatch)
    assert sites[1].propose(0) == [] and sites[1].table is not None   # tester-only: view only
    assert "builds its view only" in fake.prompts[0]
```

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import shutil
import threading
import time
from collections import OrderedDict
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Callable

import pandas as pd

from agentcoop import llm, store
from agentcoop.agents import harness, workspace
from agentcoop.coop import tester
from agentcoop.coop.claims import Claim, Vocabulary, parse_claims
from agentcoop.coop.tester import TesterParams, TestResult
from agentcoop.coop.view import EXCLUDE, OTHER, SiteTable, SiteView, load_view, site_table

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from agentcoop.coop.protocol import SiteHandle

SPEC_KEYS = ("contrasts", "sample_unit", "normalization", "gene_mapping", "covariates",
             "exclusions")
STATS_COLUMNS = ["gene", "effect", "se", "n1", "n2"]
TRANSCRIPT_STEPS = 40   # last steps of an agent run kept with its stored result
TABLE_CACHE = 32        # site tables kept in memory, one per (view, data file)
POLL_S = 0.2            # mailbox polling interval
BAD_POLLS = 10          # polls a request may stay unparsable (still being written) before an error
```

Then append to the end of the file:

```python
class SiteFailed(RuntimeError):
    """The site agent's run failed as a process (timeout, harness error). It is charged and
    counted but never cached, since the same request may succeed when asked again."""


def _sha(text: str) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(text.encode()).hexdigest()


def _record(run: harness.AgentRun) -> dict:
    """What a stored call keeps of its run, for inspection. The run's model usage is already in
    the ledger under the memo's tag (the harness ledgers Codex usage there too), so it is part
    of the memo's ``nominal_usd``; returning it as ``nominal_usd_external`` would count it twice.
    """
    return {"agent_nominal_usd": run.nominal_usd_external, "seconds": run.seconds,
            "transcript": run.transcript[-TRANSCRIPT_STEPS:]}


_TABLES: OrderedDict[tuple[str, str, str], SiteTable] = OrderedDict()
_TABLES_LOCK = threading.Lock()


def _table(blob: str, view: SiteView, data_path: Path, kind: str) -> SiteTable:
    """The site table of a stored view, computed once per process: the Site objects of every
    arm restore the same step-1 view."""
    k = (blob, file_digest(data_path), kind)
    with _TABLES_LOCK:
        if k in _TABLES:
            _TABLES.move_to_end(k)
            return _TABLES[k]
    table = site_table(view, data_path, kind=kind)
    with _TABLES_LOCK:
        _TABLES[k] = table
        if len(_TABLES) > TABLE_CACHE:
            _TABLES.popitem(last=False)
    return table


class Site:
    """One site behind the ``SiteHandle`` interface (``agentcoop.coop.protocol``)."""

    def __init__(self, name: str, data_path: Path, card: dict, agent: str, case: CaseSpec, *,
                 task_id: str, run_id: str, proposes: bool = True, timeout: int = 900) -> None:
        self.name, self.data_path, self.agent, self.case = name, Path(data_path), agent, case
        self.task_id, self.run_id, self.proposes, self.timeout = task_id, run_id, proposes, timeout
        self.calls, self.nominal_usd = 0, 0.0
        self.view: SiteView | None = None
        self.table: SiteTable | None = None
        self.view_blob: str | None = None   # store id of the view now in ws/view
        self.errors: list[str] = []         # view and claim validation messages, "<op>: ..."
        self.failures: list[dict] = []      # failed agent calls: {"op", "error"}
        self._card = card
        self._key: dict | None = None       # store key of the step-1 proposal
        self.ws = workspace.new_workspace(
            f"{run_id}/{name}", {self.data_path.name: self.data_path, **case.resources})
        (self.ws / "codebook.json").write_text(json.dumps(card, indent=1))

    def card(self) -> dict:
        return self._card

    def spec(self) -> dict:
        return self.view.spec if self.view is not None else {}

    # -- agent calls ---------------------------------------------------------

    def _identity(self) -> dict:
        return {"v": 1, "case": self.case.name, "task": self.task_id, "site": self.name,
                "agent": self.agent, "data": file_digest(self.data_path)}

    def _call(self, op: str, key: dict, compute: Callable[[], dict]) -> dict | None:
        """One site-agent call through the store, counted and charged whether it is served from
        the store, runs or fails. A failure is charged what the ledger holds under its tag (the
        same amount ``memo`` would have stored), recorded, never cached, and returns None."""
        self.calls += 1
        with llm.tag(f"{self.run_id}/{self.name}/{op}.{time.time_ns()}"):
            calls_inside = llm.current_tag() + "/"   # store.memo nests the run's own tag here
            try:
                out = store.memo(key, compute, tag=f"{self.name}.{op}")
            except SiteFailed as e:
                self.nominal_usd += llm.spent(calls_inside)["nominal_usd"]
                self.failures.append({"op": op, "error": str(e)})
                return None
        self.nominal_usd += out["nominal_usd"]
        return out

    def _run(self, prompt: str, sample: int) -> harness.AgentRun:
        """Run the agent once in the workspace. A failed run leaves the view as it was before
        the call and raises, so it is charged but never cached."""
        run = harness.run_agent(self.agent, self.ws, prompt, sample=sample, timeout=self.timeout)
        if not run.ok:
            self._restore(self.view_blob)
            raise SiteFailed(f"{self.agent} at site {self.name}: {run.error}")
        return run

    def _capture(self) -> None:
        view = self.ws / "view"
        self.view_blob = store.put_blob(view) if view.is_dir() else None

    def _restore(self, blob: str | None) -> None:
        """Make ws/view the stored view ``blob`` (None: no view)."""
        shutil.rmtree(self.ws / "view", ignore_errors=True)
        if blob is not None:
            store.get_blob(blob, self.ws / "view")
        self.view_blob = blob

    def _load(self, op: str) -> None:
        """Validate the view in ws/view and compute its site table. A view that does not load
        leaves the site without a table, so its tests come back untestable(view)."""
        self.view = self.table = None
        if self.view_blob is None:
            self.errors.append(f"{op}: no view/ written")
            return
        self.view, errors = load_view(self.ws / "view", kind=self.case.kind, vocab=self.case.vocab)
        self.errors += [f"{op}: {e}" for e in errors]
        if self.view is None:
            return
        try:
            self.table = _table(self.view_blob, self.view, self.data_path, self.case.kind)
        except (KeyError, ValueError) as e:   # a view the data file cannot be read with
            self.errors.append(f"{op}: site table: {e}")

    def propose(self, sample: int) -> list[Claim]:
        prompt = propose_prompt(self.case, self._card, self.data_path.name,
                                proposes=self.proposes)
        self._key = self._identity() | {"op": "propose", "prompt": _sha(prompt), "sample": sample}

        def compute() -> dict:
            self._restore(None)
            (self.ws / "claims.json").unlink(missing_ok=True)
            run = self._run(prompt, sample)
            self._capture()
            raw, errors = None, []
            if self.proposes:
                try:
                    raw = json.loads((self.ws / "claims.json").read_text())
                except FileNotFoundError:
                    errors.append("no claims.json written")
                except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
                    errors.append(f"claims.json is not valid JSON: {e}")
            return {"claims": raw, "view_blob": self.view_blob, "errors": errors,
                    "ok": self.view_blob is not None and (raw is not None or not self.proposes),
                    **_record(run)}

        out = self._call("propose", self._key, compute)
        if out is not None:
            if out["view_blob"] != self.view_blob:   # served from the store: restore its view
                self._restore(out["view_blob"])
            self.errors += [f"propose: {e}" for e in out["errors"]]
        self._load("propose")
        if out is None or out["claims"] is None:
            return []
        claims, errors = parse_claims(out["claims"], site=self.name, vocab=self.case.vocab,
                                      max_claims=self.case.max_claims)
        self.errors += [f"claims: {e}" for e in errors]
        return claims

    # -- deterministic, inside the site boundary -----------------------------

    def holds(self, population: str, contrast: tuple[str, str]) -> bool:
        """Whether the view has units of ``population`` in both groups. A site without a usable
        view cannot rule anything out: the test is routed and comes back untestable(view), so a
        broken view shows in the record instead of being skipped silently."""
        if self.table is None:
            return True
        units = self.table.units
        if self.table.kind == "scrna":   # bulk units are whole samples (population "all")
            units = units[units["population"] == population]
        return all((units["group"] == g).any() for g in contrast)

    def test(self, claim: Claim, *, test: str = "replication",
             contrast: tuple[str, str] | None = None) -> TestResult:
        if self.table is None:
            return TestResult(claim_id=claim.id, site=self.name, test=test, status="untestable",
                              effect=None, se=None, ci95=None, n_per_group=None, mde=None, p=None,
                              competitive_ok=None, reason="view")
        return tester.test_claim(self.table, claim, self.case.tester, site=self.name, test=test,
                                 contrast=contrast)

    def export_stats(self, population: str, contrast: tuple[str, str]) -> pd.DataFrame:
        if self.table is None:
            return pd.DataFrame(columns=STATS_COLUMNS)
        return tester.export_gene_stats(self.table, population, contrast, self.case.tester)


def make_sites(site_specs: list[dict], case: CaseSpec, *, task_id: str, run_id: str,
               agents: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> list[Site]:
    """The Site objects of one run. ``agents`` overrides the agent per site name (e.g. the
    all-Codex robustness run); an optional ``timeout`` per spec sets the wall-clock cap in
    seconds (real cases: 1800)."""
    agents = agents or {}
    return [Site(s["name"], Path(s["data_path"]), s["card"],
                 agents[s["name"]] if s["name"] in agents else s["agent"], case,
                 task_id=task_id, run_id=run_id, proposes=s["proposes"],
                 timeout=int(s.get("timeout", 900)))
            for s in site_specs]
```

```python
def test_repair_changes_the_view_and_the_next_test_sees_it(env, monkeypatch):
    install(monkeypatch, map_genes=False)            # step 1 leaves Ensembl ids unmapped
    site = new_site(env)
    claim = site.propose(0)[0]
    assert site.test(claim).status == "untestable"   # its genes are not in the view
    before = view_files(site)
    install(monkeypatch, fail=True)
    assert not site.repair(claim, DEFECT, 0)         # a failed repair leaves the view as it was
    assert view_files(site) == before
    fake = install(monkeypatch)
    assert site.repair(claim, DEFECT, 0)
    assert "SYM0" in site.table.genes and site.test(claim).status != "untestable"
    assert DEFECT in fake.prompts[0] and "up_genes" not in fake.prompts[0]
    assert site.calls == 3 and site.nominal_usd == pytest.approx(RUN_USD + FAIL_USD + RUN_USD)
    replay = new_site(env, run_id="r2")              # another run replays both calls
    replay.propose(0)
    assert replay.repair(claim, DEFECT, 0) and len(fake.prompts) == 1
    assert view_files(replay) == view_files(site)


def test_self_check_and_ask_are_charged_memoized_follow_ups(env, monkeypatch):
    fake = install(monkeypatch, map_genes=False)
    site = new_site(env)
    site.propose(0)
    assert site.self_check(0) and "SYM0" in site.table.genes   # the checklist fixed the ids
    assert "Checklist" in fake.prompts[-1]
    before = view_files(site)
    answer = site.ask("How many donors per group?", 0)
    assert answer.startswith("site answer")
    assert view_files(site) == before               # answering never changes the view
    assert site.ask("How many donors per group?", 0) == answer and len(fake.prompts) == 3
    assert site.calls == 4 and site.nominal_usd == pytest.approx(4 * RUN_USD)
```

- [ ] **Step 13: Implement the follow-ups**

In `agentcoop/coop/site.py`, insert the two follow-up prompts immediately above the line `def _dispatch(msg: object, handlers: dict[str, Callable[[dict], dict]]) -> dict:`, leaving two blank lines between definitions:

```python
def _self_check_prompt(case: CaseSpec) -> str:
    """The untargeted view check given to MA-genome (§4): a fixed checklist, no claim."""
    items = [
        "Every symbol in view/genes.tsv is the HGNC symbol of its native_id (use the mapping "
        "resources in this directory).",
        f"Every group in view/samples.tsv is the case group (or {EXCLUDE}) that codebook.json "
        "gives for the sample's own code.",
        "One sample per donor.",
    ]
    if case.kind == "scrna":
        items.insert(2, f"Every population in view/cells.tsv is a case population (or {OTHER}) "
                        "that matches the cell's own label as codebook.json describes it.")
    return (
        _setting(case)
        + "\nChecklist: check your site view in view/ against each item and fix what is wrong, "
        "in place.\n" + "".join(f"{i}. {t}\n" for i, t in enumerate(items, 1))
        + "Change only files under view/ and keep their formats.\n" + _view_format(case.kind)
    )


def _ask_prompt(case: CaseSpec, request: str) -> str:
    return (
        _setting(case)
        + f"\nRequest from the study coordinator:\n{request}\n\n"
        "Answer from your site's data and view. Write the answer to answer.md in this directory: "
        "plain text, summary statistics only, no cell- or sample-level data. Do not change files "
        "under view/."
    )
```

Then insert these methods into `class Site`, immediately above the line `    # -- deterministic, inside the site boundary -----------------------------`:

```python
    def _followup_key(self, op: str, prompt: str, sample: int) -> dict:
        """A follow-up depends on the step-1 call it follows and on the view it starts from."""
        return self._identity() | {"op": op, "after": self._key, "view": self.view_blob,
                                   "prompt": _sha(prompt), "sample": sample}

    def _edit_view(self, op: str, key: dict, prompt: str, sample: int) -> bool:
        """A follow-up in which the agent may change only its view; the site then tests on the
        reloaded view. True iff the call succeeded and the view loads."""
        def compute() -> dict:
            run = self._run(prompt, sample)
            self._capture()
            return {"view_blob": self.view_blob, **_record(run)}

        out = self._call(op, key, compute)
        if out is None:
            return False
        if out["view_blob"] != self.view_blob:
            self._restore(out["view_blob"])
        self._load(op)
        return self.table is not None

    def repair(self, claim: Claim, defect: str, sample: int) -> bool:
        prompt = repair_prompt(self.case, claim, defect)
        key = self._followup_key("repair", prompt, sample) | {"claim": claim.id,
                                                              "defect": _sha(defect)}
        return self._edit_view("repair", key, prompt, sample)

    def self_check(self, sample: int) -> bool:
        prompt = _self_check_prompt(self.case)
        return self._edit_view("self_check", self._followup_key("self_check", prompt, sample),
                               prompt, sample)

    def ask(self, request: str, sample: int) -> str:
        prompt = _ask_prompt(self.case, request)

        def compute() -> dict:
            answer = self.ws / "answer.md"
            answer.unlink(missing_ok=True)
            run = self._run(prompt, sample)
            self._restore(self.view_blob)   # answering a request never changes the view
            return {"answer": answer.read_text() if answer.exists() else "", **_record(run)}

        out = self._call("ask", self._followup_key("ask", prompt, sample), compute)
        if out is None:
            return f"(site {self.name} did not answer: its agent call failed)"
        return out["answer"] or f"(site {self.name} wrote no answer)"
```

### Task 8: Controlled suite generator and grader

Case 3 (spec §7) is built on a frozen OneK1K donor subset. Each task has proposing sites A, B, C (4 control + 4 treated donors each) and a tester-only mimic site M (4 control + 4 mimic donors). Effects are planted by binomial thinning. There are two knobs: `artefact`, and `ids_labels`, which covers the spec's three gene-ID schemes (symbol / Ensembl / alias), renamed cell types and renamed condition codes. The answer key and grader live on the evaluation side. The v3 planting primitives (`FAMILIES`, `MIN_CORE`, `load_gene_sets`, `core_gene_sets`, `_expressed`, `_thin`) move into `cases/planted.py`, and `agentcoop/tasks/` is deleted.

**Files:**
- Create: `agentcoop/cases/__init__.py`
- Create: `agentcoop/cases/planted.py`
- Create: `agentcoop/eval/__init__.py` (overwrite it if Task 1 left the v3 one)
- Create: `agentcoop/eval/planted.py`
- Create: `tests/test_planted.py`
- Modify: `findings.md` (append the base-freeze record)
- Delete: `agentcoop/tasks/` (the whole package). Also delete `agentcoop/eval/grader.py`, `agentcoop/eval/bixbench_grader.py` and `agentcoop/eval/case_validation.py` if Task 1 left them, because they import `agentcoop.tasks`.
- Data, not in git: `data/bench/onek1k/hgnc_complete_set_2026-10-06.txt` (downloaded in Step 10), `data/bench/onek1k/base.h5ad`, `data/bench/onek1k/base.json`

**Interfaces:**

Consumes (Tasks 3, 5, 6, 7):
```python
from agentcoop.coop.claims import Claim, Vocabulary          # Claim.contrast, .up_genes, .down_genes, .effect
from agentcoop.coop.tester import TesterParams, TestResult   # TestResult.test, .status
from agentcoop.coop.protocol import SiteHandle                # .name, .holds(population, contrast)
from agentcoop.coop.site import CaseSpec, file_digest
```

Produces:
```python
# ---- agentcoop/cases/planted.py ----
BASE: Path = ROOT / "data" / "bench" / "onek1k" / "base.h5ad"
POPULATIONS = ("CD4 T", "CD8 T", "NK", "B", "CD14 Mono", "CD16 Mono")
CASE_VOCAB: Vocabulary   # groups ("control", "treated", "mimic"); contrasts (("treated","control"), ("mimic","control"))
GO_BP: Path              # the gene-set library given to site agents (Enrichr text format)
SCHEMES = ("symbol", "ensembl", "alias")   # gene-ID schemes of the ids_labels knob
def prepare_base(src: Path, dst: Path = BASE, *, n_donors: int = 80, seed: int = 0) -> dict
    # {"dev_donors", "test_donors", "sha256", ...}; base.var columns gene_ids (Ensembl), alias
def build_suite(name: str, seeds: list[int], *, pool: str, knobs: list[tuple[bool, bool]],
                seal: Callable[[Path, dict], None]) -> list[Path]
def load_case(task_dir: Path) -> tuple[CaseSpec, list[dict]]   # site specs {"name","data_path","card","proposes"}
def scope_tests(claim: Claim, sites: list[SiteHandle]) -> list[tuple[SiteHandle, tuple[str, str]]]
def scope_rule(claim: Claim, results: list[TestResult]) -> str  # "shared" | "specific" | "inconclusive"
# ---- agentcoop/eval/planted.py ----
def write_truth(task_dir: Path, truth: dict) -> None           # the only writer of hidden/truth.json
def build(name: str, seeds: list[int], *, pool: str) -> list[Path]   # build_suite(..., seal=write_truth), knobs balanced
def load_truth(task_dir: Path) -> dict
def match(claim: Claim, truth: dict) -> dict | None            # best-matching planted record
def grade(claims: list[Claim], truth: dict, *, labels: dict[str, str] | None = None,
          causes: dict[str, dict] | None = None, scopes: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> dict
    # keys: precision, recall, f1, n_claims, artefact_rejected, scope_accuracy, cause_accuracy
```

**Case resources** (`load_case(...)[0].resources`; keys are paths inside each site workspace):
```python
{"symbols.tsv": <task>/public/symbols.tsv,                       # columns ensembl_id, symbol, alias
 "genesets/GO_Biological_Process_2023.txt": GO_BP}              # gene-set libraries live under genesets/
```
Task 9's `gene_set_library(case)` reads every resource whose key starts with `genesets/`. Anything else that needs the library the site agents were given, such as Task 10's MA-tools reference, must call `gene_set_library(case)` and not look up a fixed key. `new_workspace` (Task 2) creates the `genesets/` subdirectory.

**Contract deviation (one, deliberate):** `build_suite` takes one argument more than the contract lists, a required keyword `seal`. The Global Constraints forbid anything under `agentcoop/cases/` from mentioning `hidden/`, `truth.json` or `load_truth`, and spec §8 puts "case builders that write hidden references" in `eval/`. So the generator passes each task's planting record to `seal`, and suites are built from the eval side, with `agentcoop.eval.planted.build(...)` or `python -m agentcoop.eval.planted <suite> <pool> <lo> <hi>`. The name, positional arguments and return type are unchanged. Additions that change no contract signature: `prepare_base` reads the module constant `HGNC`, its metadata has more keys than the contract's three, and `base.h5ad` gains a `var["alias"]` column.

**Task layout and planting record** (read by Tasks 9–11):
```
data/tasks/<suite>/<suite>-<seed:03d>/
  public/task.json       {"task_id", "question", "sites": [{"name", "data", "card", "proposes"}], "symbols"}
  public/cards/<S>.json  {"site", "columns", "features", "codes": {code: text}, "donors_per_group", "assay_values", "cell_labels"}
  public/symbols.tsv     ensembl_id <TAB> symbol <TAB> alias   (workspace resource for the ID knob; a bijection)
  sites/<S>.h5ad         obs = sample, condition (code), cell_label, assay; opaque cell/sample IDs; var index = native gene IDs
  hidden/truth.json      written only by eval.planted.write_truth:
    {"task_id", "seed", "pool", "base_sha256", "knobs": {"artefact", "ids_labels"},
     "programs": [{"id", "kind": reproducible|site_specific|artefact, "program", "family", "cell_types",
                   "up_genes", "down_genes", "log2fc", "sites", "shared_with_mimic"}],
     "composition": {"id": "C1", "kind": "composition", "population", "sign": -1, "fraction_removed": 0.4, "sites"},
     "artefact_site": str | None,
     "sites": {S: {"scheme": symbol|ensembl|alias, "codes": {code: group}, "labels": {native: population},
                   "donors": {group: [donor]}, "samples": {sample: donor}}}}   # correct codebook, for view-error rates (§6)
```

**Alias scheme.** `prepare_base` fixes one legacy name per gene from a dated HGNC release and stores it in `var["alias"]`, so the names are frozen with the base and covered by its hash. The rule is the gene's first previous HGNC symbol, else its first alias symbol. A name counts only if HGNC lists it for exactly one gene and it is not an approved or base symbol (case-insensitive); a gene with no such name keeps its symbol. This keeps symbol ↔ alias a bijection: a correct view is always possible, and an alias never reads as another gene. The names are real ones. On the real base 16,244 of 26,296 genes get a legacy name, for example ATP5F1D → ATP5D and POLR2I → RPB9, which is the outdated-annotation problem real sites have.

Generator invariants, pinned by the tests:
- Reproducible programs are planted at A, B and C. Each is also planted in M's mimic donors with probability 0.5.
- At most one program per family per task, and programs never share genes.
- The site-specific program and the artefact always sit at different proposing sites. At an assay-confounded site, an effect seen only there could not be told apart from the artefact, so its cause would be ungradable.
- The composition shift drops 40% of one population's cells in A/B/C treated donors. That population carries no program, so DE power is untouched.
- Under `ids_labels`, A, B and C get the three gene-ID schemes in random order and M draws one, so each scheme is measured at the same number of sites (the view-error rates of §6). Without the knob every site uses symbols, vocabulary population names and group names as codes.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the base-preparation, scope and boundary tests**

Create `tests/test_planted.py`:

```python
"""Planted suite: base preparation, task generation, case spec and the grader. Offline: tiny
synthetic data and fake libraries stand in for OneK1K, HGNC, Hallmark and GO."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path

import anndata as ad
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import pytest
import scipy.sparse as sp

from agentcoop.cases import planted
from agentcoop.coop import tester
from agentcoop.coop.claims import Claim

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
GENES = [f"G{i:03d}" for i in range(300)]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Base preparation
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _onek1k_like(path: Path) -> None:
    """OneK1K's layout in miniature: float32 CSR counts, Ensembl var index plus a categorical
    feature_name (one ambiguous symbol, one gene never detected), categorical donor_id /
    cell_type with labels outside the case populations. Gene MARK holds row index + 1."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    labels = [*planted.CELL_TYPES, "platelet", "plasmablast"]
    rows = []
    for d in range(12):
        for label in labels:
            if d == 1 and planted.CELL_TYPES.get(label) == "CD16 Mono":
                continue                      # D01 lacks a population
            rows += [(f"D{d:02d}", label)] * (3 if d == 0 else 12)   # D00 has too few cells
    obs = pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=["donor_id", "cell_type"]).astype("category")
    obs.index = [f"bc{i}" for i in range(len(obs))]
    symbols = ["MARK", "DUP", "DUP", "NEVER", *[f"G{i:02d}" for i in range(1, 17)]]
    counts = rng.poisson(1.0, size=(len(obs), len(symbols))).astype(np.float32)
    counts[:, 0] = np.arange(len(obs)) + 1
    counts[:, 3] = 0
    var = pd.DataFrame({"feature_name": pd.Categorical(symbols)},
                       index=[f"ENSG{i:011d}" for i in range(len(symbols))])
    ad.AnnData(X=sp.csr_matrix(counts), obs=obs, var=var).write_h5ad(path)


def _hgnc(path: Path) -> Path:
    """HGNC's complete-set layout (pipe lists quoted, empty fields empty) for genes G01-G06 of
    _onek1k_like (Ensembl index = number + 3), plus one approved gene outside the base."""
    rows = [("HGNC:1", "G01", "AKA1", '"OLD1|OLD1B"', "ENSG00000000004"),   # previous symbol first
            ("HGNC:2", "G02", '"AKA2|AKA2B"', "", "ENSG00000000005"),       # else first alias
            ("HGNC:3", "G03", "SHARED", "", "ENSG00000000006"),             # alias of two genes
            ("HGNC:4", "G04", '"shared|AKA4"', "", "ENSG00000000007"),
            ("HGNC:5", "G05", "AKA5", "g06", "ENSG00000000008"),            # a base symbol
            ("HGNC:6", "G06", "APPROVED", "", "ENSG00000000009"),           # an approved symbol
            ("HGNC:7", "APPROVED", "", "", "ENSG99999999999")]
    lines = ["hgnc_id\tsymbol\talias_symbol\tprev_symbol\tensembl_gene_id",
             *["\t".join(r) for r in rows]]
    path.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
    return path


def test_prepare_base_streams_eligible_donors_and_freezes_the_subset(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    src, dst = tmp_path / "onek1k.h5ad", tmp_path / "base.h5ad"
    _onek1k_like(src)
    monkeypatch.setattr(planted, "HGNC", _hgnc(tmp_path / "hgnc.txt"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(planted, "MIN_DONOR_CELLS", 100)
    monkeypatch.setattr(planted, "MIN_POP_CELLS", 10)
    monkeypatch.setattr(planted, "MAX_DONOR_CELLS", 150)
    monkeypatch.setattr(planted, "ROW_BLOCK", 37)          # many blocks, selections straddle them
    meta = planted.prepare_base(src, dst, n_donors=8, seed=0)

    assert len(meta["dev_donors"]) == len(meta["test_donors"]) == 4
    assert not set(meta["dev_donors"]) & set(meta["test_donors"])
    assert not {"D00", "D01"} & set(meta["dev_donors"] + meta["test_donors"])
    assert json.loads(dst.with_suffix(".json").read_text()) == meta
    assert meta["sha256"] == hashlib.sha256(dst.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    assert meta["hgnc_sha256"] == hashlib.sha256((tmp_path / "hgnc.txt").read_bytes()).hexdigest()

    base = ad.read_h5ad(dst)
    assert list(base.obs.columns) == ["donor", "population"]
    assert sp.issparse(base.X) and base.X.dtype == np.int32
    assert not {"DUP", "NEVER"} & set(base.var_names)
    assert base.var.loc["G01", "gene_ids"] == "ENSG00000000004"
    expected = {"G01": "OLD1", "G02": "AKA2", "G04": "AKA4", "G05": "AKA5"}
    assert dict(base.var["alias"]) == {g: expected.get(g, g) for g in base.var_names}
    assert meta["n_aliased"] == 4 and base.var["alias"].str.upper().is_unique
    per = base.obs.groupby(["donor", "population"], observed=True).size().unstack()
    assert sorted(per.index) == sorted(meta["dev_donors"] + meta["test_donors"])
    assert set(per.columns) == set(planted.POPULATIONS)
    assert (per.sum(1) <= 150).all() and (per.min(1) >= 10).all()

    # every kept cell is an intact copy of its source row (MARK identifies the row)
    source = ad.read_h5ad(src)
    rows = base[:, "MARK"].X.toarray().ravel() - 1
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(base.X.toarray(),
                                  source[rows, list(base.var["gene_ids"])].X.toarray())
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(base.obs["donor"].astype(str),
                                  source.obs["donor_id"].astype(str).to_numpy()[rows])
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(
        base.obs["population"].astype(str),
        source.obs["cell_type"].astype(str).map(planted.CELL_TYPES).to_numpy()[rows])

    with pytest.raises(FileExistsError):
        planted.prepare_base(src, dst, n_donors=8, seed=0)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Scope table and rule
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _claim(cid: str = "c1", population: str = "NK", up=(), down=(), *, kind: str = "de",
           target: str = "treated", effect: float | None = None) -> Claim:
    return Claim(id=cid, site="A", kind=kind, population=population, target=target,
                 reference="control", up_genes=tuple(up), down_genes=tuple(down), effect=effect,
                 n_units=8, rank=1)


def _result(status: str, test: str = "scope") -> tester.TestResult:
    return tester.TestResult(claim_id="c1", site="M", test=test, status=status, effect=None,
                             se=None, ci95=None, n_per_group=None, mde=None, p=None,
                             competitive_ok=None, reason="")


class _Site:
    def __init__(self, name: str, mimic: bool) -> None:
        self.name, self._mimic = name, mimic

    def holds(self, population: str, contrast: tuple[str, str]) -> bool:
        return self._mimic or contrast == ("treated", "control")


def test_scope_tests_send_treated_claims_to_sites_holding_the_mimic_contrast():
    a, b, m = _Site("A", False), _Site("B", False), _Site("M", True)
    assert planted.scope_tests(_claim(up=GENES[:3]), [a, b, m]) == [(m, ("mimic", "control"))]
    assert planted.scope_tests(_claim(target="mimic", up=GENES[:3]), [a, b, m]) == []


def test_scope_rule_reads_only_scope_tests_and_never_counts_untestable():
    claim = _claim(up=GENES[:3])

    def rule(*statuses: str) -> str:
        return planted.scope_rule(claim, [_result(s) for s in statuses])

    assert rule("supported", "absent") == "shared"
    assert rule("absent") == rule("contradicted", "untestable") == "specific"
    assert rule("absent", "inconclusive") == "inconclusive"
    assert rule("untestable") == rule() == "inconclusive"
    assert planted.scope_rule(claim, [_result("supported", test="replication"),
                                      _result("absent")]) == "specific"


def test_the_case_module_has_no_path_to_answers():
    text = (ROOT / "agentcoop" / "cases" / "planted.py").read_text()
    for needle in ("agentcoop.eval", "load_truth", "truth.json", "hidden/", '"hidden"'):
        assert needle not in text, needle
```

Create `agentcoop/cases/planted.py`:

```python
"""Case 3: a controlled multi-site suite planted into real healthy PBMCs (spec §7).

Cells, donors and gene-gene structure are real (a frozen OneK1K donor subset, used by no case or
held-out cohort); only the effects are planted, by binomial thinning (Gerard 2020): to make gene
g β log2 units higher in the target group, its counts in the lower group's cells are thinned with
probability 2^-β. Thinned counts are still counts, and the planted log fold change is exact in
expectation. Condition is a donor attribute (unpaired), as in the real cases.

Each task has three proposing sites A, B, C (4 control + 4 treated donors each) and a tester-only
site M (4 control + 4 mimic donors). It plants
* 1-3 reproducible programs at A, B and C, each also planted in M's mimic donors with
  probability 0.5 (so scope has a known answer: shared with the mimic, or specific);
* 0-1 site-specific program (one proposing site: real there, not replicable);
* one reproducible composition shift (40% of one population's cells dropped in treated donors);
* knob `artefact`: a coherent program thinned in one proposing site's control donors only, with
  an `assay` column that differs between that site's control and treated donors (as in Berlin);
* knob `ids_labels`: per-site gene ID scheme (symbol / Ensembl / alias), site-native cell-type
  names and condition codes that only the site's card explains.

This module writes only what sites and the coordinator may see. Each task's planting record is
handed to a caller-supplied `seal`, so `cases/` holds no path to answers (spec §8); suites are
built from the evaluation side, which owns that path.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Callable

import anndata as ad
import h5py
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import scipy.sparse as sp

from agentcoop.coop.claims import Claim, Vocabulary
from agentcoop.coop.protocol import SiteHandle
from agentcoop.coop.site import CaseSpec, file_digest
from agentcoop.coop.tester import TesterParams, TestResult

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
BASE: Path = ROOT / "data" / "bench" / "onek1k" / "base.h5ad"
TASKS = ROOT / "data" / "tasks"
HALLMARK = ROOT / "data" / "genesets" / "hallmark_enrichr.txt"
GO_BP = ROOT / "data" / "genesets" / "GO_Biological_Process_2023.txt"
#: HGNC complete set, a dated (immutable) monthly release: previous and alias symbols for the
#: alias gene-ID scheme. Read once, by prepare_base; the chosen aliases are frozen into the base.
HGNC_URL = ("https://storage.googleapis.com/public-download-files/hgnc/archive/archive/monthly/"
            "tsv/hgnc_complete_set_2026-10-06.txt")
HGNC = ROOT / "data" / "bench" / "onek1k" / "hgnc_complete_set_2026-10-06.txt"

POPULATIONS = ("CD4 T", "CD8 T", "NK", "B", "CD14 Mono", "CD16 Mono")
CASE_VOCAB = Vocabulary(populations=POPULATIONS, groups=("control", "treated", "mimic"),
                        contrasts=(("treated", "control"), ("mimic", "control")))
MIMIC = ("mimic", "control")

#: OneK1K Cell Ontology labels pooled into the case populations; every other label is dropped.
CELL_TYPES: dict[str, str] = {label: population for population, labels in {
    "CD4 T": ("CD4-positive, alpha-beta T cell",
              "naive thymus-derived CD4-positive, alpha-beta T cell",
              "central memory CD4-positive, alpha-beta T cell",
              "effector memory CD4-positive, alpha-beta T cell",
              "regulatory T cell", "CD4-positive, alpha-beta cytotoxic T cell"),
    "CD8 T": ("CD8-positive, alpha-beta T cell",
              "naive thymus-derived CD8-positive, alpha-beta T cell",
              "central memory CD8-positive, alpha-beta T cell",
              "effector memory CD8-positive, alpha-beta T cell"),
    "NK": ("natural killer cell", "CD16-negative, CD56-bright natural killer cell, human"),
    "B": ("memory B cell", "naive B cell", "transitional stage B cell"),
    "CD14 Mono": ("CD14-positive monocyte",),
    "CD16 Mono": ("CD14-low, CD16-positive monocyte",),

MIN_DONOR_CELLS = 1000   # spec §7: donors with >= 1,000 cells

FAMILIES: dict[str, dict] = {

                    "go_terms": ["Cholesterol Biosynthetic Process",
```
