}

#: Site-native cell-type names under the ids_labels knob. Each is unambiguous to an immunologist:
#: mapping them is a handoff step, not a riddle.
NATIVE_LABELS: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
    "CD4 T": ("helper T cells", "CD4+ T lymphocytes", "T helper cells"),
    "CD8 T": ("cytotoxic T cells", "CD8+ T lymphocytes", "killer T cells"),
    "NK": ("natural killer cells", "NK lymphocytes", "CD56+ NK cells"),
    "B": ("B lymphocytes", "CD19+ B cells", "B-cells"),
    "CD14 Mono": ("classical monocytes", "CD14+ monocytes", "CD14++ CD16- monocytes"),
    "CD16 Mono": ("non-classical monocytes", "FCGR3A+ monocytes", "CD14+ CD16++ monocytes"),
}
GROUP_TEXT = {"control": "untreated control donors",
              "treated": "donors exposed to the study treatment",
              "mimic": "donors exposed to a comparator stimulus (mimic), not the study treatment"}

QUESTION = (
    "Four sites each profiled PBMCs from 8 donors by scRNA-seq (one sample per donor). At sites "
    "A, B and C, 4 donors received an uncharacterized study treatment and 4 are untreated "
    "controls; at site M, 4 donors received a comparator stimulus (mimic) and 4 are untreated "
    "controls. Raw data cannot leave a site. Which cell-type-specific transcriptional programs "
    "and cell-type composition changes does the treatment cause that replicate across sites, "
    "and which of them does the mimic share?"
)
DEFAULT_SPEC = {"unit": "donor", "normalization": "CPM", "one_sample_per_donor": True,
                "groups": "map site codes per the codebook"}


def load_gene_sets(path: Path) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
    sets = {}
    for line in path.read_text().strip().split("\n"):
        parts = line.split("\t")
        sets[parts[0]] = [g for g in parts[2:] if g]
    return sets


def core_gene_sets(hallmark: dict[str, list[str]], go_path: Path) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
    """Core genes of each plantable program: Hallmark members that are also annotated to one of
    the family's defining GO terms. Two independent libraries agree these genes *are* the
    program, which is what makes a planted subset recognizable."""
    go = load_gene_sets(go_path)
    by_name = {k.split(" (GO:")[0].lower(): set(v) for k, v in go.items()}
    core = {}
    for spec in FAMILIES.values():
        defining = set()
        for term in spec["go_terms"]:
            defining |= by_name.get(term.lower(), set())
        for program in spec["programs"]:
            core[program] = [g for g in hallmark.get(program, []) if g in defining]
    return core


def _expressed(counts: sp.csr_matrix, min_mean: float, min_frac: float) -> np.ndarray:
    mean = np.asarray(counts.mean(0)).ravel()
    frac = np.asarray((counts > 0).mean(0)).ravel()
    return (mean >= min_mean) & (frac >= min_frac)


def _thin(X: sp.csc_matrix, row_mask: np.ndarray, col: int, p: float,
          rng: np.random.Generator) -> None:
    """In place on a CSC matrix: X[row_mask, col] ~ Binomial(X[row_mask, col], p)."""
    lo, hi = X.indptr[col], X.indptr[col + 1]
    hit = row_mask[X.indices[lo:hi]]
    values = X.data[lo:hi]
    values[hit] = rng.binomial(values[hit].astype(np.int64), p)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Frozen base: a donor subset of OneK1K
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _strings(node: h5py.Dataset | h5py.Group) -> np.ndarray:
    """An h5ad string or categorical column as an object array (anndata >= 0.8 encoding)."""
    if isinstance(node, h5py.Group):
        categories = node["categories"].asstr()[...]
        codes = node["codes"][...]
        out = np.full(codes.shape, None, dtype=object)
        out[codes >= 0] = categories[codes[codes >= 0]]
        return out
    return node.asstr()[...]


def _read_rows(X: h5py.Group, rows: np.ndarray) -> sp.csr_matrix:
    """Sorted rows of an on-disk CSR matrix as int32 counts, reading ROW_BLOCK rows per slice so
    the full matrix is never in memory."""
    indptr = X["indptr"][...]
    data, indices, lengths = [], [], []
    for start in range(0, len(indptr) - 1, ROW_BLOCK):
        i0, i1 = np.searchsorted(rows, [start, start + ROW_BLOCK])
        if i0 == i1:
            continue
        lo, hi = indptr[rows[i0]], indptr[rows[i1 - 1] + 1]
        block_data = np.rint(X["data"][lo:hi]).astype(np.int32)
        block_indices = X["indices"][lo:hi].astype(np.int32)
        for r in rows[i0:i1]:
            a, b = indptr[r] - lo, indptr[r + 1] - lo
            data.append(block_data[a:b])
            indices.append(block_indices[a:b])
            lengths.append(b - a)
    return sp.csr_matrix((np.concatenate(data), np.concatenate(indices),
                          np.concatenate([[0], np.cumsum(lengths)])),
                         shape=(len(rows), int(X.attrs["shape"][1])))


def _allocate(counts: np.ndarray, cap: int, floor: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """Cells kept per population: proportional to the donor's composition, at least `floor` (so
    rare populations stay testable), at most `cap` in total."""
    if counts.sum() <= cap:
        return counts
    keep = np.maximum(np.minimum(counts, floor), counts * cap // counts.sum())
    keep[np.argmax(keep)] -= max(0, int(keep.sum()) - cap)
    return keep


def _aliases(gene_ids: np.ndarray, symbols: np.ndarray, hgnc: Path) -> np.ndarray:
    """One frozen legacy name per gene for the alias ID scheme: its first previous HGNC symbol,
    else its first alias symbol, else the symbol itself. A name qualifies only if HGNC lists it
    for exactly one gene and it is no approved or base symbol (case-insensitive), so
    symbol <-> alias is a bijection and an alias never reads as another gene."""
    table = pd.read_csv(hgnc, sep="\t", dtype=str, keep_default_na=False,
                        usecols=["symbol", "prev_symbol", "alias_symbol", "ensembl_gene_id"])
    names = [[n for n in f"{r.prev_symbol}|{r.alias_symbol}".split("|") if n]
             for r in table.itertuples()]
    listed = Counter(u for ns in names for u in {n.upper() for n in ns})
    taken = {s.upper() for s in table["symbol"]} | {s.upper() for s in symbols}
    names_of = {eid: ns for eid, ns in zip(table["ensembl_gene_id"], names) if eid}
    out = []
    for eid, symbol in zip(gene_ids, symbols):
        usable = [n for n in names_of.get(eid, []) if listed[n.upper()] == 1
                  and n.upper() not in taken]
        out.append(usable[0] if usable else symbol)
    return np.asarray(out, dtype=object)


def prepare_base(src: Path, dst: Path = BASE, *, n_donors: int = 80, seed: int = 0) -> dict:
    """Freeze the donor subset every planted suite draws from; writes `dst` and `dst`.json.

    Streams `src` with h5py (OneK1K is 1.2M cells: only obs and the chosen CSR rows are read),
    keeps the case populations, donors with >= MIN_DONOR_CELLS such cells and >= MIN_POP_CELLS in
    every population, draws `n_donors`, splits them half dev / half test (disjoint pools for
    fsdev and fstest) and subsamples each donor to <= MAX_DONOR_CELLS, stratified by population.
    Genes with an ambiguous symbol or no counts are dropped, so symbol <-> Ensembl ID is a
    bijection (the ID knob relies on it); each gene's legacy name for the alias scheme is chosen
    from HGNC once, here, and frozen into var["alias"].
    """
    src, dst = Path(src), Path(dst)
    if dst.exists():
        raise FileExistsError(f"{dst} exists; the base is frozen (suites record its hash)")
    if not HGNC.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"{HGNC} is missing; download {HGNC_URL}")
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    with h5py.File(src, "r") as f:
        obs = pd.DataFrame({
            "donor": _strings(f["obs"]["donor_id"]),
            "population": pd.Series(_strings(f["obs"]["cell_type"])).map(CELL_TYPES).to_numpy(),
        }).dropna()
        counts = (obs.groupby(["donor", "population"]).size().unstack(fill_value=0)
                  .reindex(columns=list(POPULATIONS), fill_value=0))
        eligible = counts.index[(counts.sum(1) >= MIN_DONOR_CELLS)
                                & (counts.min(1) >= MIN_POP_CELLS)]
        if len(eligible) < n_donors:
            raise ValueError(f"{len(eligible)} eligible donors, {n_donors} requested")
        chosen = [str(d) for d in rng.choice(sorted(eligible), size=n_donors, replace=False)]
        cells = obs.groupby(["donor", "population"]).indices
        source_row = obs.index.to_numpy()
        picked = []
        for donor in sorted(chosen):
            pools = [source_row[cells[(donor, p)]] for p in POPULATIONS]
            keep = _allocate(np.array([len(x) for x in pools]), MAX_DONOR_CELLS, MIN_POP_CELLS)
            picked += [rng.choice(x, size=k, replace=False) for x, k in zip(pools, keep)]
        rows = np.sort(np.concatenate(picked))
        X = _read_rows(f["X"], rows)
        gene_ids = _strings(f["var"][f["var"].attrs["_index"]])
        symbols = _strings(f["var"]["feature_name"])
    X.eliminate_zeros()
    genes = (np.bincount(X.indices, minlength=X.shape[1]) > 0) & ~pd.Series(symbols).duplicated(
        keep=False).to_numpy()
    names = symbols[genes].astype(str)
    base = ad.AnnData(
        X=X[:, genes],
        obs=pd.DataFrame({"donor": obs.loc[rows, "donor"].to_numpy(),
                          "population": obs.loc[rows, "population"].to_numpy()},
                         index=rows.astype(str)).astype("category"),
        var=pd.DataFrame({"gene_ids": gene_ids[genes],
                          "alias": _aliases(gene_ids[genes], names, HGNC)}, index=names))
    dst.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    base.write_h5ad(dst, compression="gzip")
    half = n_donors // 2
    meta = {"source": src.name, "seed": seed, "dev_donors": sorted(chosen[:half]),
            "test_donors": sorted(chosen[half:]), "n_cells": int(base.n_obs),
            "n_genes": int(base.n_vars),
            "n_aliased": int((base.var["alias"] != base.var_names).sum()),
            "thresholds": {"min_donor_cells": MIN_DONOR_CELLS, "min_pop_cells": MIN_POP_CELLS,
                           "max_donor_cells": MAX_DONOR_CELLS},
            "hgnc": HGNC.name, "hgnc_sha256": file_digest(HGNC), "sha256": file_digest(dst)}
    dst.with_suffix(".json").write_text(json.dumps(meta, indent=2))
    return meta


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Tasks
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _target(site: str) -> str:
    return "mimic" if site == MIMIC_SITE else "treated"


def _tokens(rng: np.random.Generator, n: int) -> list[str]:
    """Opaque unique IDs: nothing in a sample or cell ID reveals donor, group or order."""
    return [f"{int(v):08x}" for v in rng.choice(16 ** 8, size=n, replace=False)]


def _draw_program(rng: np.random.Generator, kind: str, X: sp.csr_matrix, obs: pd.DataFrame,
                  gene_sets: dict[str, list[str]], index: dict[str, int],
                  taken: list[dict]) -> dict:
    """A program from a family not used yet in this task, on genes not used yet, planted where
    at least MIN_CORE of its core genes are expressed. One-site programs (site-specific,
    artefact) never share a site: at an assay-confounded site an effect seen only there could
    not be told apart from the artefact, so its cause would be ungradable."""
    used = {g for p in taken for g in p["up_genes"] + p["down_genes"]}
    families = sorted(set(FAMILIES) - {p["family"] for p in taken})
    busy = {s for p in taken if p["kind"] != "reproducible" for s in p["sites"]}
    free_sites = [s for s in PROPOSERS if s not in busy]
    for _ in range(200):
        family = str(rng.choice(families))
        program = str(rng.choice(FAMILIES[family]["programs"]))
        n_types = 2 if kind == "reproducible" and rng.random() < 0.4 else 1
        cell_types = sorted(str(c) for c in rng.choice(POPULATIONS, size=n_types, replace=False))
        ok = _expressed(X[obs["population"].isin(cell_types).to_numpy()], 0.08, 0.05)
        candidates = [g for g in gene_sets.get(program, [])
                      if g in index and ok[index[g]] and g not in used]
        if len(candidates) < MIN_CORE:
            continue
        n = int(np.clip(round(rng.uniform(0.7, 1.0) * len(candidates)), MIN_CORE, 30))
        genes = sorted(str(g) for g in rng.choice(candidates, size=n, replace=False))
        up = kind == "artefact" or bool(rng.random() < 0.5)
        return {"id": f"P{len(taken) + 1}", "kind": kind, "program": program, "family": family,
                "cell_types": cell_types, "up_genes": genes if up else [],
                "down_genes": [] if up else genes,
                "log2fc": round(float(rng.uniform(0.8, 1.4)), 3),
                "sites": (list(PROPOSERS) if kind == "reproducible"
                          else [str(rng.choice(free_sites))]),
                "shared_with_mimic": kind == "reproducible" and bool(rng.random() < 0.5)}
    raise RuntimeError(f"no plantable {kind} program in 200 draws")


def plant_task(base: ad.AnnData, gene_sets: dict[str, list[str]], *, task_id: str, seed: int,
               donors: list[str], artefact: bool, ids_labels: bool,
               ) -> tuple[dict[str, ad.AnnData], dict[str, dict], dict]:
    """One task: (site file by site, site card by site, planting record)."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    sites = (*PROPOSERS, MIMIC_SITE)
    need = 2 * PER_GROUP * len(sites)
    if len(donors) < need:
        raise ValueError(f"the pool has {len(donors)} donors; a task needs {need}")
    drawn = [str(d) for d in rng.choice(sorted(donors), size=need, replace=False)]
    blocks = [drawn[i: i + 2 * PER_GROUP] for i in range(0, need, 2 * PER_GROUP)]
    layout = {site: {"control": sorted(block[:PER_GROUP]), _target(site): sorted(block[PER_GROUP:])}
              for site, block in zip(sites, blocks)}
    where = {d: (s, g) for s, groups in layout.items() for g, ds in groups.items() for d in ds}
    cells = base[base.obs["donor"].isin(drawn).to_numpy()]
    obs = pd.DataFrame({"donor": cells.obs["donor"].astype(str).to_numpy(),
                        "population": cells.obs["population"].astype(str).to_numpy()})
    obs["site"] = [where[d][0] for d in obs["donor"]]
    obs["group"] = [where[d][1] for d in obs["donor"]]
    X = sp.csr_matrix(cells.X, dtype=np.int32)
    index = {g: i for i, g in enumerate(cells.var_names)}

    kinds = ["reproducible"] * int(rng.integers(1, 4))
    if rng.random() < 0.5:
        kinds.append("site_specific")
    if artefact:
        kinds.append("artefact")
    programs: list[dict] = []
    for kind in kinds:
        programs.append(_draw_program(rng, kind, X, obs, gene_sets, index, programs))

    # Thin the lower group: control donors for an up program, target donors for a down one.
    Xc = X.tocsc()
    for prog in programs:
        planted = prog["sites"] + ([MIMIC_SITE] if prog["shared_with_mimic"] else [])
        lower = (obs["population"].isin(prog["cell_types"]) & obs["site"].isin(planted)
                 & ((obs["group"] == "control") == bool(prog["up_genes"]))).to_numpy()
        for g in prog["up_genes"] + prog["down_genes"]:
            beta = max(0.3, prog["log2fc"] + rng.normal(0, 0.2))
            _thin(Xc, lower, index[g], 2.0 ** -beta, rng)
    Xc.eliminate_zeros()
    X = Xc.tocsr()

    # Composition shift in a population no program uses, so DE power is untouched by it.
    free = [p for p in POPULATIONS if not any(p in q["cell_types"] for q in programs)]
    population = str(rng.choice(free or list(POPULATIONS)))
    keep = np.ones(len(obs), dtype=bool)
    for site in PROPOSERS:
        for donor in layout[site]["treated"]:
            idx = np.flatnonzero(((obs["donor"] == donor)
                                  & (obs["population"] == population)).to_numpy())
            keep[rng.choice(idx, size=int(round(DROP * len(idx))), replace=False)] = False
    artefact_site = next((p["sites"][0] for p in programs if p["kind"] == "artefact"), None)

    # Every scheme at one proposing site, so per-scheme view-error rates (§6) rest on equal counts.
    schemes = dict.fromkeys(sites, "symbol")
    if ids_labels:
        schemes = {**{s: str(x) for s, x in zip(PROPOSERS, rng.permutation(SCHEMES))},
                   MIMIC_SITE: str(rng.choice(SCHEMES))}
    native_ids = {"symbol": np.asarray(cells.var_names, dtype=str),
                  "ensembl": cells.var["gene_ids"].to_numpy(dtype=str),
                  "alias": cells.var["alias"].to_numpy(dtype=str)}

    files, cards, maps = {}, {}, {}
    for site in sites:
        target = _target(site)
        if ids_labels:
            first, second = (str(c) for c in rng.permutation(["arm A", "arm B"]))
            codes = {first: "control", second: target}
            labels = {str(rng.choice(NATIVE_LABELS[p])): p for p in POPULATIONS}
        else:
            codes = {"control": "control", target: target}
            labels = {p: p for p in POPULATIONS}
        code_of = {g: c for c, g in codes.items()}
        label_of = {p: n for n, p in labels.items()}
        site_donors = layout[site]["control"] + layout[site][target]
        sample_of = dict(zip(site_donors, _tokens(rng, len(site_donors))))
        assay = {d: ASSAY_ALT if site == artefact_site and where[d][1] == target else ASSAY
                 for d in site_donors}
        rows = rng.permutation(np.flatnonzero(keep & (obs["site"] == site).to_numpy()))
        donor = obs["donor"].to_numpy()[rows]
        files[site] = ad.AnnData(
            X=X[rows].astype(np.float32),
            obs=pd.DataFrame({
                "sample": [sample_of[d] for d in donor],
                "condition": [code_of[where[d][1]] for d in donor],
                "cell_label": [label_of[p] for p in obs["population"].to_numpy()[rows]],
                "assay": [assay[d] for d in donor],
            }, index=_tokens(rng, len(rows))).astype("category"),
            var=pd.DataFrame(index=native_ids[schemes[site]]))
        cards[site] = {
            "site": site,
            "columns": {"sample": "sample ID (one sample per donor)",
                        "condition": "condition code; see codes",
                        "cell_label": "cell-type annotation made at this site",
                        "assay": "library chemistry"},
            "features": FEATURES[schemes[site]],
            "codes": {c: GROUP_TEXT[g] for c, g in codes.items()},
            "donors_per_group": {c: PER_GROUP for c in codes},
            "assay_values": {c: sorted({assay[d] for d in layout[site][g]})
                             for c, g in codes.items()},
            "cell_labels": sorted(labels),
        }
        maps[site] = {"scheme": schemes[site], "codes": codes, "labels": labels,
                      "donors": layout[site], "samples": {s: d for d, s in sample_of.items()}}

    record = {"task_id": task_id, "seed": seed,
              "knobs": {"artefact": artefact, "ids_labels": ids_labels},
              "programs": programs,
              "composition": {"id": "C1", "kind": "composition", "population": population,
                              "sign": -1, "fraction_removed": DROP, "sites": list(PROPOSERS)},
              "artefact_site": artefact_site, "sites": maps}
    return files, cards, record


def _write_task(task_dir: Path, files: dict[str, ad.AnnData], cards: dict[str, dict],
                var: pd.DataFrame) -> None:
    (task_dir / "sites").mkdir(parents=True)
    (task_dir / "public" / "cards").mkdir(parents=True)
    for site, adata in files.items():
        adata.write_h5ad(task_dir / "sites" / f"{site}.h5ad", compression="gzip")
        card = task_dir / "public" / "cards" / f"{site}.json"
        card.write_text(json.dumps(cards[site], indent=2))
    pd.DataFrame({"ensembl_id": var["gene_ids"].to_numpy(), "symbol": var.index.to_numpy(),
                  "alias": var["alias"].to_numpy()}).to_csv(
        task_dir / "public" / "symbols.tsv", sep="\t", index=False)
    (task_dir / "public" / "task.json").write_text(json.dumps({
        "task_id": task_dir.name, "question": QUESTION,
        "sites": [{"name": s, "data": f"sites/{s}.h5ad", "card": f"public/cards/{s}.json",
                   "proposes": s != MIMIC_SITE} for s in files],
        "symbols": "public/symbols.tsv",
    }, indent=2))


def build_suite(name: str, seeds: list[int], *, pool: str, knobs: list[tuple[bool, bool]],
                seal: Callable[[Path, dict], None]) -> list[Path]:
    """Write tasks `<name>-<seed>` under TASKS/<name>; `knobs[i]` = (artefact, ids_labels) of
    seed i. Each task's planting record (with its pool and the base's sha256) goes to
    `seal(task_dir, record)`.

    Refuses existing task directories: runs and grades are stored by task id, so a task rebuilt
    in place would pair old runs with new data. Build a new suite instead.
    """
    if pool not in ("dev", "test"):
        raise ValueError(f"pool must be 'dev' or 'test', not {pool!r}")
    if len(knobs) != len(seeds):
        raise ValueError("one (artefact, ids_labels) pair per seed")
    dirs = [TASKS / name / f"{name}-{seed:03d}" for seed in seeds]
    for task_dir in dirs:
        if task_dir.exists():
            raise FileExistsError(f"{task_dir} exists; build a new suite instead")
    frozen = json.loads(BASE.with_suffix(".json").read_text())
    base = ad.read_h5ad(BASE)
    gene_sets = core_gene_sets(load_gene_sets(HALLMARK), GO_BP)
    for task_dir, seed, (artefact, ids_labels) in zip(dirs, seeds, knobs):
        files, cards, record = plant_task(base, gene_sets, task_id=task_dir.name, seed=seed,
                                          donors=frozen[f"{pool}_donors"], artefact=artefact,
                                          ids_labels=ids_labels)
        _write_task(task_dir, files, cards, base.var)
        seal(task_dir, {**record, "pool": pool, "base_sha256": frozen["sha256"]})
    return dirs


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Case spec
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def scope_tests(claim: Claim, sites: list[SiteHandle]) -> list[tuple[SiteHandle, tuple[str, str]]]:
    """Fixed scope table: a treated-vs-control claim is tested as mimic-vs-control wherever a site
    holds that contrast for the claim's population."""
    if claim.contrast != ("treated", "control"):
        return []
    return [(s, MIMIC) for s in sites if s.name != claim.site and s.holds(claim.population, MIMIC)]


def scope_rule(claim: Claim, results: list[TestResult]) -> str:
    """`shared` if any scope test supports the claim in the mimic; `specific` if every scope test
    that could run found it absent or reversed; otherwise `inconclusive`."""
    scope = [r for r in results if r.test == "scope"]
    if any(r.status == "supported" for r in scope):
        return "shared"
    testable = [r for r in scope if r.status != "untestable"]
    if testable and all(r.status in ("absent", "contradicted") for r in testable):
        return "specific"
    return "inconclusive"


def load_case(task_dir: Path) -> tuple[CaseSpec, list[dict]]:
    """The case spec of a planted task and its site specs ({"name", "data_path", "card",
    "proposes"}); M is tester-only. Resource keys are workspace paths; gene-set libraries go
    under `genesets/`, which is how MA-genome finds the library the site agents were given."""
    task_dir = Path(task_dir)
    meta = json.loads((task_dir / "public" / "task.json").read_text())
    case = CaseSpec(name="planted", kind="scrna", vocab=CASE_VOCAB, question=meta["question"],
                    default_spec=dict(DEFAULT_SPEC), tester=TesterParams(),
                    resources={"symbols.tsv": task_dir / meta["symbols"],
                               f"genesets/{GO_BP.name}": GO_BP},
                    scope_tests=scope_tests, scope_rule=scope_rule)
    sites = [{"name": s["name"], "data_path": task_dir / s["data"],
              "card": json.loads((task_dir / s["card"]).read_text()), "proposes": s["proposes"]}
             for s in meta["sites"]]
    return case, sites


if __name__ == "__main__":  # pragma: no cover
    import sys

    # python -m agentcoop.cases.planted data/bench/onek1k/onek1k.h5ad
    meta = prepare_base(Path(sys.argv[1]))
    print(json.dumps({k: meta[k] for k in ("n_cells", "n_genes", "n_aliased", "sha256")}, indent=2))

from agentcoop.eval import planted as grader

Then append to the end of the file:

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Task generation (one small suite, built once for the module)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

SEEDS = [19, 20, 21, 22]   # KNOB_CELLS in this order; together they plant every program kind
SITES = ("A", "B", "C", "M")
ALIASES = [f"OLD{i:03d}" if i % 2 else g for i, g in enumerate(GENES)]   # base var["alias"]


def _fake_base(root: Path) -> Path:
    """40 donors x 6 populations x 30 cells x 300 genes of Poisson counts, in base layout."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(1)
    donors = [f"D{i:02d}" for i in range(40)]
    obs = pd.DataFrame([(d, p) for d in donors for p in planted.POPULATIONS for _ in range(30)],
                       columns=["donor", "population"]).astype("category")
    obs.index = obs.index.astype(str)
    X = sp.csr_matrix(rng.poisson(rng.uniform(1, 4, len(GENES)), (len(obs), len(GENES))),
                      dtype=np.int32)
    var = pd.DataFrame({"gene_ids": [f"ENSG{i:011d}" for i in range(len(GENES))],
                        "alias": ALIASES}, index=GENES)
    path = root / "base.h5ad"
    ad.AnnData(X=X, obs=obs, var=var).write_h5ad(path)
    path.with_suffix(".json").write_text(json.dumps({"dev_donors": donors, "test_donors": [],
                                                     "sha256": "f" * 64}))
    return path


def _fake_libraries(root: Path) -> tuple[Path, Path]:
    """Hallmark and GO in Enrichr text format: 12 genes per program, all annotated to the
    family's first defining GO term, so every gene is a core gene."""
    genes = iter(GENES)
    hallmark, go = [], []
    for spec in planted.FAMILIES.values():
        members = []
        for program in spec["programs"]:
            core = [next(genes) for _ in range(12)]
            hallmark.append("\t".join([program, "", *core]))
            members += core
        go.append("\t".join([f"{spec['go_terms'][0]} (GO:{len(go):07d})", "", *members]))
    (root / "hallmark.txt").write_text("\n".join(hallmark))
    (root / "go.txt").write_text("\n".join(go))
    return root / "hallmark.txt", root / "go.txt"


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def suite(tmp_path_factory):
    root = tmp_path_factory.mktemp("planted")
    with pytest.MonkeyPatch.context() as mp:
        mp.setattr(planted, "BASE", _fake_base(root))
        hallmark, go = _fake_libraries(root)
        mp.setattr(planted, "HALLMARK", hallmark)
        mp.setattr(planted, "GO_BP", go)
        mp.setattr(planted, "TASKS", root / "tasks")
        yield dict(zip(grader.KNOB_CELLS, grader.build("fx", SEEDS, pool="dev")))


def _site(task_dir: Path, site: str) -> tuple[ad.AnnData, dict]:
    return (ad.read_h5ad(task_dir / "sites" / f"{site}.h5ad"),
            json.loads((task_dir / "public" / "cards" / f"{site}.json").read_text()))


def _effect(task_dir: Path, truth: dict, site: str, cell_types: list[str],
            genes: list[str]) -> float:
    """Donor-pseudobulk log2 CPM of a gene set, target group minus control, read from the site
    file through the site's own codebook (as a correct site view would)."""
    a, _ = _site(task_dir, site)
    m = truth["sites"][site]
    if m["scheme"] != "symbol":
        table = pd.read_csv(task_dir / "public" / "symbols.tsv", sep="\t").set_index("symbol")
        genes = list(table.loc[genes, "ensembl_id" if m["scheme"] == "ensembl" else "alias"])
    cols = [a.var_names.get_loc(g) for g in genes]
    group = a.obs["condition"].astype(str).map(m["codes"]).to_numpy()
    in_pop = a.obs["cell_label"].astype(str).map(m["labels"]).isin(cell_types).to_numpy()
    scores = {"control": [], "target": []}
    for sample in a.obs["sample"].unique():
        rows = (a.obs["sample"] == sample).to_numpy() & in_pop
        X = a.X[rows]
        scores["control" if group[rows][0] == "control" else "target"].append(
            np.log2(X[:, cols].sum() / X.sum() * 1e6 + 1))
    return float(np.mean(scores["target"]) - np.mean(scores["control"]))


def test_planted_programs_show_their_sign_and_size_exactly_where_planted(suite):
    """Reproducible programs at A, B, C; site-specific and artefact programs at one site; the
    mimic site only for programs shared with the mimic."""
    shared = set()
    for task_dir in suite.values():
        truth = grader.load_truth(task_dir)
        for prog in truth["programs"]:
            sign = 1 if prog["up_genes"] else -1
            genes = prog["up_genes"] + prog["down_genes"]
            carriers = prog["sites"] + (["M"] if prog["shared_with_mimic"] else [])
            for site in SITES:
                effect = _effect(task_dir, truth, site, prog["cell_types"], genes)
                if site in carriers:
                    assert abs(sign * effect - prog["log2fc"]) < 0.4, (prog["id"], site, effect)
                else:
                    assert abs(effect) < 0.3, (prog["id"], site, effect)
            if prog["kind"] == "reproducible":
                shared.add(prog["shared_with_mimic"])
    kinds = {p["kind"] for d in suite.values() for p in grader.load_truth(d)["programs"]}
    assert shared == {True, False} and kinds == {"reproducible", "site_specific", "artefact"}


def test_composition_shift_drops_cells_in_treated_donors_of_proposing_sites_only(suite):
    task_dir = suite[(False, False)]
    truth = grader.load_truth(task_dir)
    population = truth["composition"]["population"]
    for site in SITES:
        a, _ = _site(task_dir, site)
        m = truth["sites"][site]
        obs = a.obs.astype(str)
        frac = (obs["cell_label"].map(m["labels"]) == population).groupby(obs["sample"]).mean()
        group = obs.groupby("sample")["condition"].first().map(m["codes"])
        diff = frac[group != "control"].mean() - frac[group == "control"].mean()
        assert diff < -0.03 if site != "M" else abs(diff) < 1e-9


def test_artefact_sits_at_one_proposing_site_whose_groups_differ_in_assay(suite):
    task_dir = suite[(True, False)]
    truth = grader.load_truth(task_dir)
    artefacts = [p for p in truth["programs"] if p["kind"] == "artefact"]
    assert len(artefacts) == 1 and artefacts[0]["up_genes"]
    assert artefacts[0]["sites"] == [truth["artefact_site"]] and truth["artefact_site"] in "ABC"
    for site in SITES:
        a, card = _site(task_dir, site)
        m = truth["sites"][site]
        group = a.obs["condition"].astype(str).map(m["codes"])
        assays = {g: set(a.obs["assay"].astype(str)[group == g]) for g in m["codes"].values()}
        if site == truth["artefact_site"]:
            assert assays == {"control": {"10x 3' v2"}, "treated": {"10x 3' v3"}}
        else:
            assert len(set(a.obs["assay"])) == 1
        assert card["assay_values"] == {c: sorted(assays[g]) for c, g in m["codes"].items()}
    clean = grader.load_truth(suite[(False, False)])
    assert clean["artefact_site"] is None
    assert all(p["kind"] != "artefact" for p in clean["programs"])
    pairs = [(t["artefact_site"], p["sites"][0]) for t in map(grader.load_truth, suite.values())
             for p in t["programs"] if p["kind"] == "site_specific" and t["artefact_site"]]
    assert pairs and all(a != s for a, s in pairs)      # site biology never sits at the artefact


def test_ids_labels_knob_gives_each_site_its_own_ids_names_and_codes(suite):
    task_dir = suite[(False, True)]
    truth = grader.load_truth(task_dir)
    assert sorted(truth["sites"][s]["scheme"] for s in "ABC") == sorted(planted.SCHEMES)
    table = pd.read_csv(task_dir / "public" / "symbols.tsv", sep="\t")
    assert list(table.columns) == ["ensembl_id", "symbol", "alias"]
    assert list(table["symbol"]) == GENES and list(table["alias"]) == ALIASES
    native = {"symbol": GENES, "ensembl": list(table["ensembl_id"]), "alias": ALIASES}
    for site in SITES:
        a, card = _site(task_dir, site)
        m = truth["sites"][site]
        assert list(a.var_names) == native[m["scheme"]]
        assert card["features"] == planted.FEATURES[m["scheme"]]
        assert set(a.obs["condition"]) == set(card["codes"]) == set(m["codes"])
        assert set(m["codes"]) == {"arm A", "arm B"}
        assert {card["codes"][c] for c in m["codes"]} == {planted.GROUP_TEXT[g]
                                                         for g in m["codes"].values()}
        assert set(a.obs["cell_label"]) == set(card["cell_labels"]) == set(m["labels"])
        assert sorted(m["labels"].values()) == sorted(planted.POPULATIONS)
        assert not set(m["labels"]) & set(planted.POPULATIONS)
    plain = suite[(False, False)]
    for site in SITES:
        a, _ = _site(plain, site)
        assert list(a.var_names) == GENES
        assert set(a.obs["condition"]) == {"control", "mimic" if site == "M" else "treated"}
        assert set(a.obs["cell_label"]) <= set(planted.POPULATIONS)


def test_answers_are_written_only_under_hidden(suite):
    visible = {"public/task.json", "public/symbols.tsv",
               *[f"public/cards/{s}.json" for s in SITES], *[f"sites/{s}.h5ad" for s in SITES]}
    for task_dir in suite.values():
        truth = grader.load_truth(task_dir)
        assert (truth["task_id"], truth["pool"], truth["base_sha256"]) == (task_dir.name, "dev",
                                                                          "f" * 64)
        files = {p.relative_to(task_dir).as_posix() for p in task_dir.rglob("*") if p.is_file()}
        assert files - {"hidden/truth.json"} == visible
        for path in visible:
            if path.endswith(".json"):
                text = (task_dir / path).read_text()
                for word in ('"programs"', '"seed"', '"knobs"', "shared_with_mimic",
                             "reproducible", "site_specific", "artefact"):
                    assert word not in text, (path, word)
        for site in SITES:
            a, _ = _site(task_dir, site)
            assert list(a.obs.columns) == ["sample", "condition", "cell_label", "assay"]
            assert a.var.shape[1] == 0 and not a.uns and not a.obsm and not a.layers
            assert a.obs["sample"].nunique() == 8


def test_build_refuses_an_existing_task(suite):
    with pytest.raises(FileExistsError):
        grader.build("fx", [SEEDS[0]], pool="dev")


def test_load_case_returns_the_case_spec_and_site_specs(suite):
    case, sites = planted.load_case(suite[(True, True)])
    assert (case.name, case.kind, case.vocab) == ("planted", "scrna", planted.CASE_VOCAB)
    assert case.vocab.contrasts == (("treated", "control"), ("mimic", "control"))
    assert case.default_spec == {"unit": "donor", "normalization": "CPM",
                                 "one_sample_per_donor": True,
                                 "groups": "map site codes per the codebook"}
    assert case.tester == tester.TesterParams() and case.max_claims == 10
    assert set(case.resources) == {"symbols.tsv", f"genesets/{planted.GO_BP.name}"}
    assert all(p.exists() for p in case.resources.values())
    assert case.scope_tests is planted.scope_tests and case.scope_rule is planted.scope_rule
    assert [(s["name"], s["proposes"]) for s in sites] == [("A", True), ("B", True), ("C", True),
                                                           ("M", False)]
    assert all(s["data_path"].exists() and set(s["card"]["codes"]) == {"arm A", "arm B"}
               for s in sites)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Grader
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

TRUTH = {
    "programs": [
        {"id": "P1", "kind": "reproducible", "cell_types": ["NK", "CD8 T"], "up_genes": GENES[0:10],
         "down_genes": [], "sites": ["A", "B", "C"], "shared_with_mimic": True},
        {"id": "P2", "kind": "reproducible", "cell_types": ["B"], "up_genes": [],
         "down_genes": GENES[10:20], "sites": ["A", "B", "C"], "shared_with_mimic": False},
        {"id": "P3", "kind": "site_specific", "cell_types": ["CD14 Mono"],
         "up_genes": GENES[20:30], "down_genes": [], "sites": ["B"], "shared_with_mimic": False},
        {"id": "P4", "kind": "artefact", "cell_types": ["CD4 T"], "up_genes": GENES[30:40],
         "down_genes": [], "sites": ["C"], "shared_with_mimic": False},
    ],
    "composition": {"id": "C1", "kind": "composition", "population": "CD16 Mono", "sign": -1,
                    "fraction_removed": 0.4, "sites": ["A", "B", "C"]},
    "artefact_site": "C",
}


def test_an_exact_claim_is_precise_and_recalls_one_of_four_items():
    g = grader.grade([_claim(up=GENES[0:10])], TRUTH)
    assert g == {"precision": 1.0, "recall": 0.25, "f1": pytest.approx(0.4), "n_claims": 1,
                 "artefact_rejected": True, "scope_accuracy": None, "cause_accuracy": None}


def test_partial_overlap_matches_from_jaccard_point_two():
    hit = _claim("c1", up=GENES[0:3] + GENES[50:52])      # 3 / 12 = 0.25
    miss = _claim("c2", up=GENES[0:2] + GENES[50:53])     # 2 / 13 = 0.15
    assert grader.match(hit, TRUTH)["id"] == "P1" and grader.match(miss, TRUTH) is None
    assert grader.grade([hit, miss], TRUTH)["precision"] == 0.5


def test_wrong_direction_population_or_contrast_never_matches():
    claims = [_claim("c1", down=GENES[0:10]), _claim("c2", population="B", up=GENES[0:10]),
              _claim("c3", target="mimic", up=GENES[0:10]), _claim("c4", population="B",
                                                                    up=GENES[10:20])]
    g = grader.grade(claims, TRUTH)
    assert (g["precision"], g["recall"], g["f1"]) == (0.0, 0.0, 0.0)


def test_composition_claims_match_by_population_and_sign():
    def comp(population: str, effect: float | None) -> Claim:
        return _claim(population=population, kind="composition", effect=effect)

    assert grader.match(comp("CD16 Mono", -0.4), TRUTH)["id"] == "C1"
    for claim in (comp("CD16 Mono", 0.4), comp("CD16 Mono", None), comp("NK", -0.4)):
        assert grader.match(claim, TRUTH) is None


def test_no_claims_has_no_precision_and_zero_recall():
    assert grader.grade([], TRUTH) == {"precision": None, "recall": 0.0, "f1": 0.0, "n_claims": 0,
                                       "artefact_rejected": True, "scope_accuracy": None,
                                       "cause_accuracy": None}


def test_artefact_counts_as_claimed_only_when_it_is_a_finding():
    art = _claim("a", population="CD4 T", up=GENES[30:40])
    g = grader.grade([art], TRUTH)
    assert g["artefact_rejected"] is False and g["precision"] == 0.0
    assert grader.grade([art], TRUTH, labels={"a": "site-specific"})["artefact_rejected"] is True
    no_artefact = {**TRUTH, "programs": TRUTH["programs"][:3], "artefact_site": None}
    assert grader.grade([art], no_artefact)["artefact_rejected"] is None


def test_a_claim_counts_once_for_its_best_match():
    truth = {**TRUTH, "programs": [
        {**TRUTH["programs"][0], "cell_types": ["NK"]},
        {**TRUTH["programs"][2], "cell_types": ["NK"]}]}
    claim = _claim(up=GENES[0:4] + GENES[20:28])           # P1: 4/18, P3: 8/14
    assert grader.match(claim, truth)["id"] == "P3"
    assert grader.grade([claim], truth)["precision"] == 0.0


def test_scope_accuracy_is_over_findings_matching_reproducible_programs():
    claims = [_claim("s1", up=GENES[0:10]), _claim("s2", population="B", down=GENES[10:20]),
              _claim("s3", population="CD8 T", up=GENES[0:10]), _claim("s4", up=GENES[0:10])]
    scopes = {"s1": "shared", "s2": "shared", "s3": "inconclusive", "s4": "specific"}
    g = grader.grade(claims, TRUTH, scopes=scopes, labels={"s4": "unsupported"})
    assert g["scope_accuracy"] == 0.5 and g["n_claims"] == 3


def test_cause_accuracy_needs_the_planted_category_and_site():
    claims = [_claim("k1", population="CD4 T", up=GENES[30:40]),
              _claim("k2", population="CD14 Mono", up=GENES[20:30]),
              _claim("k3", population="CD14 Mono", up=GENES[20:30]),
              _claim("k4", up=GENES[0:10])]
    causes = {"k1": {"cause": "site-artefact", "site": "C", "defect": "assay differs"},
              "k2": {"cause": "site-artefact", "site": "B", "defect": ""},
              "k3": {"cause": "site-biology", "site": "A", "defect": ""},
              "k4": {"cause": "handoff-loss", "site": "B", "defect": "gene ids"}}
    labels = {"k1": "site-specific", "k2": "site-specific", "k3": "site-specific",
              "k4": "corroborated"}
    g = grader.grade(claims, TRUTH, labels=labels, causes=causes)
    assert g["cause_accuracy"] == pytest.approx(1 / 3) and g["n_claims"] == 1
    assert grader.grade(claims, TRUTH)["cause_accuracy"] is None
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement the answer key and grader**

Create `agentcoop/eval/__init__.py`, overwriting any v3 file:

```python
"""Answer keys and graders. Only the evaluation harness imports this package (spec §8)."""
```

Create `agentcoop/eval/planted.py`:

```python
"""Answer key and grader of the planted suite (spec §7). Only the evaluation harness imports this.

Suites are built here because this side owns the answer path (spec §8): the generator in
`agentcoop.cases.planted` hands each task's planting record to `write_truth`.

A DE claim matches a planted program when its population is one of the program's cell types,
its contrast is (treated, control) and its signed genes overlap the program's with Jaccard >= 0.2
(frozen); a composition claim matches the composition shift by population and sign. A claim that
matches several planted items counts once, for its best match.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

from agentcoop.cases.planted import build_suite
from agentcoop.coop.claims import Claim

MIN_JACCARD = 0.2
#: The 2 x 2 knob cells (artefact, ids_labels); `build` cycles through them per seed.
KNOB_CELLS = ((False, False), (True, False), (False, True), (True, True))
#: What a correct cause record says about a claim that matches a non-reproducible program.
EXPECTED_CAUSE = {"artefact": "site-artefact", "site_specific": "site-biology"}


def write_truth(task_dir: Path, truth: dict) -> None:
    hidden = Path(task_dir) / "hidden"
    hidden.mkdir()
    (hidden / "truth.json").write_text(json.dumps(truth, indent=2))


def build(name: str, seeds: list[int], *, pool: str) -> list[Path]:
    """A suite balanced over the knob cells: seed i gets KNOB_CELLS[i % 4]."""
    knobs = [KNOB_CELLS[i % len(KNOB_CELLS)] for i in range(len(seeds))]
    return build_suite(name, seeds, pool=pool, knobs=knobs, seal=write_truth)


def load_truth(task_dir: Path) -> dict:
    return json.loads((Path(task_dir) / "hidden" / "truth.json").read_text())


def _signed(up: list[str] | tuple[str, ...], down: list[str] | tuple[str, ...]) -> frozenset:
    return frozenset({(g.upper(), 1) for g in up} | {(g.upper(), -1) for g in down})


def match(claim: Claim, truth: dict) -> dict | None:
    """The planted record (program or composition shift) a claim best matches, or None."""
    if claim.contrast != ("treated", "control"):
        return None
    if claim.kind == "composition":
        shift = truth["composition"]
        sign = 0 if claim.effect is None else (claim.effect > 0) - (claim.effect < 0)
        return shift if claim.population == shift["population"] and sign == shift["sign"] else None
    genes = _signed(claim.up_genes, claim.down_genes)
    best, best_j = None, 0.0
    for prog in truth["programs"]:
        if claim.population in prog["cell_types"]:
            planted = _signed(prog["up_genes"], prog["down_genes"])
            j = len(genes & planted) / len(genes | planted)
            if j >= MIN_JACCARD and j > best_j:
                best, best_j = prog, j
    return best


def items(truth: dict) -> set[tuple[str, str]]:
    """What recall is over: (program id, cell type) of every reproducible program, plus the
    composition shift."""
    out = {(p["id"], ct) for p in truth["programs"] if p["kind"] == "reproducible"
           for ct in p["cell_types"]}
    out.add((truth["composition"]["id"], truth["composition"]["population"]))
    return out


def _mean(values: list[bool]) -> float | None:
    return sum(values) / len(values) if values else None


def grade(claims: list[Claim], truth: dict, *, labels: dict[str, str] | None = None,
          causes: dict[str, dict] | None = None, scopes: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> dict:
    """Grade one arm's claims on one task.

    `claims` are the claims the arm reports. With `labels`, its findings are the claims labelled
    `corroborated` (an unlabelled claim counts as a finding); without, every claim is a finding.
    Precision, recall, F1, artefact rejection and scope accuracy are over findings. Cause accuracy
    is over every claim with a recorded cause (causes mostly explain demoted claims); a cause is
    correct when both its category and its site match the planted record.
    """
    labels, causes, scopes = labels or {}, causes or {}, scopes or {}
    best = {c.id: match(c, truth) for c in claims}
    findings = [c for c in claims if labels.get(c.id, "corroborated") == "corroborated"]
    wanted = items(truth)
    item_of = {c.id: (best[c.id]["id"], c.population) for c in claims if best[c.id] is not None}
    hits = [c for c in findings if item_of.get(c.id) in wanted]
    precision = len(hits) / len(findings) if findings else None
    recall = len({item_of[c.id] for c in hits}) / len(wanted)
    f1 = 2 * precision * recall / (precision + recall) if precision and recall else 0.0
    artefact = next((p["id"] for p in truth["programs"] if p["kind"] == "artefact"), None)
    rejected = (None if artefact is None
                else not any(best[c.id] is not None and best[c.id]["id"] == artefact
                             for c in findings))
    scoped = [(scopes[c.id] == "shared") == best[c.id]["shared_with_mimic"] for c in findings
              if best[c.id] is not None and best[c.id]["kind"] == "reproducible"
              and scopes.get(c.id) in ("shared", "specific")]
    caused = [causes[c.id].get("cause") == EXPECTED_CAUSE[best[c.id]["kind"]]
              and causes[c.id].get("site") == best[c.id]["sites"][0] for c in claims
              if causes.get(c.id) and best[c.id] is not None
              and best[c.id]["kind"] in EXPECTED_CAUSE]
    return {"precision": precision, "recall": recall, "f1": f1, "n_claims": len(findings),
            "artefact_rejected": rejected, "scope_accuracy": _mean(scoped),
            "cause_accuracy": _mean(caused)}


if __name__ == "__main__":  # pragma: no cover
    import sys

    # python -m agentcoop.eval.planted fsdev dev 800 808   (suite, donor pool, seeds [lo, hi))
    suite, pool, lo, hi = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], int(sys.argv[3]), int(sys.argv[4])
    for path in build(suite, list(range(lo, hi)), pool=pool):
        print(path, flush=True)
```

- [ ] **Step 11: Record the freeze and its design reasons in `findings.md`**

Append to `findings.md` (use the hashes printed in Step 10):

```markdown
### v4 Case 3 base (OneK1K, frozen 2026-10-14)
- `data/bench/onek1k/base.h5ad` = `python -m agentcoop.cases.planted data/bench/onek1k/onek1k.h5ad`
  (seed 0): 80 of the 205 OneK1K donors that have >= 1,000 cells in the six case populations and
  >= 20 cells in each; 40 dev (fsdev) / 40 test (fstest) donors, disjoint (lists in base.json);
  <= 1,500 cells per donor, stratified by population; 105,091 cells x 26,296 genes (genes with an
  ambiguous symbol or no counts dropped, so symbol <-> Ensembl is a bijection for the ID knob).
  sha256 437758b247c74dff3718407982d33b0ef9692026c86f6553c0ea0ebed08917d6.
- Alias gene-ID scheme (spec §7 lists symbol / Ensembl / alias): each gene's first previous HGNC
  symbol, else its first alias symbol, is used if HGNC lists that name for one gene only and it is
  no approved or base symbol; otherwise the gene keeps its symbol. Source: HGNC complete set
  2026-10-06 (dated archive, sha256 8bf6f686e640dbc864a346484626953621b0500d900d26cd085a35c2782d725d),
  frozen into base var["alias"]; 16,244 of 26,296 genes get a legacy name (e.g. ATP5F1D -> ATP5D).
  Reason for previous symbols first: outdated official symbols are what real legacy annotations
  carry. Reason for the uniqueness rules: symbol <-> alias stays a bijection, so a correct view
  exists and view errors are gradable.
- Under ids_labels, A/B/C get the three schemes in random order (M draws one). Reason: each
  scheme is then measured at the same number of sites in the §6 view-error rate.
- Resource keys: gene-set libraries go under `genesets/` in CaseSpec.resources
  (`genesets/GO_Biological_Process_2023.txt`), which is where MA-genome's `gene_set_library` reads
  them; the symbol table is `symbols.tsv` (ensembl_id, symbol, alias).
- Reason for the 20-cell floor in subsampling: the median OneK1K donor has 20 CD14 and 10 CD16
  monocytes; proportional subsampling alone would push rare populations under the tester's
  20-cell minimum (TesterParams.min_cells) and make them untestable by construction.
- Reason one-site programs never share a site: at the assay-confounded artefact site an effect seen
  only there cannot be told apart from the artefact, so its cause (site-biology vs site-artefact)
  would be ungradable.
- Answers: `cases/planted.py` hands each planting record to a `seal` callback; only
  `eval/planted.py` writes hidden/truth.json (spec §8; build suites with
  `python -m agentcoop.eval.planted <suite> <pool> <lo> <hi>`, knobs balanced 2 x 2 by seed order).
```

- Create: `tests/fakes.py` (shared offline fakes; Task 6/7 tests may import it, their files are not changed here)
- Create: `tests/test_arms.py`
- Uses, unchanged: `tests/__init__.py` (makes `from tests.fakes import ...` importable; Step 1 checks it exists)

**Interfaces:**

Consumes (contracts, exact names):
- `store.memo(key: dict, compute: Callable[[], dict], *, tag: str) -> dict`
- `agents.workspace.new_workspace(name: str, files: dict[str, Path]) -> Path`
- `agents.harness.AgentRun`; `agents.harness.run_agent(agent: str, ws: Path, prompt: str, *, sample: int, timeout: int) -> AgentRun` (called as `harness.run_agent` so tests can monkeypatch it)
- `coop.claims.Claim`, `Vocabulary`, `parse_claims(raw, *, site, vocab, max_claims) -> tuple[list[Claim], list[str]]`, `claim_to_dict(c) -> dict`, `same_claim(a, b, min_jaccard=0.2) -> bool`
- `coop.tester.TesterParams`, `TestResult`, `apply_bh(results, params) -> list[TestResult]`
- `coop.protocol.SiteHandle`, `ClaimRecord` (including Task 6's published addition `results_step3: list[TestResult]`, the same layout as `results` but from before step 4), `ProtocolRun` (`records`, `followups`, `calls`, `nominal_usd`, `findings()`, `findings_step3()`), `Coordinator(case)`, `run_protocol(sites, case, *, sample, followup_budget, coordinator=None) -> ProtocolRun`
- `coop.site.CaseSpec`, `make_sites(site_specs, case, *, task_id, run_id, agents=None) -> list[Site]`, `file_digest(path) -> str`, `serve_mailbox(ws, handlers, stop) -> threading.Thread`
- `llm.chat_json(messages, *, sample, ...) -> tuple[Any, ChatResult]`, `llm.ChatResult`, `llm.current_tag()`, `llm.exact_tag(label)`, `llm.tag(label)`, `llm.spent(prefix)`; the fakes also use `llm.record_replay(model, nominal_usd)` and `llm.backbone()` (all already exist in `agentcoop/llm.py`)

Produces (contract):
- `ArmResult` (fields in contract order), `run_arms(arms: list[str], site_specs: list[dict], case: CaseSpec, *, task_id: str, sample: int) -> dict[str, ArmResult]`, `ARMS: tuple[str, ...]`. "single" and "single_codex" yield `single:<site>` / `single_codex:<site>` per proposing site.

Produces (additional public names, for Task 10 and the MA-tools reference of the case plans):
- `fixed_effect(stats: pd.DataFrame, key: str = "gene", *, min_sites: int = 2) -> pd.DataFrame` (columns key, effect, se, z, p, fdr, Q, Q_p, n_sites, n_agree)
- `gene_set_library(case: CaseSpec) -> dict[str, frozenset[str]]`, `enrich(genes: set[str], universe: set[str], library: dict[str, frozenset[str]]) -> list[dict]`
- `ma_genome(sites: list[SiteHandle], case: CaseSpec, arm: str, *, min_sites: int = 2) -> tuple[list[Claim], dict]`. The arms use the default. Task 10's headroom gate calls it with `min_sites=1` on one site as its scripted single-site analysis, so the gate and the arms share one implementation. This signature is in the plan header's contract block and Task 10 must not change it.
- `mailbox_handlers(sites, case, budget, sample, log, tag) -> dict[str, Callable[[dict], dict]]` (`"ask_site"`, `"test_claim"`), `Budget`, `AgentFailed`, `ASK_SITE`, `TEST_CLAIM`, `CLAIM_SCHEMA`
- `GENESET_PREFIX = "genesets/"`; `MA_SELF_CHECKS: int | None = None` (set once at `v4-freeze`, see the design decisions)
- `ArmResult.meta` keys read downstream:
  - ours: `meta["all_claims"]`, one entry per protocol record, demoted claims included: `{"claim": claim_to_dict(record.claim), "label": record.label, "cause": record.cause, "scope": record.scope}`. Also `meta["errors"]` = `run.errors` (failed site-agent calls) and `meta["followups"]`. Task 10 grades ours on the claims of `all_claims` (via `claim_from_dict`) with the labels, causes and scopes carried there, so cause accuracy is computable; precision and recall still use the findings in `claims`. Otherwise cause accuracy sees only corroborated claims, while causes mostly explain demoted ones.
  - no_resolution: `meta["all_claims"]` in the same layout with the step-3 labels (`record.label_step3`, cause always None), and `meta["errors"]`.
  - oracle: `meta["pooled_input"] == "site files"`.
  - ma_genome: `meta["tables"]` and `meta["self_checked"]`.
  - ma_claims: `meta["z"]`.
  - a failed lead-agent run: `meta["error"]`.
- `tests/fakes.py`: `FakeSite` (full `SiteHandle`, with a `checks` counter of self-check calls), `SiteFactory` (a `make_sites` stand-in), `FakeMemo` (ledger-based like `store.memo`: nominal = ledger spend under the compute's tag + `nominal_usd_external`), `charge_ledger(usd)` (a fake agent's usage, ledgered as `run_codex` does), `fake_workspace`, `case_spec`, `claim`, `result`, `gene_stats`, `program_of`, `standard_sites`, `site_specs`, `VOCAB`, `TREATED`, `MIMIC`, `PROGRAMS`, `IFN_A`, `HB_A`, `IFN_B`, `NOISE_B`, `CCL_C`

What this task relies on from other tasks (the implementer checks each against Tasks 2-8 as merged; the drafts of Tasks 3, 5, 6 and 7 were checked):
1. `parse_claims` accepts both a `{"claims": [...]}` object and `claim_to_dict` output, stamps `site` on each claim, and gives ids `<site>:<k>` (Task 3). So ids are unique across sites within one run, which matters because arms key labels and the test matrix by id.
2. `TestResult.effect` is oriented so that positive means the claimed direction, and it is centred on 0 for every kind (Task 5):
   - de: module score, up minus down;
   - composition: logit change times the sign of `claim.effect` (a missing effect counts as an increase);
   - bulk: AUC − 0.5.
   Arms therefore use z = effect / se, with no sign or null correction. MA-genome's composition probe has effect +1, so the effects it reads are target minus reference.
3. Site counters and failures (Task 7):
   - `Site.propose` of a tester-only site builds the view and returns `[]`.
   - `Site.calls` and `Site.nominal_usd` grow on every agent call, store replays and failures included (nominal semantics).
   - `Site.ask` is memoized by (request, sample) and never raises: a failed call returns a text.
4. The `serve_mailbox` protocol (Task 7):
   - The client writes `req_<id>.json` atomically in the workspace root. Its temporary name, `.req_<id>.tmp`, is outside the server's `req_*.json` glob.
   - The server passes the whole `{"op": <handler name>, ...}` dict to the handler, on the mailbox thread. It writes `resp_<id>.json` atomically and deletes the request.
   - The client prints the response and deletes it.
   `test_mailbox_clients_speak_serve_mailbox` pins this against the real `serve_mailbox`. If it fails, change `_client` in `arms.py`, not Task 7's server.
5. Gene-set libraries are the `case.resources` entries whose key starts with `genesets/` (the header's resource-key convention), in Enrichr text or GMT layout. Task 8's `load_case` keys GO BP as `genesets/GO_Biological_Process_2023.txt` next to `symbols.tsv`. The COVID and TB case plans must key theirs the same way. Anything else that needs the library (Task 10's headroom gate) calls `gene_set_library(case)`.
6. Codex cost and workspaces (Tasks 2 and 7):
   - `run_codex` ledgers Codex usage exactly once, through `llm.record_external(..., billed=False)` under the caller's tag (`run_agent` carries the caller's context onto its worker thread). Inside a memo compute that tag is the memo's, so `store.memo`'s `nominal_usd` already includes the lead agent's cost. `AgentRun.nominal_usd_external` repeats that amount: arms keep it only as `agent_nominal_usd` for inspection and never return it as `nominal_usd_external` or add it to a charge (Task 7's rule).
   - `new_workspace` may reuse the directory of an existing name, so arms delete a stale `claims.json` before each run.
7. Task 6's `ClaimRecord.results_step3` holds the proposer gate, the replication tests and the scope tests from before step 4 (Task 6 adds it for `findings_step3`). MA-claims reads it.

Design decisions pinned by the tests:
- **One shared step 1.** There is one S0 site set per `run_arms` call (`<task>/base/s<sample>`), with one `propose` per site. Its claim x site matrix (proposer gate, then BH-adjusted replication tests on the same memoized views) is computed before any arm asks a site anything. It feeds single, union, vote, no-routing and the compositional arms. Review focus 4 is pinned here by `test_single_union_vote_and_no_routing_share_one_step1`; across site sets it rests on Task 7's propose memo key.
- **Mechanism arms read ours' own `ProtocolRun`:**
  - no-resolution uses its step-3 labels;
  - MA-claims uses the non-proposer replication tests in `results_step3`: Stouffer of z = effect / se, BH over claims, kept when FDR < 0.05 and the pooled z > 0;
  - MA-genome-repaired runs on ours' final views.
  The decomposition is therefore paired with ours even when a step-1 call failed in one site set and not in another (failures are never cached).
  Costs: no-resolution and MA-claims are charged ours' step 1, taken from the S0 entries of the same memoized calls; MA-genome-repaired is charged ours' whole run.
  Pinned by `test_ma_claims_stouffer_over_ours_step1_tests`, which includes a decreasing composition claim that a second sign flip would reject, and by `test_ours_and_mechanism_arms_read_one_protocol_run`.
- **Ours reports every claim.** `meta["all_claims"]` lists every claim the protocol labelled, each with its label, cause and scope; findings stay in `claims`. `meta["errors"]` copies `run.errors`. No-resolution carries the same list with its step-3 labels. Pinned by `test_ours_and_mechanism_arms_read_one_protocol_run`, with one promoted claim (HB_a, handoff loss) and one demoted claim (NOISE_b, site artefact).
- **Budget.** B = 2S counts the S step-1 calls, so S budgeted `ask_site` calls remain (the same as ours' F = S). `test_claim` is deterministic and costs no call, like ours' tests.
- **Cost accounting.**
  - `calls` counts external-agent runs: site agents, the Codex coordinator and the oracle. Plain LLM turns (prior-only, the group-chat coordinator) are charged in `nominal_usd` only.
  - Shared step-1 calls are charged to every arm that uses them: proposing sites only for single, union, vote and no-routing; every site for the arms that use the matrix.
- **Claim cap.** Single-output arms (prior-only, oracle, codex_coord, group_chat) may emit up to K x S claims, which is what the federation can propose.
- **Failed lead-agent runs.** A failed run of the oracle or codex_coord raises `AgentFailed` inside `compute`. It is charged what the ledger holds under that call's tag (as Task 7's `Site` charges a failure) plus the cost `AgentFailed` reports from outside that tag (codex_coord's asks), returned as an empty `ArmResult` with `meta["error"]`, and never memoized (review focus 3). Pinned by `test_failed_oracle_run_is_charged_but_not_cached`, whose fake agent ledgers its usage as `run_codex` does, so the exact cost also pins that the run is charged once.
- **Mailbox thread spend.** Mailbox handlers run on the mailbox thread, outside the memo's ledger tag. The coordinator's own Codex usage is already in the memo's `nominal_usd` (ledgered by `run_codex`), so codex_coord's `nominal_usd_external` is only the asks' site-counter deltas (`calls` / `nominal_usd`); `AgentRun.nominal_usd_external` is never added. Nothing is counted twice, and a replay returns the same total. Pinned by `test_codex_coord_tools_enforce_the_budget_and_replay`.
- **MA-genome's single-site mode.** `ma_genome(..., min_sites=1)` (and `fixed_effect(..., min_sites=1)`) on one site pools nothing: the "meta" estimate is the site's own, Q_p is undefined, and genes are kept on FDR < 0.05 alone. The arms never use it; it exists so that Task 10's headroom gate reuses this code instead of a second implementation. Pinned by `test_ma_genome_min_sites_one_is_the_single_site_analysis`.
- **MA-genome self-check count.** Spec §4 gives one self-check call per site, with the number of calls equal to ours' median follow-up count on `fsdev`, frozen. `MA_SELF_CHECKS` is that count. That many distinct sites get one self-check each, chosen in site order from a start fixed by sha256(task id), which knows nothing about which view is wrong and balances over tasks. Before the freeze it is `None`, which checks every site. Pinned by `test_ma_genome_self_checks_the_frozen_number_of_sites`.
- **Pooled oracle: a deliberate deviation from §4.** §4 specifies one Codex agent "with all site views concatenated by the case script". No case script builds such a pool (Task 8 writes per-site files only), and arms cannot concatenate the sites correctly without the case's ID and label maps.
  - What it does instead: the oracle gets every site's data file and card plus the case resources, and harmonizes them itself.
  - The bias: this is a weaker reference than a pre-harmonized pool, so silo-cost recovery measured against it favours ours.
  - How it is marked: `meta["pooled_input"] = "site files"`, and the analysis must report it so.
  Switch to the pooled input once a case script provides one.

```python
"""Offline stand-ins shared by the coop tests: scripted sites, a make_sites factory, a memo store
and a workspace maker. No agent, network or API key involved."""

from __future__ import annotations

import copy
import json
import shutil
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Callable

import pandas as pd
from scipy.stats import norm

from agentcoop import llm
from agentcoop.coop.claims import Claim, Vocabulary
from agentcoop.coop.site import CaseSpec
from agentcoop.coop.tester import TesterParams, TestResult

VOCAB = Vocabulary(populations=("CD14 Mono", "B"), groups=("control", "treated", "mimic"),
                   contrasts=(("treated", "control"), ("mimic", "control")))
TREATED = ("treated", "control")
MIMIC = ("mimic", "control")
PROGRAMS = {
    "IFN": frozenset({"IFI6", "ISG15", "MX1", "OAS1", "IFIT1", "IFIT3"}),
    "HB": frozenset({"HBA1", "HBA2", "HBB", "HBD"}),
    "CCL": frozenset({"CCL3", "CCL4", "CXCL8", "IL1B"}),
    "NOISE": frozenset({"ACTB", "GAPDH", "B2M"}),
}
STATS_COLUMNS = ["gene", "effect", "se", "n1", "n2"]


def case_spec(**overrides) -> CaseSpec:
    fields = dict(name="fake", kind="scrna", vocab=VOCAB,
                  question="Which cell-type programs change with treatment?",
                  default_spec={"unit": "donor"}, tester=TesterParams(), max_claims=10)
    return CaseSpec(**{**fields, **overrides})


def claim(cid: str, site: str, up: tuple[str, ...] = (), *, down: tuple[str, ...] = (),
          kind: str = "de", population: str = "CD14 Mono", contrast: tuple[str, str] = TREATED,
          effect: float = 1.0, rank: int = 1) -> Claim:
    return Claim(id=cid, site=site, kind=kind, population=population, target=contrast[0],
                 reference=contrast[1], up_genes=tuple(up), down_genes=tuple(down), effect=effect,
                 n_units=8, rank=rank)


def program_of(c: Claim) -> str | None:
    """The planted program a claim is about: its population for composition, else the program
    sharing most genes with it."""
    if c.kind == "composition":
        return f"comp:{c.population}"
    genes = set(c.up_genes) | set(c.down_genes)
    best = max(PROGRAMS, key=lambda p: len(PROGRAMS[p] & genes))
    return best if PROGRAMS[best] & genes else None


def result(c: Claim, site: str, raw: float | None, se: float | None, *,
           test: str = "replication") -> TestResult:
    """A test result in Task 5's convention from a raw effect (target minus reference) and its
    SE: ``effect`` is oriented so that positive = the claimed direction (a composition claim with
    a negative effect flips it), centred on 0; supported or contradicted when |z| >= 1.96 with or
    against the claim; untestable without data."""
    if raw is None or se is None:
        return TestResult(claim_id=c.id, site=site, test=test, status="untestable", effect=None,
                          se=None, ci95=None, n_per_group=None, mde=None, p=None,
                          competitive_ok=None, reason="fake: not measured")
    effect = -raw if c.kind == "composition" and (c.effect or 0.0) < 0 else raw
    z = effect / se
    status = "supported" if z >= 1.96 else "contradicted" if z <= -1.96 else "inconclusive"
    return TestResult(claim_id=c.id, site=site, test=test, status=status, effect=effect, se=se,
                      ci95=(effect - 1.96 * se, effect + 1.96 * se), n_per_group=(4, 4), mde=None,
                      p=float(2 * norm.sf(abs(z))), competitive_ok=True, reason="fake")


def gene_stats(effects: dict[str, tuple[float, float]],
               n: tuple[int, int] = (4, 4)) -> pd.DataFrame:
    return pd.DataFrame([{"gene": g, "effect": e, "se": s, "n1": n[0], "n2": n[1]}
                         for g, (e, s) in effects.items()], columns=STATS_COLUMNS)


@dataclass
class FakeSite:
    """A SiteHandle with scripted claims, per-program effects, gene stats and replies. Every
    agent call (propose, repair, self_check, ask) costs ``usd`` nominal, like a real Site."""
    name: str
    claims: list[Claim] = field(default_factory=list)
    effects: dict[str, tuple[float, float]] = field(default_factory=dict)  # program -> (raw, se)
    held: set = field(default_factory=lambda: {(p, TREATED) for p in VOCAB.populations})
    stats: dict = field(default_factory=dict)          # (population, contrast) -> gene stats
    replies: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)   # ask() answers in order, then "ok"
    proposes: bool = True
    agent: str = "codex"
    usd: float = 0.01
    calls: int = 0
    nominal_usd: float = 0.0
    asked: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    exported: list = field(default_factory=list)
    checks: int = 0

    def _call(self) -> None:
        self.calls += 1
        self.nominal_usd += self.usd

    def propose(self, sample: int) -> list[Claim]:
        self._call()
        return list(self.claims) if self.proposes else []

    def card(self) -> dict:
        return {"site": self.name}

    def spec(self) -> dict:
        return {"unit": "donor"}

    def holds(self, population: str, contrast: tuple[str, str]) -> bool:
        return (population, tuple(contrast)) in self.held

    def test(self, claim: Claim, *, test: str = "replication",
             contrast: tuple[str, str] | None = None) -> TestResult:
        program = program_of(claim)
        if program not in self.effects or not self.holds(claim.population, claim.contrast):
            return result(claim, self.name, None, None, test=test)
        return result(claim, self.name, *self.effects[program], test=test)

    def repair(self, claim: Claim, defect: str, sample: int) -> bool:
        self._call()
        return True

    def self_check(self, sample: int) -> bool:
        self._call()
        self.checks += 1
        return True

    def export_stats(self, population: str, contrast: tuple[str, str]) -> pd.DataFrame:
        self.exported.append((population, tuple(contrast)))
        return self.stats.get((population, tuple(contrast)), gene_stats({}))

    def ask(self, request: str, sample: int) -> str:
        self._call()
        self.asked.append(request)
        return self.replies.pop(0) if self.replies else "ok"


class SiteFactory:
    """make_sites stand-in: fresh FakeSites per call from ``templates`` (site name -> FakeSite
    kwargs), with ``proposes`` and the agent taken from the site spec unless ``agents`` overrides
    it; ``made`` logs (run_id, sites) per call."""

    def __init__(self, templates: dict[str, dict]) -> None:
        self.templates = templates
        self.made: list[tuple[str, list[FakeSite]]] = []

    def __call__(self, site_specs: list[dict], case: CaseSpec, *, task_id: str, run_id: str,
                 agents: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> list[FakeSite]:
        sites = [FakeSite(**{**copy.deepcopy(self.templates[s["name"]]), "name": s["name"],
                             "proposes": s["proposes"],
                             "agent": (agents or {}).get(s["name"], s["agent"])})
                 for s in site_specs]
        self.made.append((run_id, sites))
        return sites

    def sites(self, run_id: str) -> list[FakeSite]:
        return next(sites for rid, sites in self.made if rid == run_id)


def charge_ledger(usd: float) -> None:
    """Ledger ``usd`` nominal ($0 actual) under the current tag: what run_codex does for a Codex
    run's usage (llm.record_external(..., billed=False)), without pricing tokens. The autouse
    conftest fixture keeps the ledger in tmp_path."""
    llm.record_replay(llm.backbone(), usd)


class FakeMemo:
    """store.memo stand-in: first writer wins, exceptions are never cached (their spend stays in
    the ledger), and nominal_usd is what store.memo stores: the ledger spend under the compute's
    own nested tag plus any nominal_usd_external the compute reports."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.entries: dict[str, dict] = {}
        self.computes = 0

    def __call__(self, key: dict, compute: Callable[[], dict], *, tag: str) -> dict:
        k = json.dumps(key, sort_keys=True, default=str)
        if k not in self.entries:
            self.computes += 1
            with llm.tag(f"{tag}#{self.computes}.{time.time_ns()}"):
                exec_tag = llm.current_tag()
                out = compute()
            nominal = llm.spent(exec_tag)["nominal_usd"] + out.get("nominal_usd_external", 0.0)
            self.entries.setdefault(k, {**out, "nominal_usd": nominal})
        return dict(self.entries[k])


def fake_workspace(root: Path) -> Callable[[str, dict[str, Path]], Path]:
    """new_workspace stand-in: plain copies under ``root`` (a reused name keeps its directory)."""
    def new_workspace(name: str, files: dict[str, Path]) -> Path:
        ws = root / name
        ws.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        for rel, src in files.items():
            (ws / rel).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            shutil.copy(src, ws / rel)
        return ws
    return new_workspace


# The standard federation: A, B, C propose; M is a tester-only mimic site.
IFN_A = claim("IFN_a", "A", ("IFI6", "ISG15", "MX1"))
HB_A = claim("HB_a", "A", ("HBA1", "HBB", "HBD"), rank=2)
IFN_B = claim("IFN_b", "B", ("ISG15", "MX1", "OAS1"))
NOISE_B = claim("NOISE_b", "B", ("ACTB", "GAPDH", "B2M"), rank=2)
CCL_C = claim("CCL_c", "C", ("CCL3", "CCL4", "CXCL8"))


def standard_sites() -> dict[str, dict]:
    """Own-tester z: IFN_a 5, HB_a 3, IFN_b 2.5, NOISE_b 0.2 (fails), CCL_c 6."""
    return {
        "A": {"claims": [IFN_A, HB_A],
              "effects": {"IFN": (1.0, 0.2), "HB": (0.6, 0.2), "CCL": (0.8, 0.2),
                          "NOISE": (0.0, 0.5)}},
        "B": {"claims": [IFN_B, NOISE_B],
              "effects": {"IFN": (0.75, 0.3), "HB": (0.1, 0.2), "CCL": (0.8, 0.2),
                          "NOISE": (0.1, 0.5)}},
        "C": {"claims": [CCL_C],
              "effects": {"IFN": (0.45, 0.3), "HB": (-0.04, 0.2), "CCL": (1.2, 0.2),
                          "NOISE": (0.0, 0.5)}},
        "M": {"held": {(p, MIMIC) for p in VOCAB.populations}},
    }


def site_specs(root: Path) -> list[dict]:
    specs = []
    for name, agent in (("A", "codex"), ("B", "finch"), ("C", "codex"), ("M", "finch")):
        path = root / "data" / f"{name}.h5ad"
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        path.write_text(f"site {name} counts")
        specs.append({"name": name, "data_path": path, "card": {"site": name},
                      "proposes": name != "M", "agent": agent})
    return specs
```

"""Arms of spec §4 on scripted sites: selection semantics, MA math, budgets and costs. Offline."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import math
import subprocess
import sys
import threading
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pandas as pd
import pytest
from scipy.stats import chi2, false_discovery_control, norm

from agentcoop.agents import harness
from agentcoop.agents.harness import AgentRun
from agentcoop.coop import arms

from agentcoop.coop.protocol import ClaimRecord, ProtocolRun

from agentcoop.llm import ChatResult
