```python
def test_run_writes_one_file_per_arm_and_skips_existing(runner, tmp_path):
    out = tmp_path / "out" / "fx"
    (out / "fx-000").mkdir(parents=True)
    (out / "fx-000" / "ours__s0.json").write_text('{"old": true}')
    run.main(["--suite", "fx", "--arms", "single", "ours", "--parallel", "1"])

    assert sorted(p.name for p in (out / "fx-000").iterdir()) == [
        "ours__s0.json", "single-A__s0.json", "single-B__s0.json"]
    assert json.loads((out / "fx-000" / "ours__s0.json").read_text()) == {"old": True}
    rec = json.loads((out / "fx-001" / "single-A__s0.json").read_text())
    assert (rec["arm"], rec["task"], rec["sample"], rec["calls"]) == ("single:A", "fx-001", 0, 2)
    assert rec["claims"][0]["id"] == "single:A:1" and rec["claims"][0]["up_genes"] == IFN[:3]
    assert rec["meta"] == {"k": 1}
    assert rec["agents"] == {"A": "codex", "B": "finch", "M": "codex"}
    assert sorted((t, a) for t, _, a, _ in runner) == [
        ("fx-000", ["single"]), ("fx-001", ["single", "ours"])]
    assert json.loads((out / "agents.json").read_text()) == rec["agents"]

    runner.clear()
    run.main(["--suite", "fx", "--arms", "single", "ours"])
    assert runner == []                                   # everything exists: nothing re-run


def test_site_agents_default_alternates_proposers_and_gives_testers_codex():
    """load_case's site specs carry no "agent" key; the runner adds it before run_arms."""
    specs = [{"name": n, "proposes": n != "M"} for n in ("A", "B", "C", "M")]
    assert run.site_agents(specs, {}) == {"A": "codex", "B": "finch", "C": "codex", "M": "codex"}
    assert run.site_agents(specs, {"M": "finch"})["M"] == "finch"


def test_run_draws_agent_overrides_and_guards(runner, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    run.main(["--suite", "fx", "--tasks", "fx-001", "--arms", "vote", "--draws", "0", "1",
              "--agents", "B=codex"])
    assert [(t, s) for t, s, _, _ in runner] == [("fx-001", 0), ("fx-001", 1)]
    assert runner[0][3] == {"A": "codex", "B": "codex", "M": "codex"}

        run.main(["--suite", "fx", "--arms", "vote"])      # default assignment differs
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):

    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):

    monkeypatch.delenv("AGENTCOOP_BUDGET_USD")
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match="AGENTCOOP_BUDGET_USD"):
        run.main(["--suite", "fx", "--arms", "vote", "--agents", "B=codex"])


def test_run_failed_task_stores_nothing_and_the_others_finish(runner, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """A harness failure inside run_arms (Review Focus 3) must not become a stored result."""
    fake = run.run_arms

    def flaky(arm_names, specs, case, *, task_id, sample):
        if task_id == "fx-000":
            raise RuntimeError("codex timed out")
        return fake(arm_names, specs, case, task_id=task_id, sample=sample)

    monkeypatch.setattr(run, "run_arms", flaky)
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match="1 task"):
        run.main(["--suite", "fx", "--arms", "vote"])
    assert not (tmp_path / "out" / "fx" / "fx-000").exists()
    assert (tmp_path / "out" / "fx" / "fx-001" / "vote__s0.json").exists()
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Create `experiments/run.py`**

```python
"""Run arms x tasks x draws on a v4 suite and store every ArmResult (no grading here).

    AGENTCOOP_BUDGET_USD=180 python experiments/run.py --suite fsdev --tasks fsdev-800 \
        --arms ours ma_genome single --draws 0 [--parallel 2] [--agents A=codex,B=finch,C=codex]

Writes runs/v4/<suite>/<task>/<arm>__s<k>.json (the per-site key "single:A" is stored as
single-A__s0.json; the key itself is inside the file) and never recomputes a file that exists, so
an interrupted run resumes where it stopped. Step-1 proposals are shared through the store, so
arms requested in separate invocations still see identical step-1 claims. A non-default
backbone writes to runs/v4@<model>/, so a robustness run never skips over or overwrites the main
one.

The site -> harness assignment is fixed per suite: the first invocation records it in
runs/v4/<suite>/agents.json and a later invocation with a different one is refused (mixing
assignments inside a suite would confound arm differences with harness choice). Default: codex
and finch alternating over the case's proposing sites in site order; a tester-only site (the
planted mimic M) defaults to codex. Task 8's load_case gives site specs without an "agent" key;
run_task adds it from this assignment before calling run_arms.

Printed per arm: its site-agent calls, nominal USD and the wall time of the run_arms call that
produced it (one call per task and draw computes all missing arms together, sharing their sites,
so per-arm time is not separable). A task whose run_arms raises stores nothing, the other tasks
finish, and the run exits non-zero after printing the spend (a harness failure is never a
result). Grading is experiments/analyze.py, so nothing on this path touches hidden answers.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import concurrent.futures as cf
import json
import os
import sys
import time
import traceback
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from agentcoop import llm  # noqa: E402
from agentcoop.cases.planted import TASKS, load_case  # noqa: E402
from agentcoop.coop.arms import ARMS, ArmResult, run_arms  # noqa: E402
from agentcoop.coop.claims import claim_to_dict  # noqa: E402

OUT = ROOT / "runs" / ("v4" if llm.backbone() == llm.DEFAULT_MODEL else f"v4@{llm.backbone()}")
HARNESSES = ("codex", "finch", "react")
#: Arms that yield one result per proposing site, keyed "<arm>:<site>".
PER_SITE = ("single", "single_codex")


def parse_agents(text: str | None) -> dict[str, str]:
    """Parse "A=codex,B=finch" into {"A": "codex", "B": "finch"}."""
    out: dict[str, str] = {}
    for item in (text or "").split(","):
        if not item.strip():
            continue
        site, _, agent = (s.strip() for s in item.partition("="))
        if not site or agent not in HARNESSES:
            raise ValueError(f"bad --agents item {item!r}: expected SITE={'|'.join(HARNESSES)}")
        out[site] = agent
    return out


def site_agents(site_specs: list[dict], overrides: dict[str, str]) -> dict[str, str]:
    """Harness per site: codex/finch alternating over the proposing sites in site order, codex
    for a tester-only site, then overrides."""
    names = [s["name"] for s in site_specs]
    unknown = sorted(set(overrides) - set(names))
    if unknown:
        raise ValueError(f"--agents names unknown sites {unknown}; the suite's sites: {names}")
    proposers = [s["name"] for s in site_specs if s["proposes"]]
    default = {n: ("codex", "finch")[i % 2] for i, n in enumerate(proposers)}
    return {n: overrides.get(n, default.get(n, "codex")) for n in names}


def fix_agents(suite: str, agents: dict[str, str]) -> None:
    """Record the suite's assignment on first use; refuse a different one afterwards."""
    path = OUT / suite / "agents.json"
    if path.exists():
        stored = json.loads(path.read_text())
        if stored != agents:
            raise SystemExit(f"suite {suite} runs with agents {stored}, not {agents} "
                             f"(the assignment is fixed per suite: {path})")
        return
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(json.dumps(agents, indent=1))


def result_keys(arm: str, site_specs: list[dict]) -> list[str]:
    if arm in PER_SITE:
        return [f"{arm}:{s['name']}" for s in site_specs if s["proposes"]]
    return [arm]


def result_path(suite: str, task_id: str, key: str, sample: int) -> Path:
    return OUT / suite / task_id / f"{key.replace(':', '-')}__s{sample}.json"


def result_record(key: str, r: ArmResult, *, task_id: str, sample: int, agents: dict[str, str],
                  seconds: float) -> dict:
    return {"arm": key, "task": task_id, "sample": sample, "agents": agents,
            "claims": [claim_to_dict(c) for c in r.claims], "labels": r.labels,
            "causes": r.causes, "scopes": r.scopes, "calls": r.calls,
            "nominal_usd": r.nominal_usd, "meta": r.meta, "seconds": round(seconds, 1)}


def write_json(path: Path, record: dict) -> None:
    """Atomic, so a crash mid-write cannot leave a partial file that later runs would skip."""
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    tmp = path.with_name(path.name + ".tmp")
    tmp.write_text(json.dumps(record, indent=1))
    os.replace(tmp, path)


def run_task(suite: str, task_dir: Path, arms: list[str], draws: list[int],
             agents: dict[str, str]) -> None:
    task_id = task_dir.name
    case, site_specs = load_case(task_dir)
    names = [s["name"] for s in site_specs]
    if sorted(names) != sorted(agents):
        raise ValueError(f"{task_id}: sites {names} differ from the suite's agents {agents}")
    site_specs = [{**s, "agent": agents[s["name"]]} for s in site_specs]
    with llm.tag(f"v4/{suite}/{task_id}"):
        for sample in draws:
            todo = [a for a in arms if any(not result_path(suite, task_id, k, sample).exists()
                                           for k in result_keys(a, site_specs))]
            if not todo:
                continue
            t0 = time.time()
            results = run_arms(todo, site_specs, case, task_id=task_id, sample=sample)
            seconds = time.time() - t0
            for key, r in results.items():
                path = result_path(suite, task_id, key, sample)
                if path.exists():
                    continue
                write_json(path, result_record(key, r, task_id=task_id, sample=sample,
                                               agents=agents, seconds=seconds))
                print(f"{task_id} s{sample} {key:<24} calls={r.calls:<3} "
                      f"nominal ${r.nominal_usd:.3f} {seconds:.0f}s", flush=True)


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> None:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--suite", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--tasks", nargs="+", default=["all"], help="task names, or all")
    ap.add_argument("--arms", nargs="+", default=list(ARMS), choices=ARMS)
    ap.add_argument("--draws", nargs="+", type=int, default=[0], help="sample indices")
    ap.add_argument("--parallel", type=int, default=2, help="tasks run in parallel")
    ap.add_argument("--agents", help="site -> harness, e.g. A=codex,B=finch,C=codex")
    args = ap.parse_args(argv)
    if not os.environ.get("AGENTCOOP_BUDGET_USD"):
        raise SystemExit("set AGENTCOOP_BUDGET_USD (hard cap on actual spend) before running arms")
    suite_dir = TASKS / args.suite
    task_dirs = sorted(d for d in suite_dir.iterdir() if d.is_dir()) if suite_dir.is_dir() else []
    if args.tasks != ["all"]:
        missing = sorted(set(args.tasks) - {d.name for d in task_dirs})
        if missing:
            ap.error(f"no such task(s) in {suite_dir}: {missing}")
        task_dirs = [d for d in task_dirs if d.name in args.tasks]
    if not task_dirs:
        ap.error(f"no tasks in {suite_dir}")
    try:
        agents = site_agents(load_case(task_dirs[0])[1], parse_agents(args.agents))
    except ValueError as e:
        ap.error(str(e))
    fix_agents(args.suite, agents)

    failed = []
    with cf.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=args.parallel) as pool:
        futures = {pool.submit(run_task, args.suite, d, args.arms, args.draws, agents): d
                   for d in task_dirs}
        for fut in cf.as_completed(futures):
            try:
                fut.result()
            except Exception:
                failed.append(futures[fut].name)
                print(f"FAILED {futures[fut].name}\n{traceback.format_exc()}", flush=True)
    print("spent:", llm.spent("v4/"), flush=True)
    if failed:
        raise SystemExit(f"{len(failed)} task(s) failed: {sorted(failed)}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

```python
# ------------------------------------------------- analyze.py and eval.planted.view_errors

analyze = _script("analyze")

OURS = [0.8, 0.7, 0.6, 0.9, 0.5, 0.75]
MA = [0.6, 0.7 + 1e-12, 0.65, 0.5, 0.5, 0.45]      # task 1: tie within 1e-9; task 4: exact tie
ORACLE = [0.9, 0.9, 0.8, 1.0, 0.7, 0.85]
SINGLE_A = [0.5, 0.6, 0.3, 0.4, 0.2, 0.5]
SINGLE_B = [0.4, 0.65, 0.5, 0.6, 0.3, 0.35]


def test_analyze_paired_wilcoxon_and_silo_cost_recovery(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """Each stored result carries its F1 in its claim's effect, so a fake grader returns known
    scores and the statistics can be checked by hand."""
    scores = {"ours": OURS, "ma_genome": MA, "oracle": ORACLE, "single:A": SINGLE_A,
              "single:B": SINGLE_B}
    for i in range(6):
        task_dir = tmp_path / "out" / "fx" / f"fx-{i:03d}"
        task_dir.mkdir(parents=True)
        for arm, f1 in scores.items():
            rec = {"arm": arm, "task": task_dir.name, "sample": 0,
                   "claims": [claim_to_dict(claim("c", "A", tuple(IFN[:3]), effect=f1[i]))],
                   "labels": {}, "causes": {}, "scopes": {}, "calls": 3, "nominal_usd": 0.02,
                   "meta": {}}
            (task_dir / f"{arm.replace(':', '-')}__s0.json").write_text(json.dumps(rec))

    def fake_grade(claims, truth, *, labels=None, causes=None, scopes=None):
        f1 = claims[0].effect
        return {"precision": f1, "recall": f1, "f1": f1, "n_claims": len(claims),
                "artefact_rejected": None, "scope_accuracy": None, "cause_accuracy": None}

    monkeypatch.setattr(analyze, "OUT", tmp_path / "out")
    monkeypatch.setattr(analyze, "load_truth", lambda d: {})
    monkeypatch.setattr(analyze, "grade", fake_grade)
    analyze.main(["--suite", "fx"])
    res = json.loads((tmp_path / "out" / "fx_analysis.json").read_text())

    vs_ma = res["paired_f1_vs_ours"]["ma_genome"]
    assert (vs_ma["n"], vs_ma["wins"], vs_ma["losses"], vs_ma["ties"]) == (6, 3, 1, 2)
    nonzero = [0.8 - 0.6, 0.6 - 0.65, 0.9 - 0.5, 0.75 - 0.45]
    assert vs_ma["p"] == pytest.approx(stats.wilcoxon(nonzero).pvalue)
    assert vs_ma["p"] == pytest.approx(0.25)              # exact: W- = 1 of 16 sign patterns x 2

    best = np.maximum(SINGLE_A, SINGLE_B)                 # per task, not the better site's mean
    assert res["arms"]["best_single"]["f1"] == pytest.approx(best.mean())
    rec = res["silo_cost_recovery_f1"]
    assert rec["value"] == pytest.approx((np.mean(OURS) - best.mean())
                                         / (np.mean(ORACLE) - best.mean()))
    assert rec["value"] == pytest.approx(0.2 / 0.35)
    assert len(rec["ci95"]) == 2 and rec["n"] == 6
    assert [m["arm"] for m in res["mechanism"]] == ["ours", "ma_genome"]
    assert res["mechanism"][1]["vs_ours"]["wins"] == 3
    assert res["view_errors"] == [] and res["views"] == []   # no arm recorded a view

    same = {t: {"ours": {"f1": 0.5}, "vote": {"f1": 0.5}} for t in ("a", "b")}
    assert analyze.paired(same, "ours", "vote") == {"n": 2, "wins": 0, "losses": 0, "ties": 2,
                                                    "mean_diff": 0.0, "p": None}


def test_analyze_grades_causes_over_every_claim_ours_labelled(monkeypatch):
    """Ours' findings are its corroborated claims only; the cause it gave a demoted claim is
    scored from meta["all_claims"], with the real grader, while precision and recall stay over
    the findings."""
    p1, art = [f"G{i:03d}" for i in range(10)], [f"G{i:03d}" for i in range(30, 40)]
    truth = {"programs": [
        {"id": "P1", "kind": "reproducible", "cell_types": ["NK"], "up_genes": p1,
         "down_genes": [], "sites": ["A", "B", "C"], "shared_with_mimic": True},
        {"id": "P4", "kind": "artefact", "cell_types": ["CD4 T"], "up_genes": art,
         "down_genes": [], "sites": ["C"], "shared_with_mimic": False}],
        "composition": {"id": "C1", "kind": "composition", "population": "CD16 Mono",
                        "sign": -1, "sites": ["A", "B", "C"]},
        "artefact_site": "C"}

@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def built(tmp_path_factory):
    """One planted task with both knobs on, built by Task 8's generator on its fake base and
    libraries (seed 22 is the (True, True) seed of Task 8's own suite). Yielded inside the patch,
    so load_case still finds the fake gene-set library."""

                                         seal=grader.write_truth)
        yield task_dir


def _true_view(task_dir: Path, site: str, root: Path) -> None:
    """The correct view of a planted site, written from the answer key (the test's oracle)."""
    import anndata as ad

    m = grader.load_truth(task_dir)["sites"][site]
    a = ad.read_h5ad(task_dir / "sites" / f"{site}.h5ad")
    table = pd.read_csv(task_dir / "public" / "symbols.tsv", sep="\t", dtype=str)
    column = {"symbol": "symbol", "ensembl": "ensembl_id", "alias": "alias"}[m["scheme"]]
    symbol_of = dict(zip(table[column], table["symbol"]))
    group_of = {d: g for g, donors in m["donors"].items() for d in donors}
    samples = pd.DataFrame([{"sample": s, "donor": d, "group": group_of[d]}
                            for s, d in sorted(m["samples"].items())])
    cells = pd.DataFrame({"cell": a.obs_names, "sample": a.obs["sample"].astype(str).to_numpy(),
                          "population": a.obs["cell_label"].astype(str).map(m["labels"])})
    write_view(root, genes={v: symbol_of[v] for v in a.var_names}, samples=samples,
               cells=cells.reset_index(drop=True), spec={"unit": "donor"})


def _load(root: Path):
    view, errors = load_view(root, kind="scrna", vocab=planted.CASE_VOCAB)
    assert view is not None, errors
    return view


def test_view_errors_count_wrong_and_missing_items(built, tmp_path):
    truth = grader.load_truth(built)
    site = next(s for s in "ABC" if truth["sites"][s]["scheme"] == "ensembl")
    _true_view(built, site, tmp_path / "good")
    good = _load(tmp_path / "good")
    assert set(grader.view_errors(good, built, site).values()) == {0.0}

    ids = list(good.genes)
    genes = {i: good.genes[i] for i in ids[30:]}                     # 30 ids left out
    genes.update({i: "WRONG" + good.genes[i] for i in ids[30:45]})   # 15 ids misnamed
    samples = good.samples.copy()
    samples.loc[0, "group"] = "treated" if samples.loc[0, "group"] == "control" else "control"
    cells = good.cells.copy()
    nk = cells["population"] == "NK"
    cells.loc[nk, "population"] = "B"                                # NK cells called B
    write_view(tmp_path / "bad", genes=genes, samples=samples, cells=cells.iloc[10:],
               spec={"unit": "donor"})                               # 10 cells left out
    e = grader.view_errors(_load(tmp_path / "bad"), built, site)
    n_genes, n_cells, relabelled = len(ids), len(cells), int(nk.iloc[10:].sum())
    assert (e["genes_missing"], e["genes_wrong"]) == (30 / n_genes, 15 / n_genes)
    assert (e["groups_wrong"], e["groups_missing"]) == (1 / 8, 0.0)
    assert (e["populations_missing"], e["populations_wrong"]) == (10 / n_cells,
                                                                  relabelled / n_cells)
    assert e["error_rate"] == pytest.approx(max(45 / n_genes, 1 / 8,
                                                (10 + relabelled) / n_cells))


def test_analyze_restores_recorded_views_and_tabulates_their_errors(built, tmp_path,
                                                                    monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(store, "STORE", tmp_path / "store")
    _true_view(built, "A", tmp_path / "good")
    good = _load(tmp_path / "good")
    samples = good.samples.copy()
    samples.loc[0, "group"] = "treated" if samples.loc[0, "group"] == "control" else "control"
    write_view(tmp_path / "bad", genes=good.genes, samples=samples, cells=good.cells,
               spec={"unit": "donor"})
    (tmp_path / "invalid").mkdir()
    (tmp_path / "invalid" / "spec.json").write_text("{}")             # no view files
    good_id, bad_id, invalid_id = (store.put_blob(tmp_path / d) for d in ("good", "bad", "invalid"))
    agents = {"A": "finch", "B": "codex", "C": "finch", "M": "codex"}
    results = [{"arm": arm, "agents": agents, "meta": meta} for arm, meta in [
        ("single:A", {"views": {"A": good_id}}),
        ("single:B", {"views": {"B": invalid_id}}),
        ("single_codex:A", {"views": {"A": good_id}}),
        ("ma_genome", {"views": {"A": good_id, "B": None}}),
        ("ours", {"views": {"A": bad_id}}),
        ("ours", {"views": {"A": bad_id}}),                             # a second draw, same view
        ("union", {})]]

    rows = analyze.view_rows(built, results)
    assert len(rows) == 6 and {(r["stage"], r["site"], r["agent"], r["valid"],
                                r.get("error_rate")) for r in rows} == {
        ("step 1", "A", "finch", True, 0.0),
        ("step 1", "B", "codex", False, None),
        ("step 1 (all Codex)", "A", "codex", True, 0.0),
        ("self-checked", "A", "finch", True, 0.0),
        ("self-checked", "B", "codex", False, None),
        ("repaired", "A", "finch", True, 0.125)}
    assert all(r["ids_labels"] and r["artefact"] and r["task"] == built.name for r in rows)
    summary = {(s["stage"], s["agent"]): (s["n_views"], s["n_invalid"], s["error_rate"],
                                          s["any_error"])
               for s in analyze.view_error_summary(rows)}
    assert summary == {("step 1", "finch"): (1, 0, 0.0, 0.0),
                       ("step 1", "codex"): (1, 1, None, None),
                       ("step 1 (all Codex)", "codex"): (1, 0, 0.0, 0.0),
                       ("self-checked", "finch"): (1, 0, 0.0, 0.0),
                       ("self-checked", "codex"): (1, 1, None, None),
                       ("repaired", "finch"): (1, 0, 0.125, 1.0)}
    assert analyze.view_rows(built, results[-1:]) == []
```

Then insert this block immediately above the line `if __name__ == "__main__":  # pragma: no cover`:

```python
#: Components of a site view the view-error rate is measured on (spec §6).
VIEW_PARTS = ("genes", "groups", "populations")


def view_errors(view: SiteView, task_dir: Path, site: str) -> dict:
    """How far a site view is from the site's correct codebook (spec §6: the view-error rate).

    Per component, the fraction of the site's items the view maps wrongly (``<part>_wrong``) and
    the fraction it leaves out (``<part>_missing``): genes over the site file's feature ids (the
    correct symbol comes from the task's symbol table under the site's scheme: symbol, Ensembl or
    alias; symbols compare case-insensitively), groups over the site's samples,
    populations over its cells. Planted site files hold no cell a correct analysis would exclude,
    so a left-out item is an error too. ``error_rate`` is the worst component's wrong + missing.
    """
    task_dir = Path(task_dir)
    truth = load_truth(task_dir)["sites"][site]
    adata = ad.read_h5ad(task_dir / "sites" / f"{site}.h5ad", backed="r")
    obs, features = adata.obs.copy(), [str(v) for v in adata.var_names]
    adata.file.close()
    # Task 8's gene-ID schemes: the site's var_names are the symbol table's symbol, ensembl_id or
    # alias column (a bijection with symbol), so the correct symbol is one lookup away.
    table = pd.read_csv(task_dir / "public" / "symbols.tsv", sep="\t", dtype=str)
    column = {"symbol": "symbol", "ensembl": "ensembl_id", "alias": "alias"}[truth["scheme"]]
    symbol_of = dict(zip(table[column], table["symbol"].str.upper()))
    group_of = {donor: group for group, donors in truth["donors"].items() for donor in donors}
    correct = {
        "genes": {f: symbol_of[f] for f in features},
        "groups": {sample: group_of[donor] for sample, donor in truth["samples"].items()},
        "populations": dict(zip(obs.index.astype(str),
                                obs["cell_label"].astype(str).map(truth["labels"]))),
    }
    seen = {
        "genes": {f: str(s).upper() for f, s in view.genes.items()},   # claims upper-case too
        "groups": dict(zip(view.samples["sample"], view.samples["group"])),
        "populations": ({} if view.cells is None
                        else dict(zip(view.cells["cell"], view.cells["population"]))),
    }
    out = {}
    for part in VIEW_PARTS:
        items, mapped = correct[part], seen[part]
        out[f"{part}_wrong"] = sum(k in mapped and mapped[k] != v
                                   for k, v in items.items()) / len(items)
        out[f"{part}_missing"] = sum(k not in mapped for k in items) / len(items)
    out["error_rate"] = max(out[f"{p}_wrong"] + out[f"{p}_missing"] for p in VIEW_PARTS)
    return out
```

- [ ] **Step 14: Create `experiments/analyze.py`**

```python
"""Grade stored v4 runs and tabulate them (evaluation side: reads the hidden answers).

    python experiments/analyze.py --suite fsdev [--tasks fsdev-800 fsdev-801]

Reads runs/v4/<suite>/<task>/*.json (written by experiments/run.py), grades every stored result
with eval.planted.grade and averages a task's draws, because the task is the statistical unit
(§7). Precision, recall, F1, artefact rejection and scope accuracy are over the stored findings
(``claims``). Cause accuracy is over every claim the arm labelled: for ours and no_resolution,
Task 9 stores them with their labels, causes and scopes in meta["all_claims"], because causes
mostly explain demoted claims, which are not findings. Derived rows best_single / best_single_codex take, per task, the maximum over proposing
sites on each quality endpoint (an after-the-fact choice that favours the baseline, §4); their
claims, calls and cost are the mean of one site's run. Printed, and written to
runs/v4/<suite>_analysis.json:

* per arm: mean precision, recall and F1 with task-bootstrap 95% CIs, artefact rejection rate,
  scope and cause accuracy (over the tasks where they are defined), mean claims, site-agent
  calls and nominal USD;
* ours vs every other arm on F1: paired Wilcoxon signed-rank over tasks, differences within
  1e-9 dropped as ties (a mean of equal draws is not exactly equal);
* silo-cost recovery on F1 = (ours - best single) / (oracle - best single), from task means,
  with a task-bootstrap 95% CI; undefined when the oracle does not beat the best single site;
* the mechanism decomposition (§4): ours, no_resolution, ma_claims, ma_genome,
  ma_genome_repaired, no_routing, each with its paired F1 comparison to ours;
* the view-error rate of agent-built views (§6) per stage, harness and ids_labels knob: every
  view an arm recorded in meta["views"] (step 1 from single / single_codex, self-checked from
  ma_genome, repaired from ours) is restored from the store and compared with the task's correct
  codebook by eval.planted.view_errors. If it is about 0 with the knob on, §6 drops the
  view-repair claim and the knob.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import sys
import tempfile
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
from scipy import stats

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from agentcoop import llm, store  # noqa: E402
from agentcoop.cases.planted import TASKS, load_case  # noqa: E402
from agentcoop.coop.arms import ARMS  # noqa: E402
from agentcoop.coop.claims import claim_from_dict  # noqa: E402
from agentcoop.coop.view import load_view  # noqa: E402
from agentcoop.eval.planted import grade, load_truth, view_errors  # noqa: E402

OUT = ROOT / "runs" / ("v4" if llm.backbone() == llm.DEFAULT_MODEL else f"v4@{llm.backbone()}")
ENDPOINTS = ("precision", "recall", "f1")
RATES = ("artefact_rejected", "scope_accuracy", "cause_accuracy")
COUNTS = ("n_claims", "calls", "nominal_usd")
MECHANISM = ("ours", "no_resolution", "ma_claims", "ma_genome", "ma_genome_repaired", "no_routing")
#: Arm family -> which views its meta["views"] holds.
STAGES = {"single": "step 1", "single_codex": "step 1 (all Codex)", "ma_genome": "self-checked",
          "ours": "repaired"}
TIE = 1e-9
N_BOOT = 10_000

PerTask = dict[str, dict[str, dict]]     # task -> arm -> metric -> value


def load_results(suite: str, tasks: list[str] | None) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
    """task -> its stored results (every arm and draw)."""
    return {d.name: [json.loads(f.read_text()) for f in sorted(d.glob("*.json"))]
            for d in sorted((OUT / suite).iterdir())
            if d.is_dir() and (not tasks or d.name in tasks)}


def _mean(values: list) -> float | None:
    """Mean over the defined values (booleans count 0/1); None when none is defined."""
    vals = [float(v) for v in values if v is not None]
    return float(np.mean(vals)) if vals else None


def grade_task(task_dir: Path, results: list[dict]) -> dict[str, dict]:
    """arm -> graded metrics averaged over the arm's draws, plus the best-single rows."""
    truth = load_truth(task_dir)
    draws: dict[str, list[dict]] = defaultdict(list)
    for r in results:
        g = grade([claim_from_dict(c) for c in r["claims"]], truth, labels=r["labels"] or None,
                  causes=r["causes"] or None, scopes=r["scopes"] or None)
        every = r["meta"].get("all_claims")
        if every:
            claims = [claim_from_dict(d["claim"]) for d in every]
            full = grade(claims, truth,
                         labels={c.id: d["label"] for c, d in zip(claims, every)},
                         causes={c.id: d["cause"] for c, d in zip(claims, every) if d["cause"]},
                         scopes={c.id: d["scope"] for c, d in zip(claims, every) if d["scope"]})
            g["cause_accuracy"] = full["cause_accuracy"]
        draws[r["arm"]].append({**g, "calls": r["calls"], "nominal_usd": r["nominal_usd"]})
    keys = (*ENDPOINTS, *RATES, *COUNTS)
    per_arm = {arm: {k: _mean([d[k] for d in ds]) for k in keys} for arm, ds in draws.items()}
    for family in ("single", "single_codex"):
        sites = [m for arm, m in per_arm.items() if arm.startswith(family + ":")]
        if sites:
            per_arm[f"best_{family}"] = {
                **{k: max((m[k] for m in sites if m[k] is not None), default=None)
                   for k in (*ENDPOINTS, *RATES)},
                **{k: _mean([m[k] for m in sites]) for k in COUNTS}}
    return per_arm


def _boot_ci(x: np.ndarray) -> list[float] | None:
    """Percentile 95% CI of the mean over resampled tasks (seeded, so tables reproduce)."""
    if len(x) < 2:
        return None
    idx = np.random.default_rng(0).integers(0, len(x), (N_BOOT, len(x)))
    return [float(v) for v in np.quantile(x[idx].mean(axis=1), [0.025, 0.975])]


def summarize(per_task: PerTask, arm: str) -> dict:
    rows = [arms[arm] for arms in per_task.values() if arm in arms]
    out: dict = {"n_tasks": len(rows)}
    for k in ENDPOINTS:
        x = np.array([r[k] for r in rows if r[k] is not None], dtype=float)
        out[k] = float(x.mean()) if len(x) else None
        out[f"{k}_ci95"] = _boot_ci(x)
    for k in (*RATES, *COUNTS):
        out[k] = _mean([r[k] for r in rows])
    return out


def paired(per_task: PerTask, a: str, b: str, metric: str = "f1") -> dict:
    """Wilcoxon signed-rank of a - b over the tasks holding both; |difference| <= TIE is a tie
    and is dropped before the test. p is None when no difference is left."""
    d = np.array([arms[a][metric] - arms[b][metric] for arms in per_task.values()
                  if a in arms and b in arms and arms[a][metric] is not None
                  and arms[b][metric] is not None], dtype=float)
    nz = d[np.abs(d) > TIE]
    return {"n": len(d), "wins": int((nz > 0).sum()), "losses": int((nz < 0).sum()),
            "ties": int(len(d) - len(nz)), "mean_diff": float(d.mean()) if len(d) else None,
            "p": float(stats.wilcoxon(nz).pvalue) if len(nz) else None}


def silo_cost_recovery(per_task: PerTask, metric: str = "f1") -> dict:
    """(ours - best single) / (oracle - best single) on task means, over tasks holding all three;
    a resample (or the estimate) whose oracle gap is <= TIE has no silo cost to recover."""
    x = np.array([[arms[a][metric] for a in ("ours", "best_single", "oracle")]
                  for arms in per_task.values()
                  if all(a in arms and arms[a][metric] is not None
                         for a in ("ours", "best_single", "oracle"))], dtype=float).reshape(-1, 3)
    if not len(x):
        return {"n": 0, "value": None, "ci95": None}
    ours, single, oracle = (float(v) for v in x.mean(axis=0))
    value = (ours - single) / (oracle - single) if oracle - single > TIE else None
    ci = None
    if len(x) > 1:
        m = x[np.random.default_rng(0).integers(0, len(x), (N_BOOT, len(x)))].mean(axis=1)
        gap = m[:, 2] - m[:, 1]
        ok = gap > TIE
        if ok.any():
            ci = [float(v) for v in np.quantile((m[ok, 0] - m[ok, 1]) / gap[ok], [0.025, 0.975])]
    return {"n": len(x), "ours": ours, "best_single": single, "oracle": oracle, "value": value,
            "ci95": ci}


def view_rows(task_dir: Path, results: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
    """One row per distinct (stage, site, view) among the views the results recorded: stage,
    harness, the task's knobs and eval.planted.view_errors of the restored view. A site that had
    no view (failed call) or an invalid one is a row with valid False and no rates."""
    entries = {(STAGES[r["arm"].split(":")[0]], site, blob,
                "codex" if r["arm"].startswith("single_codex:") else r["agents"][site])
               for r in results if r["arm"].split(":")[0] in STAGES
               for site, blob in r["meta"].get("views", {}).items()}
    if not entries:
        return []
    case, _ = load_case(task_dir)
    knobs = load_truth(task_dir)["knobs"]
    rows = []
    for stage, site, blob, agent in sorted(entries, key=str):
        errors = None
        if blob is not None:
            with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
                store.get_blob(blob, Path(tmp) / "view")
                view, _ = load_view(Path(tmp) / "view", kind=case.kind, vocab=case.vocab)
                errors = None if view is None else view_errors(view, task_dir, site)
        rows.append({"task": task_dir.name, "stage": stage, "site": site, "agent": agent,
                     "view": blob, **knobs, "valid": errors is not None, **(errors or {})})
    return rows


def view_error_summary(rows: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
    """Per (stage, harness, ids_labels knob): views, invalid views, mean error rate of the valid
    ones and the share of valid views with any error."""
    groups: dict[tuple, list[dict]] = defaultdict(list)
    for row in rows:
        groups[(row["stage"], row["agent"], row["ids_labels"])].append(row)
    return [{"stage": stage, "agent": agent, "ids_labels": knob, "n_views": len(rs),
             "n_invalid": sum(not r["valid"] for r in rs),
             "error_rate": _mean([r["error_rate"] for r in rs if r["valid"]]),
             "any_error": _mean([r["error_rate"] > 0 for r in rs if r["valid"]])}
            for (stage, agent, knob), rs in sorted(groups.items(), key=str)]


def _order(arm: str) -> tuple[int, str]:
    base = arm.split(":")[0].removeprefix("best_")
    return (ARMS.index(base) if base in ARMS else len(ARMS), arm)


def analyze(per_task: PerTask) -> dict:
    arms = sorted({a for t in per_task.values() for a in t}, key=_order)
    summary = {a: summarize(per_task, a) for a in arms}
    vs_ours = {a: paired(per_task, "ours", a) for a in arms if a != "ours"}
    return {"tasks": sorted(per_task), "per_task": per_task, "arms": summary,
            "paired_f1_vs_ours": vs_ours, "silo_cost_recovery_f1": silo_cost_recovery(per_task),
            "mechanism": [{"arm": a, **{k: summary[a][k] for k in ENDPOINTS},
                           "vs_ours": vs_ours.get(a)} for a in MECHANISM if a in summary]}


def _f(x: float | None, nd: int = 2) -> str:
    return "-" if x is None else f"{x:.{nd}f}"


def _ci(s: dict, k: str) -> str:
    ci = s[f"{k}_ci95"]
    return f"{_f(s[k])} " + ("[-]" if ci is None else f"[{ci[0]:.2f},{ci[1]:.2f}]")


def print_report(res: dict) -> None:
    print(f"{'arm':<22}{'n':>3}  {'precision':<18}{'recall':<18}{'F1':<18}"
          f"{'art.rej':>7}{'scope':>7}{'cause':>7}{'claims':>7}{'calls':>7}{'nominal$':>9}")
    for arm, s in res["arms"].items():
        print(f"{arm:<22}{s['n_tasks']:>3}  {_ci(s, 'precision'):<18}{_ci(s, 'recall'):<18}"
              f"{_ci(s, 'f1'):<18}{_f(s['artefact_rejected']):>7}{_f(s['scope_accuracy']):>7}"
              f"{_f(s['cause_accuracy']):>7}{_f(s['n_claims'], 1):>7}{_f(s['calls'], 1):>7}"
              f"{_f(s['nominal_usd'], 3):>9}")
    print("\nours vs arm on F1 (paired Wilcoxon over tasks; W/L/T)")
    for arm, p in res["paired_f1_vs_ours"].items():
        print(f"  {arm:<22} n={p['n']:<3} {p['wins']}/{p['losses']}/{p['ties']}  "
              f"mean diff {_f(p['mean_diff'], 3)}  p={_f(p['p'], 4)}")
    r = res["silo_cost_recovery_f1"]
    ci = "-" if r["ci95"] is None else f"[{r['ci95'][0]:.2f}, {r['ci95'][1]:.2f}]"
    print(f"\nsilo-cost recovery (F1, n={r['n']}): {_f(r['value'])} {ci}")
    print("\nmechanism decomposition (F1; ours - arm)")
    for m in res["mechanism"]:
        p = m["vs_ours"]
        diff = "" if p is None else (f"  diff {_f(p['mean_diff'], 3)} "
                                     f"({p['wins']}/{p['losses']}/{p['ties']}, p={_f(p['p'], 4)})")
        print(f"  {m['arm']:<20} F1 {_f(m['f1'])}  P {_f(m['precision'])}  "
              f"R {_f(m['recall'])}{diff}")
    print("\nview-error rate of agent-built views (§6)")
    for v in res["view_errors"]:
        print(f"  {v['stage']:<20} {v['agent']:<6} ids_labels={str(v['ids_labels']):<5} "
              f"views={v['n_views']:<3} invalid={v['n_invalid']:<3} "
              f"error rate {_f(v['error_rate'], 3)}  any error {_f(v['any_error'])}")


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> None:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--suite", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--tasks", nargs="*", help="default: every task with stored results")
    args = ap.parse_args(argv)
    if not (OUT / args.suite).is_dir():
        ap.error(f"no stored runs in {OUT / args.suite} (experiments/run.py writes them)")
    results = load_results(args.suite, args.tasks)
    per_task = {t: grade_task(TASKS / args.suite / t, rs) for t, rs in results.items()}
    views = [row for t, rs in results.items() for row in view_rows(TASKS / args.suite / t, rs)]
    res = {"suite": args.suite, **analyze(per_task), "view_errors": view_error_summary(views),
           "views": views}
    (OUT / f"{args.suite}_analysis.json").write_text(json.dumps(res, indent=1))
    print_report(res)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- headroom.py

headroom = _script("headroom")


def test_load_case_gives_ma_genome_the_gene_set_library(built):
    """The library the site agents get must reach MA-genome (arms and gate alike)."""
    case, _ = planted.load_case(built)
    assert arms.gene_set_library(case), sorted(case.resources)


def test_tool_views_match_the_truth_and_a_wrong_one_is_refused(built, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    case, specs = planted.load_case(built)
    truth = grader.load_truth(built)
    symbols = pd.read_csv(case.resources[headroom.SYMBOLS], sep="\t", dtype=str)
    assert {truth["sites"][s]["scheme"] for s in "ABC"} == set(planted.SCHEMES)   # ids_labels
    for spec in specs:                        # renamed codes and native labels at every site
        groups, populations = headroom.tool_codebook(spec["card"])
        assert groups == truth["sites"][spec["name"]]["codes"]
        assert populations == truth["sites"][spec["name"]]["labels"]
        headroom.tool_view(tmp_path / spec["name"], spec["data_path"], spec["card"], symbols)
        errors = grader.view_errors(_load(tmp_path / spec["name"]), built, spec["name"])
        assert errors["error_rate"] == 0.0, (spec["name"], errors)

    real, flip = headroom.tool_codebook, {"control": "treated", "treated": "control",
                                          "mimic": "control"}
    monkeypatch.setattr(headroom, "tool_codebook", lambda card: (
        {code: flip[g] for code, g in real(card)[0].items()}, real(card)[1]))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="differs from the truth"):
        headroom.tool_sites(case, specs, built)              # the gate refuses wrong tools


def test_headroom_gate_on_a_built_task(built):
    row = headroom.evaluate_task(built)
    assert set(row["single"]) == {"A", "B", "C"}               # the mimic site never proposes
    assert row["knobs"] == {"artefact": True, "ids_labels": True}
    ma, ours = row["ma_tools"], row["scripted_ours"]
    assert ma["recall"] > 0 and row["best_single"]["recall"] > 0
    assert row["n_claims"]["scripted_ours"] <= row["n_claims"]["ma_tools"]
    # Pooling one strong site with two null ones keeps the one-site artefact; cross-testing at
    # the other sites, with the correct resolution, drops it.
    assert ma["artefact_rejected"] is False and ours["artefact_rejected"] is True
    assert ours["precision"] - ma["precision"] >= headroom.GAIN
    assert 0 < row["union_covers_ma_hits"] <= 1


class _CannedSite:
    """Canned test results per claim id (only what scripted_ours calls)."""

    def __init__(self, name: str, statuses: dict[str, str]) -> None:
        self.name, self.statuses = name, statuses

    def holds(self, population: str, contrast: tuple[str, str]) -> bool:
        return True

    def test(self, c, *, test: str = "replication",
             contrast: tuple[str, str] | None = None) -> TestResult:
        status = self.statuses[c.id]
        effect = {"supported": 1.0, "contradicted": -1.0, "absent": 0.0}[status]
        return TestResult(claim_id=c.id, site=self.name, test=test, status=status,
                          effect=effect, se=0.05, ci95=(effect - 0.1, effect + 0.1),
                          n_per_group=(4, 4), mde=0.2, p=0.9 if status == "absent" else 1e-6,
                          competitive_ok=True, reason="module")


def test_scripted_ours_sets_aside_only_the_artefact_sites_contradictions():
    c1, c2 = claim("MA:1", "ma_tools", tuple(IFN[:3])), claim("MA:2", "ma_tools", tuple(IFN[3:]))
    sites = [_CannedSite("A", {"MA:1": "supported", "MA:2": "supported"}),
             _CannedSite("B", {"MA:1": "supported", "MA:2": "absent"}),
             _CannedSite("C", {"MA:1": "contradicted", "MA:2": "absent"})]
    case = case_spec()
    assert headroom.scripted_ours([c1, c2], sites, case, "C") == [c1]   # correct resolution
    assert headroom.scripted_ours([c1, c2], sites, case, None) == []    # disputed without it
    assert headroom.scripted_ours([c1, c2], sites, case, "B") == []     # only that site's


def test_headroom_verdict_pass_rule():
    def row(single_f1, ma, ours):
        return {"best_single": {"f1": single_f1},
                "ma_tools": dict(zip(("precision", "recall", "f1"), ma)),
                "scripted_ours": dict(zip(("precision", "recall", "f1"), ours))}

    rows = {"t1": row(0.5, (0.30, 0.6, 0.4), (0.35, 0.6, 0.6)),     # +0.05 precision (in float
            "t2": row(0.5, (0.5, 0.6, 0.5), (0.5, 0.6, 0.5)),       # 0.35 - 0.30 < 0.05)
            "t3": row(0.4, (0.5, 0.5, 0.5), (0.5, 0.56, 0.55)),     # +0.06 recall
            "t4": row(0.0, (None, 0.0, 0.0), (None, 0.0, 0.0))}     # no claims: no gain
    v = headroom.verdict(rows)
    assert v["tasks_with_gain"] == ["t1", "t3"] and v["pass"]
    rows["t3"]["scripted_ours"]["recall"] = 0.5
    assert not headroom.verdict(rows)["pass"]                       # gain in 1 of 4 tasks
    rows["t3"]["scripted_ours"]["recall"] = 0.56
    for r in rows.values():
        r["best_single"]["f1"] = 0.9
    assert not headroom.verdict(rows)["pass"]                       # (iii) does not beat (i)
```

- [ ] **Step 19: Create `experiments/headroom.py`**

```python
"""Headroom gate (spec §6) on a planted suite: is there anything left for cross-examination? $0.

    python experiments/headroom.py --suite fsdev [--tasks fsdev-800 ...]

Design-time and evaluation side: it reads the hidden truth and never calls a model, and its
verdict decides, before any LLM spend, whether the suite is worth running. Fixed tools (no agent)
build each site's *tool view*: feature ids (symbol, Ensembl or alias) -> symbols through the
task's symbol table, condition
codes and cell labels -> case vocabulary through the codebook tables committed in
agentcoop/cases/planted.py (GROUP_TEXT, NATIVE_LABELS). The tool views are checked against the
truth (eval.planted.view_errors must be 0: the gate measures headroom, not tool failures) and the
testers' site tables are computed from them. Three analyses are graded with eval.planted.grade:

  (i)   best single site: each proposing site alone runs MA-genome's statistics and enrichment on
        its own table (arms.ma_genome(..., min_sites=1)) and keeps the claims its own tester
        supports; best = per task, the maximum over sites on each endpoint;
  (ii)  MA-tools: arms.ma_genome over every site's tool view (the ma_genome arm's code);
  (iii) scripted ours: MA-tools' claims tested at every site holding them (one BH family), the
        artefact site's `contradicted` results set aside using the truth (the correct
        resolution, applied by script), kept when the shared label rule, with the most
        significant supporting site as proposer, says `corroborated`. A ceiling for ours:
        passing is necessary, not sufficient.

Pass rule (fixed before computing; copy it into findings.md before the first run):
    PASS iff (iii) - (ii) >= 0.05 in precision or in recall on at least half of the tasks,
    and the mean F1 of (iii) over the tasks exceeds the mean F1 of (i).
A difference within 1e-9 of the 0.05 margin reaches it (float ties); an undefined precision (no
claims) shows no gain. Also recorded per task: the fraction of MA-tools' true hits that some
single-site claim matches (if it is low, ours is capped below MA on recall and the suite can
support precision claims only). The view-error rate of *agent-built* views, the other §6
quantity, needs agent runs and is reported by experiments/analyze.py. Writes
runs/v4/<suite>_headroom.json.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import sys
import tempfile
from collections import defaultdict
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from agentcoop.cases.planted import (  # noqa: E402
    GROUP_TEXT, NATIVE_LABELS, POPULATIONS, TASKS, load_case,
)
from agentcoop.coop.arms import ma_genome  # noqa: E402
from agentcoop.coop.claims import Claim, same_claim  # noqa: E402
from agentcoop.coop.protocol import label_claim  # noqa: E402
from agentcoop.coop.site import CaseSpec  # noqa: E402
from agentcoop.coop.tester import (  # noqa: E402
    TesterParams, TestResult, apply_bh, export_gene_stats, test_claim,
)
from agentcoop.coop.view import SiteTable, load_view, site_table, write_view  # noqa: E402
from agentcoop.eval.planted import grade, load_truth, view_errors  # noqa: E402

OUT = ROOT / "runs" / "v4"
#: Resource name of the task's symbol table (columns ensembl_id, symbol, alias).
SYMBOLS = "symbols.tsv"
#: Site-file obs columns by role (the planted layout of cases/planted.plant_task). Planted sites
#: hold one sample per donor, so the sample id is the donor id.
SAMPLE, CONDITION, LABEL = "sample", "condition", "cell_label"
GAIN = 0.05
TIE = 1e-9
ENDPOINTS = ("precision", "recall", "f1")


def tool_codebook(card: dict) -> tuple[dict[str, str], dict[str, str]]:
    """(condition code -> case group, cell label -> case population) from the committed tables: a
    code's card description is GROUP_TEXT's, a label is a NATIVE_LABELS name or a case
    population. A code or label outside them raises: fixed tools must cover the whole suite."""
    group_of_text = {text: group for group, text in GROUP_TEXT.items()}
    population_of = {p: p for p in POPULATIONS} | {
        name: p for p, names in NATIVE_LABELS.items() for name in names}
    return ({code: group_of_text[text] for code, text in card["codes"].items()},
            {label: population_of[label] for label in card["cell_labels"]})


def tool_view(root: Path, data_path: Path, card: dict, symbols: pd.DataFrame) -> None:
    """Write the view fixed tools build for one site. Symbols, Ensembl ids and aliases all
    resolve through the symbol table (Task 8 keeps symbol <-> alias a bijection and no alias is
    another gene's symbol), so the site's gene id scheme needs no detection."""
    import anndata as ad    # heavy; only scRNA cases get here

    adata = ad.read_h5ad(data_path, backed="r")
    obs, features = adata.obs.copy(), [str(v) for v in adata.var_names]
    adata.file.close()
    symbol_of = (dict(zip(symbols["symbol"], symbols["symbol"]))
                 | dict(zip(symbols["alias"], symbols["symbol"]))
                 | dict(zip(symbols["ensembl_id"], symbols["symbol"])))
    groups, populations = tool_codebook(card)
    sample = obs[SAMPLE].astype(str).to_numpy()
    samples = (pd.DataFrame({"sample": sample, "donor": sample,
                             "group": obs[CONDITION].astype(str).map(groups).to_numpy()})
               .drop_duplicates().sort_values("sample", ignore_index=True))
    cells = pd.DataFrame({"cell": obs.index.astype(str), "sample": sample,
                          "population": obs[LABEL].astype(str).map(populations).to_numpy()})
    write_view(root, genes={f: symbol_of[f] for f in features if f in symbol_of},
               samples=samples, cells=cells,
               spec={"built_by": "fixed tools (headroom gate)", "gene_mapping": SYMBOLS,
                     "labels": "committed codebook (GROUP_TEXT, NATIVE_LABELS)",
                     "unit": "donor", "normalization": "CPM"})


@dataclass
class TableSite:
    """A site seen through fixed tools: the Site methods MA-genome and cross-testing call
    (holds / export_stats / test, as coop.site.Site answers them), from a tool-view table."""

    name: str
    table: SiteTable
    params: TesterParams

    def holds(self, population: str, contrast: tuple[str, str]) -> bool:
        units = self.table.units[self.table.units["population"] == population]
        return all((units["group"] == g).any() for g in contrast)

    def export_stats(self, population: str, contrast: tuple[str, str]) -> pd.DataFrame:
        return export_gene_stats(self.table, population, contrast, self.params)

    def test(self, claim: Claim, *, test: str = "replication",
             contrast: tuple[str, str] | None = None) -> TestResult:
        return test_claim(self.table, claim, self.params, site=self.name, test=test,
                          contrast=contrast)


def tool_sites(case: CaseSpec, site_specs: list[dict], task_dir: Path) -> dict[str, TableSite]:
    """Tool view and site table of every site; the views live in a temporary directory (the
    tables are all the gate needs). A view the truth calls wrong raises."""
    symbols = pd.read_csv(case.resources[SYMBOLS], sep="\t", dtype=str)
    sites = {}
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
        for spec in site_specs:
            name, root, data = spec["name"], Path(tmp) / spec["name"], Path(spec["data_path"])
            tool_view(root, data, spec["card"], symbols)
            view, problems = load_view(root, kind=case.kind, vocab=case.vocab)
            if view is None:
                raise ValueError(f"{task_dir.name} site {name}: invalid tool view: {problems}")
            errors = view_errors(view, task_dir, name)
            if errors["error_rate"] > 0:
                raise ValueError(f"{task_dir.name} site {name}: tool view differs from the "
                                 f"truth: {errors}")
            sites[name] = TableSite(name, site_table(view, data, kind=case.kind), case.tester)
    return sites


def single_site_claims(site: TableSite, case: CaseSpec) -> list[Claim]:
    """(i): MA-genome's statistics and enrichment on one site's table, kept when the site's own
    tester supports the claim (the gate of the single-site arm)."""
    claims, _ = ma_genome([site], case, f"single:{site.name}", min_sites=1)
    return [c for c in claims if site.test(c).status == "supported"]


def scripted_ours(claims: list[Claim], sites: list, case: CaseSpec,
                  artefact: str | None) -> list[Claim]:
    """(iii): each claim tested at every site holding its population and contrast, one BH
    family; the artefact site's contradictions are set aside (the correct resolution); a claim
    is kept when the shared label rule, with its most significant supporting site as proposer,
    says `corroborated`."""
    pairs = [(c, s.test(c)) for c in claims for s in sites if s.holds(c.population, c.contrast)]
    results: dict[str, list[TestResult]] = defaultdict(list)
    for (c, _), r in zip(pairs, apply_bh([r for _, r in pairs], case.tester)):
        if not (r.site == artefact and r.status == "contradicted"):
            results[c.id].append(r)
    kept = []
    for c in claims:
        supported = [r for r in results[c.id] if r.status == "supported"]
        if not supported:
            continue
        proposer = min(supported, key=lambda r: r.p)
        others = [r for r in results[c.id] if r is not proposer]
        if label_claim(proposer, others, case.tester) == "corroborated":
            kept.append(c)
    return kept


def union_coverage(ma_claims: list[Claim], single: list[Claim], truth: dict) -> float | None:
    """Fraction of MA-tools' true hits that some single-site claim matches (None: no hits)."""
    hits = [c for c in ma_claims if grade([c], truth)["precision"] == 1.0]
    if not hits:
        return None
    return sum(any(same_claim(c, s) for s in single) for c in hits) / len(hits)


def evaluate_task(task_dir: Path) -> dict:
    case, site_specs = load_case(task_dir)
    truth = load_truth(task_dir)
    sites = tool_sites(case, site_specs, task_dir)
    single = {s["name"]: single_site_claims(sites[s["name"]], case)
              for s in site_specs if s["proposes"]}
    ma_tools, _ = ma_genome(list(sites.values()), case, "ma_tools")
    ours = scripted_ours(ma_tools, list(sites.values()), case, truth["artefact_site"])
    graded = {name: grade(claims, truth) for name, claims in single.items()}
    return {
        "knobs": truth["knobs"],
        "single": graded,
        "best_single": {m: max((g[m] for g in graded.values() if g[m] is not None), default=None)
                        for m in ENDPOINTS},
        "ma_tools": grade(ma_tools, truth),
        "scripted_ours": grade(ours, truth),
        "n_claims": {"ma_tools": len(ma_tools), "scripted_ours": len(ours),
                     **{f"single:{n}": len(c) for n, c in single.items()}},
        "union_covers_ma_hits": union_coverage(
            ma_tools, [c for claims in single.values() for c in claims], truth),
    }


def _gain(ours: float | None, ma: float | None) -> bool:
    return ours is not None and ma is not None and ours - ma >= GAIN - TIE


def verdict(rows: dict[str, dict]) -> dict:
    """The pass rule of the module docstring."""
    gain = sorted(t for t, r in rows.items()
                  if any(_gain(r["scripted_ours"][m], r["ma_tools"][m])
                         for m in ("precision", "recall")))
    ours = float(np.mean([r["scripted_ours"]["f1"] for r in rows.values()]))
    single = float(np.mean([r["best_single"]["f1"] for r in rows.values()]))
    return {"tasks_with_gain": gain, "n_tasks": len(rows), "scripted_ours_f1": ours,
            "best_single_f1": single, "pass": 2 * len(gain) >= len(rows) and ours - single > TIE}


def _f(x: float | None) -> str:
    return "-" if x is None else f"{x:.2f}"


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> None:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--suite", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--tasks", nargs="*", help="default: every task of the suite")
    args = ap.parse_args(argv)
    rows = {}
    for task_dir in sorted(d for d in (TASKS / args.suite).iterdir() if d.is_dir()):
        if args.tasks and task_dir.name not in args.tasks:
            continue
        rows[task_dir.name] = r = evaluate_task(task_dir)
        print(f"{task_dir.name} {r['knobs']}: " + "  ".join(
            f"{k} P={_f(r[k]['precision'])} R={_f(r[k]['recall'])} F1={_f(r[k]['f1'])}"
            for k in ("best_single", "ma_tools", "scripted_ours"))
            + f"  union covers MA hits: {_f(r['union_covers_ma_hits'])}", flush=True)
    if not rows:
        ap.error(f"no tasks in {TASKS / args.suite}")
    v = verdict(rows)
    OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (OUT / f"{args.suite}_headroom.json").write_text(
        json.dumps({"suite": args.suite, "tasks": rows, "verdict": v}, indent=1))
    print(f"gate {'PASS' if v['pass'] else 'FAIL'}: (iii)-(ii) >= {GAIN} in "
          f"{len(v['tasks_with_gain'])}/{v['n_tasks']} tasks; mean F1 scripted ours "
          f"{v['scripted_ours_f1']:.3f} vs best single {v['best_single_f1']:.3f}", flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

- [ ] **Step 21: Record the pass rule and the reasons in `findings.md`**

Append to `findings.md`:

```markdown
### v4 experiments: runner, analysis, headroom gate (Task 10)
- Headroom gate pass rule for planted suites (spec §6), fixed before the gate is computed
  (`experiments/headroom.py`): PASS iff scripted ours - MA-tools >= 0.05 in precision or in
  recall on at least half of the suite's tasks, and the mean F1 of scripted ours exceeds the mean
  F1 of the best single site (per task, the maximum over proposing sites). An undefined precision
  (no claims) shows no gain, and a difference within 1e-9 of 0.05 reaches the margin. Tool views
  must match the truth exactly (the gate refuses otherwise).
- The gate's scripted single-site analysis is Task 9's `arms.ma_genome(..., min_sites=1)`.
  Reason: it must be MA-genome's own statistics and enrichment, so the gate's MA-tools and
  single-site references cannot drift from the primary baseline. The arms keep the default 2.
- The gate and the arms get the gene-set library only through `arms.gene_set_library(case)`,
  which reads the `genesets/` resources Task 8's `load_case` provides (pinned end to end by
  `test_load_case_gives_ma_genome_the_gene_set_library`).
- `experiments/analyze.py` grades cause accuracy over every claim ours (and no_resolution)
  labelled, from `meta["all_claims"]`, and precision/recall over the findings. Reason: causes
  mostly explain demoted claims, so grading findings only would leave cause accuracy undefined
  on almost every task.
- The runner adds each site's harness to the site specs (`load_case` gives none): codex/finch
  alternating over the proposing sites, codex for the tester-only mimic site, fixed per suite.
- View-error rate (spec §6): the single, single_codex, ma_genome and ours arms record each site's
  view id in `meta["views"]`. `experiments/analyze.py` restores the views and reports
  `eval.planted.view_errors` per stage, harness and ids_labels knob. A left-out cell counts as an
  error, because planted site files hold no cell a correct analysis would exclude. Decision rule
  as in §6: if the rate is about 0 with the knob on, drop the view-repair claim and the knob
  before `fstest`.
```

### Task 11: Invariant tests, docs, end-to-end smoke on one `fsdev` task

This task adds the invariants no single module owns (spec §8), rewrites the project docs for v4, and runs the first paid end-to-end pass on one `fsdev` task. It then stops for review. The tests are guards over code Tasks 1–10 already wrote, so they are expected to pass on first run. Two things prove they can fail: probe tests that feed known violations into the same checks, and one step that plants a violation in the real tree.

**Files:**

**Interfaces:**

Consumes (contract names; then the Task 7/8/10 names this task relies on):

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the invariant tests**

Create `tests/test_invariants.py`:

```python
"""Invariants of v4 that no single module owns (spec §8). Offline and fast: a tiny OneK1K-like
source, no agent runs, no API key.

1. Nothing outside agentcoop/eval/ has a path to the answers.
2. A planted task's site files carry only whitelisted columns, and nothing a site agent or the
   coordinator can be given (site files, cards, resources) carries the answer.
3. Site workspaces are created under WS_ROOT, outside the repo.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import ast
import hashlib
import json
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import anndata as ad
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import pytest
import scipy.sparse as sp

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
PACKAGE = ROOT / "agentcoop"
#: Modules the scan must reach; a rename that silently drops one from it fails loudly instead.
REQUIRED = ("store.py", "proxy.py", "llm.py", "coop/claims.py", "coop/view.py",
            "coop/tester.py", "coop/protocol.py", "coop/site.py", "coop/arms.py",
            "agents/workspace.py", "agents/codex.py", "agents/react.py", "agents/finch.py",
            "agents/harness.py", "cases/planted.py")
FORBIDDEN_TEXT = ("hidden/", "truth.json", "load_truth")
#: The only obs columns a planted site file may carry (spec §7).
SITE_OBS = {"sample", "condition", "cell_label", "assay"}
#: Planting-record keys that state the answer (or, with the generator, regenerate it).
ANSWER_KEYS = {"programs", "knobs", "seed", "artefact_site", "shared_with_mimic", "composition",
               "log2fc", "up_genes", "down_genes", "fraction_removed"}
SIDE_CHANNELS = ("uns", "obsm", "obsp", "varm", "varp", "layers")
GENES = [f"G{i:03d}" for i in range(300)]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. No path to the answers outside agentcoop/eval/
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _imported(source: str, package: str) -> set[str]:
    """Fully qualified names a module imports: import statements (relative ones resolved
    against ``package``) and importlib.import_module / __import__ calls on a literal."""
    names: set[str] = set()
    for node in ast.walk(ast.parse(source)):
        if isinstance(node, ast.Import):
            names.update(a.name for a in node.names)
        elif isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):
            base = node.module or ""
            if node.level:
                parts = package.split(".")
                base = ".".join([*parts[:len(parts) - node.level + 1], *filter(None, [base])])
            names.add(base)
            names.update(f"{base}.{a.name}" for a in node.names)
        elif (isinstance(node, ast.Call) and node.args and isinstance(node.args[0], ast.Constant)
              and isinstance(node.args[0].value, str)
              and (isinstance(node.func, ast.Name) and node.func.id == "__import__"
                   or isinstance(node.func, ast.Attribute)
                   and node.func.attr == "import_module")):
            names.add(node.args[0].value)
    return names


def _violations(source: str, package: str) -> list[str]:
    out = [f"imports {n}" for n in sorted(_imported(source, package))
           if n == "agentcoop.eval" or n.startswith("agentcoop.eval.")]
    return out + [f"mentions {t!r}" for t in FORBIDDEN_TEXT if t in source]


def _answer_free_files() -> list[Path]:
    """Every module outside agentcoop/eval/: all of it runs at protocol time."""
    return sorted(p for p in PACKAGE.rglob("*.py") if p.relative_to(PACKAGE).parts[0] != "eval")


def test_protocol_side_has_no_path_to_the_answers():
    bad = {}
    for f in _answer_free_files():
        package = ".".join(f.relative_to(ROOT).parent.parts)
        if found := _violations(f.read_text(), package):
            bad[f.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()] = found
    assert not bad, bad


def test_the_scan_reaches_every_v4_module():
    scanned = {f.relative_to(PACKAGE).as_posix() for f in _answer_free_files()}
    assert set(REQUIRED) <= scanned, sorted(set(REQUIRED) - scanned)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("source,package", [
    ("import agentcoop.eval.planted", "agentcoop.coop"),
    ("from agentcoop.eval import planted", "agentcoop.coop"),
    ("from agentcoop import eval", "agentcoop.cases"),
    ("from ..eval.planted import grade", "agentcoop.coop"),
    ("from .. import eval", "agentcoop.agents"),
    ("from .eval import planted", "agentcoop"),
    ("import importlib\nimportlib.import_module('agentcoop.eval.planted')", "agentcoop"),
    ("TRUTH = 'hidden/truth.json'", "agentcoop.cases"),
    ("from agentcoop.coop.site import load_truth", "agentcoop.coop"),
])
def test_the_scan_catches_every_route_to_the_answers(source, package):
    assert _violations(source, package)


def test_the_scan_passes_lookalikes():
    assert not _violations("import agentcoop.evaluation\nfrom agentcoop import llm\n"
                           "from ..coop import claims", "agentcoop.cases")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Planted tasks: whitelisted site files, no answer in anything a site or coordinator gets
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(path, "rb") as fh:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: fh.read(1 << 20), b""):
            h.update(chunk)
    return h.hexdigest()


def _keys(node: object) -> set[str]:
    if isinstance(node, dict):
        return {str(k) for k in node} | {k for v in node.values() for k in _keys(v)}
    if isinstance(node, list):
        return {k for v in node for k in _keys(v)}
    return set()


def _string_lists(node: object) -> list[set[str]]:
    """Every list of >= 3 strings in a JSON document, as a set."""
    if isinstance(node, dict):
        return [g for v in node.values() for g in _string_lists(v)]
    if isinstance(node, list):
        if len(node) >= 3 and all(isinstance(x, str) for x in node):
            return [set(node)]
        return [g for v in node for g in _string_lists(v)]
    return []


def assert_planted_task_boundary(task_dir: Path) -> None:
    """Everything a site agent or the coordinator can be given (site files, cards, the case's
    resources) carries no part of the answer, and site files carry only whitelisted obs columns
    and no side channels. Run by the tests, and on every real planted suite before any spend."""
    from agentcoop.cases.planted import load_case
    from agentcoop.eval.planted import load_truth

    task_dir = Path(task_dir)
    visible = (task_dir / "public", task_dir / "sites")
    case, sites = load_case(task_dir)
    universe: set[str] = set()
    for s in sites:
        path = Path(s["data_path"])
        assert path.is_relative_to(task_dir / "sites"), f"{path}: site file outside sites/"
        a = ad.read_h5ad(path, backed="r")
        try:
            assert set(a.obs.columns) <= SITE_OBS, f"{path}: obs columns {list(a.obs.columns)}"
            assert a.var.shape[1] == 0, f"{path}: var columns {list(a.var.columns)}"
            filled = [k for k in SIDE_CHANNELS if len(getattr(a, k))]
            assert not filled, f"{path}: side channels {filled}"
            universe |= set(a.var_names)
        finally:
            a.file.close()
    planted_genes = [g for g in _string_lists(load_truth(task_dir)) if g <= universe]
    assert planted_genes, f"{task_dir}: no planted gene list to check against"
    hidden = {_sha256(p) for p in task_dir.rglob("*")
              if p.is_file() and not any(p.is_relative_to(v) for v in visible)}
    exposed = {Path(s["data_path"]) for s in sites} | {Path(p) for p in case.resources.values()}
    exposed |= {p for v in visible for p in v.rglob("*") if p.is_file()}
    for p in sorted(exposed):
        assert not p.is_relative_to(task_dir) or any(p.is_relative_to(v) for v in visible), \
            f"{p}: exposed but outside public/ and sites/"
        assert _sha256(p) not in hidden, f"{p}: a copy of a file outside public/ and sites/"
        if p.suffix == ".json":
            doc = json.loads(p.read_text())
            leaked = _keys(doc) & ANSWER_KEYS
            assert not leaked, f"{p}: answer keys {sorted(leaked)}"
            for strings in _string_lists(doc):
                assert all(len(strings & g) < len(g) / 2 for g in planted_genes), \
                    f"{p}: lists planted genes"


def _onek1k_like(path: Path) -> Path:
    """OneK1K's CELLxGENE layout in miniature: 64 donors x 6 case populations x 30 cells plus
    platelets (not a case population), float32 Poisson counts, an Ensembl var index with a
    categorical feature_name, and the metadata a real file carries beyond the whitelist (sex,
    age, pool, Azimuth labels, an embedding, uns), so a leaked column would show."""
    from agentcoop.cases import planted

    rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    first_label: dict[str, str] = {}
    for label, population in planted.CELL_TYPES.items():
        first_label.setdefault(population, label)
    labels = [*first_label.values(), "platelet"]
    rows = [(f"{d}_{d}", label) for d in range(64) for label in labels
            for _ in range(5 if label == "platelet" else 30)]
    obs = pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=["donor_id", "cell_type"])
    donors = obs["donor_id"].unique()
    obs["assay"] = "10x 3' v2"
    obs["sex"] = obs["donor_id"].map(dict(zip(donors, rng.choice(["female", "male"], 64))))
    obs["age"] = obs["donor_id"].map(dict(zip(donors, rng.integers(20, 90, 64)))).astype(int)
    obs["pool_number"] = obs["donor_id"].map(dict(zip(donors, rng.integers(1, 9, 64)))).astype(str)
    obs["predicted.celltype.l2"] = obs["cell_type"].map(planted.CELL_TYPES).fillna("Platelet")
    obs = obs.astype({c: "category" for c in obs.columns if c != "age"})
    obs.index = [f"bc{i}" for i in range(len(obs))]
    var = pd.DataFrame({"feature_name": pd.Categorical(GENES)},
                       index=[f"ENSG{i:011d}" for i in range(len(GENES))])
    X = sp.csr_matrix(rng.poisson(rng.uniform(1, 4, len(GENES)), (len(obs), len(GENES))),
                      dtype=np.float32)
    ad.AnnData(X=X, obs=obs, var=var, obsm={"X_umap": rng.normal(size=(len(obs), 2))},
               uns={"title": "synthetic OneK1K", "schema_version": "5.0.0"}).write_h5ad(path)
    return path


def _libraries(root: Path) -> tuple[Path, Path]:
    """Hallmark and GO BP in Enrichr text format over GENES: 12 genes per program, all annotated
    to the family's first defining GO term, so every gene of a program is a core gene."""
    from agentcoop.cases import planted

    genes = iter(GENES)
    hallmark, go = [], []
    for i, spec in enumerate(planted.FAMILIES.values()):
        members = []
        for program in spec["programs"]:
            core = [next(genes) for _ in range(12)]
            hallmark.append("\t".join([program, "", *core]))
            members += core
        go.append("\t".join([f"{spec['go_terms'][0]} (GO:{i:07d})", "", *members]))
    (root / "hallmark.txt").write_text("\n".join(hallmark))
    (root / "go.txt").write_text("\n".join(go))
    return root / "hallmark.txt", root / "go.txt"


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def planted_suite(tmp_path_factory) -> list[Path]:
    """One task per knob cell, built the way real suites are (prepare_base on a OneK1K-like
    source, then the evaluation-side build) under a temporary TASKS root."""
    from agentcoop.cases import planted
    from agentcoop.eval import planted as grader

    root = tmp_path_factory.mktemp("planted")
    hallmark, go = _libraries(root)
    with pytest.MonkeyPatch.context() as mp:
        mp.setattr(planted, "MIN_DONOR_CELLS", 100)    # the source has 180 case cells per donor
        mp.setattr(planted, "BASE", root / "base.h5ad")
        mp.setattr(planted, "HALLMARK", hallmark)
        mp.setattr(planted, "GO_BP", go)
        mp.setattr(planted, "TASKS", root / "tasks")
        planted.prepare_base(_onek1k_like(root / "onek1k.h5ad"), root / "base.h5ad", n_donors=64)
        yield grader.build("inv", [0, 1, 2, 3], pool="dev")


def test_planted_tasks_expose_only_whitelisted_columns_and_no_answer(planted_suite):
    assert len(planted_suite) == 4
    for task_dir in planted_suite:
        assert_planted_task_boundary(task_dir)


def test_the_boundary_check_catches_a_leaked_column_and_a_copied_answer(planted_suite, tmp_path):
    leaked = shutil.copytree(planted_suite[0], tmp_path / "leaked")
    site = leaked / "sites" / "A.h5ad"
    a = ad.read_h5ad(site)
    a.obs["donor"] = "D00"
    a.write_h5ad(site)
    with pytest.raises(AssertionError, match="obs columns"):
        assert_planted_task_boundary(leaked)

    copied = shutil.copytree(planted_suite[0], tmp_path / "copied")
    shutil.copy(copied / "hidden" / "truth.json", copied / "public" / "notes.json")
    with pytest.raises(AssertionError, match="notes.json"):
        assert_planted_task_boundary(copied)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Workspaces under WS_ROOT, never inside the repo
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_workspace_root_is_outside_the_repo(tmp_path):
    from agentcoop.agents import workspace

    assert not workspace.WS_ROOT.resolve().is_relative_to(ROOT.resolve()), workspace.WS_ROOT
    home = tmp_path / "home"
    env = {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items() if not k.startswith("AGENTCOOP_")}
    printed = subprocess.run(
        [sys.executable, "-c", "from agentcoop.agents.workspace import WS_ROOT; print(WS_ROOT)"],
        cwd=ROOT, env={**env, "HOME": str(home)}, capture_output=True, text=True, check=True)
    assert Path(printed.stdout.strip()) == home / "agentcoop_ws"


def test_site_workspaces_are_created_under_ws_root(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    from agentcoop.agents import workspace
    from agentcoop.cases.planted import CASE_VOCAB
    from agentcoop.coop.site import CaseSpec, make_sites
    from agentcoop.coop.tester import TesterParams

    root = tmp_path / "ws_root"
    monkeypatch.setattr(workspace, "WS_ROOT", root)
    silo = tmp_path / "silo"
    silo.mkdir()
    (silo / "symbols.tsv").write_text("ensembl_id\tsymbol\n")
    case = CaseSpec(name="planted", kind="scrna", vocab=CASE_VOCAB, question="q", default_spec={},
                    tester=TesterParams(), resources={"symbols.tsv": silo / "symbols.tsv"})
    specs = []
    for name in ("A", "M"):
        (silo / f"{name}.h5ad").write_bytes(name.encode())
        specs.append({"name": name, "data_path": silo / f"{name}.h5ad", "card": {"site": name},
                      "proposes": name != "M", "agent": "codex"})
    for site in make_sites(specs, case, task_id="t1", run_id="t1/ours/s0"):
        assert site.ws.is_dir() and site.ws.is_relative_to(root), site.ws
        assert not site.ws.resolve().is_relative_to(ROOT.resolve()), site.ws
```
